{ 1.4.10.3} vāyum eva tad anuvatsaram āpnoti | tasmāt-śunāsīrīyeṇa yajamānaḥ | anuvatsarīṇāṃ svastim āśāsta ity āśāsīta | saṃvatsaraṃ vā eṣa īpsati-ity āhuḥ | yaś cāturmāsyair yajata iti | eṣa ha tvai saṃvatsaram āpnoti | ya evaṃ vidvāṃś cāturmāsyair yajate | viśve devāḥ samayajanta | te 'gnim eva-ayajanta | ta etaṃ lokam ajayan |

{ 1.4.10.4} yasminn agniḥ | yad vaiśvadevena yajate | etam eva lokaṃ jayati | yasminn agniḥ | agner eva sāyujyam upaiti | yadā vaiśvadevena yajate | atha saṃvatsarasya gṛhapatim āpnoti | yadā saṃvatsarasya gṛhapatim āpnoti | atha sahasrayājinam āpnoti | yadā sahasrayājinam āpnoti |

{ 1.4.10.5} atha gṛhamedhinam āpnoti | yadā gṛhamedhinam āpnoti | atha-agnir bhavati | yadā-agnir bhavati | atha gaur bhavati | eṣā vai vaiśvadevasya mātrā | etad vā eteṣām avamam | ato-'to vā uttarāṇi śreyāṃsi bhavanti | yad viśve devāḥ samayajanta | tad vaiśvadevasya vaiśvadevatvam |

{ 1.4.10.6} atha-ādityo varuṇaṃ rājānaṃ varuṇapraghāsair ayajata | sa etaṃ lokam ajayat | yasminn ādityaḥ | yad varuṇapraghāsair yajate | etam eva lokaṃ jayati | yasminn ādityaḥ | ādityasyaiva sāyujyam upaiti | yad ādityo varuṇaṃ rājānaṃ varuṇapraghāsair ayajata | tad varuṇapraghāsānaṃ varuṇapraghāsatvam | atha somo rājā chandāṃsi sākamedhair ayajata |

{ 1.4.10.7} sa etaṃ lokam ajayat | yasmiṃś candramā vibhāti | yat sākamedhair yajate | etam eva lokaṃ jayati | yasmiṃś candramā vibhāti | candramasa eva sāyujyam upaiti | somo vai candramāḥ | eṣa ha tvai sākṣāt somaṃ bhakṣayati | ya evaṃ vidvān sākamedhair yajate | yat somaś ca rājā chandāṃsi ca samaidhanta |

{ 1.4.10.8} tat sākamedhānāṃ sākamedhātvam | atha-ṛtavaḥ pitaraḥ prajāpatiṃ pitaraṃ pitṛyajñena-ayajanta | ta etaṃ lokam ajayan | yasminn ṛtavaḥ | yat pitṛyajñena yajate | etam eva lokaṃ jayati | yasminn ṛtavaḥ | ṛtūnām eva sāyujyam upaiti | yad ṛtavaḥ pitaraḥ prajāpatiṃ pitaraṃ pitṛyajñena-ayajanta | tat pitṛyajñasya pitṛyajñatvam |

{ 1.4.10.9} atha-oṣadhaya imaṃ devaṃ tryambakair ayajanta prathemahi-iti | tato vai tā aprathanta | ya evaṃ vidvāṃs tryambakair yajate | prathate prajayā paśubhiḥ | atha vāyuḥ parameṣṭhinaṃ śunāsīrīyeṇa-ayajata | sa etaṃ lokam ajayat | yasmin vāyuḥ | yat-śunāsīrīyeṇa yajate | etam eva lokaṃ jayati | yasmin vāyuḥ |

{ 1.4.10.10} vāyor eva sāyujyam upaiti | brahmavādino vadanti | pra cāturmāsyayājī mīyatā3 na pramīyatā3 iti | jīvan vā eṣa ṛtūn apyeti | yadi vasantā pramīyate | vasanto bhavati | yadi grīṣme grīṣmaḥ | yadi varṣāsu varṣāḥ | yadi śaradi śarat | yadi heman hemantaḥ | ṛtur bhūtvā saṃvatsaram apyeti | saṃvatsaraḥ prajāpatiḥ | prajāpatir vāvaiṣaḥ |



prapāṭhaka: 5 nakṣatreṣṭakā anuvāka 1 vaikṛtinakṣatreṣṭakāmantrāḥ { 1.5.1.1} agneḥ kṛttikāḥ | śukraṃ parastāt- jyotir avastāt | prajāpate rohiṇī | āpaḥ parastād oṣadhayo 'vastāt | somasya-invakā | vitatāni parastād vayanto 'vastāt | rudrasya bāhū | mṛgayavaḥ parastād vikṣāro 'vastāt | adityai punarvasū | vātaḥ parastād ārdram avastāt |

{ 1.5.1.2} bṛhaspates tiṣyaḥ | juhvataḥ parastād yajamānā avastāt | sarpāṇām āśreṣāḥ | abhyāgacchantaḥ parastād abhyānṛtyanto 'vastāt | pitṛṇāṃ maghāḥ | rudantaḥ parastād apabhraṃśo 'vastāt | aryamṇaḥ pūrve phalgunī | jāyā parastād ṛṣabho 'vastāt | bhagasya-uttare | vahatavaḥ parastād vahamānā avastāt |

{ 1.5.1.3} devasya savitur hastaḥ | prasavaḥ parastāt sanir avastāt | indrasya citrā | ṛtaṃ parastāt satyam avastāt | vāyor niṣṭyā | vratatiḥ parastād asiddhir avastāt | indrāgniyor viśākhe | yugāni parastāt kṛṣamāṇā avastāt | mitrasya-anūrādhāḥ | abhyārohat parastād abhyārūḍham avastāt |

{ 1.5.1.4} indrasya rohiṇī | śṛṇat parastāt pratiśṛṇad avastāt | nirṛtyai mūlavarhaṇī | pratibhañjantaḥ parastāt pratiśṛṇanto 'vastāt | apāṃ pūrvā aṣāḍhāḥ | varcaḥ parastāt samitir avastāt | viśveṣāṃ devānām uttarāḥ | abhijayat parastād abhijitam avastāt | viṣṇoḥ śroṇā | pṛcchamānāḥ parastāt panthā avastāt |

{ 1.5.1.5} vasūnāṃ śraviṣṭhāḥ | bhūtaṃ parastād bhūtir avastāt | indrasya śatabhiṣak | viśvavyacāḥ parastād viśvakṣitir avastāt | ajasya-ekapadaḥ pūrve proṣṭhapadāḥ | vaiśvānaraṃ parastād vaiśvāvasavam avastāt | aher budhniyasya-uttare | abhiṣiñcantaḥ parastād abhiṣuṇvanto 'vastāt | pūṣṇo revatī | gāvaḥ parastād vatsā avastāt | aśvinor aśvayujau | grāmaḥ parastāt senā-avastāt | yamasya-apabharaṇīḥ | apakarṣantaḥ parastād apavahanto 'vastāt | pūrṇā paścād yat te devā adadhuḥ |

anuvāka 2 karmānuṣṭhānāya puṇyakālaḥ { 1.5.2.1} yat puṇyaṃ nakṣatram | tad baṭ kurvīta-upavyuṣam | yadā vai sūrya udeti | atha nakṣatraṃ na-eti | yāvati tatra sūryo gacchet | yatra jaghanyaṃ paśyet | tāvati kurvīta yatkārī syāt | puṇyāha eva kurute | evaṃ ha vai yajñeṣuṃ ca śatadyumnaṃ ca mātsyo niravasāyayāñ cakāra |

{ 1.5.2.2} yo vai nakṣatriyaṃ prajāpatiṃ veda | ubhayor enaṃ lokayor viduḥ | hasta eva-asya hastaḥ | citrā śiraḥ | niṣṭyā hṛdayam | ūrū viśākhe | pratiṣṭhā-anūrādhāḥ | eṣa vai nakṣatriyaḥ prajāpatiḥ | ya evaṃ veda | ubhayor enaṃ lokayor viduḥ |

{ 1.5.2.3} asmiṃś ca-amuṣmiṃś ca | yāṃ kāmayeta duhitaraṃ priyā syād iti | tāṃ niṣṭyāyāṃ dadyāt | priyā-eva bhavati | naiva tu punar āgacchati | abhijit- nāma nakṣatram | upariṣṭād aṣāḍhānām | avastāt-śroṇāyai | devāsurāḥ saṃyattā āsan | te devās tasmin nakṣatre 'bhyajayan |

{ 1.5.2.4} yad abhyajayan | tad abhijito 'bhijittvam | yaṃ kāmayeta-anapajayyaṃ jayed iti | tam etasmin nakṣatre yātayet | anapajayyam eva jayati | pāpaparājitam iva tu | prajāpatiḥ paśūn asṛjata | te nakṣatraṃnakṣatram upātiṣṭhanta | te samāvanta eva-abhavan | te revatīm upātiṣṭhanta |

{ 1.5.2.10} te revatyāṃ prābhavan | tasmād revatyāṃ paśūnāṃ kurvīta | yat kiṃ ca-arvācīnaṃ somāt | pra-eva bhavanti | salilaṃ vā idam antarāsīt | yad ataran | tat tārakāṇāṃ tārakatvam | yo vā iha yajate | amuṃ sa lokaṃ nakṣate | tan nakṣatrāṇāṃ nakṣatratvam |

{ 1.5.2.6} devagṛhā vai nakṣatrāṇi | ya evaṃ veda | gṛhy eva bhavati | yāni vā imāni pṛthivyāś citrāṇi | tāni nakṣatrāṇi | tasmād aślīlanāmaṃś citre | na-āvasyen na yajeta | yathā pāpāhe kurute | tādṛg eva tat | devanakṣatrāṇi vā anyāni |

{ 1.5.2.7} yamanakṣattrāṇy anyāni | kṛttikāḥ prathamam | viśākhe uttamam | tāni devanakṣatrāṇi | anūrādhāḥ prathamam | apabharaṇīr uttamam | tāni yamanakṣatrāṇi | yāni devanakṣatrāṇi | tāni dakṣiṇena pariyanti | yāni yamanakṣatrāṇi |

{ 1.5.2.8} tāny uttareṇa | anv eṣām arātsma-iti | tad anūrādhāḥ | jyeṣṭham eṣām avadhiṣma-iti | taj jyeṣṭhaghnī | mūlam eṣām avṛkṣāma-iti | tan mūlabarhaṇī { mūlavarhaṇī} | yan na-asahanta | tad aṣāḍhāḥ | yad aśloṇat |

{ 1.5.2.9} tat-śroṇā | yad aśṛṇot | tat-śraviṣṭhāḥ | yat-śatam abhiṣajyan | tat-śatabhiṣak | proṣṭhapadeṣu-udayacchanta | revatyām aravanta | aśvayujor ayuñjata | apabharaṇīṣv apāvahan | tāni vā etāni yamanakṣatrāṇi | yāny eva devanakṣatrāṇi | teṣu kurvīta yatkārī syāt | puṇyāha eva kurute |

anuvāka 3 divasabhāgopajīvanena puṇyakālaḥ { 1.5.3.1} devasya savituḥ prātaḥ prasvaḥ prāṇaḥ | varuṇasya sāyam āsavo 'pānaḥ | yat pratīcīnaṃ prātastanāt | prācīnaṃ saṃgavāt | tato devā agniṣṭomaṃ niramimata | tat tad āttavīryaṃ nirmārgaḥ | mitrasya saṃgavaḥ | tat puṇyaṃ tejasvy ahaḥ | tasmā tarhi paśavaḥ samāyanti | yat pratīcīnaṃ saṃgavāt |

{ 1.5.3.2} prācīnaṃ madhyaṃdināt | tato devā ukthyaṃ niramimata | tat tad āttavīryaṃ nirmārgaḥ | bṛhaspater madhyaṃdinaḥ | tat puṇyaṃ tejasvy ahaḥ | tasmāt tarhi tekṣṇiṣṭhaṃ tapati | yat pratīcīnaṃ madhyaṃdināt | prācīnam aparāhṇāt | tato devāḥ ṣoḍaśinaṃ niramimata | tat tad āttavīryaṃ nirmārgaḥ |

{ 1.5.3.17} bhagasya-aparāhṇaḥ | tat puṇyaṃ tejasvyahaḥ | tasmād aparāhṇe kumāryo bhagam icchamānāś caranti | yat pratīcīnam aparāhṇāt | prācīnaṃ sāyāt | tato devā atirātraṃ niramimata | tat tad āttavīryaṃ nirmārgaḥ | varuṇasya sāyam | tat puṇyaṃ tejasvyahaḥ | tasmāt tarhi na-anṛtaṃ vadet |

{ 1.5.3.4} brāhmaṇo vā aṣṭāviṃśo nakṣatrāṇām | samānasya-ahnaḥ pañca puṇyāni nakṣatrāṇi | catvāry aślīlāni | tāni nava | yac ca parastān nakṣatrāṇāṃ yac ca-avastāt | tāny ekādaśa | brāhmaṇo dvādaśaḥ | ya evaṃ vidvān saṃvatsaraṃ vrataṃ carati | saṃvatsareṇaivāsya vrataṃ guptaṃ bhavati | samānasya-ahnaḥ pañca puṇyāni nakṣatrāṇi | catvāry aślīlāni | tāni nava | āgneyī rātriḥ | aindram ahaḥ | tāny ekādaśa | ādityo dvādaśaḥ | ya evaṃ vidvān saṃvatsaraṃ vrataṃ carati | saṃvatsareṇaivāsya vrataṃ guptaṃ bhavati |

anuvāka 4 somaśeṣabhūtā pātrastutiḥ { 1.5.4.1} brahmavādino vadanti | kati pātrāṇi yajñaṃ vahanti-iti | trayodaśa-iti | brūyāt | sa yad brūyāt | kas tāni niramimīta-iti | prajāpatir iti brūyāt | sa yad brūyāt | kutas tāni niramimīta-iti | ātmana iti | prāṇāpānābhyām eva-upāṃśvantaryāmau niramimīta |

{ 1.5.4.2} vyānād upāṃśusavanam | vāca aindravāyavam | dakṣakratubhyāṃ maitrāvaruṇam | śrotrād āśvinam | cakṣuṣaḥ śukrāmanthinau | ātmana āgrayaṇam | aṅgebhya ukthyam | āyuṣo dhruvam | pratiṣṭhāyā ṛtupātre | yajñaṃ vāva taṃ prajāpatir niramimīta | sa nirmito na-ādhriyata samavlīyata | sa etān prajāpatir apivāpān apaśyat | tān niravapat | tair vai sa yajñam apyavapat | yad apivāpā bhavanti | yajñasya dhṛtyā asaṃvlayāya |

anuvāka 5 cāturmāsyaśeṣāḥ vapanamantrāḥ { 1.5.5.1} ṛtam eva parameṣṭhi | ṛtaṃ na-atyeti kiṃ cana | ṛte samudra āhitaḥ | ṛte bhūmir iyaṃ śritā | agnis tigmena śociṣā | tapa ākrāntam uṣṇihā | śiras tapasy āhitam | vaiśvānarasya tejasā | ṛtena-asya nivartaye | satyena parivartaye |

{ 1.5.5.2} tapasā'sya-anuvartaye | śivena-asya-upavartaye | śagmena-asya-abhivartaye | tad ṛtaṃ tat satyam | tad vrataṃ tat-śakeyam | tena śakeyaṃ tena rādhyāsam | yad gharmaḥ paryavartayat | antān pṛthivyā divaḥ | agnir īśāna ojasā | varuṇo dhītibhiḥ saha |

{ 1.5.5.3} indro marudbhiḥ sakhibhiḥ saha | agnis tigmena śociṣā | tapa ākrāntam uṣṇihā | śiras tapasy āhitam | vaiśvānarasya tejasā | ṛtena-asya nivartaye | satyena parivartaye | tapasā'sya-anuvartaye | śivena-asya-upavartaye | śagmena-asya-abhivartaye |

{ 1.5.5.4} tad ṛtaṃ tat satyam | tad vrataṃ tat-śakeyam | tena śakeyaṃ tena rādhyāsam | yo asyāḥ pṛthivyās tvaci | nivartayaty oṣadhīḥ | agnir īśāna ojasā | varuṇo dhītibhiḥ saha | indro marudbhiḥ sakhibhiḥ saha | agnis tigmena śociṣā | tapa ākrāntam uṣṇihā |

{ 1.5.5.5} śiras tapasy āhitam | vaiśvānarasya tejasā | ṛtena-asya nivartaye | satyena parivartaye | tapasā'sya-anuvartaye | śivena-asya-upavartaye | śagmena-asya-abhivartaye | tad ṛtaṃ tat satyam | tad vrataṃ tat-śakeyam | tena śakeyaṃ tena rādhyāsam |

{ 1.5.5.6} ekaṃ māsam udasṛjat | parameṣṭhī prajābhyaḥ | tena-ābhyo maha āvahat | amṛtaṃ martyābhyaḥ | prajām anu prajāyase | tad u te martya-amṛtam | yena māsā ardhamāsāḥ | ṛtavaḥ parivatsarāḥ | yena te te prajāpate | ījānasya nyavartayan |

{ 1.5.5.7} tena-aham asya brahmaṇā | nivartayāmi jīvase | agnis tigmena śociṣā | tapa ākrāntam uṣṇihā | śiras tapasy āhitam | vaiśvānarasya tejasā | ṛtena-asya nivartaye | satyena parivartaye | tapasā'sya-anuvartaye | śivena-asya-upavartaye | śagmena-asya-abhivartaye | tad ṛtaṃ tat satyam | tad vrataṃ tat-śakeyam | tena śakeyaṃ tena rādhyāsam |

anuvāka 6 cāturmāsyaśeṣo vapanavidhiḥ { 1.5.6.1} devā vai yad yajñe 'kurvata | tad asurā akurvata | te 'surā ūrdhvaṃ pṛṣṭhebhyo na-apaśyan | te keśān agre 'vapanta | atha śmaśrūṇi | atha-upapakṣau | tatas te 'vāñca āyan | parābhavan | yasyaivaṃ vapanti | avāṅ eti |

{ 1.5.6.2} atho parā-iva bhavati | atha devā ūrdhvaṃ pṛṣṭhebhyo 'paśyan | ta upapakṣāv agre 'vapanta | atha śmaśrūṇi | atha keśān | tatas te 'bhavan | suvargaṃ lokam āyan | yasyaivaṃ vapanti | bhavaty ātmanā | atho suvargaṃ lokam eti |

{ 1.5.6.3} athaitan manur vaptre mithunam apaśyat | sa śmaśrūṇy agre 'vapata | atha-upapakṣau | atha keśān | tato vai sa prājāyata prajayā paśubhiḥ | yasyaivaṃ vapanti | pra prajayā paśubhir mithunair jāyate | deva-asurāḥ saṃyattā āsan | te saṃvatsare vyāyacchanta | tān devāś cāturmāsyair eva-abhi prāyuñjata |

{ 1.5.6.4} vaiśvadevena caturo māso 'vṛñjata-indrarājānaḥ | tān-śīrṣan {ānaṃdā. tāñ chīrṣaṃ} ni ca-avartayanta pari ca | varuṇapraghāsaiś caturo māso 'vṛñjata varuṇarājānaḥ | tāñ chīrṣaṃ ni ca-avartayanta pari ca | sākamedhaiś caturo māso 'vṛñjata somarājānaḥ | tāñ chīrṣaṃ ni ca-avartayanta pari ca | yā saṃvatsara upajīvā-āsīt | tām eṣām avṛñjata | tato devā abhavan | parā-asurāḥ |

{ 1.5.6.5} ya evaṃ vidvāṃś cāturmāsyair yajate | bhrātṛvyasyaiva māso vṛktvā | śīrṣan ni ca vartayate pari ca | yaiṣā saṃvatsara upajīvā | vṛṅkte tāṃ bhrātṛvyasya | kṣudhā-asya bhrātṛvyaḥ parābhavati | lohitāyasena nivartayate | yad vā imām agnir ṛtāv āgate nivartayati | etad evaināṃ rūpaṃ kṛtvā nivartayati | sā tataḥ śvaḥ-śvo bhūyasī bhavanty eti |

{ 1.5.6.6} prajāyate | ya evaṃ vidvān lohitāyasena nivartayate | etad eva rūpaṃ kṛtvā nivartayate | sa tataḥ śvaḥ-śvo bhūyān bhavann eti | pra-eva jāyate | treṇyā śalalyā nivartayeta | trīṇi trīṇi vai devānām ṛddhāni | trīṇi cchandāṃsi | trīṇi savanāni | traya ime lokāḥ |

{ 1.5.6.7} ṛdbhyām eva tad vīrya eṣu lokeṣu pratitiṣṭhati | yac cāturmāsyayājy ātmano na-avadyet | devebhya āvṛścyeta | catṛṣu-catṛṣu māseṣu nivartayeta | parokṣam eva tad devebhya ātmano 'vadyaty anāvraskāya | devānāṃ vā eṣa ānītaḥ | yaś cāturmāsyayājī ya evaṃ vidvān ni ca vartayate pari ca | devatā eva-apyeti | na-asya rudraḥ prajāṃ paśūn abhimanyate |

anuvāka 7 saṃtatākhyeṣṭakā mantrāḥ { 1.5.7.1} āyuṣaḥ prāṇaṃ saṃtanu | prāṇād apānaṃ saṃtanu | apānād vyānaṃ saṃtanu | vyānāc cakṣuḥ saṃtanu | cakṣuṣaḥ śrotraṃ saṃtanu | śrotrān manaḥ saṃtanu | manaso vācaṃ saṃtanu | vāca ātmānaṃ saṃtanu | ātmanaḥ pṛthivīṃ saṃtanu | pṛthivyā antarikṣaṃ saṃtanu | antarikṣād divaṃ saṃtanu | divaḥ suvaḥ saṃtanu |

anuvāka 8 atharvaśirobhidhānānāṃ iṣṭakānāṃ mantrāḥ { 1.5.8.1} indro dadhīco asthabhiḥ | vṛtrāṇy apratiṣkutaḥ | jaghāna navatīr nava | icchann aśvasya yat-śiraḥ | parvateṣv apaśritam | tad vidat-śaryaṇāvati | atra-āha goramanvata | nāma tvaṣṭur apīcyam | itthā candramaso gṛhe | indram id gāthino bṛhat |

{ 1.5.8.2} indram arkebhir arkiṇaḥ | indraṃ vāṇīr anūṣata | indra id- haryoḥ sacā | saṃmiśla ā vacoyujā | indro vajrī hiraṇyayaḥ | indro dīrghāya cakṣase | ā sūryaṃ rohayad divi | vi gobhir adrimairayat | indra vājeṣu no ava | sahasrapradhaneṣu ca |

{ 1.5.8.3} ugra ugrābhir ūtibhiḥ | tam indraṃ vājayāmasi | mahe vṛtrāya hantave | sa vṛṣā vṛṣabho'bhuvat} | indraḥ sa dāmane kṛtaḥ | ojiṣṭhaḥ sa bale hitaḥ | dyumnī ślokī sa saumyaḥ | girā vajro na saṃbhṛtaḥ | sabalo anapacyutaḥ | vavakṣur ugro astṛtaḥ |

anuvāka 9 dīkṣaṇīyādīni bahiṣpavamānopakramāntāni somāṅgāni { 1.5.9.1} deva-asurāḥ saṃyattā āsan | sa prajāpatir indraṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ putram apa nyadhatta | na-id enam asurā balīyāṃso 'hanann iti | prahrādo ha vai kāyādhavaḥ | virocanaṃ svaṃ putram apa nyadhatta | na-id enaṃ devā ahanann iti | te devāḥ prajāpatim upasametya-ūcuḥ | na-arājakasya yuddham asti | indram anvicchāma-iti | taṃ yajñakratubhir anvaicchan |

{ 1.5.9.2} taṃ yajñakratubhir na-anvavindan | tam iṣṭibhir anvaicchan | tam iṣṭibhir anvavindan | tad iṣṭīnām iṣṭitvam | eṣṭayo ha vai nāma | tā iṣṭaya ity ācakṣate parokṣeṇa | parokṣapriyā iva hi devāḥ | tasmā etam āgnāvaiṣṇavam ekādaśakapālaṃ dīkṣaṇīyaṃ niravapan | tad apadrutya-atanvata | tān patnīsaṃyājān ta upānayan |

{ 1.5.9.3} te tadantam eva kṛtvā-udadravan | te prāyaṇīyam abhi samārohan | tad apadrutya-atanvata | tān-śaṃyvanta upānayan | te tadantam eva kṛtvā-udadravan | ta ātithyam abhi samārohan | tad apadrutya-atanvata | tān iḍānta upānayan | te tadantam eva kṛtvā-udadravan | tasmād etā etadantā iṣṭayaḥ saṃtiṣṭhante |

{ 1.5.9.4} evaṃ hi devā akurvata | iti devā akurvata | ity u vai manuṣyāḥ kurvate | te devā ūcuḥ | yad vā idam uccair yajñena carāma | tan no 'surāḥ pāpmā-anuvindanti | upāṃśu-upasadā carāma | tathā no 'surāḥ pāpmā na-anuvetsyanti-iti | ta upāṃśu-upasadam atanvata | tisra eva sāmidhenīr anūcya |

{ 1.5.9.5} sruveṇa-āghāram āghārya | tisraḥ parācīr āhutīr hutvā | sruveṇa-upasadaṃ juhavāñ cakruḥ | ugraṃ vaco apāvadhīṃ tveṣaṃ vaco apāvadhīṃ svāhā-iti | aśanayāpipāse ha vā ugraṃ vacaḥ | enaś ca vairahatyaṃ ca tveṣaṃ vacaḥ | etaṃ ha vāva tac caturdhāvihitaṃ pāpmānaṃ devā apajaghnire | tatho evaitad evaṃvid yajamānaḥ | tisra eva sāmidhenīr anūcya | sruveṇa-āghāram āghārya |

{ 1.5.9.6} tisraḥ parācīr āhutīr hutvā | sruveṇa-upasadaṃ juhoti | ugraṃ vaco apāvadhīṃ tveṣaṃ vaco apāvadhīṃ svāhā-iti | aśanayāpipāse ha vā ugraṃ vacaḥ | enaś ca vairahatyaṃ ca tveṣaṃ vacaḥ | etam eva tat- caturdhāvihitaṃ pāpmānaṃ yajamāno 'pahate | te 'bhinīya-eva-ahaḥ paśum ālabhanta | ahna eva tad devā avartiṃ pāpmānaṃ mṛtyum apajaghnire | tena-abhinīya-iva rātreḥ prācaran | rātriyā eva tad devā avartiṃ pāpmānaṃ mṛtyum apajaghnire |

{ 1.5.9.7} tasmād abhinīya-eva-ahaḥ paśum ālabheta | ahna eva tad yajamāno 'vartiṃ pāpmānaṃ bhrātṛvyān apanudate | tena-abhinīya-iva rātreḥ pracaret | rātriyā eva tad yajamāno 'vartiṃ pāpmānaṃ bhrātṛvyān apanudate | sa eṣa upavasathīye 'han dvidevatyaḥ paśur ālabhyate | dvayaṃ vā asmiṃl loke yajamānaḥ | asthi ca māṃsaṃ ca | asthi caiva tena māṃsaṃ ca yajamānaḥ saṃskurute | tā vā etāḥ pañca devatāḥ | agnīṣomāv agnir mitrāvaruṇau | { 1.5.9.7} pañca pañcī vai yajamānaḥ | tvaṅ māṃsaṃ snāva-asthi majjā | etam eva tat pañcadhā vihitam ātmānaṃ varuṇapāśān muñcati | bheṣajatāyai nirvaruṇatvāya | taṃ saptabhiś chandobhiḥ prātar ahvayan | tasmāt sapta caturuttarāṇi chandāṃsi prātaranuvāke 'nūcyante | tam etayā-upasametya-upāsīdan | upa-asmai gāyatā nara iti | tasmād etayā bahiṣpavamāna upasadyaḥ |

anuvāka 10 trivṛdādi stomāḥ { 1.5.10.1} sa samudra uttarataḥ prājvalad bhūmyantena | eṣa vāva sa samudraḥ | yac cātvālaḥ | eṣa u veva sa bhūmyantaḥ | yad vedyantaḥ | tad etat triśalaṃ tripūruṣam | tasmāt taṃ trivitastaṃ khananti | sa suvarṇarajatābhyāṃ kuśībhyāṃ parigṛhīta āsīt | taṃ yad asyā adhyajanayan | tasmād ādityaḥ |

{ 1.5.10.2} atha yat suvarṇarajatābhyāṃ kuśībhyāṃ parigṛhīta āsīt | sāsya kauśikatā | taṃ trivṛtā-abhi prāstuvata | taṃ trivṛtā-ādadata | taṃ trivṛtā-aharan | yāvatī trivṛto mātrā | taṃ pañcadaśena-abhi prāstuvata | taṃ pañcadaśenā-ādadata | taṃ pañcadaśena-aharan | yāvatī pañcadaśasya mātrā |

{ 1.5.10.3} taṃ saptadaśena-abhi prāstuvata | taṃ saptadaśena-ādadata | taṃ saptadaśena-aharan | yāvatī saptadaśasya mātrā | tasya saptadaśena hriyamāṇasya tejo haro 'patat | tam ekaviṃśena-abhi prāstuvata | tam ekaviṃśena-ādadata | tam ekaviṃśena-aharan | yāvaty ekaviṃśasya mātrā | te yat trivṛtā stuvate |

{ 1.5.10.4} trivṛtā-eva tad yajamānam ādadate | taṃ trivṛtā-eva haranti | yāvatī trivṛto mātrā | agnir vai trivṛt | yāvad vā agner dahato dhūma udetya-anu vyeti | tāvatī trivṛto mātrā | agner evainaṃ tat | mātrāṃ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ gamayanti | atho yat pañcadaśena stuvate | 0 pañcadaśena-eva tad yajamānam ādadate |

{ 1.5.10.5} taṃ pañcadaśena-eva haranti | yāvatī pañcadaśasya mātrā | candramā vai pañcadaśaḥ | eṣa hi pañcadaśyām apakṣīyate | pañcadaśyām āpūryate | candramasa evainaṃ tat | mātrāṃ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ gamayanti | atha yat saptadaśena stuvate | saptadaśena-eva tad yajamānam ādadate | taṃ saptadaśena-eva haranti |

{ 1.5.10.6} yāvatī saptadaśasya mātrā | prajāpatir vai saptadaśaḥ | prajāpater evainaṃ tat | mātrāṃ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ gamayanti | atha yad ekaviṃśena stuvate | ekaviṃśena-eva tad yajamānam ādadate | tam ekaviṃśena-eva haranti | yāvaty ekaviṃśasya mātrā | asau vā āditya ekaviṃśaḥ | ādityasyaivainaṃ tat |

{ 1.5.10.7} mātrāṃ sāyujyaṃ salokatāṃ gamayanti | te kuśyau | vyaghnan | te ahorātre abhavatām | ahar eva suvarṇā-abhavat | rajatā rātriḥ | sa yad āditya udeti | etām eva tat suvarṇāṃ kuśīm anu sameti | atha yad astameti | etām eva tad rajatāṃ kuśīm anu saṃviśati | prahrādo ha vai kāyādhavaḥ | virocanaṃ svaṃ putram udāsyat | sa pradaro 'bhavat | tasmāt pradarād udakaṃ na-ācāmet |

anuvāka 11 savanīya puroḍāśāḥ { 1.5.11.1} ye vai catvāraḥ stomāḥ | kṛtaṃ tat | atha ye pañca | kaliḥ saḥ | tasmāc catuṣṭomaḥ | tac catuṣṭomasya catuṣṭomatvam | tad āhuḥ | katamāni tāni jyotīṃṣi | ya etasya stomā iti | trivṛt pañcadaśaḥ saptadaśa ekaviṃśaḥ |

{ 1.5.11.2} etāni vāva tāni jyotīṃṣi | ya etasya stomāḥ | so 'bravīt | saptadaśena hriyamāṇo vyaleśiṣi | bhiṣajyata mā-iti | tam aśvinau dhānābhir abhiṣajyatām | pūṣā karambheṇa | bhāratī parivāpeṇa | mitrāvaruṇau payasyayā | tad āhuḥ |

{ 1.5.11.3} yad aśvibhyāṃ dhānāḥ | pūṣṇaḥ karambhaḥ | bhāratyai parivāpaḥ | mitrāvaruṇayoḥ payasyātha | kasmād eteṣāṃ haviṣām indram eva yajanti-iti | etā hy enaṃ devatā iti brūyāt | etair havirbhir abhiṣajyaṃs tasmād iti | taṃ vasavo 'ṣṭākapālena prātaḥsavane 'bhiṣajyan | rudrā ekādaśakapālena mādhyandine savane | viśve devā dvādaśakapālena tṛtīyasavane |

{ 1.5.11.4} sa yad aṣṭākapālān prātaḥsavane kuryāt | ekādaśa kapālān mādhyandine savane | dvādaśakapālāṃs tṛtīyasavane | viloma tad yajñasya kriyeta | ekādaśakapālān eva prātaḥsavane kuryāt | ekādaśakapālān mādhyandine savane | ekādaśakapālāṃs tṛtīyasavane | yajñasya salomatvāya | tad āhuḥ | yad vasūnāṃ prātaḥsavanam | rudrāṇāṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanam | viśveṣāṃ devānāṃ tṛtīyasavanam | atha kasmād eteṣāṃ haviṣām indram eva yajanti-iti | etā hy enaṃ devatā iti brūyāt | etair havirbhir abhiṣajyaṃs tasmād iti |

anuvāka 12 sapta chandāṃsi { 1.5.12.1} tasyā vāco 'vapādād abibhayuḥ | tam eteṣu saptasu chandaḥsv aśrayan | yad aśrayan | tat-śrāyantīyasya śrāyantīyatvam | yad avārayan | tad vāravantīyasya vāravantīyatvam | tasyā vāca eva-avapādād abibhayuḥ | tasmā etāni sapta caturuttarāṇi chandāṃsy upādadhuḥ | teṣām ati trīṇy aricyanta | na trīṇy udabhavan |

{ 1.5.12.2} sa bṛhatīm eva-aspṛśat | dvābhyām akṣarābhyām | ahorātrābhyām eva | tad āhuḥ | katamā sā devākṣarā bṛhatī | yasyāṃ tat pratyatiṣṭhat | dvādaśa paurṇamāsyaḥ | dvādaśa-aṣṭakāḥ | dvādaśa-amāvāsyāḥ | eṣā vāva sā devākṣarā bṛhatī |

{ 1.5.12.3} yasyāṃ tat pratyatiṣṭhad iti | yāni ca chandāṃsy atyaricyanta | yāni ca na-udabhavan | tāni nirvīryāṇi hīnāny amanyanta | sā'bravīd bṛhatī | mām eva bhūtvā | mām upa saṃśrayata-iti | caturbhir akṣarair anuṣṭug bṛhatīṃ na-udabhavat | caturbhir akṣaraiḥ paṅktir bṛhatīm atyaricyata | tasyām etāni catvāry akṣarāṇy apacchidya-ādadhāt |

{ 1.5.12.4} te bṛhatī eva bhūtvā | bṛhatīm upa samaśrayatām | aṣṭābhir akṣarair uṣṇig bṛhatīṃ na-udabhavat | aṣṭābhir akṣarais triṣṭug bṛhatīm atyaricyata | tasyām etāny aṣṭāv akṣarāṇy apacchidya-ādadhāt | te bṛhatī eva bhūtvā | bṛhatīm upa samaśrayatām | dvādaśabhir akṣarair gāyatrī bṛhatīṃ na-udabhavat | dvādaśabhir akṣarair jagatī bṛhatīm atyaricyata | tasyām etāni dvādaśa-akṣarāṇy apacchidya-ādadhāt |

{ 1.5.12.5} te bṛhatī eva bhūtvā | bṛhatīm upa samaśrayatām | so 'bravīt prajāpatiḥ | chandāṃsi ratho me bhavat | yuṣmābhir aham etam adhvānam anu saṃcarāṇi-iti | tasya gāyatrī ca jagatī ca pakṣāv abhavatām | uṣṇik ca triṣṭup ca praṣṭyau | anuṣṭup ca paṅktiś ca dhuryau | bṛhaty eva-uddhir abhavat | sa etaṃ chandoratham āsthāya | etam adhvānam anu samacarat | etaṃ ha vai chandoratham āsthāya | etam adhvānam anu saṃcarati | yenaiṣa etat saṃcarati | ya evaṃ vidvān somena yajate | ya u cainam evaṃ veda |




prapāṭhaka: 6 rājasūyaḥ anuvāka 1 { 1.6.1.1} anumatyai puroḍāśam aṣṭākapālaṃ nirvapati | ye pratyañcaḥ śamyāyā avaśīyante | tan nairṛtam ekakapālam | iyaṃ vā anumatiḥ | iyaṃ nirṛtiḥ | nairṛtena pūrveṇa pracarati | pāpmānam eva nirṛtiṃ pūrvāṃ niravadayate | ekakapālo bhavati | ekadhā-eva nirṛtiṃ niravadayate | yad ahutvā gārhapatya īyuḥ |

{ 1.6.1.2} rudro bhūtvā-agnir anūtthāya | adhvaryuṃ ca yajamānaṃ ca hanyāt | vīhi svāhā-āhutiṃ juṣāṇa ity āha | āhutyā-evainaṃ śamayati | na-ārtim ārchaty adhvaryur na yajamānaḥ | eka-ulmukena yanti | tad dhi nirṛtyai bhāgadheyam | imāṃ diśam nayati | eṣā vai nirṛtyai dik | svāyām eva diśi nirṛtiṃ niravadayate |

{ 1.6.1.3} svakṛta iriṇe juhoti pradare vā | etad vai nirṛtyā āyatanam | sva evā''yatane nirṛtiṃ niravadayate | eṣa te nirṛte bhāga ity āha | nirdiśaty evainām | bhūte haviṣmaty asi-ity āha | bhūtim eva-upāvartate | muñca-imam aṃhasa ity āha | aṃhasa evainaṃ muñcati | aṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ juhoti |

{ 1.6.1.4} antata eva nirṛtiṃ niravadayate | kṛṣṇaṃ vāsaḥ kṛṣṇatūṣaṃ dakṣiṇā | etad vai nirṛtyai rūpam | rūpeṇaiva nirṛtiṃ niravadayate | apratīkṣam āyanti | nirṛtyā antarhityai | svāhā namo ya idaṃ cakāra-iti punar etya gārhapatye juhoti | āhutyā-eva namasyanto gārhapatyamupāvartante | ānumatena pracarati | iyaṃ vā anumatiḥ |

{ 1.6.1.5} iyam evāsmai rājyam anumanyate | dhenur dakṣiṇā | imām eva dhenuṃ kurute | ādityaṃ caruṃ nirvapati | ubhayīṣv eva prajāsv abhiṣicyate | daivīṣu ca mānuṣīṣu ca | varo dakṣiṇā | varo hi rājyaṃ samṛddhyai | āgnāvaiṣṇavam ekādaśakapālaṃ nirvapati | agniḥ sarvā devatāḥ |

{ 1.6.1.6} viṣṇur yajñaḥ | devatāś caiva yajñaṃ ca-avarundhe | vāmano vahī dakṣiṇā | yad vahī | tena-āgneyaḥ | yad vāmanaḥ | tena vaiṣṇavaḥ samṛddhyai | agnīṣomīyam ekādaśakapālaṃ nirvapati | agnīṣomābhyāṃ vā indro vṛtram ahann iti | yad agnīṣomīyam ekādaśakapālaṃ nirvapati |

{ 1.6.1.7} vārtraghnam eva vijityai | hiraṇyaṃ dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | indro vṛtraṃ hatvā | devatābhiś ca-indriyeṇa ca vyārdhyata | sa etam aindrāgnam ekādaśakapālam apaśyat | taṃ niravapat | tena vai sa devatāś ca-indriyaṃ cāvārundhe| yad aindrāgnam ekādaśakapālaṃ nirvapati | devatāś caiva tena-indriyaṃ ca yajamāno 'varundhe | ṛṣabho vahī dakṣiṇā |

{ 1.6.1.8} yad vahī | tena-āgneyaḥ | yad ṛṣabhaḥ | tena-aindraḥ samṛddhyai | āgneyam aṣṭākapālaṃ nirvapati | aindraṃ dadhi | yad āgneyo bhavati | agnir vai yajñamukham | yajñamukham eva-ṛddhiṃ purāstād dhatte | yad aindraṃ dadhi |

{ 1.6.1.9} indriyam eva-avarundhe | ṛṣabho vahī dakṣiṇā | yad vahī | tena-āgneyaḥ | yad ṛṣabhaḥ | tena-aindraḥ samṛddhyai | yāvatīr vai prajā oṣadhīnām ahutānām āśnan | tāḥ parābhavan | āgrayaṇaṃ bhavati hutādyāya | yajamānasya-aparābhāvāya |

{ 1.6.1.10} devā vā oṣadhīṣv ājim ayuḥ | tā indrāgnī udajayatām | tāv etam aindrāgnaṃ dvādaśakapālaṃ niravṛṇātām | yad aindrāgno bhavaty ujjityai | dvādaśakapālo bhavati | dvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ | saṃvatsareṇaivāsmā annam avarundhe | vaiśvadevaś carur bhavati | vaiśvadevaṃ vā annam | annam evāsmai svadayati |

{ 1.6.1.11} prathamajo vatso dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | saumyaṃ śyāmākaṃ caruṃ nirvapati | somo vā akṛṣṭapacyasya {akṛṣṭapacasya} rājā | akṛṣṭapacyam evāsmai svadayati | vāso dakṣiṇā | saumyaṃ hi devatayā vāsaḥ samṛddhyai | sarasvatyai caruṃ nirvapati | sarasvate carum | mithunam eva-avarundhe | mithunau gāvau dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | eti vā eṣa yajñamukhād ṛddhyāḥ | yo 'gner devatāyā eti | aṣṭāv etāni havīṃṣi bhavanti | aṣṭākṣarā gāyatrī | gāyatro 'gniḥ | tenaiva yajñamukhād ṛddhyā agner devatāyai na-eti |

anuvāka 2 rājasūye vaiśvadevaḥ { 1.6.2.1} vaiśvadevena vai prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata | tāḥ sṛṣṭā na prājāyanta | so 'gnir akāmayata | aham imāḥ prajanayeyam iti | sa prajāpataye | śucam adadhāt | so 'śocat prajām icchamānaḥ | tasmād yaṃ ca prajā bhunakti yaṃ ca na | tāv ubhau śocataḥ prajām icchamānau | tāsv agnim apyasṛjat | tā agnir adhyait |

{ 1.6.2.2} somo reto 'dadhāt | savitā prājanayat | sarasvatī vācam adadhāt | pūṣā-apoṣayat | te vā ete triḥ saṃvatsarasya prayujyante | ye devāḥ puṣṭipatayaḥ | saṃvatsaro vai prajāpatiḥ | saṃvatsareṇaivāsmai prajāḥ prājanayat | tāḥ prajā jātā maruto 'ghnan | asmān api na prāyukṣata-iti |

{ 1.6.2.3} sa etaṃ prajāpatir mārutaṃ saptakapālam apaśyat | taṃ niravapat | tato vai prajābhyo 'kalpata | yan māruto nirupyate | yajñasya klṛptyai | prajānām aghātāya | saptakapālo bhavati | saptagaṇā vai marutaḥ | gaṇaśa evāsmai viśaṃ kalpayati | sa prajāpatir aśocat |

{ 1.6.2.4} yāḥ pūrvāḥ prajā asṛkṣi | marutas tā avadhiṣuḥ | katham aparāḥ sṛjeyā-iti | tasya śuṣma āṇḍaṃ bhūtaṃ niravartata | tad vyudaharat | tad apoṣayat | tat prājāyata | āṇḍasya vā etad rūpam | yad āmikṣā | yad vyuddharati |

{ 1.6.2.5} prajā eva tad yajamānaḥ poṣayati | vaiśvadevy āmikṣā bhavati | vaiśvadevyo vai prajāḥ | prajā evāsmai prajanayati | vājinam ānayati | prajāsv eva prajātāsu reto dadhāti | dyāvāpṛthivya ekakapālo bhavati | prajā eva prajātā dyāvāpṛthivībhyām ubhayataḥ parigṛhṇāti | deva-asurāḥ saṃyattā āsan | so 'gnir abravīt |

{ 1.6.2.6} mām agre yajata | mayā mukhena-asurāñ jeṣyatha-iti | māṃ dvitīyam iti somo 'bravīt | mayā rājñā jeṣyatha-iti | māṃ tṛtīyam iti savitā | mayā prasūtā jeṣyatha-iti | māṃ caturthīm iti sarasvatī | indriyaṃ vo 'haṃ dhāsyāmi-iti māṃ pañcamam iti pūṣā | mayā pratiṣṭhayā jeṣyatha-iti |

{ 1.6.2.7} te 'gninā mukhena-asurān ajayan | somena rājñā | savitrā prasūtāḥ | sarasvatī-indriyam adadhāt | pūṣā pratiṣṭhā-āsīt | tato vai devā vyajayanta | yad etāni havīṃṣi nirupyante vijityai | na-uttaravedim upavapati | paśavo vā uttaravediḥ | ajātā iva hy etarhi paśavaḥ |

anuvāka 3 rājasūye vaiśvadevaḥ { 1.6.3.1} trivṛd barhir bhavati | mātā pitā putraḥ | tad eva tan mithunam | ulbaṃ garbho jarāyu | tad eva tan mithunam | tredhā barhiḥ saṃnaddhaṃ bhavati | traya ime lokāḥ | eṣv eva lokeṣu pratitiṣṭhati | ekadhā punaḥ saṃnaddhaṃ bhavati | eka iva hy ayaṃ lokaḥ |

{ 1.6.3.2} asminn eva tena loke pratitiṣṭhati | prasuvo bhavanti | prathamajām eva puṣṭim avarundhe | prathamajo vatso dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | pṛṣadājyaṃ gṛhṇāti | paśavo vai pṛṣadājyam | paśūnevāvarundhe | pañcagṛhītaṃ bhavati | pāṅktā hi paśavaḥ | bahurūpaṃ bhavati |

{ 1.6.3.3} bahurūpā hi paśavaḥ samṛddhyai | agniṃ manthanti | agnimukhā vai prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata | yad agniṃ manthanti | agnimukhā eva tat prajā yajamānaḥ sṛjate | nava prayājā ijyante | nava-anūyājāḥ | aṣṭau havīṃṣi | dvāv āghārau | dvāv ājyabhāgau |

{ 1.6.3.4} triṃśat saṃpadyante | triṃśadakṣarā virāṭ | annaṃ virāṭ | virājā-eva-annādyam avarundhe | yajamāno vā ekakapālaḥ | teja ājyam | yad ekakapāla ājyam ānayati | yajamānam eva tejasā samardhayati | yajamāno vā ekakapālaḥ | paśava ājyam |

{ 1.6.3.5} yad ekakapāla ājyam ānayati | yajamānam eva paśubhiḥ samardhayati | yad alpam ānayet | alpā enaṃ paśavo bhuñjanta upatiṣṭheran | yad bahu- ānayet | bahava enaṃ paśavo bhuñjanta upatiṣṭheran | bahv ānīya-āviḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ kuryāt | bahava evainaṃ paśavo bhuñjanta upatiṣṭhante | yajamāno vā ekakapālaḥ | yad ekakapālasya-avadyet |

{ 1.6.3.6} yajamānasya-avadyet | ud vā madyed yajamānaḥ | pra vā mīyeta | sakṛd eva hotavyaḥ | sakṛd iva hi suvargo lokaḥ | hutvā-abhijuhoti | yajamānam eva suvargaṃ lokaṃ gamayitvā | tejasā samardhayati | yajamāno vā ekakapālaḥ | suvargo loka āhavanīyaḥ |

{ 1.6.3.7} yad ekakapālam āhavanīye juhoti | yajamānam eva suvargaṃ lokaṃ gamayati | yad dhastena juhuyāt | suvargāl lokād yajamānamavavidhyet | srucā juhoti | suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai | yat prāṅ padyeta | devalokam abhijayet | yad dakṣiṇā pitṛlokam | yat pratyak |

{ 1.6.3.8} rakṣāṃsi yajñaṃ hanyuḥ | yad udaṅ | manuṣyalokam abhijayet | pratiṣṭhito hotavyaḥ | ekakapālaṃ vai pratitiṣṭhantaṃ dyāvāpṛthivī anu pratitiṣṭhataḥ | dyāvāpṛthivī ṛtavaḥ | ṛtūn yajñaḥ | yajñaṃ yajamānaḥ | yajamānaṃ prajāḥ | tasmāt pratiṣṭhito hotavyaḥ |

{ 1.6.3.9} vājino yajati | agnir vāyuḥ sūryaḥ | te vai vājinaḥ | tāneva tad yajati | atho khalv āhuḥ | chandāṃsi vai vājina iti | tāny eva tad yajati | ṛksāme vā indrasya harī somapānau | tayoḥ paridhaya ādhānam | vājinaṃ bhāgadheyam |

{ 1.6.3.10} yad aprahṛtya paridhīñ juhuyāt | antarādhānābhyāṃ ghāsaṃ {dyā saṃ} prayacchet | prahṛtya paridhīñ juhoti | nirādhānābhyām eva ghāsaṃ { dyā saṃ} prayacchati | barhiṣi viṣiñcan vājinam ānayati | prajā vai barhiḥ | reto vājinam | prajāsv eva reto dadhāti | samupahūya bhakṣayanti | etat somapīthā hy ete | atho ātmann eva reto dadhate | yajamāna uttamo bhakṣayati | paśavo vai vājinam | yajamāna eva paśūn pratiṣṭhāpayanti |

anuvāka 4 rājasūye varuṇapraghāsaḥ { 1.6.4.1} prajāpatiḥ savitā bhūtvā prajā asṛjata | tā enam atyamanyanta | tā asmād apākrāman | tā varuṇo bhūtvā prajā varuṇena-agrāhayat | tāḥ prajā varuṇagṛhītāḥ | prajāpatiṃ punar upādhāvan nātham icchamānāḥ | sa etān prajāpatir varuṇapraghāsān apaśyat | tān niravapat | tair vai sa prajā varuṇapāśād amuñcat | yad varuṇapraghāsā nirupyante |

{ 1.6.4.2} prajānām avaruṇagrāhāya | tāsāṃ dakṣiṇo bāhur nyakna āsīt | savyaḥ prasṛtaḥ | sa etāṃ dvitīyāṃ dakṣiṇato vedim udahan | tato vai sa prajānāṃ dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ prāsārayat | yad dvitīyāṃ dakṣiṇato vedim uddhanti | tasmāc cāturmāsyayājy amuṣmiṃl loka ubhayābāhuḥ | yajña-abhijitaṃ hy asya | pṛthamātrād vedī asaṃbhinne bhavataḥ |

{ 1.6.4.31} tasmāt pṛthamātraṃ vyaṃsau | uttarasyāṃ vedyām uttaravedim upavapati | paśavo vā uttaravediḥ | paśūn eva-avarundhe | atho yajñaparuṣo 'nantarityai | etad brāhmaṇāny eva pañca havīṃṣi | athaiṣa aindrāgno bhavati | prāṇāpānau vā etau devānām | yad indrāgnī | yad aindrāgno bhavati |

{ 1.6.4.4} prāṇāpānāv eva-avarundhe | ojo balaṃ vā etau devānām | yad indrāgnī | yad aindrāgno bhavati | ojo balam eva-avarundhe | māruty āmikṣā bhavati | vāruṇy āmikṣā | meṣī ca meṣaś ca bhavataḥ | mithunā eva prajā varuṇapāśān muñcati | lomaśau bhavato medhyatvāya |

{ 1.6.4.5} śamīparṇāny upavapati | ghāsam evā''bhyām apiyacchati | prajāpatim annādyaṃ na-upānamat | sa etena śata-idhmena haviṣā-annādyam avārundha | yat paraḥśatāni śamīparṇāni bhavanti | annādyasya-avaruddhyai | saumyāni vai karīrāṇi | saumyā khalu vā āhutir divo vṛṣṭiṃ cyāvayati | yat karīrāṇi bhavanti | saumyayā-eva-āhutyā divo vṛṣṭim avarundhe | kāya ekakapālo bhavati | prajānāṃ kantvāya { kaṃtvāya} | pratipūruṣaṃ karambhapātrāṇi bhavanti | jātā eva prajā varuṇapāśān muñcati | ekam atiriktam | janiṣyamāṇā eva prajā varuṇapāśān muñcati |

anuvāka 5 rājasūye varuṇapraghāsaḥ { 1.6.5.1} uttarasyāṃ vedyām anyāni havīṃṣi sādayati | dakṣiṇāyāṃ mārutīm | apadhuram evaināṃ yunakti | atho oja evāsām avaharati | tasmād brahmaṇaś ca kṣattrāc ca viśo 'nyato 'pakramiṇīḥ | mārutyā pūrvayā pracarati | anṛtam evā-avayajate | vāruṇyā-uttarayā | antata eva {evaṃ} varuṇam avayajate | yad eva-adhvaryuḥ karoti |

{ 1.6.5.2} tat pratiprasthātā karoti | tasmād yat- śreyān karoti | tat pāpīyān karoti | patnīṃ vācayati | medhyām evaināṃ karoti | atho tapa evainām upanayati | yat- jāraṃ santaṃ na prabrūyāt | priyaṃ jñātiṃ rundhyāt | asau me jāra iti nirdiśet | nirdiśya-evainaṃ varuṇapāśena grāhayati |

{ 1.6.5.3} praghāsyān havāmaha iti patnīm udānayati | ahūtā-evainām { ahvataivainām} | yat patnī puronuvākyām anubrūyāt | nirvīryo yajamānaḥ syāt | yajamāno 'nvāha | ātmann eva vīryaṃ dhatte | ubhau yājyāṃ savīryatvāya | yad grāme yad araṇya ity āha | yathā-uditam eva varuṇam avayajate | yajamānadevatyo vā āhavanīyaḥ |

{ 1.6.5.4} bhrātṛvyadevatyo dakṣiṇaḥ | yad āhavanīye juhuyāt | yajamānaṃ varuṇapāśena grāhayet | dakṣiṇe 'gnau juhoti | bhrātṛvyam eva varuṇapāśena grāhayati | śūrpeṇa juhoti | anyam eva varuṇam avayajate | śīrṣann adhinidhāya juhoti | śīrṣata eva varuṇam avayajate | pratyaṅ tiṣṭhan-juhoti | {sampādaka ṭippaṇī -- catuṣkoṇagatā kaṇḍikā-iyaṃ padapāṭhabhrameṇa daṇḍapātabhrameṇa vā-aṣṭapadā jātā}

{ 1.6.5.5} pratyaṅṅ eva varuṇapāśān nirmucyate | akran karma karmakṛta ity āha | devān ṛṇaṃ niravadāya | anṛṇā gṛhān upapreta-iti vāva-etad āha | varuṇagṛhītaṃ vā etad yajñasya | yad yajuṣā gṛhītasya-atiricyate | tuṣāś ca niṣkāsaś ca | tuṣaiś ca niṣkāsena ca-avabhṛtham avaiti | varuṇagṛhītena-eva varuṇam avayajate | apo 'vabhṛtham avaiti |

{ 1.6.5.6} apsu vai varuṇaḥ | sākṣād eva varuṇam avayajate | pratiyuto varuṇasya pāśa ity āha | varuṇapāśād eva nirmucyate | apratīkṣam āyanti | varuṇasya-antarhityai | edho 'syedhiṣīmahi-ity āha | samidhā-eva-agniṃ namasyanta upāyanti | tejo 'si tejo mayi dhehi-ity āha | teja evā''tman dhatte |

anuvāka 6 rājasūye sākamedhaḥ { 1.6.6.1} deva-asurāḥ saṃyattā āsan | so 'gnir abravīt | mama-iyam anīkavatī tanūḥ | tāṃ prīṇīta | atha-asurān abhibhaviṣyatha-iti | te devā agnaye 'nīkavate puroḍāśam aṣṭākapālaṃ niravapan | so 'gnir anīkavān svena bhāgadheyena prītaḥ | caturdhā-anīkāny ajanayata | tato devā abhavan | parā-asurāḥ |

{ 1.6.6.2} yad agnaye 'nīkavate puroḍāśam aṣṭākapālaṃ nirvapati | agnim eva-anīkavantaṃ svena bhagadheyena prīṇāti | so 'gnir anīkavān svena bhāgadheyena prītaḥ | caturdhā-anīkāni janayate | asau vā ādityo 'gnir anīkavān | tasya raśmayo 'nīkāni | sākaṃ sūryeṇa-udyatā nirvapati | sākṣād evāsmā anīkāni janayati | te 'surāḥ parājitā yantaḥ | dyāvāpṛthivī upāśrayan |

{ 1.6.6.3} te devā marudbhyaḥ sāṃtapanebhyaś caruṃ niravapan | tāṃ dyāvāpṛthivībhyām eva-ubhayataḥ samatapan | yan marudbhyaḥ sāṃtapanebhyaś caruṃ nirvapati | dyāvāpṛthivībhyām eva tad ubhayato yajamāno bhrātṛvyān saṃtapati | madhyaṃdine nirvapati | tarhi hite 'kṣṇiṣṭhaṃ tapati | carur bhavati | sarvata evainān saṃtapati | te devāḥ śvovijayinaḥ santaḥ | sarvāsāṃ dugdhe gṛhamedhīyaṃ caruṃ niravapan |

{ 1.6.6.4} āśitā eva-adya-upavasāma | kasya vā ha-idam | kasya vā śvo bhaviteti | sa śṛto 'bhavat | tasya-ahutasya na-aśnann | na hi devā ahutasya-aśnanti | te 'bruvan | kasmā imaṃ hoṣyāma iti | marudbhyo gṛhamedhibhya ity abruvan | taṃ marudbhyo gṛhamedhibhyo 'juhavuḥ |

{ 1.6.6.5} tato devā abhavan | parā-asurāḥ | yasyaivaṃ viduṣo marudbhyo gṛhamedhibhyo gṛhe juhvati | bhavaty ātmanā | parā-asya bhrātṛvyo bhavati | yad vai yajñasya pākatrā kriyate | paśavyaṃ tat | pākatrā vā etat kriyate | yan na-idhmābarhir bhavati | na sāmidhenīr anvāha |

{ 1.6.6.6} na prayājā ijyante | na-anūyājāḥ | ya evaṃ veda | paśumān bhavati | ājyabhāgau yajati | yajñasyaiva cakṣuṣī na-antareti | maruto gṛhamedhino yajati | bhāgadheyena-evainān samardhayati | agniṃ sviṣṭakṛtaṃ yajati pratiṣṭhityai | iḍānto bhavati | paśavo vā iḍā | paśuṣv eva-upariṣṭāt pratitiṣṭhati |

anuvāka 7 rājasūye sākamedhaḥ { 1.6.7.1} yat patnī gṛhamedhīyasya-aśnīyāt | gṛhamedhy eva syāt | vi tv asya yajña ṛdhyeta | yan na-aśnīyāt | agṛhamedhī syāt | na-asya yajño vyr̥ddhyeta | prativeśaṃ paceyuḥ | tasya-aśnīyāt | gṛhamedhy eva bhavati | na-asya yajño vyṛdhyate |

{ 1.6.7.2} te devā gṛhamedhīyena-iṣṭvā | āśitā abhavan | āñjata-abhyañjata | anu vatsān avāsayan | tebhyo 'surāḥ kṣudhaṃ prāhiṇvan | sā deveṣu lokam avittvā | asurān punar agacchat | gṛhamedhīyena-iṣṭvā | āśitā bhavanti | āñjate 'bhyañjate |

{ 1.6.7.3} anu vatsān vāsayanti | bhrātṛvyāyaiva tad yajamānaḥ kṣudhaṃ prahiṇoti | te devā gṛhamedhīyena-iṣṭvā | indrāya niṣkāsaṃ nyadadhuḥ | asmān eva śva indro nihitabhāga upāvartiteti | tān indro nihitabhāga upāvartata | gṛhamedhīyena-iṣṭvā | indrāya niṣkāsaṃ nidadhyāt | indra evainaṃ nihitabhāga upāvartate | gārhapatye juhoti |

{ 1.6.7.4} bhāgadheyenaivainaṃ samardhayati | ṛṣabham āhvayati | vaṣaṭkāra evāsya saḥ | atho indriyam eva tad vīryaṃ yajamāno bhrātṛvyasya vṛṅkte | indro vṛtraṃ hatvā | parāṃ parāvatam agacchat | apārādham iti manyamānaḥ | so 'bravīt | ka idaṃ vediṣyati-iti | te 'bruvan maruto varaṃ vṛṇāmahai |

{ 1.6.7.5} atha vayaṃ vedāma | asmabhyam eva prathamaṃ havirnirupyātā iti | ta enam adhyakrīḍan | tat krīḍināṃ krīḍitvam | yan marudbhyaḥ krīḍibhyaḥ prathamaṃ havir nirupyate vijityai | sākaṃ sūryeṇa-udyatā nirvapati | etasmin vai loka indro vṛtram ahan samṛddhyai | etad brāhmaṇāny eva pañca havīṃṣi | etad brāhmaṇa aindrāgnaḥ | athaiṣa aindraś carur bhavati | { 1.6.7.5} uddhāraṃ vā etam indra udaharata | vṛtraṃ hatvā | anyāsu devatāsv adhi | yad eṣa aindraś carur bhavati | uddhāram eva taṃ yajamāna uddharate | anyāsu prajāsv adhi | vaiśvakarmaṇa ekakapālo bhavati | viśvāny eva tena karmāṇi yajamāno 'varundhe |

anuvāka 8 rājasūye piṇḍapitṛyajñaḥ { 1.6.8.1} vaiśvadevena vai prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata | tā varuṇapraghāsair varuṇapāśād amuñcat | sākamedhaiḥ pratyasthāpayat | tryambakair rudraṃ niravādayata | pitṛyajñena suvargaṃ lokam agamayat | yad vaiśvadevena yajate | prajā eva tad yajamānaḥ sṛjate | tā varuṇapraghāsair varuṇapāśān muñcati | sākamedhaiḥ pratiṣṭhāpayati | tryambakair rudraṃ niravadayate |

{ 1.6.8.2} pitṛyajñena suvargaṃ lokaṃ gamayati | dakṣiṇataḥ prācīnāvītī nirvapati | dakṣiṇāvṛd dhi pitṛṇām | anādṛtya tat | uttarata eva-upavīya nirvapet | ubhaye hi devāś ca pitaraś ca-ijyante | atho yad eva dakṣiṇārdhe 'dhiśrayati | tena dakṣiṇāvṛt | somāya pitṛmate puroḍāśaṃ ṣaṭkapālaṃ nirvapati | saṃvatsaro vai somaḥ pitṛmān |

{ 1.6.8.3} saṃvatsaram eva prīṇāti | pitṛbhyo barhiṣadbhyo dhānāḥ | māsā vai pitaro barhiṣadaḥ | māsān eva prīṇāti | yasmin vā ṛtau puruṣaḥ pramīyate | so 'sya-amuṣmiṃl loke bhavati | bahurūpā dhānā bhavanti | ahorātrāṇām abhijityai | pitṛbhyo 'gniṣvāttebhyo mantham | ardhamāsā vai pitaro 'gniṣvāttāḥ |

{ 1.6.8.4} ardhamāsān eva prīṇāti | abhivānyāyai dugdhe bhavati | sā hi pitṛdevatyaṃ duhe | yat pūrṇam | tan manuṣyāṇām | uparyardho devānām | ardhaḥ pitṛṇām | ardha upamanthati | ardho hi pitṛṇām | ekayā-upamanthati |

{ 1.6.8.5} ekā hi pitṛṇām | dakṣiṇā-upamanthati | dakṣiṇāvṛddhi pitṛṇām | anārabhya-upamanthati | taddhi pitṝn gacchati | imāṃ diśaṃ vedim uddhanti | ubhaye hi devāś ca pitaraś ca-ijyante | catuḥsraktir bhavati | sarvā hy anu diśaḥ pitaraḥ | akhātā bhavati |

{ 1.6.8.6} khātā hi devānām | madhyato 'gnir ādhīyate | antato hi devānām ādhīyate | varṣīyān idhma idhmād bhavati vyāvṛttyai | pariśrayati | antarhito hi pitṛloko manuṣyalokāt | yat paruṣi dinam | tad devānām | yad antarā | tan manuṣyāṇām |

{ 1.6.8.7} yat samūlam | tat pitṛṇām | samūlaṃ barhir bhavati vyāvṛttyai | dakṣiṇā stṛṇāti | dakṣiṇāvṛddhi pitṛṇām | triḥ paryeti | tṛtīye vā ito loke pitaraḥ | tān eva prīṇāti | triḥ punaḥ paryeti | ṣaṭ saṃpadyante |

{ 1.6.8.8} ṣaḍ vā ṛtavaḥ | ṛtūn eva prīṇāti | yat prastaraṃ yajuṣā gṛhṇīyāt | pramāyuko yajamānaḥ syāt | yan na gṛhṇīyāt | anāyatanaḥ syāt | tūṣṇīm eva nyasyet | na pramāyuko bhavati | na-anāyatanaḥ | yat trīn paridhīn paridadhyāt |

{ 1.6.8.9} mṛtyunā yajamānaṃ parigṛhṇīyāt | yan na paridadhyāt | rakṣāṃsi yajñaṃ hanyuḥ | dvau paridhī paridadhāti | rakṣasām apahatyai | atho mṛtyor eva yajamānam utsṛjati | yat trīṇi-trīṇi havīṃṣy udāhareyuḥ | trayas-traya eṣāṃ sākaṃ pramīyeran | ekaikam anūcīnāny udāharanti | ekaika evaiṣām anvañcaḥ pramīyate | kaśipu kaśipavyāya | upabarhaṇam upabarhaṇyāya | āñjanam āñjanyāya | abhyañjanam abhyañjanyāya | yathābhāgam evainān prīṇāti |

anuvāka 9 rājasūye pitṛyajñaḥ { 1.6.9.1} agnaye devebhyaḥ pitṛbhyaḥ samidhyamānāya-anubrūhi-ity āha | ubhaye hi devāś ca pitaraś ca-ijyante | ekām anvāha | ekā hi pitṛṇām | trir anvāha | trir hi devānām | āghārāv āghārayati | yajñaparuṣor anantarityai | na-ārṣeyaṃ vṛṇīte | na hotāram |

{ 1.6.9.2} yad ārṣeyaṃ vṛṇīte | yad hotāram | pramāyuko yajamānaḥ syāt | pramāyuko hotā | tasmān na vṛṇīte | yajamānasya hotur gopīthāya | apabarhiṣaḥ prayājān yajati | prajā vai barhiḥ | prajā eva mṛtyor utsṛjati | ājyabhāgau yajati |

{ 1.6.9.3} yajñasyaiva cakṣuṣī na-antareti | prācīnāvītī somaṃ yajati | pitṛdevatyā hi | eṣā-āhutīḥ | pañcakṛtvo 'vadyati | pañca hy etā devatāḥ | dve puro'nuvākye | yājyā devatā vaṣaṭkāraḥ | tā eva prīṇāti | santatam avadyati |

{ 1.6.9.4} ṛtūnāṃ saṃtatyai | pra-evaibhyaḥ pūrvayā puro'nuvākyayā-āha | praṇayati dvitīyayā | gamayati yājyayā | tṛtīye vā ito loke pitaraḥ | ahna evainān {evaināṃ} pūrvayā puro'nuvākyayā-atyānayati | rātriyai dvitīyayā | ā-evainān yājyayā gamayati | dakṣiṇato 'vadāya | udaṅṅ atikrāmati vyāvṛttyai |

{ 1.6.9.5} ā svadhā-ity āśrāvayati | astu svadhā-iti pratyāśrāvayati | svadhā nama iti vaṣaṭkaroti | svadhākāro hi pitṛṇām | somam agre yajati | somaprayājā hi pitaraḥ | somaṃ pitṛmantaṃ yajati | saṃvatsaro vai somaḥ pitṛmān | saṃvatsaram eva tad yajati | pitṝn barhiṣado yajati |

{ 1.6.9.6} ye vai yajvānaḥ | te pitaro barhiṣadaḥ | tān eva tad yajati | pitṝn agniṣvāttān yajati | ye vā ayajvāno gṛhamedhinaḥ | te pitaro 'gniṣvāttāḥ | tān eva tad yajati | agniṃ kavyavāhanaṃ yajati | ya eva pitṛṇām agniḥ | tam eva tad yajati |

{ 1.6.9.7} atho yathā-agniṃ sviṣṭakṛtaṃ yajati | tādṛg eva tat | etat te tata ye ca tvām anv iti tisṛṣu sraktīṣu nidadhāti | tasmād ā tṛtīyāt puruṣān nāmanigṛhṇanti { nāma na gṛhṇanti?} | etāvanto hi-ijyante | atra pitaro yathābhāgaṃ mandadhvam ity āha | hlīkā hi pitaraḥ | udañco niṣkrāmanti | eṣā vai manuṣyāṇāṃ dik | svām eva tad diśam anu niṣkrāmanti |

{ 1.6.9.8} āhavanīyam upatiṣṭhante | ny evāsmai tad dhnuvate | yat saty āhavanīye | atha-anyatra caranti | ā tamitor upatiṣṭhante | agnim eva-upadraṣṭāraṃ kṛtvā | pitṝn niravadayante | antaṃ vā ete prāṇānāṃ gacchanti | ya ā tamitor upatiṣṭhante | susaṃdṛśaṃ tvā vayam ity āha |

{ 1.6.9.9} prāṇo vai susaṃdṛk | prāṇam evā''tman dadhate | yojā nv indra te harī ity āha | prāṇam eva punar ayuṅkta | akṣann amīmadanta hi-iti gārhapatyam upatiṣṭhante | akṣann amīmadanta-atha tvā-upatiṣṭhāmaha iti vāva-etad āha | amīmadanta pitaraḥ somyā ity abhiprapadyante | amīmadanta pitaro 'tha tvā-abhiprapadyāmaha iti vāva-etad āha | apaḥ pariṣiñcati | mārjayaty evainān |

{ 1.6.9.10} atho tarpayaty eva | tṛpyati prajayā paśubhiḥ | ya evaṃ veda | apabarhiṣāv anūyājau yajati | prajā vai barhiḥ | prajā eva mṛtyor utsṛjati | caturaḥ prayājān yajati | dvāv anūyājau | ṣaṭ saṃpadyante | ṣaḍ vā ṛtavaḥ | { 1.6.9.10} ṛtūn eva prīṇāti | na patny anvāste | na saṃyājayanti | yat patny anvāsīta | yat saṃyājayeyuḥ | pramāyukā syāt | tasmān na-anvāste | na saṃyājayanti | patniyai gopīthāya |

anuvāka 10 rājasūye tryambakahaviḥ { 1.6.10.1} pratipūruṣam ekakapālān nirvapati | jātā eva prajā rudrān niravadayate | ekam atiriktam | janiṣyamāṇā eva prajā rudrān niravadayate | ekakapālā bhavanti | ekadhā-eva rudraṃ niravadayate | na-abhighārayati | yad abhighārayet | antaravacāriṇaṃ rudraṃ kuryāt | eka-ulmukena yanti( ulmuke nayanti) |

{ 1.6.10.2} tad dhi rudrasya bhāgadheyam | imāṃ diśaṃ yanti | eṣā vai rudrasya dik | svāyām eva diśi rudraṃ niravadayate | rudro vā apaśukāgrā āhutyai na-atiṣṭhata | asau te paśur iti nirdiśed yaṃ dviṣyāt | yam eva dveṣṭi | tam asmai paśuṃ nirdiśati | yadi na dviṣyāt | ākhus te paśur iti brūyāt |

{ 1.6.10.3} na grāmyān paśūn hinasti | na-araṇyān | catuṣpathe juhoti | eṣa vā agnīnāṃ paḍbīśo nāma | agnivaty eva juhoti | madhyamena parṇena juhoti | srugd hy eṣā(srugdhyeṣā) | atho khalu | antamenaiva hotavyam | antata eva rudraṃ niravadayate |

{ 1.6.10.4} eṣa te rudra bhāgaḥ saha svasrā-ambikayā ity āha | śarad vā asya-ambikā svasā | tayā vā eṣa hinasti | yaṃ hinasti | tayā-evainaṃ saha śamayati | bheṣajaṃ gava ity āha | yāvanta eva grāmyāḥ paśavaḥ | tebhyo bheṣajaṃ karoti | ava-amba rudram adimahi-ity āha | āśiṣam evaitām āśāste |

{ 1.6.10.5} tryambakaṃ yajāmaha ity āha | mṛtyor mukṣīya mā-amṛtād iti vāva-etad āha | utkiranti | bhagasya līpsante | mūte kṛtvā sajanti | yathā janaṃ yate 'vasaṃ {yatevasaṃ} karoti | tādṛg eva tat | eṣa te rudra bhāga ity āha niravattyai | apratīkṣam āyanti | apaḥ pariṣiñcati | rudrasya-antarhityai | pra vā ete 'smāl lokāc cyavante | ye tryambakaiś caranti | ādityaṃ caruṃ punar etya nirvapati | iyaṃ vā aditiḥ | asyām eva pratitiṣṭhanti |




prapāṭhaka: 7 anuvāka 1 rājasūye śunāsīraḥ { 1.7.1.1} etad brāhmaṇāny eva pañca havīṃṣi | atha-indrāya śunāsīrāya puroḍāśaṃ dvādaśakapālaṃ nirvapati | saṃvatsaro vā indrā śunāsīraḥ | saṃvatsareṇaivāsmā annam avarundhe | vāyavyaṃ payo bhavati | vāyur vai vṛṣṭyai pradāpayitā | sa evāsmai vṛṣṭiṃ pradāpayati | saurya ekakapālo bhavati | sūryeṇa vā amuṣmiṃl loke vṛṣṭir dhṛtā | sa evāsmai vṛṣṭiṃ niyacchati |

{ 1.7.1.2} dvādaśagavaṃ sīraṃ dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | deva-asurāḥ saṃyattā āsan | te devā agnim abruvan | tvayā vīreṇa-asurān abhibhavāma-iti | so 'bravīt | tredhā-aham ātmānaṃ vikariṣya iti | sa tredhā-ātmānaṃ vyakuruta | agniṃ tṛtīyam | rudraṃ tṛtīyam | varuṇaṃ tṛtīyam |

{ 1.7.1.3} so 'bravīt | ka idaṃ turīyam iti | aham iti-indro 'bravīt | saṃ tu sṛjāvahā iti | tau samasṛjetām | sa indras turīyam abhavat | yad indras turīyam abhavat | tad indraturīyasya-indraturīyatvam | tato vai devā vyajayanta | yad indraturīyaṃ nirupyate vijityai |

{ 1.7.1.4} vahinī dhenur dakṣiṇā | yad vahinī tena-āgneyī | yad gauḥ | tena raudrī | yad dhenuḥ | tena-aindrī | yat strī satī dāntā | tena vāruṇī samṛddhyai | prajāpatir yajñam asṛjata |

{ 1.7.1.5} taṃ sṛṣṭaṃ rakṣāṃsy ajighāṃsan | sa etāḥ prajāpatir ātmano devatā niramimīta | tābhir vai sa digbhyo rakṣāṃsi prāṇudata | yat pañcāvattīyaṃ juhoti | digbhya eva tad yajamāno rakṣāṃsi praṇudate | samūḍhaṃ rakṣaḥ saṃdagdhaṃ rakṣa ity āha | rakṣāṃsy eva saṃdahati | agnaye rakṣoghne svāhā-ity āha | devatābhya eva vijigyānābhyo bhāgadheyaṃ karoti | praṣṭivāhī ratho dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai |

{ 1.7.1.6} indro vṛtraṃ hatvā | asurān parābhāvya | namucim āsuraṃ na-alabhata[na-ālabhata?] | taṃ śacyāgṛhṇāt | tau samalabhetām | so 'smād abhiśunataro 'bhavat | so 'bravīt | saṃdhāṃ saṃdadhāvahai | atha tvā-vaḥ { tvāva} srakṣyāmi | na mā śuṣkeṇa na-ārdreṇa hanaḥ |

{ 1.7.1.7} na divā na naktam iti | sa etam apāṃ phenam asiñcat | na vā eṣa śuṣko na-ārdro vyuṣṭā-āsīt | anuditaḥ sūryaḥ | na vā etad divā na naktam | tasyaitasmiṃl loke | apāṃ phenena śira udavartayat | tad enam anvavartata | mitradhrug iti |

{ 1.7.1.8} sa etān apāmārgān ajanayat | tān ajuhot | tair vai sa rakṣāṃsy apāhata {apāhan} | yad apāmārgahomo bhavati | rakṣasām apahatyai | eka-ulmukena yanti | taddhi rakṣasāṃ bhāgadheyam | imāṃ diśaṃ yanti | eṣā vai rakṣasāṃ dik | svāyām eva diśi rakṣāṃsi hanti |

{ 1.7.1.9} svakṛta iriṇe juhoti pradare vā | etad vai rakṣasām āyatanam | sva evā''yatane rakṣāṃsi hanti | parṇamayena sruveṇa juhoti | brahma vai parṇaḥ | brahmaṇā-eva rakṣāṃsi hanti | devasya tvā savituḥ prasava ity āha | savitṛprasūta eva rakṣāṃsi hanti | hataṃ rakṣo vadhiṣma rakṣa ity āha | rakṣasāṃ stṛtyai | yad vaste tad dakṣiṇā niravattyai | apratīkṣam āyanti | rakṣasām antarhityai |

anuvāka 2 rājasūye devikāhavīṃṣi { 1.7.2.1} dhātre puroḍāśaṃ dvādaśakapālaṃ nirvapati | saṃvatsaro vai dhātā | saṃvatsareṇaivāsmai prajāḥ prajanayati | anv evāsmā anumatir manyate | rāte rākā | pra sinīvālī janayati | prajāsv eva prajātāsu kuhvā vācaṃ dadhāti | mithunau gāvau dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | āgnāvaiṣṇavam ekādaśakapālaṃ nirvapati | aindrāvaiṣṇavam ekādaśakapālam |

{ 1.7.2.2} vaiṣṇavaṃ trikapālam | vīryaṃ vā agniḥ | vīryam indraḥ | vīryaṃ viṣṇuḥ | prajā eva prajātā vīrye pratiṣṭhāpayati | tasmāt prajā vīryāvatīḥ | vāmana ṛṣabho vahī dakṣiṇā | yad vahī tena-āgneyaḥ | yad ṛṣabhaḥ |

{ 1.7.2.3} tena-aindraḥ | yad vāmanaḥ | tena vaiṣṇavaḥ samṛddhyai | agnīṣomīyam ekādaśakapālaṃ nirvapati | indrāsomīyam ekādaśakapālam | saumyaṃ carum | somo vai retodhāḥ | agniḥ prajānāṃ prajanayitā | vṛddhānām indraḥ pradāpayitā | soma evāsmai reto dadhāti |

{ 1.7.2.4} agniḥ prajāṃ prajanayati | vṛddhām indraḥ prayacchati | babhrur dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | somāpauṣṇaṃ caruṃ nirvapati | aindrāpauṣṇaṃ carum | somo vai retodhāḥ | pūṣā paśūnāṃ prajanayitā | vṛddhānām indraḥ pradāpayitā | soma evāsmai reto dadhāti | pūṣā paśūn prajanayati |

{ 1.7.2.5} vṛddhān indraḥ prayacchati | pauṣṇaś caru bhavati | iyaṃ vai pūṣā | asyām eva pratitiṣṭhati | śyāmo dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | bahu vai puruṣo 'medhyam {medhyam?} upagacchati | vaiśvānaraṃ dvādaśakapālaṃ nirvapati | saṃvatsaro vā agnir vaiśvānaraḥ | saṃvatsareṇaivainaṃ svadayati | hiraṇyaṃ dakṣiṇā |

{ 1.7.2.6} pavitraṃ vai hiraṇyam | punāty evainam | bahu vai rājanyo 'nṛtaṃ karoti | upa jāmyai harate | jināti brāhmaṇam | vadaty anṛtam | anṛte khalu vai kriyamāṇe varuṇo gṛhṇāti | vāruṇaṃ yavamayaṃ caruṃ nirvapati | varuṇapāśād evainaṃ muñcati | aśvo dakṣiṇā | vāruṇo hi devatayā-aśvaḥ samṛddhyai |

anuvāka 3 rājasūye ratnināṃ haviḥ { 1.7.3.1} ratninām etāni havīṃṣi bhavanti | ete vai rāṣṭrasya pradātāraḥ | ete 'pādātāraḥ (pādātāraḥ) | ya eva rāṣṭrasya pradātāraḥ | ye 'pādātāraḥ (pādātāraḥ) | ta evāsmai rāṣṭraṃ prayacchanti | rāṣṭram eva bhavati | yat samāhṛtya nirvapet | aratninaḥ syuḥ | yathāyathaṃ nirvapati ratnitvāya |

{ 1.7.3.2} yat sadyo nirvapet | yāvatīm ekena haviṣā-āśiṣam avarundhe | tāvatīm avarundhīta | anvahaṃ nirvapati | bhūyasīm evā''śiṣam avarundhe | bhūyaso yajñakratūn upaiti | bārhaspatyaṃ caruṃ nirvapati brahmaṇo gṛhe | mukhata evāsmai brahma saṃśyati | atho brahmann eva kṣattram anvārambhayati | śitipṛṣṭho dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai |

{ 1.7.3.3} aindram ekādaśakapālaṃ rājanyasya gṛhe | indriyam eva-avarundhe | ṛṣabho dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | ādityaṃ caruṃ mahiṣyai gṛhe | iyaṃ vā aditiḥ | asyām eva pratitiṣṭhati | dhenur dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | bhagāya caruṃ vāvātāyai gṛhe | bhagam evāsmin dadhāti | vicittagarbhā paṣṭhauhī dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai |

{ 1.7.3.4} nairṛtaṃ caruṃ parivṛktyai gṛhe kṛṣṇānāṃ vrīhīṇāṃ nakhanirbhinnam | pāpmānam eva nirṛtiṃ niravadayate kṛṣṇā kūṭām̐ (kūṭā) dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | āgneyam aṣṭākapālaṃ senānyo gṛhe | senām evāsya saṃśyati | hiraṇyaṃ dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | vāruṇaṃ daśakapālaṃ sūtasya gṛhe | varuṇasavam eva-avarundhe | mahāniraṣṭo dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | mārutaṃ saptakapālaṃ grāmaṇyo gṛhe |

{ 1.7.3.5} annaṃ vai marutaḥ | annam eva-avarundhe | pṛśnir dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | sāvitraṃ dvādaśakapālaṃ kṣattur gṛhe prasūtyai | upadhvasto dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | āśvinaṃ dvikapālaṃ saṃgrahītur gṛhe | aśvinau vai devānāṃ bhiṣajau | tābhyām evāsmai bheṣajaṃ karoti | savātyau dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | pauṣṇaṃ caruṃ bhāgadughasya gṛhe |

{ 1.7.3.6} annaṃ vai pūṣā | annam eva-avarundhe | śyāmo dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | raudraṃ gāvīdhukaṃ carum akṣāvāpasya gṛhe | antata eva rudraṃ niravadayate | śabala udvāro dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai | dvādaśa-etāni havīṃṣi bhavanti | dvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ | saṃvatsareṇa-evāsmai rāṣṭram avarundhe | rāṣṭram eva bhavati |

{ 1.7.3.7} yan na pratinirvapet | ratnina āśiṣo 'varundhīrann | pratinirvapati | indrāya sutrāmṇe puroḍāśam ekādaśakapālam | indrāya-aṃhomuce | āśiṣa eva-avarundhe | āyan no { ayaṃ no} rājā vṛtrahā rājā | bhūtvā vṛtraṃ vadhyād ity āha | āśiṣam evaitām āśāste | maitrābārhaspatyaṃ bhavati | śvetāyai śvetavatsāyai dugdhe |

{ 1.7.3.8} bārhaspatye maitram apidadhāti | brahma caivāsmai kṣattraṃ ca samīcī dadhāti | atho brahmann eva kṣattraṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati | bārhaspatyena pūrveṇa pracarati | mukhata evāsmai brahma saṃśyati | atho brahmann eva kṣattram anvārambhayati | svayaṃkṛtā vedir bhavati | svayaṃdinaṃ barhiḥ | svayaṃkṛta idhmaḥ | anabhijitasya-abhijityai | tasmād rājñām araṇyam abhijitam | saiva śvetā śvetavatsā dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai |

anuvāka 4 rājasūye devasuvāṃ haviḥ { 1.7.4.1} devasuvām etāni havīṃṣi bhavanti | etāvanto {etāvantau} vai devānāṃ savāḥ | ta evāsmai savān prayacchanti | ta enaṃ suvante | agnir evainaṃ gṛhapatīnāṃ suvate | somo vanaspatīnām | rudraḥ paśūnām | bṛhaspatir vācām | indro jyeṣṭhānām | mitraḥ satyānām |

{ 1.7.4.2} varuṇo dharmapatīnām | etad eva sarvaṃ bhavati | savitā tvā prasavānāṃ suvatām iti hastaṃ gṛhṇāti prasūtyai | ye devā devasuvaḥ stha-ity āha | yathāyajur evaitat | mahate kṣattrāya mahata ādhipatyāya mahate jānarājyāya-ity āha | āśiṣam evaitām āśāste | eṣa vo bharatā rājā somo 'smākaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ rājā-ity āha | tasmāt somarājāno brāhmaṇāḥ | prati tyan nāma rājyam adhāyi-ity āha |

{ 1.7.4.3} rājyam evāsmin pratidadhāti | svāṃ tanuvaṃ varuṇo aśiśred ity āha | varuṇasavam eva-avarundhe | śucer mitrasya vratyā abhūma-ity āha | śucim evainaṃ vratyaṃ karoti | amanmahi mahata ṛtasya nāma-ity āha | manuta evainam | sarve vrātā varuṇasya-abhūvann ity āha | sarvavrātam evainaṃ karoti | vi mitra evair arātim {vi mitrarāvair arātim} atārīd ity āha {= tai.saṃ.1.8.10.f} |

{ 1.7.4.4} arātim evainaṃ tārayati | asūṣudanta yajñiyā ṛtena-ity āha | svadayaty evainam | vy u trito jarimāṇaṃ na ānaḍ ity āha {= tai.saṃ 1.8.10.f} | āyur evāsmin dadhāti | dvābhyāṃ vimṛṣṭe | dvipād yajamānaḥ pratiṣṭhityai | agnīṣomīyasya ca-ekādaśakapālasya devasuvāṃ ca haviṣām agnaye sviṣṭakṛte samavadyati | devatābhir evainam ubhayataḥ parigṛhṇāti | viṣṇukramān kramate | viṣṇur eva bhūtvā-imān lokān abhijayati |

anuvāka 5 rājasūye jalagrahaṇam jalāharaṇam { 1.7.5.1} artha-itaḥ stha-iti juhoti | āhutyā-evainā niṣkrīya gṛhṇāti | atho haviṣkṛtānām eva-abhighṛtānāṃ gṛhṇāti | vahantīnāṃ gṛhṇāti | etā vā apāṃ rāṣṭram | rāṣṭram evāsmai gṛhṇāti | atho śriyam evainam abhivahanti | apāṃ patir asi-ity āha | mithunam eva-akaḥ | vṛṣā-asy ūrmir ity āha |

{ 1.7.5.2} ūrmimantam evainaṃ karoti | vṛṣaseno 'si-ity āha | senām evāsya saṃśyati | vrajakṣitaḥ stha-ity āha | etā vā apāṃ viśaḥ | viśam evāsmai paryūhati | marutām ojaḥ stha-ity āha | annaṃ vai marutaḥ | annam eva-avarundhe | sūryavarcasaḥ stha-ity āha |

{ 1.7.5.3} rāṣṭram eva varcasvy akaḥ | sūryatvacasaḥ stha-ity āha | satyaṃ vā etat | yad varṣati | anṛtaṃ yadātapati varṣati | satya-anṛte eva-avarundhe | na-enaṃ satya-anṛte udite hiṃstaḥ | ya evaṃ veda | māndā stha-ity āha | rāṣṭram eva brahmavarcasy akaḥ |

{ 1.7.5.4} vāśāḥ stha-ity āha | rāṣṭram eva vaśy akaḥ | śakvarīḥ stha-ity āha | paśavo vai śakvarīḥ | paśūn eva-avarundhe | viśvabhṛtaḥ stha-ity āha | rāṣṭram eva payasvy akaḥ | janabhṛtaḥ stha-ity āha | rāṣṭram eva-indriyāvyakaḥ | agnes tejasyāḥ stha-ity āha |

{ 1.7.5.5} rāṣṭram eva tejasvy akaḥ | apām oṣadhīnāṃ rasaḥ stha-ity āha | rāṣṭram eva madhavyam akaḥ | sārasvataṃ grahaṃ gṛhṇāti | eṣā vā apāṃ pṛṣṭham | yat sarasvatī | pṛṣṭham evainaṃ samānānāṃ karoti | ṣoḍaśabhir gṛhṇāti | ṣoḍaśakalo vai puruṣaḥ | yāvān eva puruṣaḥ | tasmin vīryaṃ dadhāti | ṣoḍaśabhir juhoti ṣoḍaśabhir gṛhṇāti | dvātriṃśat saṃpadyante | dvātriṃśadakṣarā-anuṣṭuk | vāg anuṣṭup sarvāṇi cchandāṃsi | vācā-evainaṃ sarvebhiś chandobhir abhiṣiñcati |

anuvāka 6 rājasūye jalasaṃskāraḥ { 1.7.6.1} devīr āpaḥ saṃ madhumatīr madhumatībhiḥ sṛjyadhvam ity āha | brahmaṇā-evaināḥ saṃsṛjati | anādhṛṣṭāḥ sīdata-ity āha | brahmaṇā-evaināḥ sādayati | antarā hotuś ca dhiṣṇiyaṃ brāhmaṇācchaṃsinaś ca sādayati | āgneyo vai hotā | aindro brāhmaṇācchaṃsī | tejasā caiva-indriyeṇa cobhayato rāṣṭraṃ parigṛhṇāti | hiraṇyena-utpunāti | āhutyai hi pavitrābhyām utpunanti vyāvṛttyai |

{ 1.7.6.2} śatamānaṃ bhavati | śatāyuḥ | puruṣaś śata-indriyaḥ | āyuṣy eva-indriye pratitiṣṭhati | anibhṛṣṭam asi-ity āha | anibhṛṣṭaṃ hy etat | vāco bandhur ity āha | vāco hy eṣa bandhuḥ | tapojā ity āha | tapojā hy etat | somasya dātram asi-ity āha |

{ 1.7.6.3} somasya hy etad dātram | śukrā vaḥ śukreṇa-utpunāmi-ity āha | śukrā hy āpaḥ | śukraṃ hiraṇyam | candrāś candreṇa-ity āha | candrā hy āpaḥ | candraṃ hiraṇyam | amṛtā amṛtena-ity āha | amṛtā hy āpaḥ | amṛtaṃ hiraṇyam |

{ 1.7.6.4} svāhā rājasūyāya-ity āha | rājasūyāya hy enā utpunāti | sadhamādo dyumni nīruja { nīrūrja} etā iti vāruṇyā-ṛcā gṛhṇāti | varuṇasavam eva-avarundhe | ekayā gṛhṇāti | ekadhā-eva yajamāne vīryaṃ dadhāti | kṣatrasya-ulbam asi kṣatrasya yonir asi-iti tārpyaṃ ca-uṣṇīṣaṃ ca prayacchati sayonitvāya | ekaśatena darbhapuñjīlaiḥ pavayati | śatāyur vai puruṣaḥ śatavīryaḥ | ātmā-ekaśataḥ |

{ 1.7.6.5} yāvān eva puruṣaḥ | tasmin vīryaṃ dadhāti | dadhy āśayati | indriyam eva-avarundhe | udumbaram āśayati | annādyasya-avaruddhyai | śaṣpāṇy āśayati | surābalim evainaṃ karoti | āvida etā bhavanti | āvidam evainaṃ gamayanti |

{ 1.7.6.6} agnir evainaṃ gārhapatyena-avati | indra indriyeṇa | pūṣā paśubhiḥ | mitrāvaruṇau prāṇāpānābhyām | indro vṛtrāya vajram udayacchat | sa divam alikhat | so 'ryamṇaḥ panthā abhavat | sa āvinne dyāvāpṛthivī dhṛtavrate iti dyāvāpṛthivī upādhāvat | sa ābhyām eva prasūta indro vṛtrāya vajraṃ prāharat | āvinne dyāvāpṛthivī dhṛtavrate iti yad āha |

{ 1.7.6.7} ābhyām eva prasūto yajamāno vajraṃ bhrātṛvyāya praharati | āvinnā devy aditir viśvarūpī-ity āha | iyaṃ vai devy aditir viśvarūpī | asyām eva pratitiṣṭhati | āvinno 'yam asāv āmuṣyāyaṇo 'syāṃ viśy asmin rāṣṭra ity āha | viśā-evainaṃ rāṣṭreṇa samardhayati | mahate kṣatrāya mahata ādhipatyāya mahate jānarājyāyā-ity āha | āśiṣam evaitām āśaste | eṣa vo bharatā rājā somo 'smākaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ rājā-ity āha | tasmāt somarājāno brāhmaṇāḥ |

{ 1.7.6.8} indrasya vajro 'si vārtraghna iti dhanuḥ prayacchati vijityai | śatrubādhanāḥ stha-iti-iṣūn | śatrūn evāsya bādhante | pāta mā pratyañcaṃ pāta mā tiryañcam anvañcaṃ mā pāta-ity āha | tisro vai śaravyāḥ | pratīcī tiraścy anūcī | tabhya evainaṃ pānti | digbhyo mā pāta-ity āha | digbhya evainaṃ pānti | viśvābhyo mā nāṣṭrābhyaḥ pāta-ity āha | aparimitād evainaṃ pānti | hiraṇyavarṇāv uṣasāṃ viroka iti triṣṭubhā bāhū udgṛhṇāti | indriyaṃ vai vīryaṃ triṣṭuk | indriyam eva vīryam upariṣṭād ātman dhatte |

anuvāka 7 rājasūye digjayadhyānama { 1.7.7.41} diśo vyāsthāpayati | diśām abhijityai | yad anuprakrāmet | abhi diśo jayet | ut tu mādyet | manasonuprakrāmati | abhi diśo jayati | na-unmādyati | samidham ātiṣṭha-ity āha | teja eva-avarundhe |

{ 1.7.7.2} ugrām ātiṣṭha-ity āha indriyam eva-avarundhe | virājam ātiṣṭha-ity āha | annādyam eva-avarundhe | udīcīm ātiṣṭha-ity āha | paśūn eva-avarundhe | ūrdhvām ātiṣṭha-ity āha | suvargam eva lokam abhijayati | anūjjihīte | suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai |

{ 1.7.7.3} māruta eṣa bhavati | annaṃ vai marutaḥ | annam eva-avarundhe | ekaviṃśatikapālo bhavati pratiṣṭhityai | yo 'raṇye 'nuvākyo gaṇaḥ | taṃ madhyata upadadhāti | grāmyair eva paśubhir āraṇyān paśūn parigṛhṇāti | tasmād grāmyaiḥ paśubhir āraṇyāḥ paśavaḥ parigṛhītāḥ | pṛthir vainyaḥ | abhyaṣicyata |

{ 1.7.7.4} sa rāṣṭraṃ na-abhavat | sa etāni pārthāny apaśyat | tāny ajuhot | tair vai sa rāṣṭram abhavat | yat pārthāni juhoti | rāṣṭram eva bhavati | bārhaspatyaṃ pūrveṣām uttamaṃ bhavati | aindram uttareṣāṃ prathamam | brahma caivāsmai kṣatraṃ ca samīcī dadhāti | atho brahmann eva kṣatraṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati |

{ 1.7.7.5} ṣaṭ purastād abhiṣekasya juhoti | ṣaḍ upariṣṭāt | dvādaśa saṃpadyante | dvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ | saṃvatsaraḥ khalu vai devānāṃ pūḥ | devānām eva puraṃ madhyato vyavasarpati | tasya na kutaś cana-upāvyādho bhavati | bhūtānām aveṣṭīr {eveṣṭīr} juhoti | atra-atra vai mṛtyur jāyate | yatra-yatraiva mṛtyur jāyate | tata evainam avayajate | tasmād rājasūyena-ījānaḥ sarvam āyur eti | sarve hy asya mṛtyavo 'veṣṭāḥ | tasmād rājasūyena-ījāno na-abhicaritavai | pratyag enam abhicāraḥ stṛṇute |

anuvāka 8 rājasūye abhiṣekaḥ { 1.7.8.1} somasya tviṣir asi tava-iva me tviṣir bhūyād iti śārdūlacarma-upastṛṇāti | yaiva some tviṣiḥ | yā śārdūle | tām eva-avarundhe | mṛtyor vā eṣa varṇaḥ | yat-śārdūlaḥ | amṛtaṃ hiraṇyam | amṛtam asi mṛtyor mā pāhi-iti hiraṇyam upāsyati | amṛtam eva mṛtyor antardhatte | śatamānaṃ bhavati |

{ 1.7.8.2} śata-āyuḥ puruṣaḥ śata-indriyaḥ | āyuṣy eva-indriye pratitiṣṭhati | didyon mā pāhi-ity upariṣṭād adhinidadhāti | ubhayata evāsmai śarma dadhāti | aveṣṭā dandaśūkā iti klībaṃ sīsena vidhyati | dandaśūkān eva-avayajate | tasmāt klībaṃ dandaśūkā daṃśukāḥ | nirastaṃ namuceḥ śira iti lohitāyasaṃ nirasyati | pāpmānam eva namuciṃ niravadayate | prāṇā ātmanaḥ pūrve 'bhiṣicyā ity āhuḥ |

{ 1.7.8.3} somo rājā varuṇaḥ | devā dharmasuvaś ca ye | te-te vācaṃ suvantāṃ te-te prāṇaṃ suvantām ity āha | prāṇān evā''tmanaḥ pūrvān abhiṣiñcati | yad brūyāt | agnes tvā tejasā'bhiṣiñcāmi-iti | tejasvy eva syāt | duścarmā tu bhavet | somasya tvā dyumnena-abhiṣiñcāmi-ity āha | saumyo vai devatayā puruṣaḥ |

{ 1.7.8.4} svayā-evainaṃ devatayā-abhiṣiñcati | agnes tejasā-ity āha | teja evāsmin dadhāti | sūryasya varcasā-ity āha | varca evāsmin dadhāti | indrasya-indriyeṇa-ity āha | indriyam evāsmin dadhāti | mitrāvaruṇayor vīryeṇa-ity āha | vīram evāsmin dadhāti | marutām ojasā-ity āha |

{ 1.7.8.5} oja evāsmin dadhāti | kṣatrāṇāṃ kṣatrapatir asi-ity āha | kṣatrāṇām evainaṃ kṣatrapatiṃ karoti | ati divas pāhi-ity āha | aty anyān pāhi-iti vāva-etad āha | samāvavṛtrann adharāg udīcīr ity āha | rāṣṭram evāsmin dhruvam akaḥ | uccheṣaṇena juhoti | uccheṣaṇabhāgo vai rudraḥ | bhāgadheyena-eva rudraṃ niravadayate |

{ 1.7.8.6} udaṅ paretya-agnīddhre juhoti | eṣā vai rudrasya dik | svāyām eva diśi rudraṃ niravadayate | rudra yat te krayī paraṃ nāma-ity āha | yad vā asya krayī paraṃ nāma | tena vā eṣa hinasti | yaṃ hinasti | tenaivainaṃ saha śamayati | tasmai hutam asi yameṣṭam asi-ity āha | yamād evāsya mṛtyum avayajate |

{ 1.7.8.7} prajāpate na tvad etāny anya iti tasyai gṛhe juhuyāt | yāṃ kāmayeta rāṣṭram asyai prajā syād iti | rāṣṭram evāsyai prajā bhavati | parṇamayena-adhvaryur abhiṣiñcati | brahmavarcasam evāsmin tviṣiṃ dadhāti | audumbareṇa rājanyaḥ | ūrjam evāsminn annādyaṃ dadhāti | āśvatthena vaiśyaḥ | viśam evāsmin puṣṭiṃ dadhāti | naiyagrodhena janyaḥ | mitrāṇy evāsmai kalpayati | atho pratiṣṭhityai |

anuvāka 9 rājasūye rathārohaṇena vijayaḥ { 1.7.9.1} indrasya vajro 'si vārtraghna iti ratham upāvaharati vijityai | mitrāvaruṇayos tvā praśāstroḥ praśiṣā yunajmi-ity āha | brahmaṇā-evainaṃ devatābhyāṃ yunakti | praṣṭivāhinaṃ yunakti | praṣṭivāhī vai devarathaḥ | devaratham evāsmai yunakti | trayo 'śvā bhavanti | rathaś caturthaḥ | dvau savyeṣṭhasārathī | ṣaṭ saṃpadyante |

{ 1.7.9.2} ṣaḍ vā ṛtavaḥ | ṛtubhir evainaṃ yunakti | viṣṇukramān kramate | viṣṇur eva bhūtvā-imāṃl lokān abhijayati | yaḥ kṣatriyaḥ pratihitaḥ | so 'nvārabhate | rāṣṭram eva bhavati | triṣṭubhā-anvārabhate | indriyaṃ vai triṣṭuk | indriyam eva yajamāne dadhāti |

{ 1.7.9.3} marutāṃ prasave jeṣam ity āha | marudbhir eva prasūta ujjayati | āptaṃ mana ity āha | yad eva {ānaṃdā. yadaiva} manasaipsīt | tad āpat | rājanyaṃ jināti | anākrānta evā''kramate | vi vā eṣa indriyeṇa vīryeṇa-ṛdhyate | yo rājanyaṃ jināti | sam aham indriyeṇa vīryeṇa-ity āha |

{ 1.7.9.4} indriyam eva vīryam ātman dhatte | paśūnāṃ manyur asi tava-iva me manyur bhūyād iti vārāhī upānahāv upamuñcate | paśūnāṃ vā eṣa manyuḥ | yad varāhaḥ | tena-eva paśūnāṃ manyum ātman dhatte | abhi vā iyaṃ suṣuvāṇaṃ kāmayate | tasya-īśvarā-indriyaṃ vīryam ādātoḥ | vārāhī upānahāv upamuñcate | asyā eva-antardhatte | indriyasya vīryasya-anāttyai {anātyai, 3.3.10.2 meṃ bhī} |

{ 1.7.9.5} namo mātre pṛthivyā ity āha-ahiṃsāyai | iyad asy āyur asy āyur me dhehi-ity āha | āyurevā''tmandhatte| ūrk-asi-ūrjaṃ me dhehi-ity āha | ūrjam evā''tman dhatte | yuṅṅ asi varco 'si varco mayi dhehi-ity āha | varca evā''tman dhatte | ekadhā brahmaṇa upaharati | ekadhā-eva yajamāna āyur ūrjaṃ varco dadhāti | rathavimocanīyā juhoti pratiṣṭhityai |

{ 1.7.9.6} trayo 'śvā bhavanti | rathaś caturthaḥ | tasmāc catur juhoti | yad ubhau saha-avatiṣṭhetām | samānaṃ lokam iyātām | saha saṃgrahītrā rathavāhane ratham ādadhāti | suvargād evainaṃ lokād antardadhāti | haṃsaḥ śuciṣad ity ādadhāti | brahmaṇā-evainam upāvaharati | brahmaṇā dadhāti | aticchandasā-ādadhāti | aticchandā vai sarvāṇi chandāṃsi | sarvebhir evainaṃ chandobhir ādadhāti | varṣma vā eṣā chandasām | yad aticchandāḥ | yad aticchandasā dadhāti {aticchandasā-ādadhāti?} | varṣma-evainaṃ samānānāṃ karoti |

anuvāka 10 rājasūye vijayādūrdhvaṃ yajamānasya savaiḥ sevyatvam { 1.7.10.1} mitro 'si varuṇo 'si-ity āha | maitraṃ vā ahaḥ | vāruṇī rātriḥ | ahorātrābhyām evainam upāvaharati | mitro 'si varuṇo 'si-ity āha | maitro vai dakṣiṇaḥ | vāruṇaḥ savyaḥ | vaiśvadevy āmikṣā | svam evainau bhāgadheyam upāvaharati | sam ahaṃ viśvair devair ity āha |

{ 1.7.10.2} vaiśvadevyo vai prajāḥ | tā eva-ādyāḥ kurute | kṣatrasya nābhir asi kṣatrasya yonir asi-ity adhīvāsam āstṛṇāti sayonitvāya | syonām āsīda suṣadām āsīda-ity āha | yathāyajur evaitat | mā tvā hiṃsīn mā-mā hiṃsīd ity āha-ahiṃsāyai | niṣasāda dhṛtavrato varuṇaḥ pastyāsv ā sāmrājyāya sukratur ity āha | sāmrājyam evainaṃ sukratuṃ karoti | brahmām̐3 tvaṃ rājan brahmā-asi savitā'si satyasava ity āha | savitāram evainaṃ satyasavaṃ karoti |

{ 1.7.10.61} brahmām̐3 tvaṃ rājan brahmā-asi-indro 'si satya-ojā ity āha | indram evainaṃ satya-ojasaṃ karoti | brahmām̐3 tvaṃ rājan brahmā-asi mitro 'si suśeva ity āha | mitram evainaṃ suśevaṃ karoti | brahmām̐3 tvaṃ rājan brahmā-asi varuṇo 'si satyadharmā-ity āha | varuṇam evainaṃ satyadharmāṇaṃ karoti | savitā-asi satyasava ity āha | gāyatrīm evaitena-abhivyāharati | indro 'si satya-ojā ity āha | triṣṭubham evaitena-abhivyāharati |

{ 1.7.10.4} mitro 'si suśeva ity āha | jagatīm evaitena-abhivyāharati | satyam etā devatāḥ | satyam etāni chandāṃsi | satyam eva-avarundhe | varuṇo 'si satyadharmā-ity āha | anuṣṭubham evaitena-abhivyāharati | satya-anṛte vā anuṣṭup | satya-anṛte varuṇaḥ | satya-anṛte eva-avarundhe |

{ 1.7.10.5} na-enaṃ satya-anṛte udite hiṃstaḥ | ya evaṃ veda | indrasya vajro 'si vārtraghna iti sphyaṃ prayacchati | vajro vai sphyaḥ | vajreṇaivāsmā avaraparaṃ randhayati | evaṃ hi tat-śreyaḥ | yad asmā ete radhyeyuḥ | diśo 'bhy ayaṃ rājā-abhūd iti pañcākṣān prayacchati | ete vai sarve 'yāḥ | aparājayinam evainaṃ karoti |

{ 1.7.10.6} odanam udbruvate | parameṣṭhī vā eṣaḥ | yad odanaḥ | paramām evainaṃ śriyaṃ gamayati | suślokā3ṃ sumaṅgalā3ṃ { suślokā4ṃ sumaṅgalā4ṃ} satyarājā3n ity āha | āśiṣam evaitām āśāste | śaunaḥśepam { śyaunaḥśepam} ākhyāpayate | varuṇapāśād evainaṃ muñcati | paraḥśataṃ bhavati | śata-āyuḥ puruṣaḥ śata-indriyaḥ | āyuṣy eva-indriye pratitiṣṭhati | mārutasya ca-ekaviṃśatikapālasya vaiśvadevyai ca-āmikṣāyā agnaye sviṣṭakṛte samavadyati | devatābhir evainam ubhayataḥ parigṛhṇāti | apāṃ naptre svāhā-ūrjo naptre svāhā-agnaye gṛhapataye svāhā-iti tisra āhutīr juhoti | traya ime lokāḥ | eṣv eva lokeṣu pratitiṣṭhati |



prapāṭhaka: 8 anuvāka 1 rājasūye saṃsṛpāṃ haviḥ { 1.8.1.1} varuṇasya suṣuvāṇasya daśadhā-indriyaṃ vīryaṃ parāpatat | tat saṃsṛdbhir anusamasarpat | tat saṃsṛpāṃ saṃsṛttvam | agninā devena prathame 'hann anuprāyuṅkta | sarasvatyā vācā dvitīye | savitrā prasavena tṛtīye | pūṣṇā paśubhiś caturthe | bṛhaspatinā brahmaṇā pañcame | indreṇa devena ṣaṣṭhe | varuṇena svayā devatayā saptame |

{ 1.8.1.2} somena rājñā-aṣṭame | tvaṣṭrā rūpeṇa navame | viṣṇunā yajñena-āpnot | yat saṃsṛpo bhavanti | indriyam eva tad vīryaṃ yajamāna āpnoti | pūrvā-pūrvā vedir bhavati | indriyasya vīryasya-avaruddhyai | purastād upasadāṃ saumyena pracarati | somo vai retodhāḥ | reta eva tad dadhāti | antarā tvāṣṭreṇa | reta eva hitaṃ tvaṣṭā rūpāṇi vikaroti | upariṣṭād vaiṣṇavena | yajño vai viṣṇuḥ | yajña eva-antataḥ pratitiṣṭhati |

anuvāka 2 rājasūye daśapeyavidhiḥ { 1.8.2.1} jāmi vā etat kurvanti | yat sadyo dīkṣayanti | sadyaḥ somaṃ krīṇanti | puṇḍarisrajāṃ prayacchaty ajāmitvāya | aṅgirasaḥ suvargaṃ lokaṃ yantaḥ | apsu dīkṣātapasī prāveśayan | tat {yat} puṇḍarīkam abhavat | yat puṇḍarisrajāṃ prayacchati | sākṣād eva dīkṣātapasī avarundhe | daśabhir vatsataraiḥ somaṃ krīṇāti | daśa-akṣarā virāṭ |

{ 1.8.2.2} annaṃ virāṭ | virājā-eva-annādyam avarundhe | muṣkarā bhavanti sa-indratvāya | daśapeyo bhavati | annādyasya-avaruddhyai | śataṃ brāhmaṇāḥ pibanti | śata-āyuḥ puruṣaḥ śata-indriyaḥ | āyuṣy eva-indriye pratitiṣṭhati | saptadaśaṃ stotraṃ bhavati | saptadaśaḥ prajāpatiḥ |

{ 1.8.2.3} prajāpater āptyai | prakāśāv adhvaryave dadāti { prāksāśāvadhvaryave dadat} | prakāśam evainaṃ gamayati | srajam udgātre | vy evāsmai vāsayati | rukmaṃ hotre | ādityam evāsmā unnayati | aśvaṃ prastotṛpratihartṛbhyām | prājāpatyo vā aśvaḥ | prajāpater āptyai |

{ 1.8.2.4} dvādaśa paṣṭhauhīr brahmaṇe | āyur eva-avarundhe | vaśāṃ maitrāvaruṇāya | rāṣṭram eva vaśy akaḥ | ṛṣabhaṃ brāhmaṇācchaṃsine | rāṣṭram eva-indriyāvyakaḥ | vāsasī neṣṭāpotṛbhyāṃ | pavitre evāsyaite | sthūri yavācitam acchāvākāya | antata eva vvaruṇam avayajate |

{ 1.8.2.5} anaḍvāham agnīdhe | vahnir vā anaḍvān | vahnir agnīt | vahninā-eva vahni yajñasya-avarundhe | indrasya suṣuvāṇasya tredhā-indriyaṃ vīryaṃ parāpatat | bhṛgus tṛtīyam abhavat | śrāyantīyaṃ tṛtīyam | sarasvatī tṛtīyam | bhārgavo hotā bhavati | śrāyantīyaṃ brahmasāmaṃ bhavati | vāravantīyam agniṣṭomasāmam | sārasvatīr apo gṛhṇāti | indriyasya vīryasya-avaruddhyai | śrāyantīyaṃ brahmasāmaṃ bhavati | indriyam evāsmin vīryaṃ śrayati | vāravantīyam agniṣṭomasāmam | indriyam evāsmin vīryaṃ vārayati |

anuvāka 3 { 1.8.3.1} īśvaro vā eṣa diśo 'nūnmaditoḥ | yaṃ diśo 'nu vyāsthāpayanti | diśām aveṣṭayo bhavanti | dikṣv eva pratitiṣṭhaty anunmādāya | pañca devatā yajati | pañca diśaḥ | dikṣv eva pratitiṣṭhati | haviṣo-haviṣa iṣṭvā bārhaspatyam abhighārayayati | yajamānadevatyo vai bṛhaspatiḥ | yajamānam eva tejasā samardhayati |

{ 1.8.3.2} ādityāṃ malhāṃ {ahlāṃ} garbhiṇīm ālabhate | mārutīṃ pṛśniṃ paṣṭhauhīm | viśaṃ caivāsmai rāṣṭraṃ ca samīcī dadhāti | ādityayā pūrvayā pracarati | mārutyā-uttarayā | rāṣṭra eva viśam anubadhnāti | uccair ādityāyā āśrāvayati | upāṃśu mārutyai | tasmād rāṣṭraṃ viśam ativadati | garbhiṇy ādityā bhavati |

{ 1.8.3.3} indriyaṃ vai garbhaḥ | rāṣṭram eva-indriyāvyakaḥ | agarbhā mārutī | viḍ vai marutaḥ | viśam eva nirindriyām akaḥ | deva-asurāḥ saṃyattā āsan | te devā aśvinoḥ pūṣan vācaḥ satyaṃ saṃnidhāya | anṛtena-asurān abhyabhavan | te 'śvibhyāṃ pūṣṇe puroḍāśaṃ dvādaśakapālaṃ niravapan | tato vai te vācaḥ satyam avārundhata |

{ 1.8.3.4} yad aśvibhyāṃ pūṣṇe puroḍāśaṃ dvādaśakapālaṃ nirvapati | anṛtena-eva bhrātṛvyān abhibhūya | vācaḥ satyam avarundhe | sarasvate satyavāce carum | pūrvam eva-uditam | uttareṇa-abhigṛṇāti | savitre satyaprasavāya puroḍāśaṃ dvādaśakapālaṃ prasūtyai | dūtān prahiṇoti | āvida etā bhavanti | āvidam evainaṃ gamayanti | atho dūtebhya eva na cchidyate | tisṛdhanvaṃ śuṣkadṛtir dakṣiṇā samṛddhyai |

anuvāka 4 rājasūye prayujāṃ havirvidhiḥ { 1.8.4.1} āgneyam aṣṭākapālaṃ nirvapati | tasmāt-śiśire kurupañcālāḥ prāñco yānti | saumyaṃ carum | tasmād vasantaṃ vyavasāya-ādayanti | sāvitraṃ dvādaśakapālam | tasmāt purastād yavānāṃ savitrā virundhate | bārhaspatyaṃ carum | savitrā-eva virudhya | brahmaṇā yavān ādadhate | tvāṣṭram aṣṭākapālam |

{ 1.8.4.2} rūpāṇy eva tena kurvate | vaiśvānaraṃ dvādaśakapālam | tasmāj jaghanye naidāghe pratyañcaḥ kurupañcālā yānti | sārasvataṃ caruṃ nirvapati | tasmāt prāvṛṣi sarvā vāco vadanti | pauṣṇena vyavasyanti | maitreṇa kṛṣante | vāruṇena vidhṛtā āsate | kṣaitrapatyena pācayante | ādityena-ādadhate |

{ 1.8.4.3} māsi-māsy etāni havīṃṣi nirupyāṇi-ity āhuḥ | tenaiva-ṛtūn prayuṅkta iti | atho khalv āhuḥ | kaḥ saṃvatsaraṃ jīviṣyati-iti | ṣaḍ eva pūrvedyur nirupyāṇi | ṣaḍ uttaredyuḥ | tenaiva-ṛtūn prayuṅkte | dakṣiṇo rathavāhanavāhaḥ pūrveṣāṃ dakṣiṇā | uttara uttareṣām | saṃvatsarasyaiva-antau yunakti | suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai |

anuvāka 5 rājasūye sautrāmaṇīyāgavidhiḥ { 1.8.5.1} indrasya suṣuvāṇasya daśadhā-indriyaṃ vīryaṃ parāpatat | sa yat prathamaṃ niraṣṭhīvat | tat kvalam abhavat | yad dvitīyam | tad badaram | yat tṛtīyam | tat karkandhu | yan nastaḥ | sa siṃhaḥ | yad akṣyoḥ |

{ 1.8.5.2} sa śārdūlaḥ | yat karṇayoḥ | sa vṛkaḥ | ya ūrdhvaḥ | sa somaḥ | yā-avācī | sā surā | trayāḥ saktavo bhavanti | indriyasya-avaruddhyai | trayāṇi lomāni |

{ 1.8.5.3} tviṣim eva-avarundhe | trayo grahāḥ | vīryam eva-avarundhe | nāmnā daśamī | nava vai puruṣe prāṇāḥ | nābhir daśamī | prāṇā indriyaṃ vīryam | prāṇān eva-indriyaṃ vīryaṃ yajamāna ātman dhatte | sīsena klībāt-śaṣpāṇi krīṇāti | na vā etad ayo na hiraṇyam |

{ 1.8.5.4} yat sīsam | na strī na pumān | yat klībaḥ | na somo na surā | yat sautrāmaṇī samṛddhyai | svādvīṃ tvā svādunā-ity āha | somam evaināṃ karoti | somo 'sy aśvibhyāṃ pacyasva sarasvatyai pacyasva-indrāya sutrāmṇe pacyasva-ity āha | etābhyo hy eṣā devatābhyaḥ pacyate | tisraḥ saṃsṛṣṭā vasati |

{ 1.8.5.5} tisro hi rātrīḥ krītaḥ somo vasati | punātu te parisrutam iti yajuṣā punāti vyāvṛttyai {ānaṃdā. vyāvṛtyai} | pavitreṇa punāti | pavitreṇa hi somaṃ punanti | vāreṇa śaśvatā tanā-ity āha | vāreṇa hi somaṃ punanti | vāyuḥ pūtaḥ pavitreṇa-iti na-etayā punīyāt | vyṛddhā hy eṣā | atipavitasyaitayā punīyāt | kuvid aṅga-ity aniruktayā prājāpatyayā gṛhṇāti |

{ 1.8.5.6} aniruktaḥ prajāpatiḥ | prajāpater āptyai | ekayā-ṛcā gṛhṇāti | ekadhā-eva yajamāne vīryaṃ dadhāti | āśvinaṃ dhūmram ālabhate | aśvinau vai devānāṃ bhiṣajau | tābhyām evāsmai bheṣajaṃ karoti | sārasvataṃ meṣam | vāg vai sarasvatī | vācā-evainaṃ bhiṣajyati | aindram ṛṣabhaṃ sa-indratvāya |

anuvāka 6 rājasūye sautrāmaṇividhiḥ { 1.8.6.21} yat triṣu yūpeṣv ālabheta | bahirdhā-asmād indriyaṃ vīryaṃ dadhyāt | bhrātṛvyam asmai janayet | ekayūpa ālabhate | ekadhā-evāsminn indriyaṃ vīryaṃ dadhāti | na-asmai bhrātṛvyaṃ janayati | na-eteṣāṃ paśūnāṃ puroḍāśā bhavanti | grahapuroḍāśā hy ete | yuvaṃ surāmam aśvinā-iti sarvadevatye yājyānuvākye bhavataḥ | sarvā eva devatāḥ prīṇāti |

{ 1.8.6.2} brāhmaṇaṃ parikrīṇīyād uccheṣaṇasya pātāram | brāhmaṇo hy āhutyā uccheṣaṇasya pātā | yadi brāhmaṇaṃ na vindet | valmīkavapāyām avanayet | sā-eva tataḥ prāyaścittiḥ | yad vai sautrāmaṇyai vyṛddham | tad asyai samṛddham | nānādevatyāḥ paśavaś ca puroḍāśāś ca bhavanti samṛddhyai | aindraḥ paśūnām uttamo bhavati | aindraḥ puroḍāśānāṃ prathamaḥ |

{ 1.8.6.3} indriye evāsmai samīcī dadhāti | purastād anūyājānāṃ puroḍāśaiḥ pracarati | paśavo vai puroḍāśāḥ | paśūn eva-avarundhe | aindram ekādaśakapālaṃ nirvapati | indriyam eva-avarundhe | sāvitraṃ dvādaśakapālaṃ prasūtyai | vāruṇaṃ daśakapālam | antata eva varuṇam avayajate | vaḍavā dakṣiṇā |

{ 1.8.6.4} uta vā eṣā-aśvaṃ sūte | uta-aśvataram | uta soma uta surā | yat sautrāmaṇī samṛddhyai | bārhaspatyaṃ paśuṃ caturthamatipavitasya-ālabhate | brahma vai devānāṃ bṛhaspatiḥ | brahmaṇā-eva yajñasya vyṛddham apivapati | puroḍāśavān eṣa paśur bhavati | na hy etasya grahaṃ gṛhṇanti | somapratīkāḥ pitaras tṛpṇuta-iti śata-ātṛṇṇāyāṃ samavanayati |

{ 1.8.6.5} śata-āyuḥ puruṣaḥ śata-indriyaḥ | āyuṣy eva-indriye pratitiṣṭhati | dakṣiṇe 'gnau juhoti | pāpavasyasasya vyāvṛttyai | hiraṇyam antarā dhārayati | pūtām evaināṃ juhoti | śatamānaṃ bhavati | śata-āyuḥ puruṣaḥ śata-indriyaḥ | āyuṣy eva-indriye pratitiṣṭhati | 0 yatra-eva śata-ātṛṇṇāṃ dhārayati |

{ 1.8.6.6} tan nidadhāti pratiṣṭhityai | pitṝn vā etasya-indriyaṃ vīryaṃ gacchati | yaṃ somo 'tipavate | pitṛṇāṃ yājyānuvākyābhir upa tiṣṭhate | yad evāsya pitṝn indriyaṃ vīryaṃ gacchati | tad eva-avarundhe | tisṛbhir upatiṣṭhate | tṛtīye vā ito loke pitaraḥ | tān eva prīṇāti | atho trīṇi vai yajñasya-indriyāṇi | adhvaryur hotā brahmā | ta upatiṣṭhante | yāny eva yajñasya-indriyāṇi | tair evāsmai bheṣajaṃ karoti |

anuvāka 7 pavitrāhavidhiḥ, abhiṣecanīyavidhiḥ { 1.8.7.1} agniṣṭomam agra āharati | yajñamukhaṃ vā agniṣṭomaḥ | yajñamukham eva-ārabhya savam ākramate | atha-eṣo 'bhiṣecanīyaś catustriṃśaḥ pavamāno bhavati | trayastriṃśad vai devatāḥ | tā evā''pnoti | prajāpatiś catustriṃśaḥ | tam evā''pnoti | saṃśara eṣa stomānām ayathāpūrvam | yad viṣamāḥ stomāḥ |

{ 1.8.7.2} etāvān vai yajñaḥ | yāvān pavamānāḥ | antaḥśleṣaṇaṃ tvā anyat | yat samāḥ pavamānāḥ | tena-asaṃśaraḥ | tena yathāpūrvam | ātmanā-eva-agniṣṭomena-ṛdhnoti | ātmanā puṇyo bhavati | prajā vā ukthāni | paśava ukthāni {paśava ukthāni| prajā vā ukthāni iti pāṭha vyatyaya} | yad ukthyo bhavaty anusaṃtatyai |

anuvāka 8 rājasūye sāmamantrayorviśeṣābhidhānam { 1.8.8.29} upa tvā jāmayo gira iti pratipad bhavati | vāg vai vāyuḥ | vāca evaiṣo 'bhiṣekaḥ | sarvāsām eva prajānāṃ sūyate | sarvā enaṃ prajā rājā-iti vadanti | etam u tyaṃ daśakṣipa ity āha | ādityā vai prajāḥ | prajānām evaitena sūyate | yanti vā ete yajñamukhāt | ye sambhāryā akran |

{ 1.8.8.2} yad āha pavasva vāco agriya iti | tenaiva yajñamukhān na yanti | anuṣṭuk prathamā bhavati | anuṣṭug uttamā | vāg vā anuṣṭuk | vācā-eva prayanti | vācā-udyanti | udvatīr bhavanti | udvad vā anuṣṭubho rūpam | ānuṣṭubho rājanyaḥ |

{ 1.8.8.3} tasmād udvatīr bhavanti | saury anuṣṭug uttamā bhavati | suvargasya lokasya saṃtatyai | yo vai savād eti | na-enaṃ sava upanamati | yaḥ sāmabhya eti | pāpīyān suṣuvāṇo bhavati | etāni khalu vai sāmāni | yat pṛṣṭhāni | yat pṛṣṭhāni bhavanti |

{ 1.8.8.4} tair eva savān na-eti | yāni devarājānāṃ sāmāni | tair amuṣmiṃl loka ṛdhnoti | yāni manuṣyarājānāṃ sāmāni | tair asmiṃl loka ṛdhnoti | ubhayor eva lokayor ṛdhnoti | devaloke ca manuṣyaloke ca | ekaviṃśo 'bhiṣecanīyasya-uttamo bhavati | ekaviṃśaḥ keśavapanīyasya prathamaḥ | saptadaśo daśapeyaḥ |

{ 1.8.8.5} viḍ vā ekaviṃśaḥ | rāṣṭraṃ saptadaśaḥ | viśa evaitan madhyato 'bhiṣicyate | tasmād vā eṣa viśāṃ priyaḥ | viśo hi madhyato 'bhiṣicyate | yad vā enam ado diśo 'nu vyāsthāpayanti | tat suvargaṃ lokam abhyārohati | yad imaṃ lokaṃ na pratyavarohet | atijanaṃ vā-iyāt | ud vā mādyet | yad eṣa pratīcīnastomo bhavati | imam eva tena lokaṃ pratyavarohati | atho asminn eva loke pratitiṣṭhaty anunmādāya |

anuvāka 9 rājasūye abhiṣekagataviśeṣābhidhānam { 1.8.9.1} iyaṃ vai rajatā | asau hariṇī | yad rukmau bhavataḥ | ābhyām evainam ubhayataḥ parigṛhṇāti | varuṇasya vā abhiṣicyamānasya-āpaḥ | indriyaṃ vīryaṃ niraghnan | tat suvarṇaṃ hiraṇyam abhavat | yad rukmam antardadhāti | indriyasya vīryasya-anirghātāya | śatamāno bhavati śatakṣaraḥ | śata-āyuḥ puruṣaḥ śata-indriyaḥ | āyuṣy eva-indriye pratitiṣṭhati | āyur vai hiraṇyam | āyuṣyā evainam abhyatikṣaranti | tejo vai hiraṇyam | tejasyā evainam abhyatikṣaranti | varco vai hiraṇyam | varcasyā evainam abhyatikṣaranti |

anuvāka 10 { 1.8.10.1} apratiṣṭhito vā eṣa ity āhuḥ | yo rājasūyena yajata iti | yadā vā eṣa etena dvirātreṇa yajate | atha pratiṣṭhā | atha saṃvatsaram āpnoti | yāvanti saṃvatsarasya-ahorātrāṇi | tāvatīr etasya stotrīyāḥ | ahorātreṣv eva pratitiṣṭhati | agniṣṭomaḥ pūrvam ahar bhavati | atirātra uttaram |
