{ 1.8.10.2} nānā-eva-ahorātrayoḥ pratitiṣṭhati | paurṇamāsyāṃ pūrvam ahar bhavati | vyaṣṭakāyām uttaram | nānā-eva-ardhamāsayoḥ pratitiṣṭhati | amāvāsyāyāṃ pūrvam ahar bhavati | uddṛṣṭa uttaram | nānā-eva māsayoḥ pratitiṣṭhati | atho khalu | ye eva samānapakṣe puṇyāhe syātām | tayoḥ kāryaṃ pratiṣṭhityai |

{ 1.8.10.3} apaśavyo dvirātra ity āhuḥ | dve hy ete chandasī | gāyatraṃ ca traiṣṭubhaṃ ca | jagatīm antaryanti | na tena jagatī kṛtety āhuḥ | yad enāṃ tṛtīyasavane kurvanti-iti | yadā vā eṣā-ahīnasya-ahar bhajate | sa-ahnasya vā savanam | athaiva jagatī kṛtā | atha paśavyaḥ | vyuṣṭir vā eṣa dvirātraḥ | ya evaṃ vidvān dvirātreṇa yajate | vy evāsmā ucchati | atho tama eva-apahate | agniṣṭomam antata āharati | agniḥ sarvā devatāḥ | devatāsv eva pratitiṣṭhati |



kāṇḍa 2 āraṇyaka kāṇḍa?

prapāṭhaka: 1 agnihotram anuvāka 1 agnihotrasyopodghātaḥ { 2.1.1.1} aṅgiraso vai sattram āsata | teṣāṃ pṛśnir gharmadhug āsīt | sā-ṛjīṣeṇa-ajīvat | te 'bruvan | kasmai nu sattram āsmahe | ye 'syā oṣadhīr na janayāma iti | te divo vṛṣṭim asṛjanta | yāvantaḥ stokā avāpadyanta | tāvatīr oṣadhayo 'jāyanta | tā jātāḥ pitaro viṣeṇa-alimpan |

{ 2.1.1.2} tāsāṃ jagdhvā rupyantyait | te 'bruvan | ka idam ittham akar iti | vayaṃ bhāgadheyam icchamānā iti pitaro 'bruvan | kiṃ vo bhāgadheyam iti | agnihotra eva no 'py astv ity abruvan | tebhya etad bhāgadheyaṃ prāyacchan | yad dhutvā nimārṣṭi | tato vai ta oṣadhīr asvadayan | ya evaṃ veda |

{ 2.1.1.3} svadante 'smā oṣadhayaḥ | te vatsam upāvāsṛjan | idaṃ no havyaṃ pradāpaya-iti | so 'bravīd varaṃ vṛṇai | daśa mā rātrīr jātaṃ na dohan | āsaṃgavaṃ mātrā saha carāṇi-iti | tasmād vatsaṃ jātaṃ daśa rātrīr na duhanti | āsaṃgavaṃ mātrā saha carati | vārevṛtaṃ hy asya | tasmād vatsaṃ saṃsṛṣṭadhayaṃ rudro ghātukaḥ | ati hi sandhāṃ dhayati |

anuvāka 2 agnihotra nirūpaṇam { 2.1.2.1} prajāpatir agnim asṛjata | taṃ prajā anv asṛjyanta | tam abhāga upāsta | so 'sya prajābhir apākrāmat | tam avarurutsamāno 'nvait | tam avarudhan na-aśaknot | sa tapo 'tapyata | so 'gnir upāramata-atāpi vai sya prajāpatir iti |

{ 2.1.2.5} tad ghṛtam abhavat | tasmād yasya dakṣiṇataḥ keśā unmṛṣṭāḥ | tāṃ jyeṣṭhalakṣmī prājāpatyā-ity āhuḥ | yad rarāṭād udamṛṣṭa | tasmād rarāṭe keśā na santi | tad agnau prāgṛhṇāt | tad vyacikitsat | juhavānī3 mā hauṣā3m iti | tad vicikitsāyai janma | ya evaṃ vidvān vicikitsati |

{ 2.1.2.3} vasīya eva cetayate | taṃ vāg abhyavadaj juhudhi-iti | so 'bravīt | kas tvam asi-iti | svā-eva te vāg ity abravīt | so 'juhot svāhā-iti | tat svāhākārasya janma | ya evaṃ svāhākārasya janma veda | karoti svāhākāreṇa vīryam | yasyaivaṃ viduṣaḥ svāhākāreṇa juhvati |

{ 2.1.2.4} bhogāya-evāsya hutaṃ bhavati | tasyā āhutyai puruṣam asṛjata | dvitīyam ajuhot | so 'śvam asṛjata | tṛtīyam ajuhot | sa gām asṛjata | caturtham ajuhot | so 'vimasṛjata | pañcamam ajuhot | so 'jām asṛjata |

{ 2.1.2.5} so 'gnir abibhet | āhutībhir vai mā-āpnoti-iti | sa prajāpatiṃ punaḥ prāviśat | taṃ prajāpatir abravīt | jāyasva-iti | so 'bravīt | kiṃ bhāgadheyam abhijaniṣya iti | tubhyam eva-idaṃ hūyātā ity abravīt | sa etad bhāgadheyam abhyajāyata | yad agnihotram |

{ 2.1.2.6} tasmād agnihotram ucyate | tad dhūyamānam ādityo 'bravīt | mā hauṣīḥ | ubhayor vai nāv etad iti | so 'gnir abravīt | kathaṃ nau hoṣyanti-iti | sāyam eva tubhyaṃ juhavan | prātar mahyam ity abravīt | tasmād agnaye sāyaṃ hūyate | sūryāya prātaḥ |

{ 2.1.2.7} āgneyī vai rātriḥ | aindram ahaḥ | yad anudite sūrye prātar juhuyāt | ubhayam evā''gneyaṃ syāt | udite sūrye prātar juhoti | tathā-agnaye sāyaṃ hūyate | sūryāya prātaḥ | rātriṃ vā anu prajāḥ prajāyante | ahnā pratitiṣṭhanti | yat sāyaṃ juhoti |

{ 2.1.2.11} pra-eva tena jāyate | udite sūrye prātar juhoti | praty eva tena tiṣṭhati | prajāpatir akāmayata prajāyeya-iti | sa etad agnihotraṃ mithunam apaśyat | tad udite sūrye 'juhot | yajuṣā-anyat | tūṣṇīm anyat | tato vai sa prājāyata | yasyaivaṃ viduṣa udite sūrye 'gnihotraṃ juhvati |

{ 2.1.2.9} pra-eva jāyate | atho yathā divā prajānann eti | tādṛg eva tat | atho khalv āhuḥ | yasya vai dvau puṇyau gṛhe vasataḥ | yas tayor anyaṃ rādhayaty anyaṃ na | ubhau vāva sa tāv ṛcchati-iti | agniṃ vāvā''dityaḥ sāyaṃ praviśati | tasmād agnir dūrān naktaṃ dadṛśe | ubhe hi tejasī saṃpadyete |

{ 2.1.2.10} udyantaṃ vāvā''dityam agnir anusamārohati | tasmād dhūma eva-agner divā dadṛśe | yad agnaye sāyaṃ juhuyāt | ā sūryāya vṛścyeta | yat sūryāya prātar juhuyāt | ā-agnaye vṛścyeta | devatābhyaḥ samadaṃ dadhyāt | agnir jyotir jyotiḥ sūryaḥ svāhā-ity eva sāyaṃ hotavyam | sūryo jyotir jyotir agniḥ svāhā-iti prātaḥ | tathā-ubhābhyāṃ sāyaṃ hūyate |

{ 2.1.2.11} ubhābhyāṃ prātaḥ | na devatābhyaḥ samadaṃ dadhāti | agnir jyotir ity āha | agnir vai retodhāḥ | prajā jyotir ity āha | prajā evāsmai prajanayati | sūryo jyotir ity āha | prajāsv eva prajātāsu reto dadhāti | jyotir agniḥ svāhā-ity āha | prajā eva prajātā asyāṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati |

{ 2.1.2.12} tūṣṇīm uttarām āhutiṃ juhoti | mithunatvāya prajātyai | yad udite sūrye prātar juhuyāt | yathā-atithaye pradrutāya śūnyāya-āvasathāya-āhāryaṃ haranti | tādṛg eva tat | kva-aha tatas tad bhavati-ity āhuḥ | yat sa na veda | yasmai taddharanti-iti | tasmād yad auṣasaṃ juhoti | tad eva saṃprati | atho yathā prārtham auṣasaṃ pariveveṣṭi | tādṛg eva tat |

anuvāka 3 agnihotre adhiśrayaṇādikā haviḥsaṃskārā { 2.1.3.1} rudro vā eṣaḥ | yad agniḥ | patnī sthālī | yan madhye 'gner adhiśrayet | rudrāya patnīm apidadhyāt | pramāyukā syāt | udīco 'ṅgārān nirūhya-adhiśrayati | patniyai gopīthāya | vyantān karoti | tathā patny apramāyukā bhavati |

{ 2.1.3.2} gharmo vā eṣo 'śāntaḥ | ahar-ahaḥ pravṛjyate | yad agnihotram | pratiṣiñcet paśukāmasya | śāntam iva hi paśavyam | na pratiṣiñced brahmavarcasakāmasya | samiddham iva hi brahmavarcasam | atho khalu | pratiṣicyam eva | yat pratiṣiñcati |

{ 2.1.3.3} tat paśavyam | yaj juhoti | tad brahmavarcasi | ubhayam eva-akaḥ | pracyutaṃ vā etad asmāl lokāt | agataṃ devalokam | yat-śṛtaṃ havir anabhighāritam | abhidyotayati | abhy evainad ghārayati | atho devatrā-evainad gamayati |

{ 2.1.3.4} paryagni karoti | rakṣasām apahatyai | triḥ paryagni karoti | tryāvṛd dhi yajñaḥ | atho medhyatvāya | yat prācīnam udvāsayet | yajamānaṃ śucā-arpayet | yad dakṣiṇā | pitṛdevatyaṃ syāt | yat pratyak |

{ 2.1.3.5} patnīṃ śucā-arpayet | udīcīnam udvāsayati | eṣā vai devamanuṣyāṇāṃ śāntā dik | tām evainad anūdvāsayati śāntyai | vartma karoti | yajñasya saṃtatyai | niṣṭapati | upa-eva tat stṛṇāti | catur unnayati | catuṣpādaḥ paśavaḥ |

{ 2.1.3.6} paśūn eva-avarundhe | sarvān pūrṇān unnayati | sarve hi puṇyā rāddhāḥ | anūca unnayati | prajāyā anūcīnatvāya | anūcy evāsya prajā-ardhukā bhavati | saṃmṛśati vyāvṛttyai | na-ahoṣyann upasādayet | yad ahoṣyann upasādayet | yathā-anyasmā upanidhāya |

{ 2.1.3.7} anyasmai prayacchati | tādṛg eva tat | ā-asmai vṛścyeta | yad eva gārhapatye 'dhiśrayati | tena gārhapatyaṃ prīṇāti | agnir abibhet | āhutayo mā-atyeṣyanti-iti | sa etāṃ samidham apaśyat | tām ādhatta | tato vā agnāv āhutayo 'dhriyanta |

{ 2.1.3.8} yad enaṃ samayacchat | tat samidhaḥ samittvam | samidham ādadhāti | sam evainaṃ yacchati | āhutīnāṃ dhṛtyai | atho agnihotram eva-idhmavat karoti | āhutīnāṃ pratiṣṭhityai | brahmavādino vadanti | yad ekāṃ samidham ādhāya dve āhutī juhoti | atha kasyāṃ samidhi dvitīyām āhutiṃ juhoti-iti |

{ 2.1.3.} yad dve samidhāv ādadhyāt | bhrātṛvyam asmai janayet | ekāṃ samidham ādhāya | yajuṣā-anyām āhutiṃ juhoti | ubhe eva samidvatī āhutī juhoti | na-asmai bhrātṛvyaṃ janayati | ādīptāyāṃ juhoti | samiddham iva hi brahmavarcasam | atho yathā-atithiṃ jyotiṣ kṛtvā pariveveṣṭi | tādṛg eva tat | catur unnayati | dvir juhoti | tasmād dvipād-catuṣpādam atti | atho dvipady eva catuṣpadaḥ pratiṣṭhāpayati |

anuvāka 4 agnihotre upayukta haviḥsaṃskārāḥ { 2.1.4.4} uttarāvatīṃ vai devā āhutim ajuhavuḥ | avācīm asurāḥ | tato devā abhavan | parā-asurāḥ | yaṃ kāmayeta vasīyān syād iti | kanīyas tasya pūrvaṃ hutvā | uttaraṃ bhūyo juhuyāt | eṣā vā uttarāvaty āhutiḥ | tāṃ devā ajuhavuḥ | tataste 'bhavan |

{ 2.1.4.2} yasyaivaṃ juhvati | bhavaty eva | yaṃ kāmayeta pāpīyānt syād iti | bhūyas tasya pūrvaṃ hutvā | uttaraṃ kanīyo juhuyāt | eṣā vā avācy āhutiḥ | tām asurā ajuhavuḥ | tatas te parābhavan | yasyaivaṃ juhvati | parā-eva bhavati |

{ 2.1.4.3} hutvā-upasādayaty ajāmitvāya | atho vyāvṛttyai | gārhapatyaṃ pratīkṣate | ananudhyāyinam evainaṃ karoti | agnihotrasya vai sthāṇur asti | taṃ ya ṛcchet | yajñasthāṇum ṛcchet | eṣa vā agnihotrasya sthāṇuḥ | yat pūrvā-āhutiḥ | tāṃ yad uttarayā-abhijuhuyāt |

{ 2.1.4.4} yajñasthāṇum ṛcchet | atihāya pūrvām āhutiṃ juhoti | yajñasthāṇum eva parivṛṇakti | atho bhrātṛvyam evā''ptvā-atikrāmati | avācīnaṃ sāyam upamārṣṭi | reta eva tad dadhāti | ūrdhvaṃ prātaḥ | pra janayaty eva tat | brahmavādino vadanti | catur unnayati |

{ 2.1.4.5} dvir juhoti | atha kva dve āhutī bhavata iti | agnau vaiśvānara iti brūyāt | eṣa vā agnir vaiśvānaraḥ | yad brāhmaṇaḥ | hutvā dviḥ prāśnāti | agnāv eva vaiśvānare dve āhutī juhoti | dvir juhoti | dvir nimārṣṭi | dviḥ prāśnāti |

{ 2.1.4.6} ṣaṭ saṃpadyante | ṣaḍ vā ṛtavaḥ | ṛtūn eva prīṇāti | brahmavādino vadanti | kiṃdevatyam agnihotram iti | vaiśvadevam iti brūyāt | yad yajuṣā juhoti | tad aindrāgnam | yat tūṣṇīm | tat prājāpatyam |

{ 2.1.4.7} yan nimārṣṭi | tad oṣadhīnām | yad dvitīyam | tat pitṛṇām | yat prāśnāti | tad garbhāṇām | tasmād garbhā anaśnanto vardhante | yad ācāmati | tan manuṣyāṇām | udaṅ paryāvṛtya-ācāmati |

{ 2.1.4.8} ātmano gopīthāya | nirṇenekti śuddhyai | niṣṭapati svagākṛtyai | uddiśati | saptaṛṣīn eva prīṇāti | dakṣiṇā paryāvartate | svam eva vīryam anu paryāvartate | tasmād dakṣiṇo 'rdha ātmano vīryāvattaraḥ | atho ādityasyaivā''vṛtam anu paryāvartate | hutvā-upasamindhe |

{ 2.1.4.9} brahmavarcasasya samiddhyai | na barhir anu praharet | asaṃsthito vā eṣa yajñaḥ | yad agnihotram | yad anupraharet | yajñaṃ vicchindyāt | tasmān na-anuprahṛtyam | yajñasya saṃtatyai | apo ninayati | avabhṛthasyaiva rūpam akaḥ |

anuvāka 5 agnihotre kāmyāni homadravyāṇi { 2.1.5.1} brahmavādino vadanti | agnihotraprāyaṇā yajñāḥ | kiṃprāyaṇam agnihotram iti | vatso vā agnihotrasya prāyaṇam | agnihotraṃ yajñānām | tasya pṛthivī sadaḥ | antarikṣam āgnīdhram | dyaur havirdhānam | divyā āpaḥ prokṣaṇayaḥ | oṣadhayo barhiḥ |

{ 2.1.5.2} vanaspataya idhmaḥ | diśaḥ paridhayaḥ | ādityo yūpaḥ | yajamānaḥ paśuḥ | samudro 'vabhṛthaḥ | saṃvatsaraḥ svagākāraḥ | tasmād āhitāgneḥ sarvam eva barhiṣyaṃ dattaṃ bhavati | yat sāyaṃ juhoti | rātrim eva tena dakṣiṇyāṃ kurute | yat prātaḥ |

{ 2.1.5.3} ahar eva tena dakṣiṇyaṃ kurute | yat tato dadāti | sā dakṣiṇā | yāvanto vai devā ahutam ādan | te parābhavan | ta etad agnihotraṃ sarvasyaiva samavadāya-ajuhavuḥ | tasmād āhuḥ | agnihotraṃ vai devā gṛhāṇāṃ niṣkṛtim apaśyann iti | yat sāyaṃ juhoti | rātriyā eva taddhutādyāya |

{ 2.1.5.4} yajamānasya-aparābhāvāya | yat prātaḥ | ahna eva taddhutādyāya | yajamānasya-aparābhāvāya | yat tato 'śnāti | hutam eva tat | dvayoḥ payasā juhuyāt paśukāmasya | etad vā agnihotraṃ mithunam | ya evaṃ veda | pra prajayā paśubhir mithunair jāyate |

{ 2.1.5.5} imām eva pūrvayā duhe | amūm uttarayā | adhiśritya-uttaram ānayati | yonāv eva tad retaḥ siñcati prajanane | ājyena juhuyāt tejaskāmasya | tejo vā ājyam | tejasvy eva bhavati | payasā paśukāmasya | etad vai paśūnāṃ rūpam | rūpeṇa-evāsmai paśūn avarundhe |

{ 2.1.5.6} paśumān eva bhavati | dadhnā-indriyakāmasya | indriyaṃ vai dadhi | indriyāvy eva bhavati | yavāgvā grāmakāmasya-oṣadhā vai manuṣyāḥ | bhāgadheyenaivāsmai sajātān avarundhe | grāmy eva bhavati | ayajño vā eṣaḥ | yo 'sāmā |

{ 2.1.5.7} catur unnayati | caturakṣaraṃ rathaṃtaram | rathantarasyaiṣa varṇaḥ | upari-iva harati | antarikṣaṃ vāmadevyam | vāmadevyasyaiṣa varṇaḥ | dvir juhoti | dvyakṣaraṃ bṛhat | bṛhata eṣa varṇaḥ | agnihotram eva tat sāmanvat karoti |

{ 2.1.5.8} yo vā agnihotrasya-upasado veda | upa-enam upasado namanti | vindata upasattāram | unnīya-upasādayati | pṛthivīm eva prīṇāti | hoṣyann upasādayati | antarikṣam eva prīṇāti | hutvā-upasādayati | divam eva prīṇāti | etā vā agnihotrasya-upasadaḥ |

{ 2.1.5.9} ya evaṃ veda | upa-enam upasado namanti | vindata upasattāram | yo vā agnihotrasya-āśrāvitaṃ pratyāśrāvitaṃ hotāraṃ brahmāṇaṃ vaṣaṭkāraṃ veda | tasya tv eva hutam | prāṇo vā agnihotrasya-āśrāvitam | apānaḥ pratyāśrāvitam | mano hotā | cakṣur brahmā | nimeṣo vaṣaṭkāraḥ |

{ 2.1.5.10} ya evaṃ veda | tasya tv eva hutam | sāyaṃyāvānaś ca vai devāḥ prātaryāvāṇaś ca-agnihotriṇo gṛham āgacchanti | tān yan na tarpayet | prajayā-asya paśubhir vitiṣṭheran | yat tarpayet | tṛptā enaṃ prajayā paśubhis tarpayeyuḥ | sajūr devaiḥ sāyaṃ yāvabhir iti sāyaṃ saṃmṛśati | sajūr devaiḥ prātar yāvabhir iti prātaḥ | ye caiva devāḥ sāyaṃyāvāno ye ca prātaryāvāṇaḥ |

{ 2.1.5.11} tān eva-ubhayāṃs tarpayati | ta enaṃ tṛptāḥ prajayā paśubhis tarpayanti | aruṇo ha sma-āha-aupaveśiḥ | agnihotra eva-ahaṃ sāyaṃ-prātar vajraṃ bhrātṛvyebhyaḥ praharāmi | tasmān mat pāpīyāṃso bhrātṛvyā iti | catur unnayati | dvir juhoti | samit saptamī | saptapadā śakvarī | śākvaro vajraḥ | agnihotra eva tat sāyaṃ-prātar vajraṃ yajamāno bhrātṛvyāya praharati | bhavaty ātmanā | parā-asya bhrātṛvyo bhavati |

anuvāka 6 agnihotre abhyuddravaṇaṃ homābhāvapratīkāraṃ ca { 2.1.6.1} prajāpatir akāmayata-ātmanvan me jāyeta-iti | so 'juhot | tasya-ātmanvad ajāyata | agnir vāyur ādityaḥ | te 'bruvan | prajāpatir ahauṣīd ātmanvan me jāyeta-iti | tasya vayam ajaniṣmahi | jāyatāṃ na ātmanvad iti te 'juhavuḥ | prāṇānām agniḥ | tanuvai vāyuḥ |

{ 2.1.6.2} cakṣuṣa ādityaḥ | teṣāṃ hutād ajāyata gaur eva | tasyai payasi vyāyacchanta | mama hutād ajani mama-iti | te prajāpatiṃ praśnam āyan | sa ādityo 'gnim abravīt | yataro nau jayāt | tan nau saha-asad iti | kasya-eko { kasyaiko} { kasyai ko} 'hauṣīd iti prajāpatir abravīt kasya-eka { kasyaiko} kasyai ka} iti | prāṇānām aham ity agniḥ |

{ 2.1.6.3} tanuvā aham iti vāyuḥ | cakṣuṣo 'ham ity ādityaḥ | ya eva prāṇānām ahauṣīt | tasya hutād ajani-iti | agner hutād ajani-iti | tad agnihotrasya-agnihotratvam | gaur vā agnihotram | ya evaṃ veda gaur agnihotram iti | prāṇāpānābhyām eva-agniṃ samardhayati | avyardhukaḥ prāṇāpānābhyāṃ bhavati |

{ 2.1.6.4} ya evaṃ veda | tau vāyur abravīt | anu mā-ābhajatam iti | yad eva gārhapatye 'dhiśritya-āhavanīyam abhyuddravān | tena tvāṃ prīṇān ity abrūtām | tasmād yad gārhapatye 'dhiśritya-āhavanīyam abhyuddravati | vāyum eva tena prīṇāti | prajāpatir devatāḥ sṛjamānaḥ | agnim eva devatānāṃ prathamam asṛjata | so 'nyad ālambhyam avittvā|

{ 2.1.6.5} prajāpatim abhi paryāvartata | sa mṛtyor abibhet | so 'mum ādityam ātmano niramimīta | taṃ hutvā parāṅ paryāvartata | tato vai sa mṛtyum apājayat | apa mṛtyuṃ jayati | ya evaṃ veda | tasmād yasyaivaṃ viduṣaḥ | utaikāham uta dvyahaṃ na juhvati | hutam evāsya bhavati | asau hy ādityo 'gnihotram |

anuvāka 7 agnihotre haviṣaḥ sarvadevatāsambandhena praśaṃsā { 2.1.7.7} raudraṃ gavi | vāyavyam upasṛṣṭam | āśvinaṃ duhyamānam | saumyaṃ dugdham | vāruṇam adhiśritam | vaiśvadevā bhindavaḥ | pauṣṇam udantam | sārasvataṃ viṣyandamānam | maitraṃ śaraḥ | dhātur udvāsitam | bṛhaspater unnītam | savituḥ prakrāntam | dyāvāpṛthivyaṃ hriyamāṇam | aindrāgnam upasannam | agneḥ pūrvā-āhutiḥ | prajāpater uttarā | aindraṃ hutam |

anuvāka 8 agnihotre dohanaprakāraḥ { 2.1.8.8} dakṣiṇata upasṛjati | pitṛlokam eva tena jayati | prācīm āvartayati | devalokam eva tena jayati | udīcīm āvratya {āvṛtya} dogdhi | manuṣyalokam eva tena jayati | pūrvau duhyāj jyeṣṭhasya jyaiṣṭhineyasya | yo vā gataśrīḥ {''gataśrīḥ} syāt | aparau duhyāt kaniṣṭhasya kāniṣṭhineyasya | yo vā bubhūṣet |

{ 2.1.8.2} na saṃmṛśati | pāpavasyasasya vyāvṛttyai | vāyavyaṃ vā etad upasṛṣṭam | āśvinaṃ duhyamānam | maitraṃ dugdham | aryamṇa udvāsyamānam | tvāṣṭram unnīyamānam | bṛhaspater unnītam | savituḥ prakrāntam | dyāvāpṛthivyaṃ hriyamāṇam |

{ 2.1.8.3} aindrāgnam upasāditam | sarvābhyo vā eṣa devatābhyo juhoti | yo 'gnihotraṃ juhoti | yathā khalu vai dhenuṃ tīrthe tarpayati | evam agnihotrī yajamānaṃ tarpayati | tṛpyati prajayā paśubhiḥ | pra suvargaṃ lokaṃ jānāti | paśyati putram | paśyati pautram | pra prajayā paśubhir mithunair jāyate | yasyaivaṃ viduṣo 'gnihotraṃ juhvati | ya u cainad evaṃ veda |

anuvāka 9 agnihotre asaṃsṛṣṭahomamantrau { 2.1.9.1} trayo vai praiyamedhā āsan | teṣāṃ trir eko 'gnihotram ajuhot | dvir ekaḥ | sakṛd ekaḥ | teṣāṃ yas trir ajuhot | sa ṛcā-ajuhot | yo dviḥ | sa yajuṣā | yaḥ sakṛt | sa tūṣṇīm |

{ 2.1.9.2} yaś ca yajuṣā-ajuhod yaś ca tūṣṇīm | tāv ubhāv ārdhnutām | tasmād yajuṣā-āhutiḥ pūrvā hotavyā | tūṣṇīm uttarā | ubhe eva-ṛddhī avarundhe | agnir jyotir jyotir agniḥ svāhā-iti sāyaṃ juhoti | reta eva tad dadhāti | sūryo jyotir jyotiḥ sūryaḥ svāhā-iti prātaḥ | reta eva hitaṃ prajanayati | reto vā etasya hitaṃ na prajāyate |

{ 2.1.9.3} yasya-agnihotram ahutaṃ sūryo 'bhy udeti | yady ante syāt | unnīya prāṅ udādravet | sa upasādya-ātamitor āsīta | sa yadātāmyet | atha bhūḥ svāhā-iti juhuyāt | prajāpatir vai bhūtaḥ | tam eva-upāsarat | sa evainaṃ tata unnayati | na-ārtim ārcchati yajamānaḥ |

anuvāka 10 agnihotre vahneravasthāviśeṣānusāreṇa homaprāśastyaṃ { 2.1.10.1} yad agnim uddharati | vasavas tarhy agniḥ | tasmin yasya tathāvidhe juhvati | vasuṣv evāsya-agnihotraṃ hutaṃ bhavati | nihito dhūpāyañ- śete | rudrās tarhy agniḥ | tasmin yasya tathāvidhe juhvati | rudreṣv eva-asya-agnihotraṃ hutaṃ bhavati | prathamam idhmam arcir ālabhate | ādityās tarhy agniḥ |

{ 2.1.10.2} tasmin yasya tathāvidhe juhvati | ādityeṣv evāsya-agnihotraṃ hutaṃ bhavati | sarva eva sarvaśa idhma ādīpto bhavati | viśve devās tarhy agniḥ | tasmin yasya tathāvidhe juhvati | viśveṣv evāsya deveṣv agnihotraṃ hutaṃ bhavati | nitarām arcir upāvaiti lohinīkā-iva bhavati | indras tarhy agniḥ | tasmin yasya tathāvidhe juhvati | indra evāsya-agnihotraṃ hutaṃ bhavati |

{ 2.1.10.3} aṅgārā bhavanti | tebhyo 'ṅgārebhyo 'rcir udeti | prajāpatis tarhy agniḥ | tasmin yasya tathāvidhe juhvati | prajāpatāv evāsya-agnihotraṃ hutaṃ bhavati | śaro 'ṅgārā adhyūhante | brahma tarhy agniḥ | tasmin yasya tathāvidhe juhvati | brahmann evāsya-agnihotraṃ hutaṃ bhavati | vasuṣu rudreṣv ādityeṣu viśveṣu deveṣu | indre prajāpatau brahman | aparivargam evāsyaitāsu devatāsu hutaṃ bhavati | yasyaivaṃ viduṣo 'gnihotraṃ juhvati | ya u cainad evaṃ veda |

anuvāka 11 agnihotre kālabhedena pariṣecanaṃ { 2.1.11.1} ṛtaṃ tvā satyena pariṣiñcāmi-iti sāyaṃ pariṣiñcati | satyaṃ tvā-ṛtena pariṣiñcāmi-iti prātaḥ | agnir vā ṛtam | asāv ādityaḥ satyam | agnim eva tad ādityena sāyaṃ pariṣiñcati | agninā-ādityaṃ prātaḥ saḥ | yāvad ahorātre bhavataḥ | tāvad asya lokasya | na-artir na ṛṣṭiḥ | na-anto na paryanto 'sti | yasyaivaṃ viduṣo 'gnihotraṃ juhvati | ya u cainad evaṃ veda |



prapāṭhaka: 2 daśahotrādayaḥ mantrāḥ tadbrāhmaṇaṃ anuvāka 1 { 2.2.1.1} prajāpatir akāmayata prajāḥ sṛjeya-iti | sa etaṃ daśahotāram apaśyat | taṃ manasonudrutya darbhastambe 'juhot | tato vai sa prajā asṛjata | tā asmāt sṛṣṭā apākrāman | tā graheṇa-agṛhṇāt | tad grahasya grahatvam | yaḥ kāmayeta prajāyeya-iti | sa daśahotāraṃ manasonudrutya darbhastambe juhuyāt | 0 prajāpatir vai daśahotā |

{ 2.2.1.2} prajāpatir eva bhūtvā prajāyate | manasā juhoti | mana iva hi prajāpatiḥ | prajāpater āptyai {|} pūrṇayā juhoti | pūrṇa iva hi prajāpatiḥ | prajāpaterāptyai | nyūnayā juhoti | nyūnād dhi prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata | prajānāṃ sṛṣṭyai |

{ 2.2.1.3} darbhastambe juhoti | etasmād vai yoneḥ prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata | yasmād eva yoneḥ prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata | tasmād eva yoneḥ pra jāyate | brāhmaṇo dakṣiṇata upāste | brāhmaṇo vai prajānām upadraṣṭā | upadraṣṭumaty eva prajāyate | graho bhavati | prajānāṃ sṛṣṭānāṃ dhṛtyai | 0 yaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ vidyāṃ vidvāṃsaṃ yaśo na-ṛcchet { narcchet} |

{ 2.2.1.4} so 'raṇyaṃ paretya | darbhastambam udgráthya | brāhmaṇaṃ dakṣiṇato niṣadya | caturhotṝn vyācakṣīta | etad vai devānāṃ paramaṃ guhyaṃ brahma | yac caturhotāraḥ | tad eva prakāśaṃ gamayati | tad enaṃ prakāśaṃ gatam | prakāśaṃ prajānāṃ gamayati | 0 darbhastambam udgráthya vyācaṣṭe |

{ 2.2.1.5} agnivān vai darbhastambaḥ | agnivaty eva vyācaṣṭe | brāhmaṇo dakṣiṇata upāste | brāhmaṇo vai prajānām upadraṣṭā | upadraṣṭumaty evainaṃ yaśa ṛcchati | īśvaraṃ taṃ yaśo 'rtor ity āhuḥ | yasyāṃ te vyācaṣṭa iti | varas tasmai deyaḥ | yad evainaṃ tatra-upanamati | 0 tad eva-avarundhe |

{ 2.2.1.6} agnim ādadhāno daśahotrā-araṇim avadadhyāt | prajātam evainam ādhatte | tenaiva-uddrútya-agnihotraṃ juhuyāt | prajātam evainaj juhoti | havir nirvapsyan daśahotāraṃ vyācakṣīta | prajātam evainaṃ nirvapati | sāmidhenīr anuvakṣyan daśahotāraṃ vyācakṣīta | sāmidhenir eva sṛṣṭvā-ārabhya pratanute | atho yajño vai daśahotā | 0 yajñam eva tanute |

{ 2.2.1.7} abhicaran daśahotāraṃ juhuyāt | nava vai puruṣe prāṇāḥ | nābhir daśamī | saprāṇam evainam abhicarati | etāvad vai puruṣasya svam | yāvat prāṇāḥ | yāvad evāsya-asti | tad abhicarati | svakṛta iriṇe juhoti pradare vā | 0 etad vā asyai nirṛtigṛhītam | 1 nirṛtigṛhīta evainaṃ nirṛtyā grāhayati | 2 yad vācaḥ krūram | 3 tena vaṣaṭkaroti | 4 vāca evainaṃ krūreṇa pravṛścati | 5 tā jagārtim ārcchati |

anuvāka 2 { 2.2.2.1} prajāpatir akāmayata darśapūrṇamāsau sṛjeya-iti | sa etaṃ caturhotāram apaśyat | taṃ manasonudrutya-āhavanīye 'juhot | tato vai sa darśapūrṇamāsāv asṛjata | tāv asmāt sṛṣṭāv apākrāmatām { apākrāmatām} | tau graheṇa-agṛhṇāt | tad grahasya grahatvam | darśapūrṇamāsāv ālabhamānaḥ | caturhotāraṃ manasonudrutya-āhavanīye juhuyāt | darśapūrṇamāsāv eva sṛṣṭvā-ārabhya pratanute |

{ 2.2.2.2} graho bhavati | darśapūrṇamāsayoḥ sṛṣṭayor dhṛtyai | so 'kāmayata cāturmāsyāni sṛjeya-iti | sa etaṃ pañcahotāram apaśyat | taṃ manasonudrutya-āhavanīye 'juhot | tato vai sa cāturmāsyāny asṛjata | tāny asmāt sṛṣṭāny apākrāman | tāni graheṇa-agṛhṇāt | tad grahasya grahatvam | cāturmāsyāny ālabhamānaḥ |

{ 2.2.2.3} pañcahotāraṃ manasonudrutya-āhavanīye juhuyāt | cāturmāsyāny eva sṛṣṭvā-ārabhya pratanute | graho bhavati | cāturmāsyānāṃ sṛṣṭānāṃ dhṛtyai | so 'kāmayata paśubandhaṃ sṛjeya-iti | sa etaṃ ṣaḍḍhotāram apaśyat | taṃ mansā-anudrutya-āhavanīye 'juhot | tato vai sa paśubandham asṛjata | so 'smāt sṛṣṭo 'pākrāmat | taṃ graheṇa-agṛhṇāt |

{ 2.2.2.4} tad grahasya grahatvam | paśubandhena yakṣyamāṇaḥ | ṣaḍḍhotāraṃ manasonudrutya-āhavanīye juhuyāt | paśubandham eva sṛṣṭvā-ārabhya pratanute | graho bhavati | paśubandhasya sṛṣṭasya dhṛtyai | so 'kāmayata saumyam adhvaraṃ sṛjeya-iti | sa etaṃ saptahotāram apaśyat | taṃ manasonudrutya-āhavanīye 'juhot | tato vai sa saumyam adhvaram asṛjata |

{ 2.2.2.5} so 'smāt sṛṣṭo 'pākrāmat | taṃ graheṇa-agṛhṇāt | tad grahasya grahatvam | dīkṣiṣyamāṇaḥ | saptahotāraṃ manasonudrutya-āhavanīye juhuyāt | saumyam eva-adhvaraṃ sṛṣṭvā-ārabhya pratanute | graho bhavati | saumyasya-adhvarasya sṛṣṭasya dhṛtyai | devebhyo vai yajño na prābhavat | tam etāvat-śas samabharan |

{ 2.2.2.6} yat saṃbhārāḥ | tato vai tebhyo yajñaḥ prābhavat | yat saṃbhārā bhavani | yajñasya prabhūtyai | ātithyam āsādya vyācaṣṭe | yajñamukhaṃ vā ātithyaṃ | mukhata eva yajñaṃ saṃbhṛtya pratanute | ayajño vā eṣaḥ | yo 'patnīkaḥ | na prajāḥ { |} prajāyeran | patnīr vyācaṣṭe | yajñam eva-akaḥ | prajānāṃ prajananāya | upasatsu vyācaṣṭe | etad vai patnīnām āyatanam | sva evainā āyatane 'vakalpayati |

anuvāka 3 { 2.2.3.1} prajāpatir akāmayata prajāyeya-iti | sa tapo 'tapyata | sa trivṛtaṃ stomam asṛjata | taṃ pañcadaśastomo madhyata udatṛṇat | tau pūrvapakṣaś ca-aparapakṣaś ca-abhavatām | pūrvapakṣaṃ devā anvasṛjyanta | aparapakṣam anv asurāḥ | tato devā abhavan | parā-asurāḥ | yaṃ kāmayeta vasīyānt syād iti |

{ 2.2.3.2} taṃ pūrvapakṣe yājayet | vasīyān eva bhavati | yaṃ kāmayeta pāpīyānt syād iti | tam aparapakṣe yājayet | pāpīyān eva bhavati | tasmāt pūrvapakṣo 'parapakṣāt karuṇyataraḥ | prajāpatir vai daśahotā | caturhotā pañcahotā | ṣaḍḍhotā saptahotā | ṛtavaḥ saṃvatsaraḥ |

{ 2.2.3.3} prajāḥ paśava ime lokāḥ | ya evaṃ prajāpatiṃ bahor bhūyāṃsaṃ veda | bahor eva bhūyān bhavati | prajāpatir deva-asurān asṛjata | sa indram api na-asṛjata | taṃ devā abruvan | indraṃ no janaya-iti | so 'bravīt | yathā-ahaṃ yuṣmāṃs tapasosṛkṣi | evam indraṃ janayadhvam iti |

{ 2.2.3.4} te tapo 'tapyanta | ta ātmann indram apaśyan | tam abruvan | jāyasva-iti | so 'bravīt | kim bhāgadheyam abhijaniṣya iti | ṛtūnt saṃvatsaram | prajāḥ praśūn | imān-lokān ity abruvan | taṃ vai mā-āhutyā prajanayata-ity abravīt |

{ 2.2.3.5} taṃ caturhotrā prājanayan | yaḥ kāmayeta vīro ma ājāyeta-iti | sa caturhotāraṃ juhuyāt | prajāpatir vai caturhotā | prajāpatir eva bhūtvā prajāyate | jajanad indram indriyāya svāhā-iti graheṇa juhoti | āsya vīro jāyate | vīraṃ hi devā etayā-āhutyā prājanayan | ādityāś ca-aṅgirasaś ca suvarge loke 'spardhanta | vayaṃ pūrve suvargaṃ lokam iyāma vayaṃ pūrva iti |

{ 2.2.3.6} ta ādityā etaṃ pañcahotāram apaśyan | taṃ purā prātaranuvākād āgnīdhre 'juhavuḥ | tato vai te pūrve suvargaṃ lokam āyan | yaḥ suvargakāmaḥ syāt | sa pañcahotāraṃ purā prātaranuvākād āgnīdhre juhuyāt | saṃvatsaro vai pañcahotā | saṃvatsaraḥ suvargo lokaḥ | saṃvatsara eva-ṛtuṣu pratiṣṭhāya | suvargaṃ lokam eti | te 'bruvann aṅgirasa ādityān |

{ 2.2.3.7} kva stha | kva vaḥ sadbhyo havyaṃ vakṣyāma iti | chandaḥsv ity abruvan | gāyatriyāṃ triṣṭubhi jagatyām iti | tasmāc chandaḥsu sadbhya ādityebhyaḥ | āṅgīrasīḥ prajā havyaṃ vahanti | vahanty asmai prajā balim | ā-enam apratikhyātaṃ gacchati | ya evaṃ veda | dvādaśa māsāḥ pañca-ṛtavaḥ | traya ime lokāḥ | asāv āditya ekaviṃśaḥ | etasmin vā eṣa śritaḥ | etasmin pratiṣṭhitaḥ | ya evam etaṃ śritaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ veda | praty eva tiṣṭhati |

anuvāka 4 { 2.2.4.1} prajāpatir akāmayata prajāyeya-iti | sa etaṃ daśahotāram apaśyat | tena daśadhā-ātmānaṃ vidhāya | daśahotrā-atapyata | tasya cittiḥ srug āsīt | cittam ājyam | tasyaitā̀vaty eva vāg āsīt | etāvān yajñakratuḥ | sa caturhotāram asṛjata | so 'nandat |

{ 2.2.4.2} asṛkṣi vā imam iti | tasya somo havir āsit | sa caturhotrā-atapyata | so 'tāmyat | sa bhūr iti vyāharat | sa bhūmim asṛjata | agnihotraṃ darśāpūrṇamāsau yajūṃṣi | sa dvitīyam atapyata | so 'tāmyat | sa bhuva iti vyāharat |

{ 2.2.4.3} so 'ntarikṣam asṛjata | cāturmāsyāni sāmāni | sa tṛtīyam atapyata | so 'tāmyat | sa suvar iti vyāharat | sa divam asṛjata | agniṣṭomam ukthyam atirātram ṛcaḥ | etā vai vyāhṛtaya ime lokāḥ | imān khalu vai lokān anu prajāḥ paśavaś chandāṃsi prājāyanta | ya evam etāḥ prajāpateḥ prathamā vyāhṛtīḥ prajātā veda |

{ 2.2.4.4} pra prajayā paśubhir mithunair jāyate | sa pañcahotāram asṛjata | sa havir na-avindata | tasmai somas tanuvaṃ prāyacchat | etat te havir iti | sa pañcahotrā-atapyata | so 'tāmyat | sa pratyaṅṅ abādhata | so 'surān asṛjata | tad asya-apriyam āsīt |

{ 2.2.4.5} tad durvarṇaṃ hiraṇyam abhavat | tad durvarṇasya hiraṇyasya janma | sa dvitīyam atapyata | so 'tāmyat | sa prāṅ abādhata | sa devān asṛjata | tad asya priyam asīt | tat suvarṇaṃ hiraṇyam abhavat | tat suvarṇasya hiraṇyasya janma | 0 ya evaṃ suvarṇasya hiraṇyasya janma veda |

{ 2.2.4.6} suvarṇa ātmanā bhavati | durvarṇo 'sya bhrātṛvyaḥ | tasmāt suvarṇaṃ hiraṇyaṃ bhāryam | suvarṇa eva bhavati | ā-enaṃ priyaṃ gacchati na-apriyam | sa saptahotāram asṛjata | sa saptahotrā-eva suvargaṃ lokam ait | triṇavena stomena-ebhyo lokebhyo 'surān prāṇudata | trayastriṃśena pratyatiṣṭhat | ekaviṃśena rucam adhatta |

{ 2.2.4.7} saptadaśena prājāyata | ya evaṃ vidvānt somena yajate | saptahotrā-eva suvargaṃ lokam eti | triṇavena stomena-ebhyo lokebhyo bhrātṛvyān praṇudate | trayastriṃśena pratitiṣṭhati | ekaviṃśena rucaṃ dhatte | saptadaśena prajāyate | tasmāt saptadaśaḥ stomo na nirhŕ̥tyaḥ | prajāpatir vai saptadaśaḥ | prajāpatim eva madhyato dhatte prajātyai |

anuvāka 5 { 2.2.5.1} devā vai varuṇam ayājayan | sa yasyai-yasyai devatāyai dakṣiṇām anayat | tām avlīnāt | te 'bruvan | vyāvṛtya prati gṛhṇāma | tathā no dakṣiṇā na vleṣyati-iti | te vyāvṛtya pratyagṛhṇan | tato vai tān { tāṃ} dakṣiṇā na-avlīnāt | ya evaṃ vidvān vyāvṛtya dakṣiṇāṃ pratigṛhṇāti | na-enaṃ dakṣiṇā vlīnāti |

{ 2.2.5.2} rājā tvā varuṇo nayatu devi dakṣiṇè 'gnaye hiraṇyam ity āha | āgneyaṃ vai hiraṇyam | svayā-evainad devatayā pratigṛhṇāti | somāya vāsa ity āha | saumyaṃ vai vāsaḥ | svayā-evainad devatayā pratigṛhṇāti | rudrāya gām ity āha | raudrī vai gauḥ | svayā-evaināṃ devatayā pratigṛhṇāti | varuṇāya-aśvam ity āha |

{ 2.2.5.3} vāruṇo vā aśvaḥ | svayā-evainaṃ devatayā pratigṛhṇāti | prājāpataye puruṣam ity āha | prājāpatyo vai puruṣaḥ | svayā-evainaṃ devatayā pratigṛhṇāti | manave talpam ity āha | mānavo vai talpaḥ | svayā-evainaṃ devatayā pratigṛhṇāti | uttānāyā-āṅgīrasa-ayāna ity āha | iyaṃ vā uttāna āṅgīrasaḥ |

{ 2.2.5.4} anayā-evainat pratigṛhṇāti | vaiśvānaryā-ṛcā rathaṃ pratigṛhṇāti | vaiśvānaro vai devatayā rathaḥ | svayā-evainaṃ devatayā pratigṛhṇāti | tena-amṛtatvam aśyām ity āha | amṛtam evā''tman dhatte | vayo dātra ity āha | vaya evainaṃ kṛtvā | suvargaṃ lokaṃ gamayati | mayo mahyam astu pratigrahītra ity āha |

{ 2.2.5.5} yad vai śivam | tan mayaḥ | ātmana evaiṣā parīttiḥ | ka idaṃ kasmā adād ity āha | prajāpatir vai kaḥ | sa prajāpataye dadāti | kāmaḥ kāmāya-ity āha | kāmena hi dadāti | kāmena pratigṛhṇāti | kāmo dātā kāmaḥ pratigrahītety āha |

{ 2.2.5.6} kāmo hi dātā | kāmaḥ pratigrahītā | kāmaṃ samudramāviśa-ity āha | samudra iva hi kāmaḥ | na-iva hi kāmasya-anto 'sti | na samudrasya | kāmena tvā pratigṛhṇāmi-ity āha | yena kāmena pratigṛhṇāti | sa evainam amuṣmin-loke kāma āgacchati | kāmaitat ta eṣā te kāma dakṣiṇā-ity āha | kāma eva tad yajamāno 'muṣmin-loke dakṣiṇām icchti | na pratigrahītari | ya evaṃ vidvān dakṣiṇāṃ pratigṛhṇāti | anṛṇām evaināṃ pratigṛhṇāti |

anuvāka 6 { 2.2.6.1} anto vā eṣa yajñasya | yad daśamam ahaḥ | daśame 'han sarparājñiyā ṛgbhiḥ stuvanti | yajñasyaiva-antaṃ gatvā | annādyam avarundhate | tisṛbhiḥ stuvanti | traya ime lokāḥ | ebhya eva lokebhyo 'nnādyam avarundhate | pṛśnivatīr bhavanti | annaṃ vai pṛśni |

{ 2.2.6.2} annam eva-avarundhate | manasā prastauti | manasodgāyati | manasā pratiharati | mana iva hi prajāpatiḥ | prajāpater āptyai | devā vai sarpāḥ | teṣām iyaṃ rājñī | yat sarparājñiyā ṛgbhiḥ stuvanti | asyām eva pratitiṣṭhanti |

{ 2.2.6.3} caturhotṝn hotā vyācaṣṭe | stutam anuśaṃsati śāntyai | anto vā eṣa yajñasya | yad daśamam ahaḥ | etat khalu vai devānāṃ paramaṃ guhyaṃ brahma | yac caturhotāraḥ | daśame 'haṃś caturhotṝn vyācaṣṭe | yajñasyaiva-antaṃ gatvā | paramaṃ devānāṃ guhyaṃ brahmāvarundhe | tad eva prakāśaṃ gamayati |

{ 2.2.6.4} tad enaṃ prakāśaṃ gatam | prakāśaṃ prajānāṃ gamayati | vācaṃ yacchati | yajñasya dhṛtyai | yajamānadevatyaṃ vā ahaḥ | bhrātṛvyadevatyā rātriḥ | ahnā rātriṃ dhyāyet | bhrātṛvyasyaiva tal lokaṃ vṛṅkte | yad divā vācaṃ visṛjet | ahar bhrātṛvyāya-ucchiṃṣet | yan naktaṃ visṛjet | rātriṃ bhrātṛvyāya-ucchiṃṣet | adhivṛkṣasūrye vācaṃ visṛjati | etāvantam evāsmai lokam ucchiṃṣati | yāvad ādityo 'stameti |

anuvāka 7 { 2.2.7.1} prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata | tāḥ sṛṣṭāḥ samaśliṣyan | tā rūpeṇa-anuprāviśat | tasmād āhuḥ | rūpaṃ vai prajāpatir iti | tā nāmnā-anuprāviśat | tasmād āhuḥ | nāma vai prajāpatir iti | tasmād apy āmitrau saṃgatya | nāmnā ced dhvayete |

{ 2.2.7.2} mitram eva bhavataḥ | prajāpatir deva-asurān asṛjata | sa indram api na-asṛjata | taṃ devā abruvan | indraṃ no janaya-iti | sa ātmann indram apaśyat | tam asṛjata | taṃ triṣṭug vīryaṃ bhūtvā-anuprāviśat | tasya vajraḥ pañcadaśo hasta āpadyata | tena-udayya-asurān abhyabhavat |

{ 2.2.7.3} ya evaṃ veda | abhi bhrātṛvyān bhavati | te devā asurair vijitya | suvargaṃ lokam āyan | te 'muṣmin-loke vyakṣudhyan | te 'bruvan | amutaḥ pradānaṃ vā upajijīvima-iti | te saptahotāraṃ yajñaṃ vidhāya-ayāsyam | āṅgīrasaṃ prāhiṇvan | etena-amutra kalpaya-iti |

{ 2.2.7.4} tasya vā iyaṃ klṛptiḥ | yad idaṃ kiṃ ca | ya evaṃ veda | kalpate 'smai | sa vā ayaṃ manuṣyeṣu yajñaḥ saptahotā | amutra sadbhyo devebhyo havyaṃ vahati | ya evaṃ veda | upa-enaṃ yajño namati | so 'manyata | abhi vā ime 'smāl lokād amuṃ lokaṃ kamiṣyanta iti | sa vācaspate hṛd iti vyāharat | tasmāt puttró hṛdayam | tasmād asmāl lokād amuṃ lokaṃ na-abhikāmayante | putro hi hṛdayam |

anuvāka 8 { 2.2.8.1} devā vai caturhotṛbhir yajñam atanvata | te vi pāpmanā bhrātṛvyeṇa-ajayanta | abhi suvargaṃ lokam ajayan | ya evaṃ vidvāṃś caturhotṛbhir yajñaṃ tanute | vi pāpmanā bhrātṛvyeṇa jayate | abhi suvargaṃ lokaṃ jayati | ṣaḍḍhotrā prāyaṇīyam āsādayati | amuṣmai vai lokāya ṣaḍḍhotā | ghnanti khalu vā etat somam | yad abhiṣuṇvanti |

{ 2.2.8.2} ṛjudhā-evainam amuṃ lokaṃ gamayati | caturhotrā-ātithyam | yaśo vai caturhotā | yaśa evā''tman dhatte | pañcahotrā paśum upasādayati | suvargyo vai pañcahotā | yajamānaḥ paśuḥ | yajamānam eva suvargaṃ lokaṃ gamayati | grahān gṛhītvā saptahotāraṃ juhoti | indriyaṃ vai saptahotā |

{ 2.2.8.3} indriyam evā''tman dhatte | yo vai caturhotṝn anusavanaṃ tarpayati | tṛpyati prajayā paśubhiḥ | upa-enaṃ somapītho namati | bahiṣpavamāne daśahotāraṃ vyācakṣīta | mādhyandine pavamāne caturhotāram | ārbhave pavamāne pañcahotāram | pitṛyajñe ṣaḍḍhotāram | yajñāyajñiyasya stotre saptahotāram | anusavanam evaināṃs tarpayati |

{ 2.2.8.4} tṛpyati prajayā paśubhiḥ | upa-enaṃ somapītho namati | devā vai caturhotṛbhiḥ sattram āsata | ṛddhiparimitaṃ yaśaskāmāḥ | te 'bruvan | yan naḥ prathamaṃ yaśa ṛcchāt | sarveṣāṃ nas tat saha-asad iti | somaś caturhotrā | agniḥ pañcahotrā | dhātā ṣaḍḍhotrā |

{ 2.2.8.5} indraḥ saptahotrā | prajāpatir daśahotrā | teṣāṃ somaṃ rājānaṃ yaśa ārccat | tan nyakāmayata | tena-apākrāmat | tena pralāyam acarat | taṃ devāḥ praiṣaiḥ praiṣam aicchan | tat praiṣāṇāṃ praiṣatvam | nividbhir nyavedayan | tan nividāṃ nivittvam |

{ 2.2.8.6} āprībhir āpnuvan |tad āprīṇām āpritvam | tam aghnan | tasya yaśo vyagṛhṇata | te grahā abhavan | tad grahāṇāṃ grahatvam | yasyaivaṃ viduṣo grahā gṛhyante | tasya tv eva gṛhītāḥ | te 'bruvan | yo vai naḥ śreṣṭho 'bhūt |

{ 2.2.8.7} tam avadhiṣma | punar imaṃ suvāmahā iti | taṃ chandobhir asuvanta | tac chandasāṃ chandastvam | sāmnā samānayan | tat sāmnaḥ sāmatvam | ukthair udasthāpayan | tad ukthānām ukthatvam | ya evaṃ veda | praty eva tiṣṭhati |

{ 2.2.8.8} sarvam āyur eti | somo vai yaśaḥ | ya evaṃ vidvānt somam āgacchati | yaśa evainam ṛcchati | tasmād āhuḥ | yaś caivaṃ veda yaś ca na | tāv ubhau somam āgacchataḥ | somo hi yaśaḥ | taṃ tvā va yaśa ṛcchati-ity āhuḥ | 0 yaḥ some somaṃ prāha-iti | 1 tasmāt some somaḥ procyaḥ | 2 yaśa evainam ṛcchati |

anuvāka 9 { 2.2.9.1} idaṃ vā agre na-eva kiṃ cana-āsīt | na dyaur āsīt | na pṛthivī | na-antarikṣam | tad asad eva san mano 'kuruta syām iti | tad atapyata | tasmāt tepānād dhūmo 'jāyata | tad bhūyo 'tapyata | tasmāt tepānād agnir ajāyata | tad bhūyo 'tapyata |

{ 2.2.9.2} tasmāt tepānāj jyotir ajāyata | tad bhūyo 'tapyata | tasmāt tepānād arcir ajāyata | tad bhūyo 'tapyata | tasmāt tepānān marīcayo 'jāyanta | tad bhūyo 'tapyata | tasmāt tepānād udārā ajāyanta | tad bhūyo 'tapyata | tad abbʰram iva samahanyata | tad vastim abhinat |

{ 2.2.9.3} sa samudro 'bhavat | tasmāt samudrasya na pibanti | prajananam iva hi manyante | tasmāt paśor jāyamānād āpaḥ purast ādyanti | tad daśahotā-anvasṛjyata | prajāpatir vai daśahotā | ya evaṃ tapaso vīryaṃ vidvāṃs tapyate | bhavaty eva | tad vā idam āpas salilam āsīt | so 'rodīt prajāpatiḥ |

{ 2.2.9.4} sa kasmā ajñi | yady asyā apratiṣṭhāyā iti | yad apsv avāpadyata | sā pṛthivyabhavat | yad vyamṛṣṭa | tad antarikṣam abhavat | yad ūrdhvam udamṛṣṭa | sā dyaur abhavat | yad arodīt | tad anayo rodastvam |

{ 2.2.9.5} ya evaṃ veda | na-asya gṛhe rudanti | etad vā eṣāṃ lokānāṃ janma | ya evam eṣāṃ lokānāṃ janma veda | na-eṣu lokeṣv ārtim ārcchati | sa imāṃ pratiṣṭhām avindata | sa imāṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ vittvā-akāmayata prajāyeya-iti | sa tapo 'tapyata | so 'ntarvān abhavat | sa jaghanād asurān asṛjata |

{ 2.2.9.6} tebhyo mṛnmaye pātre 'nnam aduhat | yā-asya sā tanūr āsīt | tām apāhata | sā tamisrā-abhavat | so 'kāmayata prajāyeya-iti | sa tapo 'tapyata | so 'ntarvān abhavat | sa prajananād eva prajā asṛjata | tasmād imā bhūyiṣṭhāḥ | prajananād dhy enā asṛjata |

{ 2.2.9.7} tābhyo dārumaye pātre payo 'duhata | yā-asya sā tanūr āsīt | tām apāhata sā jotsnā-abhavat | so 'kāmayata prajāyeya-iti | sa tapo 'tapyata | so 'ntarvān abhavat | sa upapakṣābhyām eva-ṛtūn asṛjata | tebhyo rajate pātre ghṛtam aduhat | yā-asya sā tanūr āsīt |

{ 2.2.9.8} tam apāhata | so 'horātrayoḥ sandhir abhavat | so 'kāmayata prajāyeya-iti | sa tapo 'tapyata | so 'natarvān abhavat | sa mukhād devān asṛjata | tebhyo harite pātre somam aduhata | yā-asya sā tanūr āsīt | tām apāhata | tad ahar abhavat |

{ 2.2.9.9} ete vai prajāpater dohāḥ | ya evaṃ veda | duha eva prajāḥ | divā vai no 'bhūd iti | tad devānāṃ devatvam | ya evaṃ devānāṃ devatvaṃ veda | devavān eva bhvati | etad vā ahorātrāṇāṃ janma | ya evam ahorātrāṇāṃ janma veda | na-ahorātreṣv ārtim ārcchati |

{ 2.2.9.10} asato 'dhi mano 'sṛjyata | manaḥ prajāpatim asṛjata | prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata | tad vā idaṃ manasy eva paramaṃ pratiṣṭhitam | yad idaṃ kiṃ ca | tad etat-śvòvasyasaṃ nāma brahma | vyucchantī vyucchanty asmai vasyasī vasyasī vyucchati | prajāyate prajayā paśubhiḥ | pra parameṣṭhino mātrām āpnoti | ya evaṃ veda |

anuvāka 10 { 2.2.10.1} prajāpatir indram asṛjata-ānujāvaraṃ devānām | taṃ prāhiṇot | parehi | eteṣāṃ devānām adhipatir edhi-iti | taṃ devā abruvan | kas tvam asi | vayaṃ vai tvat-śreyāṃsaḥ sma iti | so 'bravīt | kas tvam asi vayaṃ vai tvat-śreyāṃsaḥ sma iti mā devā avocann iti | atha vā idaṃ tarhi prajāpatau hara āsīt |

{ 2.2.10.2} yad asminn āditye | tad enam abravīt | etan me prayaccha | atha-aham eteṣāṃ devānām adhipatir bhaviṣyāmi-iti | ko 'haṃ syām ity abravīt | etat pradāya-iti | etat syā ity abravīt | yad etad bravīṣi-iti | ko ha vai nāma prajāpatiḥ | ya evaṃ veda |

{ 2.2.10.3} vidur enaṃ nāmnā | tad asmai rukmaṃ kṛtvā pratyamuñcat | tato vā indro devānām adhipatir abhavat | ya evaṃ veda | adhipatir eva samānānāṃ bhavati | so 'manyata | kiṃ-kiṃ vā akaram iti | sa candraṃ ma āhara-iti { |} prālapat | tac candramasaś candramastvam | ya evaṃ veda |

{ 2.2.10.4} candravān eva bhavati | taṃ devā abruvan | suvīryo maryā yathā gopāyata iti | tat sūryasya sūryatvam | ya evaṃ veda | nainaṃ dabhnoti | kaś ca na-asmin vā idam indriyaṃ pratyasthād iti | tad indrasya-indratvam | ya evaṃ veda | indriyāvy eva bhavati |

{ 2.2.10.5} ayaṃ vā idaṃ paramo 'bhūd iti | tat parameṣṭhinaḥ parameṣṭhitvam | ya evaṃ veda | paramām eva kāṣṭhāṃ gacchati | taṃ devāḥ samantaṃ paryaviśan | vasavaḥ purastāt | rudrā dakṣiṇataḥ | ādityāḥ paścāt | viśve devā uttarataḥ | aṅgirasaḥ pratyañcam |

{ 2.2.10.6} sādhyāḥ parāñcam | ya evaṃ veda | upa-enaṃ samānāḥ saṃviśanti | sa prajāpatir eva bhūtvā prajā āvayat | tā asmai na-atiṣṭhanta-annādyāya | tā mukhaṃ purastāt paśyantīḥ | dakṣiṇataḥ paryāyan | sa dakṣiṇataḥ paryavartayata | tā mukhaṃ purastāt paśyantīḥ | mukhaṃ dakṣiṇataḥ |

{ 2.2.10.7} paścāt paryāyan | sa paścāt paryavartayata | tā mukhaṃ purastāt paśyantīḥ | mukhaṃ dakṣiṇataḥ | mukhaṃ paścāt | uttarataḥ paryāyan | sā uttarataḥ paryavartayata | tā mukhaṃ purastāt paśyantīḥ | mukhaṃ dakṣiṇataḥ | mukhaṃ paścāt | { 2.2.10.8} mukham uttarataḥ | ūrdhvā udāyan | sa upariṣṭān nyavartayata | tāḥ sarvatomukho bhūtvā-āvayat | tato vai tasmai prajā atiṣṭhanta-annādyāya | ya evaṃ vidvān pari ca vartayate ni ca | prajāpatir eva bhutvā prajā atti | tiṣṭhante 'smai prajā annādyāya | annāda eva bhavati |

anuvāka 11 { 2.2.11.1} prajāpatir akāmayata bahor bhūyānt syām iti | sa etaṃ daśahotāram apaśyat | taṃ prāyuṅkta | tasya prayukti bahor bhūyān abhavat | yaḥ kāmayeta bahor bhūyānt syām iti | sa daśahotāraṃ prayuñjīta | bahor eva bhūyān bhavati | so 'kāmayata vīro ma ājāyeta-iti | sa daśahotuś caturhotāraṃ niramimīta | taṃ prāyuṅkta |

{ 2.2.11.2} tasya prayukti-indro 'jāyata | yaḥ kāmayeta vīro ma ājāyeta-iti | sa caturhotāraṃ prayuñjīta | ā-asya vīro jāyate | so 'kāmayata paśumānt syām iti | sa caturhotuḥ pañcahotāraṃ niramimīta | taṃ prāyuṅkta | tasya prayukti paśumān abhavat | yaḥ kāmayeta paśumānt syām iti | sa pañcahotāraṃ prayuñjīta |

{ 2.2.11.3} paśumān eva bhavati | so 'kāmayata-ṛtavo me kalperann iti | sa pañcahotuḥ ṣaḍḍhotāraṃ niramimīta | taṃ prāyuṅkta | tasya prayukty ṛtavo 'smā akalpanta | yaḥ kāmayeta-ṛtavo me kalperann iti | sa ṣaḍḍhotāraṃ prayuñjīta | kalpante 'smā ṛtavaḥ | so 'kāmayata somapaḥ somayājī syām | ā me somapaḥ somayājī jāyeta-iti |

{ 2.2.11.4} sa ṣaḍḍhotuḥ saptahotāraṃ niramimīta | taṃ prāyuṅkta | tasya prayukti somapaḥ somayājy abhavat | ā-asya somapaḥ somayājy ajāyata | yaḥ kāmayeta somapaḥ somayājī syām | ā me somapaḥ somayājī jāyeta-iti | sa saptahotāraṃ prayuñjīta | somapa eva somayājī bhavati | ā-asya somapaḥ somayājī jāyate | sa vā eṣa paśuḥ pañcadhā pratitiṣṭhati |

{ 2.2.11.5} padbhir mukhena | te devāḥ paśūn vittvā | suvargaṃ lokam āyan | te 'muṣmin-loke vyakṣudhyan | te 'bruvan | amutaḥ pradānaṃ vā upajijīvima-iti | te saptahotāraṃ yajñaṃ vidhāya-ayāsyam | āṅgīrasaṃ prāhiṇvan | etena-amutra kalpaya-iti | tasya ā iyaṃ klṛptiḥ |

{ 2.2.11.6} yad idaṃ kiñ ca | ya evaṃ veda kalpate 'smai | sa vā ayaṃ manuṣyeṣu yajñaḥ saptahotā | amutra sadbhyo devebhyo havyaṃ vahati | ya evaṃ veda | upa-enaṃ yajño namati | yo vai caturhotṛṇāṃ nidānaṃ veda | nidānavān bhavati | agnihotraṃ vai daśahotur nidānam | darśapūrṇamāsau caturhotuḥ | cāturmāsyāni pañcahotuḥ | paśubandhaḥ ṣaḍḍhotuḥ | saumyo 'dhvaraḥ saptahotuḥ | etad vai caturhotṛṇāṃ nidānam | ya evaṃ veda | nidānavān bhavati |







prapāṭhaka: 3 anuvāka 1 { 2.3.1.1} brahmavādino vadanti | kiṃ caturhotṛṇāṃ caturhotṛtvam iti | yad evaiṣu caturdhā hotāraḥ | tena caturhotāraḥ | tasmāc caturhotāra ucyante | tac caturhotṛṇāṃ caturhotṛtvam | somo vai caturhotā | agniḥ pañcahotā | dhātā ṣaḍḍhotā | indraḥ saptahotā |

{ 2.3.1.2} prajāpatir daśahotā | ya evaṃ caturhotṛṇām ṛddhiṃ veda | ṛdhnoty eva | ya eṣām evaṃ bandhutāṃ veda | bandhumān bhavati | ya eṣām evaṃ klṛptiṃ veda | kalpate 'smai | ya eṣām evam āyatanaṃ veda | āyatanavān bhavati | 0 ya eṣām evaṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ veda |

{ 2.3.1.3} praty eva tiṣṭhati | brahmavādino vadanti | daśahotā caturhotā | pañcahotā ṣaḍḍhotā saptahotā | atha kasmāc caturhotāra ucyanta iti | indro vai caturhotā | indrāḥ khalu vai śreṣṭho devatānām upadeśanāt | ya evam indraṃ śreṣṭhaṃ devatānām upadeśanād veda | vasiṣṭhaḥ samānānāṃ bhavati | tasmāt-śreṣṭham āyantaṃ prathamena-eva-anubudhyante | ayam āgan | ayam avāsād iti | kīrtir asya pūrvā-āgacchati janatām āyataḥ | atho enaṃ prathamena-eva-anubudhyante | ayam āgan | ayam avāsād iti |

anuvāka 2 { 2.3.2.1} dakṣiṇāṃ pratigrahīṣyant saptadaśa kṛtvo 'pānyāt | ātmānam eva samindhe | tejase vīryāya | atho prajāpatir evaināṃ bhūtvā pratigṛhṇāti | ātmano 'nārtyai | yady enam ārtvijyād vṛtaṃ {ānaṃdā. vrataṃ} santaṃ nirháreran | āgnīdhre juhuyād daśahotāram | caturgṛhītena-ājyena | purastāt pratyaṅ tiṣṭhan | pratilomaṃ vigrāham |

{ 2.3.2.2} prāṇān evāsya-upadāsayati | yad enaṃ punar upaśikṣeyuḥ | āgnīdhra eva juhuyād daśahotāram | caturgṛhītena-ājyena | paścāt prāṅ āsīnaḥ | anulomam avigrāham | prāṇān evāsmai kalpayati | prāyaścittī vāgg-hotety ṛtumukha ṛtumukhe juhoti | ṛtūn evāsmai kalpayati | kalpante 'smā ṛtavaḥ |

{ 2.3.2.3} klṛptā asmā ṛtava āyanti | ṣaḍḍhotā vai bhūtvā prajāpatir idaṃ sarvam asṛjata | sa mano 'sṛjata | manaso 'dhi gāyatrīm asṛjata | tad gāyatrīṃ yaśa ārcchat | tām ālabhata | gāyatriyā adhi chandāṃsy asṛjata | chandobhyo 'dhi sāma | tat sāma yaśa ārcchat | tad ālabhata |

{ 2.3.2.4} sāmno 'dhi yajūṃṣy asṛjata | yajurbhyo 'dhi viṣṇum | tad viṣṇuṃ yaśa ārcchat | tam ālabhata | viṣṇor adhy oṣadhīr asṛjata | oṣadhībhyo 'dhi somam | tat somaṃ yaśa ārcchat | tam ālabhata | somād adhi paśūn asṛjata | paśubhyo 'dhi-indram |

{ 2.3.2.5} tad indraṃ yaśa ārcchat | tad enaṃ na-atiprācyavata | indra iva yaśasvī bhavati | ya evaṃ veda na-enaṃ yaśo 'tipracyavate | yad vā idaṃ kiṃ ca | tat sarvam uttāna evā''ṅgīrasaḥ pratyagṛhṇāt | tad enaṃ pratigṛhītaṃ na-ahinat | yat kiṃ ca pratigṛhṇīyāt | tat sarvam uttānas tv āṅgīrasaḥ pratigṛhṇātv ity eva pratigṛhṇīyāt | iyaṃ vā uttāna āṅgīrasaḥ | anayā-evainat pratigṛhṇāti | na-enaṃ hinasti | barhiṣā pratīyād gāṃ vā-aśvaṃ vā | etad vai paśūnāṃ priyaṃ dhāma | priyeṇaivainaṃ dhāmnā pratyeti |

anuvāka 3 { 2.3.3.1} yo vā avidvān nivartayate | viśīrṣā sapāpmā-amuṣmin-loke bhavati | atha yo vidvān nivartayate | saśīrṣā vipāpmā-amuṣmin-loke bhavati | devatā vai sapta puṣṭikāmā nyavartayanta | agniś ca pṛthivī ca | vāyuś ca-antarikṣaṃ ca | ādityaś ca dyauś ca candramāḥ | agnir nyavartayata | sa sāhasram apuṣyat |

{ 2.3.3.2} pṛthivī nyavartayata | sa-oṣadhībhir vanaspatibhir apuṣyat | vāyur nyavartayata | sa marīcībhir apuṣyat | antarikṣaṃ nyavaratayata | tad vayobhir apuṣyat | ādityo nyavartayata | sa raśmibhir apuṣyat | dyaur nyavartayata | sā nakṣatrair apuṣyat | candramā nyavartayata | so 'horātrair ardhamāsair māsair ṛtubhiḥ saṃvatsarena-apuṣyat | tān poṣān puṣyati | yāṃs te 'puṣyan | ya evaṃ vidān ni ca vartayate pari ca |

anuvāka 4 { 2.3.4.1} tasya vā agner hiraṇyaṃ pratijagrahuṣaḥ | ardham indriyasya-apākrāmat | tad etenaiva pratyagṛhṇāt | tena vai so 'rdham indriyasya-ātmann upādhatta | ardham indriyasya-ātmann upādhatte | ya evaṃ vidvān hiraṇyaṃ pratigṛhṇāti | atha yo 'vidvān pratigṛhṇāti | ardham asya-indriyasya-apakrāmati | tasya vai somasya vāsaḥ pratijagrahuṣaḥ | tṛtīyam indriyasya-apākrāmat |

{ 2.3.4.2} tad etenaiva pratyagṛhṇāt | tena vai sa tṛtīyam indriyasya-ātmann upādhatta | tṛtīyam indriyasya-ātmann upādhatte | ya evaṃ vidvān vāsaḥ pratigṛhṇāti | atha yo 'vidvān pratigṛhṇāti | tṛtīyam asya-indriyasya-apakrāmati | tasya vai rudrasya gāṃ pratijagrahuṣaḥ | caturtham indriyasya-apākrāmat | tām etenaiva pratyagṛhṇāt | tena vai sa caturtham indriyasya-ātmann upādhatta |

{ 2.3.4.3} caturtham indriyasya-ātmann upādhatte | ya evaṃ vidvān gāṃ pratigṛhṇāti | atha yo 'vidvān pratigṛhṇāti | caturtham asya-indriyasya-apakrāmati | tasya vai varuṇasya-aśvaṃ pratijagrahuṣaḥ | pañcamam indriyasya-apākrāmat | tam etenaiva pratyagṛhṇāt | tena vai sa pañcamam indriyasya-ātmann upādhatta | pañcamam indriyasya-ātmann upādhatte | ya evaṃ vidvān aśvaṃ pratigṛhṇāti |

{ 2.3.4.4} atha yo 'vidvān pratigṛhṇāti | pañcamam asya-indriyasya-apakrāmati | tasya vai prajāpateḥ puruṣaṃ pratijagrahuṣaḥ | ṣaṣṭham indriyasya-apākrāmat | tam etenaiva pratyagṛhṇāt | tena vai sa ṣaṣṭham indriyasya-ātmann upādhatta | ṣaṣṭham indriyasya-ātmann upādhatte | ya evaṃ vidvān puruṣaṃ pratigṛhṇāti | atha yo 'vidvān pratigṛhṇāti | ṣaṣṭham asya-indriyasya-apakrāmati |

{ 2.3.4.5} tasya vai manos talpaṃ pratijagrahuṣaḥ | saptamam indriyasya-apākrāmat | tam etenaiva pratyagṛhṇāt | tena vai sa saptamam indriyasya-ātmann upādhatta | saptamam indriyasya-ātmann upādhatte | ya evaṃ vidvāṃs talpaṃ pratigṛhṇāti | atha yo 'vidvān pratigṛhṇāti | saptamam asya-indriyasya-apakrāmati | tasya vā uttānasya-āṅgīrasasya-aprāṇat pratijagrahuṣaḥ | aṣṭamam indriyasya-apākrāmat |

{ 2.3.4.6} tad etenaiva pratyagṛhṇāt | tena vai so 'ṣṭamam indriyasya-ātmann upādhatta | aṣṭamam indriyasya-ātmann upādhatte | ya evaṃ vidvān aprāṇat pratigṛhṇāti | atha yo 'vidvān pratigṛhṇāti | aṣṭamam asya-indriyasya-apakrāmati | yad vā idaṃ kiṃ ca | tat sarvam uttāna evā''ṅgīrasaḥ pratyagṛhṇāt | tad enaṃ pratigṛhītaṃ na-ahinat | yat kiṃ ca pratigṛhṇīyāt | tat sarvam uttānas tv āṅgīrasaḥ pratigṛhṇātv ity eva pratigṛhṇīyāt | iyaṃ vā uttāna āṅgīrasaḥ | anayā-evainat pratigṛhṇāti | na-enaṃ hinasti |

anuvāka 5 { 2.3.5.1} brahmavādino vadanti | yad daśahotāraḥ sattram āsata | kena te gṛhapatinā-ārdhnuvan | kena prajā asṛjanta-iti | prajāpatinā vai te gṛhapatinā-ārdhnuvan | tena prajā asṛjanta | yac caturhotāraḥ sattram āsata | kena te gṛhapatinā-ārdhnuvan | kena-oṣadhīr asṛjanta-iti | somena vai te gṛhapatinā-ārdhnuvan |

{ 2.3.5.2} tena-oṣadhīr asṛjanta | yat pañcahotāraḥ sattram āsata | kena te gṛhapatinā-ārdhnuvan | kena-ebhyo lokebhyo 'surān prāṇudanta | kena-eṣāṃ paśūn avṛñjata-iti | agninā vai te gṛhapatinā-ārdhnuvan | tena-ebhyo lokebhyo 'surān prāṇudanta | tena-eṣāṃ paśūn avṛñjata | yat ṣaḍḍhotāraḥ sattram āsata | kena te gṛhapatinā-ārdhnuvan |

{ 2.3.5.3} kena-ṛtūn akalpayanta-iti | dhātrā vai te gṛhapatinā-ārdhnuvan | tena-ṛtūn akalpayanta | yat saptahotāraḥ sattram āsata | kena te gṛhapatinā-ārdhnuvan | kena suvar āyan | kena-imān-lokānt samatanvann iti | aryamṇā vai te gṛhapatinā-ārdhnuvan | tena suvar āyan | tena-imān-lokān samatanvann iti |

{ 2.3.5.4} ete vai devā gṛhapatayaḥ | tān ya evaṃ vidvān | apy anyasya gārhapate dīkṣate | avāntaram eva sattriṇām ṛdhnoti | yo vā aryamaṇaṃ veda | dānakāmā asmai prajā bhavanti | yajño vā aryamā | āryā vasatir iti vai tam āhur yaṃ praśaṃsanti | āryā vasatir bhavati | ya evaṃ veda |

{ 2.3.5.5} yad vā idaṃ kiṃ ca | tat sarvaṃ caturhotāraḥ | caturhotṛbhyo 'dhi yajño nirmitaḥ | sa ya evaṃ vidvān vivadeta | aham eva bhūyo veda | yaś caturhotṝn veda-iti | sa hy eva bhūyo veda | yaś caturhotṝn veda | yo vai caturhotṛṇāṃ hotṝn veda | sarvāsu prajāsv annam atti |

{ 2.3.5.6} sarvā diśo 'bhijayati | prajāpatir vai daśahotṛṇāṃ hotā | somaś caturhotṛṇāṃ hotā | agniḥ pañcahotṛṇāṃ hotā | dhātā ṣaḍḍhotṛṇāṃ hotā | aryamā saptahotṛṇāṃ hotā | ete vai caturhotṛṇāṃ hotāraḥ | tān ya evaṃ veda | sarvāsu prajāsv annam atti | sarvā diśo 'bhijayati |

anuvāka 6 { 2.3.6.1} prajāpatiḥ prajāḥ sṛṣṭvā vyasraṃsata | sa hṛdayaṃbhūto 'śayat | ātman hā3 ity ahvayat | āpaḥ pratyaśṛṇvan | tā agnihotreṇaiva yajñakratunā-upaparyāvartanta | tāḥ kusindham upauhan | tasmād agnihotrasya yajñakratoḥ | eka ṛtvik | catuṣkṛtvo 'hvayat | agnir vāyur ādityaś candramāḥ |

{ 2.3.6.2} te pratyaśṛṇvan | te darśapūrṇamāsābhyām eva yajñakratunā-upaparyāvartanta | ta upauhaṃś catvāry aṅgāni | tasmād darśapūrṇamāsayor yajñakratoḥ | catvāra ṛtvijaḥ | pañcakṛtvo 'hvayat | paśavaḥ pratyaśṛṇvan | te cāturmāsyair eva yajñakratunā-upaparyāvartanta | ta upauhan-loma cchavīṃ māṃsam asthi majjānam | tasmāc cāturmāsyānāṃ yajñakratoḥ |

{ 2.3.6.3} pañca-ṛtvijaḥ | ṣaṭkṛtvo 'hvayat | ṛtavaḥ pratyaśṛṇvan | te paśubandhena-eva yajñakratunā-upaparyāvartanta | ta upauhaṃ stanāv āṇḍau śiśnam avāñcaṃ prāṇam | tasmāt paśubandhasya yajñakratoḥ | ṣaḍ ṛtvijaḥ | saptakṛtvo 'hvayat | hotrāḥ pratyaśṛṇvan | tāḥ saumyena-eva-adhvareṇa yajñakratunā-upaparyāvartanta |

{ 2.3.6.4} tā upauhant sapta śīrṣaṇyān prāṇān | tasmāt saumyasya-adhvarasya yajñakratoḥ | sapta hotrāḥ prācīr vaṣaṭkurvanti | daśakṛtvo 'hvayat | tapaḥ pratyaśṛṇot | tat karmaṇā-eva saṃvatsareṇa sarvair yajñakratubhir upaparyāvartata | tat sarvam ātmānam aparivargam upauhat | tasmāt saṃvatsare sarve yajñakratavo 'varudhyante | tasmād daśahotā caturhotā | pañcahotā ṣaḍḍhotā saptahotā | ekahotre baliṃ haranti haranty asmai prajā balim | ā-enam apratikhyātaṃ gacchati | ya evaṃ veda |

anuvāka 7 { 2.3.7.1} prajāpatiḥ puruṣam asṛjata | so 'gnir abravīt | mama-ayam annam astv iti | so 'bibhet | sarvaṃ vai mā-ayaṃ pradhakṣyati-iti | sa etāṃś caturhotṝn ātmasparaṇān apaśyat | tān ajuhot | tair vai sa ātmānam aspṛṇot | yad agnihotraṃ juhoti | ekahotāram eva tad yajñakratum āpnoty agnihotram |

{ 2.3.7.2} kusindhaṃ ca-ātmanaḥ spṛṇoti | ādityasya ca sāyujyaṃ gacchati | catur unnayati | caturhotāram eva tad yajñakratum āpnoti {| ānaṃdā. meṃ nahīṃ hai } darśapūrṇamāsau | catvāri ca-ātmano 'ṅgāni spṛṇoti | ādityasya ca sāyujyaṃ gacchati | catur unnayati | samit pañcamī | pañcahotāram eva tad yajñakratum āpnoti | cāturmāsyāni | loma cchavīṃ māṃsam asthi majjānam |

{ 2.3.7.3} tāni ca-ātmanaḥ spṛṇoti | ādityasya ca sāyujyaṃ gacchati | catur unnayati | dvir juhoti | ṣaḍḍhotāram eva tad yajñakratum āpnoti {| not in ānaṃdā.} paśubandham | stanāv āṇḍau śiśnam avāñcaṃ prāṇam | tāni ca-ātmanaḥ spṛṇoti | ādityasya ca sāyujyaṃ gacchati | catur unnayati | dvir juhoti |

{ 2.3.7.4} samit saptamī | saptahotāram eva tad yajñakratum āpnoti saumyam adhvaram | sapta ca-ātmanaḥ śīrṣaṇyān prāṇān spṛṇoti | ādityasya ca sāyujyaṃ gacchati | catur unnayati | dvir juhoti | dvir nimārṣṭi | dviḥ prāśnāti | daśahotāram eva tad yajñakratum āpnoti saṃvatsaram | sarvaṃ ca-ātmānam aparivargaṃ spṛṇoti | ādityasya ca sāyujyaṃ gacchati |

anuvāka 8 { 2.3.8.1} prajāpatir akāmayata prajāyeya-iti | sa tapo 'tapyata | so 'ntarvān abhavat | sa haritaḥ śyāvo 'bhavat | tasmāt stry antarvatnī | hariṇī satī śyāvā bhavati | sa vijāyamāno garbhaiṇa-atāmyat | sa tāntaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ śyāvo 'bhavat | tasmāt tāntaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ śyāvo bhavati | tasya-asur eva-ajīvat |

{ 2.3.8.2} tena-asunā-asurān asṛjata | tad asurāṇām asuratvam | ya evam asurāṇām asuratvaṃ veda | asumān eva bhavati | na-enam asur jahāti | so 'surān sṛṣṭvā piteva-amanyata | tad anu pitṝn asṛjata | tat pitṛṇāṃ pitṛtvam | ya evaṃ pitṛṇāṃ pitṛtvaṃ veda | piteva-eva {ānaṃdā. pitavaiva} svānāṃ bhavati |

{ 2.3.8.3} yanty asya pitaro havam | sa pitṝnt sṛṣṭvā-amanasyat | tad anu manuṣyān asṛjata | tan manuṣyāṇāṃ manuṣyatvam | ya evaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ manuṣyatvaṃ veda | manasvy eva bhavati | na-enaṃ manur jahāti | tasmai manuṣyānt sasṛjānāya | divā devatrā-abhavat | tad anu devān asṛjata | tad devānāṃ devatvam | ya evaṃ devānāṃ devatvaṃ veda | divā haivāsya devatrā bhavati | tāni vā etāni catvāry ambhāṃsi | devā manuṣyāḥ pitaro 'surāḥ | teṣu sarveṣv ambho nabha iva bhavati | ya evaṃ veda |

anuvāka 9 { 2.3.9.1} brahmavādino vadanti | yo vā imaṃ vidyāt | yato 'yaṃ pavate | yad abhipavate | yad abhisaṃpavate | sarvam āyur iyāt | na purā-āyuṣaḥ pramīyeta | paśumānt syāt | vindeta prajām | yo vā imaṃ veda |

{ 2.3.9.2} yato 'yaṃ pavate | yad abhipavate | yad abhisaṃpavate | sarvam āyur eti | na purā-āyuṣaḥ pramīyate | paśumān bhavati | vindate prajām | adbhyaḥ pavate | apo 'bhipavate | apo 'bhisaṃpavate |

{ 2.3.9.3} asyāḥ pavate | imām abhipavate | imām abhisaṃpavate | agneḥ pavate | agnim abhipavate | agnim abhisaṃpavate | antarikṣāt pavate | antarikṣam abhipavate | antarikṣam abhisaṃpavate | ādityāt pavate |

{ 2.3.9.4} ādityam abhipavate | ādityam abhisaṃpavate | dyoḥ pavate | divam abhipavate | divam abhisaṃpavate | digbhyaḥ pavate | diśo 'bhipavate | diśo 'bhisaṃpavate | sa yat purastād vāti | prāṇa eva bhūtvā purastād vāti |

{ 2.3.9.5} tasmāt purastād vāntam | sarvāḥ prajāḥ pratinandanti | prāṇo hi priyaḥ prajānām | prāṇa iva priyaḥ prajānāṃ bhavati | ya evaṃ veda | sa vā eṣa prāṇa eva | atha yad dakṣiṇato vāti | mātariśvā-eva bhūtvā dakṣiṇato vāti | tasmād dakṣiṇato vāntaṃ vidyāt | sarvā diśa āvāti |

{ 2.3.9.6} sarvā diśo 'nuvivāti | sarvā diśo 'nusaṃvāti-iti | sa vā eṣa mātariśvā-eva | atha yat paścād vāti | pavamāna eva bhūtvā paścād vāti | pūtam asmā āharanti | pūtam upaharanti | pūtam aśnāti | ya evaṃ veda | sa vā eṣa pavamāna eva |

{ 2.3.9.7} atha yad uttarato vāti | savitā-eva bhūtvā-uttarato vāti | saviteva svānāṃ bhavati | ya evaṃ veda | sa vā eṣa savitā-eva | te ya enaṃ purastād āyantam upavadanti | ya evāsya purastāt pāpmānaḥ | tāṃs te 'paghnanti | purastād itarān pāpmanaḥ sacante | atha ya enaṃ dakṣiṇata āyantam upavadanti |

{ 2.3.9.8} ya evāsya dakṣiṇataḥ pāpmānaḥ | tāṃs te 'paghnanti | dakṣiṇata itarān pāpmanaḥ sacante | atha ya enaṃ paścād āyantam upavadanti | ya evāsya paścāt pāpmānaḥ | tāṃs te 'paghnanti | paścād itarān pāpmanaḥ sacante | atha ya enam uttarata āyantam upavadanti | ya evāsya-uttarataḥ pāpmānaḥ | tāṃs te 'paghnanti |

{ 2.3.9.9} uttarata itarān pāpmanaḥ sacante | tasmād evaṃ vidvān | vi-iva nṛtyet | pra-iva calet | vy asya-iva-akṣyau bhāṣeta { bhāṣete} | maṇṭayed iva | krāthayed iva | śṛṅgāyeta-iva | uta mā-upavadeyuḥ | uta me pāpmānam apahanyur iti | sa yāṃ diśaṃ sanim eṣyant syāt | yadā tāṃ diśaṃ vāto vāyāt | atha praveyāt | pra vā dhāvayet | sātam eva raditaṃ vyūḍhaṃ gandham abhi pracyavate | ā-asya taṃ janapadaṃ pūrvā kīrtir gacchati | dānakāmā asmai prajā bhavanti | ya evaṃ veda |

anuvāka 10 { 2.3.10.1} prajāpatiḥ somaṃ rājānam asṛjata | taṃ trayo vedā anvasṛjyanta | tān haste 'kuruta | atha ha sītā sāvitrī | somaṃ rājānaṃ cakame | śraddhām u sa cakame | sā ha pitaraṃ prajāpatim upasasāra | taṃ ha-uvāca | namas te astu bhagavaḥ | upa tvā-ayāni |

{ 2.3.10.2} pra tvā padye | somaṃ vai rājānaṃ kāmaye | śraddhām u sa kāmayata iti | tasyā u ha sthāgaram alaṅkāraṃ kalpayitvā | daśahotāraṃ purastād vyākhyāya | caturhotāraṃ dakṣiṇataḥ | pañcahotāraṃ paścāt | ṣaḍḍhotāram uttarataḥ | saptahotāram upariṣṭāt | sambhāraiś ca patnibhiś ca mukhe 'laṅkṛtya |

{ 2.3.10.3} ā-asya-ardhaṃ vavrāja | tāṃ ha-udīkṣya-uvāca | upa mā vartasva-iti | taṃ ha-uvāca | bhogaṃ tu ma ācakṣva | etan ma ācakṣva | yat te pāṇāv iti | tasyā u ha trīn vedān pradadau | tasmād u ha striyo bhogam ā-eva hārayante | sa yaḥ kāmayeta priyaḥ syām iti |

{ 2.3.10.4} yaṃ vā kāmayeta priyaḥ syād iti | tasmā etaṃ sthāgaram alaṅkāraṃ kalpayitvā | daśahotāraṃ purastād vyākhyāya | caturhotāraṃ dakṣiṇataḥ | pañcahotāraṃ paścāt | ṣaḍḍhotāram uttarataḥ | saptahotāram upariṣṭāt | sambhāraiś ca patnibhiś ca mukhe 'laṅkṛtya | ā-asya-ardhaṃ vrajet | priyo haiva bhavati |

anuvāka 11 { 2.3.11.1} brahmā-ātmanvad asṛjata | tad akāmayata | sam ātmanā padyeya-iti | ātmann ātmann ity āmantrayata | tasmai daśamaṃ hūtaḥ pratyaśṛṇot | sa daśahūto 'bhavat | daśahūto ha vai nāmaiṣaḥ | taṃ vā etaṃ daśahūtaṃ santam | daśahotety ācakṣate {|} parokṣeṇa | parokṣapriyā iva hi devāḥ |

{ 2.3.11.2} ātmann ātmann ity āmantrayata | tasmai saptamaṃ hūtaḥ pratyaśṛṇot | sa saptahūto 'bhavat | saptahūto ha vai nāmaiṣaḥ | taṃ vā etaṃ saptahūtaṃ santam | saptahotety ācakṣate parokṣeṇa | parokṣapriyā iva hi devāḥ | ātmann ātmann ity āmantrayata | tasmai ṣaṣṭhaṃ hūtaḥ pratyaśṛṇot | sa ṣaḍḍhūto 'bhavat |

{ 2.3.11.3} ṣaḍdhūto ha vai nāmaiṣaḥ | taṃ vā etaṃ ṣaḍḍhūtaṃ santam | ṣaḍḍhotety ācakṣate parokṣeṇa | parokṣapriyā iva hi devāḥ | ātmann ātmann ity āmantrayata | tasmai pañcamaṃ hūtaḥ pratyaśṛṇot | sa pañcahūto 'bhavat | pañcahūto ha vai nāmaiṣaḥ | taṃ vā etaṃ pañcahūtaṃ santaṃ | pañcahotety ācakṣate parokṣeṇa |

{ 2.3.11.4} parokṣapriyā iva hi devāḥ | ātmann ātmann ity āmantrayata | tasmai caturthaṃ hūtaḥ pratyaśṛṇot | sa caturhūto 'bhavat | caturhūto ha vai nāmaiṣaḥ | taṃ vā etaṃ caturhūtaṃ santam | caturhotety ācakṣate {|} parokṣeṇa | parokṣapriyā iva hi devāḥ | tam abravīt | tvaṃ vai me nediṣṭhaṃ hūtaḥ pratyaśrauṣīḥ | tvayā-enān ākhyātāra iti | tasmān nu haināṃś caturhotāra ity ācakṣate | tasmāc śuśrūṣuḥ putrāṇāṃ hṛdyatamaḥ | nediṣṭho hṛdyatamaḥ | nediṣṭho brahmaṇo bhavati | ya evaṃ veda |







prapāṭhaka: 4 anuvāka 1 { 2.4.1.1} juṣṭo damūnā atithir duroṇe | imaṃ no yajñam upayāhi vidvān | viśvā agne 'bhiyujo vihatya | śatrūyatām ābharā bhojanāni | agne śardha mahate saubhagāya | tava dyumnāny uttamāni santu | saṃ jāspatyaṃ suyamam ākṛṇuṣva | śatrūyatām abhitiṣṭhā mahāṃsi | agne yo no 'bhito janaḥ | vṛko vāro jighāṃsati |

{ 2.4.1.2} tāṃs tvaṃ vṛtrahañ jahi | vasv asmabhyam ābhara | agne yo no 'bhidāsati | samāno yaś ca niṣṭyaḥ | idhmasya-iva prakṣāyataḥ | mā tasya-uccheṣi kiñ cana | tvam indra-abhibhūr asi | devo vijñātavīryaḥ | vṛtrahā purucetanaḥ | apa prāca indra viśvāṃ amitrān |

{ 2.4.1.3} apa-apāco abhibhūte nudasva | apa-udīco apa śūra-dharāca ūru | yathā tava śarman madema | tam indraṃ vājayāmasi | mahe vṛtrāya hantave | sa vṛṣā vṛṣabho bhuvat['bhuvat?] | yuje rathaṃ gaveṣaṇaṃ haribhyām | upa brahmāṇi jujuṣāṇam asthuḥ | vibādhiṣṭa-asya rodasī mahitvā | indro vṛtrāṇy apratījaghanvān |

{ 2.4.1.4} havyavāham abhimātiṣā-aham | rakṣohaṇaṃ pṛtanāsu jiṣṇum | jyotiṣmantaṃ dīdyataṃ purandhim | agniṃ sviṣṭakṛtam {F āhuvema} {ānaṃdā. āhumeva} {BI āhuvema} { āhuvema} | sviṣṭam agne abhi tat pṛṇāhi | viśvā deva pṛtanā abhiṣya | uruṃ naḥ panthāṃ pradiśan vibhāhi | jyotiṣmad dhehy ajaraṃ na {F āyuḥ} {ānaṃdā. āyuḥ} {BI āyaḥ} { āyuḥ} | tvām agne haviṣmantaḥ | devaṃ martāsa īḍate |

{ 2.4.1.5} manye tvā jātavedasam | sa havyā vakṣyā-anuṣak | viśvāni no durgahā jātavedaḥ | sindhuṃ na nāvā duritā-atiparṣi | agne atrivan manasā gṛṇānaḥ | asmākaṃ bodhyavitā tanūnām | pūṣā gā anvetu naḥ | pūṣā rakṣatv arvataḥ | pūṣā vājaṃ sanotu naḥ | pūṣā-imā āśā anuveda sarvāḥ |

{ 2.4.1.6} so asmāṃ abhayatamena neṣat | svastidā aghṛṇiḥ sarvavīraḥ | aprayucchan pura etu prajānan | {F tvam} {ānaṃdā. tvam} {BI svam} { tvam} agne saprathā asi | juṣṭo hotā vareṇyaḥ | tvayā yajñaṃ vitanvate | agnī rakṣāṃsi sedhati | śukraśocir amartyaḥ | śuciḥ pāvaka īḍyaḥ | agne rakṣāṇo aṃhasaḥ |

{ 2.4.1.7} pratiṣma deva rīṣataḥ | tapiṣṭhair ajaro daha | agne haṃsi nyatriṇam | dīdyan martyeṣv ā | sve kṣaye śucivrata | ā vāta vāhi bheṣajam | vi vāta vāhi yad rapaḥ | tvaṃ hi viśvabheṣajaḥ | devānāṃ dūta īyase | dvāv imau vātau vātaḥ |

{ 2.4.1.8} ā sindhor ā parāvataḥ | dakṣaṃ me anya āvātu | parā-anyo vātu yad rapaḥ | yad ado vāta te gṛhe | amṛtasya nidhir hitaḥ | tato no dehi jīvase | tato no dhehi bheṣajam | tato no maha āvaha | vāta āvātu bheṣajam | śambhūr mayobhūr no hṛde |

{ 2.4.1.9} pra ṇa āyūṃṣi tāriṣat | tvam agne ayāsi | ayā sanmanasā hitaḥ | ayā san havyamūhiṣe | ayā no dhehi bheṣajam | iṣṭo agnir āhutaḥ | svāhākṛtaḥ pipartu naḥ | svagā devebhya idaṃ namaḥ | kāmo bhūtasya bhavyasya | samrāḍ eko virājati |

{ 2.4.1.10} sa idaṃ pratipaprathe | ṛtūn utsṛjate vaśī | kāmas tad agre samavartata-adhi | manaso retaḥ prathamaṃ yad āsīt | sato bandhum asati niravindan | hṛdi pratīṣyā kavayo manīṣā | tvayā manyo saratham ārujantaḥ | harṣamāṇāso dhṛṣatā marutvaḥ | tigma-iṣava āyudhā saṃśiśānāḥ | upaprayanti naro agnirūpāḥ |

{ 2.4.1.11} manyur bhago manyur evā''sa devaḥ | manyur hotā varuṇo viśvavedāḥ | manyuṃ viśa īḍate devayantīḥ | pāhi no manyo tapasā śrameṇa | tvam agne vratabhṛt-śuciḥ | devāṃ āsādayā iha | agne havyāya voḍhave | vratā nu bibhrad vratapā adābhyaḥ | yajāno devāṃ ajaraḥ suvīraḥ | dadhad ratnāni suvidāno agne | gopāya no jīvase jātavedaḥ |

anuvāka 2 { 2.4.2.1} cakṣuṣo hete manaso hete | vāco hete brahmaṇo hete | yo mā-aghāyur abhidāsati | tam agne menyā-ameniṃ kṛṇu | yo mā cakṣuṣā yo manasā | yo vācā brahmaṇā-aghāyur abhidāsati | tayā-agne tvaṃ menyā | amum ameniṃ kṛṇu | yat kiṃ ca-asau manasā yac ca vācā | yajñair juhoti yajuṣā havirbhiḥ |

{ 2.4.2.2} tan mṛtyur nirṛtyā saṃvidānaḥ | purā diṣṭād āhutīr asya hantu | yātudhānā nirṛtir ād u rakṣaḥ | te asya ghnantv anṛtena satyam | indra-iṣitā ājyam asya mathnantu | mā tat samṛddhi yad asau karoti | hanmi te 'haṃ kṛtaṃ haviḥ | yo me ghoram acīkṛtaḥ | apāñcau ta ubhau bāhū | apanahyāmy āsyam |

{ 2.4.2.3} apanahyāmi te bāhū | apanahyāmy āsyam | agner devasya brahmaṇā | sarvaṃ te 'vadhiṣaṃ kṛtaṃ | purā-amuṣya vaṣaṭkārāt | yajñaṃ deveṣu nas kṛdhi | sviṣṭam asmākaṃ bhūyāt | mā-asmān prāpann arātayaḥ | anti dūre sato agne | bhrātṛvyasyā-abhidāsataḥ |

{ 2.4.2.4} vaṣaṭkāreṇa vajreṇa | kṛtyāṃ hanmi kṛtām aham | yo mā naktaṃ divā sāyam | prātaś ca-ahno nipīyati | adyā tam indra vajreṇa | bhrātṛvyaṃ pādayāmasi | indrasya gṛho 'si taṃ tvā | prapadye saguḥ sa-aśvaḥ | saha yan me asti tena | īḍe agniṃ vipaścitam |

{ 2.4.2.5} girā yajñasya sādhanam | śruṣṭīvānaṃ dhitāvānam | agne śakema te vayam | yamaṃ devasya vājinaḥ | ati dveṣāṃṣi tarema | avataṃ mā samanasau samokasau | sacetasau saretasau | ubhau mām avataṃ jātavedasau | śivau bhavatam adya naḥ | svayaṃ kṛṇvānaḥ sugam aprayāvam |

{ 2.4.2.6} tigmaśṛṅgo vṛṣabhaḥ śośucānaḥ | pratnaṃ sadhastham anupaśyamānaḥ | ā tantum agnir divyaṃ tatāna | tvaṃ nas tantur uta setur agne | tvaṃ panthā bhavasi devayānaḥ | tvayā-agne pṛṣṭhaṃ vayam āruhema { āruheya} {ānaṃdā.,BI āruhema} | athā devaiḥ sadhamādaṃ madema | ud uttamaṃ mumugdhi naḥ | vi pāśaṃ madhyamaṃ cṛta | ava-adhamāni jīvase |

{ 2.4.2.7} vayaṃ soma vrate tava | manas tanūṣu bibhrataḥ | prajāvanto aśīmahi | indrāṇī devī subhagā supatnī | ud aṃśena patividye jigāya | triṃśad asyā jaghanaṃ yojanāni | upastha indraṃ sthaviraṃ bibharti | senā ha nāma pṛthivī dhanaṃjayā | viśvavyacā aditiḥ sūryatvak | indrāṇī devī prāsahā dadānā |

{ 2.4.2.8} sā no devī suhavā śarma yacchatu | ā tvā-ahārṣam antarabhūḥ | dhruvas tiṣṭha-avicācaliḥ | viśas tvā sarvā vāñchantu | mā tvad rāṣṭram adhibhraśat | dhruvā dyaur dhruvā pṛthivī | dhruvaṃ viśvam idaṃ jagat | dhruvā ha parvatā ime | dhruvo rājā viśām ayam | ihaiva-edhi mā vyathiṣṭhāḥ |

{ 2.4.2.9} parvata iva-avicācaliḥ | indra iva-iha dhruvas tiṣṭha | iha rāṣṭram u dhāraya | abhitiṣṭha pṛtanyataḥ | adhare santu śatravaḥ | indra iva vṛtrahā tiṣṭha | apaḥ kṣetrāṇi saṃjayan | indra eṇam adīdharat | dhruvaṃ dhruveṇa haviṣā | tasmai devā adhibravan | ayaṃ ca brahmaṇaspatiḥ |

anuvāka 3 { 2.4.3.1} juṣṭī naro brahmaṇā vaḥ pitṛṇām | akṣam avyayaṃ na kilā riṣātha | yat-śakvarīṣu bṛhatā raveṇa | indre śuṣmam adadhāthā vasiṣṭhāḥ | pāvakā naḥ sarasvatī | vājebhir vājinīvatī | yajñaṃ vaṣṭu dhiyā vasuḥ | sarasvaty abhi no neṣi vasyaḥ mā paspharīḥ payasā mā na ādhak | juṣasva naḥ sakhyā veśyā { veṣyā} ca |

{ 2.4.3.2} mā tvat kṣetrāṇy araṇāni ganma | vṛñje havir namasā barhir agnau | ayāmi sruc- ghṛtavatī suvṛktiḥ | amyakṣi sadma sadane pṛthivyāḥ | aśrāyi yajñaḥ sūrye na cakṣuḥ | iha-arvāñcam atihvaye | indraṃ jaitrāya jetave { jetava} | asmākam astu kevalaḥ | arvāñcam indram amuto havāmahe | yo gojid dhanajid aśvajid yaḥ |

{ 2.4.3.3} imaṃ no yajñaṃ vihave juṣasva | asya kurmo harivo medinaṃ tvā | asaṃmṛṣṭo jāyase mātṛvoḥ śuciḥ | mandraḥ kavir udatiṣṭho vivasvataḥ | ghṛtena tvā vardhayann agna āhuta | dhūmas te ketur abhavad divi śritaḥ | agnir agre prathamo devatānām | saṃyātānām uttamo viṣṇur āsīt | yajamānāya parigṛhya devān | dīkṣayā-idaṃ havir āgacchataṃ naḥ |

{ 2.4.3.4} agniś ca viṣṇo tapa uttamaṃ mahaḥ | dīkṣāpālebhyo vanataṃ hi śakrā | viśvair devair yajñiyaiḥ saṃvidānau | dīkṣām asmai yajamānāya dhattam | pra tad viṣṇuḥ stavate vīryāya | mṛgo na bhīmaḥ kucaro giriṣṭhāḥ | yasya-ūruṣu triṣu vikramaṇeṣu | adhikṣiyanti bhuvanāni viśvā | nū marto dayate saniṣyan yaḥ | viṣṇava urugāyāya dāśat |

{ 2.4.3.5} pra yaḥ satrācā manasā yajātai | etāvantaṃ naryam āvivāsāt | vicakrame pṛthivīm eṣa etām | kṣetrāya viṣṇur manuṣe daśasyan | dhruvāso asya kīrayo janāsaḥ | urukṣitiṃ sujanimā cakāra | trir devaḥ pṛthivīm eṣa etām | vicakrame śatarcasaṃ mahitvā | pra viṣṇur astu tavasas tavīyān | tveṣaṃ hy asya sthavirasya nāma |

{ 2.4.3.6} hotāraṃ citraratham adhvarasya | yajñasya yajñasya ketuṃ ruśantam | pratyardhiṃ devasya devasya mahnā | śriyā tvā-agnim atithiṃ janānām | ā no viśvābhir ūtibhiḥ sajoṣāḥ | brahma juṣāṇo haryaśva yāhi | varīvṛjat sthavirebhiḥ suśipra | asme dadhad vṛṣaṇaṃ śuṣmam indra | indraḥ suvarṣā janayann ahāni | jigāya-uśigbhiḥ pṛtanā abhiśrīḥ |

{ 2.4.3.7} prārocayan manave ketum ahnām | avindaj jyotir bṛhate raṇāya | aśvināv avase nihvaye vām | ā nūnaṃ yātaṃ sukṛtāya viprā | prātaryuktena suvṛtā rathena | upāgacchatam avasā-āgataṃ naḥ | aviṣṭaṃ dhīṣv aśvinā na āsu | prajāvad reto ahrayaṃ no astu | āvāṃ toke tanaye tūtujānāḥ | suratnāso devavītiṃ gamema |

{ 2.4.3.8} tvaṃ soma kratubhiḥ sukratur bhūḥ | tvaṃ dakṣaiḥsudakṣo viśvavedāḥ | tvaṃ vṛṣā vṛṣatv ebhir mahitvā | dyumnebhir dyumny abhavo nṛcakṣāḥ | aṣāḍhaṃ yutsu pṛtanāsu papriyam | suvarṣām apsvāṃ vṛjanasya gopām | bhareṣujāṃ sukṣitiṃ suśravasam | jayantaṃ tvām anu madema soma | bhavā mitro na śevyo ghṛtāsutiḥ | vibhūtadyumna evayā u saprathāḥ |

{ 2.4.3.9} adhā { adho} te viṣṇo viduṣācid ṛdhyaḥ | stomo yajñasya rādhyo haviṣmataḥ | yaḥ pūrvyāya vedhase navīyase | sumajjānaye viṣṇave dadāśati | yo jātam asya mahato mahi bravāt | seduḥ śravobhir yujyaṃcid abhyasat | tam u stotāraḥ pūrvyaṃ yathā vida ṛtasya | garbhaṃ haviṣā pipartana | ā-asya jānanto nāma cid vivaktana | bṛhat te viṣṇo sumatiṃ bhajāmahe |

{ 2.4.3.10} imā dhānā ghṛtasnuvaḥ | harī iha-upavakṣataḥ | indraṃ sukhatame rathe | eṣa brahmā pra te mahe | vidathe śaṃsiṣaṃ harī | ya ṛtviyaḥ pra te vanve | vanuṣo haryataṃ madam | indro nāma ghṛtaṃ na yaḥ | haribhiś cāru secate | śruto gaṇa ā tvā viśantu |

{ 2.4.3.11} harivarpasaṃ giraḥ | ā carṣaṇiprā vṛṣabho janānām | rājā kṛṣṭīnāṃ puruhūta indraḥ | stutaḥ śravasyann avasopa madrik | yuktvā harī vṛṣaṇā-āyāhy arvāṅ | pra yat sindhavaḥ prasavaṃ yad āyan | āpaḥ samudraṃ rathyā-iva jagmuḥ | ataś cid indraḥ sadaso varīyān | yad īṃ somaḥ pṛṇāti dugdhó aṃśuḥ | hvayāmasi tvā-indra yāhy arvāṅ |

{ 2.4.3.12} araṃ te somas tanuve bhavāti | śatakrato mādayasvā suteṣu | pra-asmāṃ ava pṛtanāsu pra yutsu | indrāya somāḥ pradivo vidānāḥ | ṛbhur yebhir vṛṣaparvā vihāyāḥ | prayamyamāṇān prati ṣū gṛbhāya | indra piba vṛṣadhūtasya vṛṣṇaḥ | aheḍamāna upayāhi yajñam | tubhyaṃ pavanta indavaḥ sutāsaḥ | gāvo na vajrint svamoko accha |

{ 2.4.3.13} indra-āgahi prathamo yajñiyānām | yā te kākut sukṛtā yā variṣṭhā | yayā śaśvat pibasi madhva ūrmim | tayā pāhi pra te adhvaryur asthāt | saṃ te vajro vartatām indra gavyuḥ | prātaryujā vibodhaya | aśvināv ā-iha gacchatam | asya somasya pītaye | prātaryāvāṇā prathamā yajadhvam | purā gṛdhrād araruṣaḥ pibāthaḥ | prātar hi yajñam aśvinā dadhāte | praśaṃsanti kavayaḥ pūrvabhājaḥ | prātar yajadhvam aśvinā hinota | na sāyam asti devayā ajuṣṭam | uta-anyo asmad yajate vicāyaḥ | pūrvaḥ pūrvo yajamāno vanīyān |

anuvāka 4 { 2.4.4.1} naktaṃ jātā-asy oṣadhe | rāme kṛṣṇe asikni ca | idaṃ rajani rajaya | kilāsaṃ palitaṃ ca yat | kilāsaṃ ca palitaṃ ca | nirito nāśayā pṛṣat | ā naḥ svo aśnutāṃ varṇaḥ | parā śvetāni pātaya | asitaṃ te nilayanam | āsthānam asitaṃ tava |

{ 2.4.4.2} asikniy asy oṣadhe | nir ito nāśayā pṛṣat | asthijasya kilāsasya | tanūjasya ca yat tvaci | kṛtyayā kṛtasya brahmaṇā | lakṣma śvetam anīnaśam | sarūpā nāma te mātā | sarūpo nāma te pitā | sarūpā-asy oṣadhe sā | sarūpam idaṃ kṛdhi |

{ 2.4.4.3} śunaṃ huvema maghavānam indram | asmin bhare nṛtamaṃ vājasātau | śṛṇvantam ugramūtaye | samatsu | ghnantaṃ vṛtrāṇi saṃjitaṃ dhanānām | dhūnutha dyāṃ parvatān dāśuṣe vasu | ni vo vanā jihate yāmano bhiyā | kopayatha pṛthivīṃ pṛśnimātaraḥ | yudhe yad ugrāḥ pṛṣatīr ayugdhvam | pravepayanti parvatān | viviñcanti vanaspatīn |

{ 2.4.4.4} pro 'vārata maruto durmadā iva | devāsaḥ sarvayā viśā | purutrā hi sadṛṅṅ asi | viśo viśvā anu prabhu | samatsu tvā havāmahe | samatsv agnim avase | vājayanto havāmahe | vājeṣu citrarādhasam | saṃgacchadhvaṃ saṃvadadhvam | saṃ vo manāṃsi jānatām |

{ 2.4.4.5} devā bhāgaṃ yathā pūrve | saṃjānānā upāsata | samāno mantraḥ samitiḥ samānī | samānaṃ manaḥ saha cittam eṣām | samānaṃ keto abhi saṃrabhadhvam | saṃjñānena vo haviṣā yajāmaḥ | samānī va ākūtiḥ | samānā hṛdayāni vaḥ | samānam astu vo manaḥ | yathā vaḥ susahāsati |

{ 2.4.4.6} saṃjñānaṃ naḥ svaiḥ | saṃjñānam araṇaiḥ | saṃjñānam aśvinā yuvam | iha-asmāsu niyacchatam | saṃjñānaṃ me bṛhaspatiḥ | saṃjñānaṃ savitā-akarat | saṃjñānam aśvinā yuvam | iha mahyaṃ niyacchatam | upa cchāyām iva ghṛṇeḥ | aganma śarma te vayam |

{ 2.4.4.7} agne hiraṇyasaṃdṛśaḥ | adabdhebhiḥ savitaḥ pāyubhiṣ ṭvam | śivebhir adya paripāhi no gayam | hiranyajihvaḥ suvitāya navyase | rakṣā mākir no aghaśaṃsa īśata | made-made hi no daduḥ | yuthā gavām ṛjukratuḥ | saṃgṛbhāya purū { pūrū} śatā | ubhayā hastyā vasu | śiśīhi rāya ābhara |

{ 2.4.4.41} śiprin vājānāṃ pate | śacīvas tava daṃsanā | ā tū na indra bhājaya | goṣv aśveṣu śubhruṣu | sahasreṣu tuvīmagha | yad devā deva heḍanam | devāsaś cakṛmā vayam | ādityās tasmān mā yūyam | ṛtasya-ṛtena muñcata | 0 ṛtasya-ṛtena-ādityāḥ |

{ 2.4.4.42} yajatrā muñcata-iha mā | yajñair vo yajñavāhasaḥ | āśikṣanto na śekima | medasvatā yajamānāḥ | srucā-ājyena juhvataḥ | akāmā vo viśve devāḥ | śikṣanto na-upaśekima | yadi divā yadi naktam | ena enasyo 'karat | 0 bhūtaṃ mā tasmād bhavyaṃ ca |

{ 2.4.4.43} drupadād iva muñcatu | drupadād iva-in mumucānaḥ | svin naḥ srātvī malād iva | pūtaṃ pavitreṇa-iva-ājyam | viśve muñcantu mā-enasaḥ | ud vayaṃ tamasas pari | paśyanto jyotir uttaram | devaṃ devatrā sūryam | aganma jyotir uttamam |

anuvāka 5 { 2.4.5.44} vṛṣā so aṃśuḥ pavate haviṣmānt somaḥ | indrasya bhāga ṛtayuḥ śata-āyuḥ | sa mā vṛṣāṇaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ kṛṇotu | priyaṃ viśāṃ sarvavīraṃ suvīram | kasya vṛṣā sate sacā | niyutvān vṛṣabho raṇat | vṛtrahā somapītaye | yas te śṛṅga vṛṣo napāt | praṇapāt kuṇḍapāyyaḥ | 0 ny asmin dadhra ā manaḥ |

{ 2.4.5.45} taṃ sadhrīcīr ūtayo vṛṣṇiyāni | pauṃsyāni niyutaḥ saścur indram | samudraṃ na sindhava ukthaśuṣmāḥ | uruvyacasaṃ gira āviśanti | indrāya giro aniśitasargāḥ | apaḥ prairayant sagarasya budhnāt | yo akṣeṇa-iva cakriyā śacibhiḥ | viṣvaktastambha pṛthivīm uta dyām | akṣodayat-śavasā kṣāma budhnam | 0 vārṇavātas taviṣībhir indraḥ |

{ 2.4.5.46} dṛḍhāny aughnād uśamāna ojaḥ | avābhinat kakubhaḥ parvatānām | ā no agne suketunā | rayiṃ viśvāyupoṣasam | mārḍīkaṃ dhehi jīvase | tvaṃ soma mahe bhagam | tvaṃ yūna ṛtāyate | dakṣaṃ dadhāsi jīvase | rathaṃ yuñjate marutaḥ śubhe sugam sūro na mitrāvaruṇā gaviṣṭiṣu |

{ 2.4.5.47} rajāṃsi citrā vicaranti tanyavaḥ | divaḥ samrājā payasā na ukṣatam | vācaṃ su mitrāvaruṇāv irāvatīm | parjanyaś citrāṃ vadati tviṣīmatīm | abhrā vasata marutaḥ su māyayā | dyāṃ varṣayatam aruṇām arepasam | ayukta sapta śundhyuvaḥ | sūro rathasya naptríyaḥ | tābhir yāti svayuktibhiḥ | 0 vahiṣṭhebhir viharan yāsi { vīhaṣṭhebhir vaharan yāsi} tantum |

{ 2.4.5.48} avavyayann asitaṃ deva vasvaḥ | davidhvato raśmayaḥ sūryasya | carma-iva-avādhus tamo apsv antaḥ | parjanyāya pragāyata | divas putrāya mīḍhuṣe | sa no yavasam icchatu | acchā vada tavasaṃ gīrbhir ābhiḥ { ābhi} | stuhi parjanyaṃ namasā vivāsa | kanikradad vṛṣabho jīradānuḥ | 0 reto dadhātv oṣadhīṣu garbham |

{ 2.4.5.49} yo garbham oṣadhīnām | gavāṃ kṛṇoty arvatām | parjanyaḥ puruṣīṇām | tasmā idāsye haviḥ | juhotā madhumattamam | iḍāṃ naḥ saṃyataṃ karat | tisro yad agne śaradas tvām it | śuciṃ ghṛtena śucayaḥ saparyan | nāmāni cid dadhire yajñiyāni | 0 asūdayanta tanuvaḥ sujātāḥ |

{ 2.4.5.50} indraś ca naḥ śunāsīrau | imaṃ yajñaṃ mimikṣatam | garbhaṃ dhattaṃ svastaye | yayor idaṃ viśvaṃ bhuvanam āviveśa | yayor ānando nihito mahaś ca | śunāsīrāv ṛtubhiḥ saṃvidānau | indravantau havir idaṃ { hṛdiridaṃ} juṣethām | ā ghā ye agnim indhate | stṛṇanti barhir ānuṣak | 0 yeṣām indro yuvā sakhā | 1 agne indraś ca medinā | 2 hatho vṛtrāṇy aprati | 3 yuvaṃ hi vṛtrahantamā | 4 yābhyāṃ suvar ajayann agra eva | 5 yāv ātasthatur bhuvanasya madhye | 6 pracarṣaṇī vṛṣaṇā vajrabāhū | 7 agnī indrā vṛtrahaṇā huve vām |

anuvāka 6 { 2.4.6.51} uta naḥ priyā priyāsu | saptasvasā { saptasvasā} sujuṣṭā | sarasvatī stomyā-abhūt | imā juhvānā yuṣmad ā namobhiḥ | prati stomaṃ sarasvati juṣasva | tava śarman priyatame dadhānāḥ | upastheyāma śaraṇaṃ na vṛkṣam | trīṇi padā vicakrame | viṣṇur gopā adābhyaḥ | 0 tato dharmāṇi dhārayan |

{ 2.4.6.52} tad asya priyam abhi pātho aśyām | naro yatra devayavo madanti | urukramasya sa hi bandhur itthā | viṣṇoḥ pade parame madhva utsaḥ | kratvā dā asthu śreṣṭhaḥ | adya tvā vanvant surekṇāḥ | marta ānāśa suvṛktim | imā brahma brahmavāha | priyā ta ā barhiḥ sīda | 0 vīhi sūra puroḍāśam |

{ 2.4.6.53} upa naḥ sūnavo giraḥ | śṛṇvan tvam ṛtasya ye | sumṛḍīkā bhavantu naḥ | adyā no deva savitaḥ | prajāvat sāvīḥ saubhagam | parā duṣṣvapniyaṃ {ānaṃdā. duṣṣvapniṃ} suva | viśvāni deva savitaḥ | duritāni parāsuva | yad bhadraṃ tan ma āsuva | 0 śucim arkair bṛhaspatim |

{ 2.4.6.54} adhvareṣu namasyata | anāmy oja ācake | yā-adhārayanta devā sudakṣā dakṣapitārā | asuryāya pramahasā | sa it kṣeti sudhita okasi sve | tasmā iḍā pinvate viśvadānī | tasmai viśaḥ svayam evā''namanti | yasmin brahmā rājani pūrva eti | sakūtim indra sacyutim | 0 sacyutiṃ jaghanacyutim |

{ 2.4.6.55} kanāt kābhāṃ na ābhara | prayapsyann iva sakthyau | vi na indra mṛdho jahi | kanīkhunad iva sāpayan | abhi naḥ suṣṭutiṃ naya | prajāpatiḥ striyāṃ yaśaḥ | muṣkayor adadhāt sapam | kāmasya tṛptim ānandam | tasya-agne bhājaya-iha mā | 0 modaḥ pramoda ānandaḥ |

{ 2.4.6.56} muṣkayor nihitaḥ sapaḥ | sṛtvā-iva kāmasya tṛpyāṇi | dakṣiṇānāṃ pratigrahe | manasaś cit tam ākūtim | vācaḥ satyam aśīmahi | paśūnāṃ rūpam annasya | yaśaḥ śrīḥ śrayatāṃ mayi | yathā-aham asyā atṛpaṃ striyai pumān | yathā strī tṛpyati puṃsi priye priyā | 0 evaṃ bhagasya tṛpyāṇi |

{ 2.4.6.57} yajñasya kāmyaḥ priyaḥ | dadāmi-ity agnir vadati | tathā-iti vāyur āha tat | hanta-iti satyaṃ candramāḥ | ādityaḥ satyam om iti | āpas tat satyam ābharan | yaśo yajñasya dakṣiṇām | asau me kāmaḥ samṛdhyatām | na hi spaśam avidann anyam asmāt | 0 vaiśvānarāt puraetāram { pura tāram} agneḥ |

{ 2.4.6.58} atha-im amanthann amṛtam amūrāḥ | vaiśvānaraṃ kṣetrajityāya devāḥ | yeṣām ime pūrve armāsa āsan | ayūpāḥ sadma vibhṛtā purūṇi | vaiśvānara tvayā te nuttāḥ | pṛthivīm anyām abhitasthur janāsaḥ | pṛthivīṃ mātaraṃ mahīm | antarikṣam upabruve | bṛhatīmūtaye divam | 0 viśvaṃ bibharti pṛthivī {ānaṃdā. pṛthivīm} |

{ 2.4.6.59} antarikṣaṃ vipaprathe | duhe dyaur bṛhatī payaḥ | na tā naśanti na dabhāti taskaraḥ | na-enā amitro vyathir ādadharṣati { vyathirādadharṣati} | devāṃś ca yābhir yajate dadāti ca | jyog it tābhiḥ sacate gopatiḥ saha | na tā arvā reṇukakāṭo aśnute | na saṃskṛtatram upayanti tā abhi | urugāyam abhayaṃ tasya tā anu | 0 gāvo martyasya vicaranti yajvanaḥ |

{ 2.4.6.60} rātrī vyakhyad āyatī | purutrā devyakṣabhiḥ | viśvā adhi śriyo 'dhita | {not in } upa te gā iva-akaram | vṛṇīṣva duhitar divaḥ | rātrī stomaṃ na jigyuṣī | devīṃ vācam ajanayanta devāḥ | tāṃ viśvarūpāḥ paśavo vadanti | sā no mandreṣamūrjaṃ duhānā dhenur vāg asmān upa suṣṭutā-etu { suṣṭataitu} |

{ 2.4.6.61} yad vāg vadanty avicetanāni | rāṣṭrī devānāṃ niṣasāda mandrā | catasra ūrjaṃ duduhe payāṃsi | kva svid asyāḥ paramaṃ jagāma | gaurī mimāya salilāni takṣatī | ekapadī dvipadī sā catuṣpadī | aṣṭāpadī navapadī babhūvuṣī | sahasra-akṣarā parame vyoman | tasyāṃ samudrā adhi vikṣaranti | 0 tena jīvanti pradiśaś catasraḥ |

{ 2.4.6.62} tataḥ kṣaraty akṣaram | tad viśvam upajīvati | indrā sūrā janayan viśvakarmā | marutvāṃ astu gaṇavānt sajātavān | asya snuṣā śvaśurasya praśiṣṭim | sapatnā vācaṃ manasā upāsatām | indraḥ sūro atarad rajāṃsi | snuṣā sapatnaḥ śvaśuro 'yam astu | ayaṃ śatrūñ jayatu jarhṛṣāṇaḥ | 0 ayaṃ vājaṃ jayatu vājasātau | 1 agniḥ kṣatrabhṛd anibhṛṣṭam ojaḥ | 2 sahasriyo dīpyatām aprayucchan | 3 vibhrājamānaḥ samidhāna ugraḥ | 4 ā-antarikṣam aruhad agan dyām |

anuvāka 7 { 2.4.7.63} vṛṣā-asy aṃśur vṛṣabhāya gṛhyase | vṛṣā-ayam ugro nṛcakṣase | divyaḥ karmaṇyo hito bṛhan nāma | vṛṣabhasya yā kakut | viṣūvān viṣṇo bhavatu | ayaṃ yo māmako vṛṣā | atho indra iva devebhyaḥ | vibravītu janebhyaḥ | āyuṣmantaṃ varcasvantam | 0 atho adhipatiṃ viśām |

{ 2.4.7.64} asyāḥ pṛthivyā adhyakṣam | imam indra vṛṣabhaṃ kṛṇu | yaḥ suśṛṅgaḥ suvṛṣabhaḥ | kalyāṇo droṇa āhitaḥ | kārṣīvalapragāṇena | vṛṣabheṇa yajāmahe | vṛṣabheṇa yajamānāḥ | akrūreṇa-iva sarpiṣā | mṛdhaś ca sarvā indreṇa | 0 pṛtanāś ca jayāmasi |

{ 2.4.7.65} yasya-ayam ṛṣabho haviḥ | indrāya pariṇīyate | jayāti śatrum āyantam | atho hanti pṛtanyataḥ | nṛṇām aha praṇīr asat | agra udbhindatām asat | indra śuṣmaṃ tanuvā merayasva | nīcā viśvā abhitiṣṭha-abhimātīḥ | niśṛṇīhy ābādhaṃ yo no asti | 0 uruṃ no lokaṃ kṛṇuhi jīradāno |

{ 2.4.7.66} prehy abhiprehi prabharā sahasva | mā viveno viśṛṇuṣvā janeṣu | ud īḍito vṛṣabha tiṣṭha śuṣmaiḥ | indra śatrūn puro asmāka yudhya | agne jetā tvaṃ jaya | śatrūnt sahasa ojasā | vi śatrūn vimṛdho nuda | etaṃ te stomaṃ tuvijāta vipraḥ | rathaṃ na dhīraḥ svapā atakṣam | 0 yad īd agne prati tvaṃ deva haryāḥ |

{ 2.4.7.67} suvarvatīr apa enā jayema | yo ghṛtena-abhimānitaḥ | indra jaitrāya jajñiṣe | sa naḥ saṃkāsu pāraya | pṛtanāsāhyeṣu ca | indro jigāya pṛthivīm | antarikṣaṃ suvar mahat | vṛtrahā purucetanaḥ | indro jigāya sahasā sahāṃsi | 0 indro jigāya pṛtanāni viśvā |

{ 2.4.7.68} indro jāto vi puro ruroja | sa naḥ paraspā varivaḥ kṛṇotu | ayaṃ kṛtnur agṛbhītaḥ | viśvajid udbhid it somaḥ | ṛṣir vipraḥ kāvyena | vāyur agregā yajñaprīḥ | sākaṃ gan manasā yajñam | śivo niyudbhiḥ śivābhiḥ | vāyo śukro ayāmi te | 0 madhvo agraṃ diviṣṭiṣu |

{ 2.4.7.69} āyāhi somapītaye | svāruho deva niyutvatā | imam indra vardhaya kṣatriyāṇām | ayaṃ viśāṃ viśpatir astu rājā | asmā indra mahi varcāṃsi dhehi | avarcasaṃ kṛṇuhi śatrum asya | imam ābhaja grāme aśveṣu goṣu | nir amuṃ bhaja yo 'mitro asya | varṣman kṣatrasya kakubhi śrayasva | 0 tato na ugro vibhajā vasūni |

{ 2.4.7.70} asme dyāvāpṛthivī bhūri vāmam | saṃduhāthāṃ gharmadughā-iva dhenuḥ | ayaṃ rājā priya indrasya bhūyāt | priyo gavām oṣadhīnām uta-apām | yunajmi ta uttarāvantam indram | yena jayāsi na parājayāsai | sa tvā-akar ekavṛṣabhaṃ svānām | atho rājann uttamaṃ mānavānām | uttaras tvam adhare te sapatnāḥ | 0 ekavṛṣā indrasakhā jigīvān |
