{ 2.4.7.71} viśvā āśāḥ pṛtanāḥ saṃjayaṃ jayan | abhitiṣṭha śatrūyataḥ sahasva | tubhyaṃ bharanti kṣitayo yaviṣṭha | balim agne anti ta ota dūrāt | ā bhandiṣṭhasya sumatiṃ cikiddhi | bṛhat te agne mahi śarma bhadram | yo dehyo anamayad vadhasnáiḥ | yo aryapatnīr uṣasaś cakāra | sa nirudhyā nahuṣo yahvo agniḥ | 0 viśaś cakre balihṛtaḥ sahobhiḥ |

{ 2.4.7.72} pra sadyo agne atyeṣy anyān | āvir yasmai cārutaro babhūtha | īḍe 'nyo vapuṣyo vibhāvā | priyo viśām atithir mānuṣīṇām | brahma jyeṣṭhā vīryā saṃbhṛtāni | brahmāgre jyeṣṭhaṃ divam ātatāna | ṛtasya brahma prathamā-uta jajñe | tena-arhati brahmaṇā spardhituṃ kaḥ | brahma sruco ghṛtavatīḥ | 0 brahmaṇā svaravo mitāḥ |

{ 2.4.7.73} brahma yajñasya tantavaḥ | ṛtvijo ye haviṣkṛtaḥ | śṛṅgāṇi-iva-it-śṛṅgiṇāṃ saṃdadṛśrire | caṣālavantaḥ svaravaḥ pṛthivyām | te devāsaḥ svaravas tasthivāṃsaḥ | namaḥ sakhibhyaḥ sannān mā-avagāta | abhibhūr agnir atarad rajāṃsi | spṛdho vihatya pṛtanā abhiśrīḥ | juṣāṇo ma āhutiṃ mā mahiṣṭa | 0 hatvā sapatnān varivaskaraṃ naḥ | 1 īśānaṃ tvā bhuvanānām abhiśriyam | 2 staumy agna urukṛtaṃ suvīram | 3 havir juṣāṇaḥ sapatnāṃ abhibhūr asi | 4 jahi śatrūṃr apa mṛdho nudasva |

anuvāka 8 { 2.4.8.74} sa pratnavan navīyasā | agne dyumnena saṃyatā | bṛhat tanantha bhānunā | navaṃ nu stomam agnaye | divaḥ śyenāya jījanam | vasoḥ kuvid vanāti naḥ | svāruhā yasya śriyo dṛśe | rayir vīravato yathā | agre yajñasya cetataḥ | 0 adābhyaḥ puraetā |

{ 2.4.8.75} agnir viśāṃ mānuṣīṇām | tūrṇī rathaḥ sadā navaḥ | navaṃ somāya vājine | ājyaṃ payaso 'jani | juṣṭaṃ śucitamaṃ vasu | navaṃ soma juṣasva naḥ | pīyūṣasya-iha { pīyūṣasvyeha} tṛpṇuhi | yas te bhāga ṛtā vayam | navasya soma te vayam | 0 ā sumatiṃ vṛṇīmahe |

{ 2.4.8.76} sa no rāsva sahasriṇaḥ | navaṃ havir juṣasva naḥ | ṛtubhiḥ soma bhūtamam | tad aṅga pratiharya naḥ | rājant soma svastaye | navaṃ stomaṃ navaṃ haviḥ | indrāgnibhyāṃ nivedaya | taj juṣetāṃ sacetasā | śuciṃ nu stomaṃ navajātam adya | 0 indrāgnī vṛtrahaṇā juṣethām |

{ 2.4.8.77} ubhā hi vāṃ suhavā johavīmi | tā vājaṃ sadya uśate dheṣṭhā | agnir indro navasya naḥ | asya havyasya tṛpyatām | iha devau sahasriṇau | yajñaṃ na ā hi gacchatām | vasumantaṃ suvarvidam | asya havyasya tṛpyatām | agnir indro navasya naḥ | 0 {not in } viśvān devāṃs tarpayata |

{ 2.4.8.78} haviṣo 'sya navasya naḥ | suvarvido hi jajñire | ā -idaṃ barhiḥ suṣṭarīmā navena | ayaṃ yajño yajamānasya bhāgaḥ | ayaṃ babhūva bhuvanasya garbhaḥ | viśve devā idam adya-āgamiṣṭhāḥ | ime nu dyāvāpṛthivī samīcī | tanvāne yajñaṃ purupeśasaṃ dhiyā | ā -asmai pṛṇītāṃ bhuvanāni viśvā | 0 prajāṃ puṣṭim amṛtaṃ navena |

{ 2.4.8.79} ime dhenū amṛtaṃ ye duhāte | payasvaty uttarām etu puṣṭiḥ | imaṃ yajñaṃ juṣamāṇe navena | samīcī dyāvāpṛthivī ghṛtācī | yaviṣṭho havyavāhanaḥ | citrabhānur ghṛtāsutiḥ | navajāto virocase | agne tat te mahitvanam | tvam agne devatābhyaḥ | 0 bhāge deva na mīyase |

{ 2.4.8.80} sa enā vidvān yakṣyasi | navaṃ stomaṃ juṣasva naḥ | agniḥ prathamaḥ prāśnātu | sa hi veda yathā haviḥ | śivā asmabhyam oṣadhīḥ | kṛṇotu viśvacarṣaṇiḥ | bhadrān naḥ śreḥ samanaiṣṭa devāḥ | tvayā vasena samaśīmahi tvā | sa no mayobhūḥ pito āviśasva | 0 śaṃ tokāya tanuve syonaḥ | 1 etam u tyaṃ madhunā saṃyutaṃ yavam | 2 sarasvatyā adhimanāva carkruṣuḥ { carkr̥ṣṭuḥ} | 3 indra āsīt sīrapatiḥ śatakratuḥ | 4 kīnāśā āsan marutaḥ sudānavaḥ |







prapāṭhaka: 5 anuvāka 1 { 2.5.1.1} prāṇo rakṣati viśvam ejat | iryo bhūtvā bahudhā bahūni | sa it sarvaṃ vyānaśe | yo devo deveṣu vibhūr antaḥ | āvṛd ūdāt kṣetriyadhvagad {kṣetrī-adhvagad?} vṛṣā | tam it prāṇaṃ manasopaśikṣata | agraṃ devānām idam attu no haviḥ | manasaś cittā-idam | bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ ca gupyate | 0 tad dhi deveṣv agriyam |

{ 2.5.1.2} ā na etu puraścaram | saha devair imaṃ havam | manaḥ śreyasi-śreyasi | karman yajñapatiṃ dadhat | juṣatāṃ me vāg idaṃ haviḥ | virāḍ devī purohitā | havyavāḍ anapāyinī | yayā rūpāṇi bahudhā vadanti | peśāṃsi devāḥ parame janitre | 0 sā no virāḍ anapasphurantī |

{ 2.5.1.3} vāg devī juṣatām idaṃ haviḥ | cakṣur devānāṃ jyotir amṛte nyaktam | asya vijñānāya bahudhā nidhīyate | tasya sumnam { sumram} aśīmahi | mā no hāsīd vicakṣaṇam | āyur it -naḥ pratīryatām | anandhāś cakṣuṣā vayam | jīvā jyotir aśīmahi | suvar jyotir uta-amṛtam | 0 śrotreṇa bhadram uta śṛṇvanti satyam | 1 śrotreṇa vācaṃ bahudhā-udyamānām | 2 śrotreṇa modaś ca mahaś ca śrūyate | 3 śrotreṇa sarvā diśa āśṛṇomi | 4 yena prācyā uta dakṣiṇā | 5 pratīcyai diśaḥ śṛṇvanty uttarāt | 6 tad it -śrotraṃ bahudhā udyamānam | 7 arān na nemiḥ pari sarvaṃ babhūva |

anuvāka 2 { 2.5.2.4} udehi vājinyo asy apsv antaḥ | idaṃ rāṣṭram āviśa sūnṛtāvat | yo rohito viśvam idaṃ jajāna | sa no rāṣṭreṣu sudhitān dadhātu | rohaṃ-rohaṃ rohita āruroha | prajābhir vṛddhiṃ januṣām upastham | tābhiḥ saṃrabdho avidat ṣaḍ urvīḥ | gātuṃ prapaśyann iha rāṣṭram āhāḥ | āhārṣīd rāṣṭram iha rohitaḥ | 0 mṛdho vyāsthad abhayaṃ no astu |

{ 2.5.2.5} asmabhyaṃ dyāvāpṛthivī śakvarībhiḥ | rāṣṭraṃ duhāthām iha revatībhiḥ | vimamarśa rohito viśvarūpaḥ | samācakāṇaḥ praruho ruhaś ca | divaṃ gatvāya mahatā mahimnā | vi no rāṣṭram unattu payasā svena | yās te viśas tapasā saṃbabhūvuḥ | gāyatraṃ vatsam anu tās ta āguḥ | tās tvā viśantu mahasā svena | 0 saṃ mātā putro abhyetu rohitaḥ |

{ 2.5.2.6} yūyam ugrā marutaḥ pṛśnimātaraḥ | indreṇa sayujā pramṛṇītha śatrūn | ā vo rohito aśṛṇod abhidyavaḥ | trisaptāso marutaḥ svādusaṃmudaḥ | rohito dyāvāpṛthivī jajāna | tasmiṃs tantuṃ parameṣṭhī tatāna | tasmin-śiśriye aja ekapāt | adṛṃhad dyāvāpṛthivī balena | rohito dyāvāpṛthivī adṛṃhat | 0 tena suvaḥ stabhitaṃ tena nākaḥ |

{ 2.5.2.7} so antarikṣe rajaso vimānaḥ | tena devāḥ suvar anvavindan | suśevaṃ tvā bhānavo dīdivāṃsam | samagrāso juhvo jātavedaḥ | ukṣanti tvā vājinam ā ghṛtena | saṃsam agne yuvase bhojanāni | agne śardha mahate saubhagāya | tava dyumnāny uttamāni santu | saṃ jāspatyaṃ suyamam ākṛṇuṣva | 0 śatrūyatām abhitiṣṭhā mahāṃsi |

anuvāka 3 { 2.5.3.8} punar na indro maghavā dadātu | dhanāni śakro ghanyaḥ surādhāḥ | arvācīnam kṛṇutāṃ yācito manaḥ | śruṣṭī no asya haviṣo juṣāṇaḥ | yāni no jinan dhanāni | jahartha śūra manyunā | indra-anuvinda nas tāni | anena haviṣā punaḥ | indra āśābhyaḥ pari | 0 sarvābhyo 'bhayaṃ karat |

{ 2.5.3.9} jetā śatrūn vicarṣaṇiḥ | ākūtyai tvā kāmāya tvā samṛdhe tvā | puro dadhe amṛtatvāya jīvase | ākūtim asya-avase | kāmam asya samṛddhyai | indrasya yuñjate dhiyaḥ | ākūtiṃ devīṃ manasaḥ puro dadhe | yajñasya mātā suhavā me astu | yad icchāmi manasā sakāmaḥ | 0 videyam enad dhṛdaye niviṣṭam |

{ 2.5.3.10} sa-id agnir agnīṃr atyety anyān | yatra vājī tanayo vīḍupāṇiḥ | sahasrapāthā akṣarā sameti | āśānāṃ tvā-āśāpālebhyaḥ | caturbhyo amṛtebhyaḥ | idaṃ bhūtasya-adhyakṣebhyaḥ | vidhema haviṣā vayam | viśvā āśā madhunā saṃsṛjāmi | anamīvā āpa oṣadhayo bhavantu | 0 ayaṃ yajamāno mṛdho vyasyatām |

{ 2.5.3.11} agṛbhītāḥ paśavaḥ santu sarve | agniḥ somo varuṇo mitra indraḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ savitā yaḥ sahasrī | pūṣā no gobhir avasā sarasvatī | tvaṣṭā rūpāṇi samanaktu yajñaiḥ {ānaṃdā. yajñaḥ} | tvaṣṭā rūpāṇi dadhatī sarasvatī | pūṣā bhagaṃ savitā no dadātu | bṛhaspatir dadad indraḥ sahasram | mitro dātā varuṇaḥ somo agniḥ |

anuvāka 4 { 2.5.4.12} ā no bhara bhagam indra dyumantam | ni te deṣṇasya dhīmahi prareke | urva iva paprathe kāmo asme | tam āpṛṇā vasupate vasūnām | imaṃ kāmaṃ mandayā gobhir aśvaiḥ | candravatā rādhasā paprathaś ca | suvaryavo matibhis tubhyaṃ viprāḥ | indrāya vāhaḥ kuśikāso akran | indrasya nu vīryāṇi pravocam | 0 yāni cakāra prathamāni vajrī |

{ 2.5.4.13} ahann ahim anv apas tatarda | pra vakṣaṇā abhinat parvatānām | ahann ahiṃ parvate śiśriyāṇam | tvaṣṭā-asmai vajraṃ svaryaṃ tatakṣa | vāśrā iva dhenavaḥ syandamānāḥ | añjaḥ samudram avajagmur āpaḥ | vṛṣāyamāṇo 'vṛṇīta {ānaṃdā. vṛṇīta} somam | trikadrukeṣv apibat sutasya | ā sāyakaṃ maghavā-ādatta vajram | 0 ahann enaṃ prathamajām ahīnām |

{ 2.5.4.14} yad indra-ahan prathamajām ahīnām | āt -māyinām amināḥ prota māyāḥ | āt sūryaṃ janayan dyām uṣāsam | tādīknā śatrūn na kila-āvivitse | ahan vṛtraṃ vṛtrataraṃ vyaṃsam | indro vajreṇa mahatā vadhena | skandhāṃsi-iva kuliśenā vivṛkṇā | ahiḥ śayata upapṛk pṛthivyām | ayodhy eva durmada ā hi juhve | 0 mahāvīraṃ tu vibādham ṛjīṣam |

{ 2.5.4.15} na-ātārīr asya samṛtiṃ vadhānām | saṃ rujānāḥ | pipiṣa indraśatruḥ | viśvo vihāyā aratiḥ | vasur dadhe haste dakṣiṇe | taraṇir na śiśrathat | śravasyayā na śiśrathat | viśvasmā id iṣudhyase | devatrā havyamūhiṣe | 0 viśvasmā it sukṛte vāram ṛṇvati | 1 agnir dvārā vyṛṇvati |

{ 2.5.4.16} ud ujjihāno abhi kāmam īrayan | prapṛñcan viśvā bhuvanāni pūrvathā | ā ketunā suṣamiddho yajiṣṭhaḥ | kāmaṃ no agne abhiharya digbhyaḥ | juṣāṇo havyamam ṛteṣu dūḍhyàḥ | ā no rayiṃ bahulā gomatīm iṣam | nidhehi yakṣad amṛteṣu bhūṣan | aśvinā yajñam āgatam | dāśuṣaḥ purudaṃsasā | 0 pūṣā rakṣatu no rayim |

{ 2.5.4.17} imaṃ yajñam aśvinā vardhayantā | imau rayiṃ yajamānāya dhattam | imau paśūn rakṣatāṃ viśvato naḥ | pūṣā naḥ pātu sadam aprayucchan | pra te mahe sarasvati | subhage vājinīvati | satyavāce bhare matim | idaṃ te havyaṃ ghṛtavat sarasvati | satyavāce prabharemā havīṃṣi | 0 imāni te duritā saubhagāni | 1 tebhir vayaṃ subhaḥ syāma |

anuvāka 5 { 2.5.5.18} yajño rāyo yajña īśe vasūnām | yajñaḥ sasyānām uta sukṣitīnām | yajña iṣṭaḥ pūrvacittiṃ dadhātu | yajño brahmaṇvāṃ apyetu devān | ayaṃ yajño vardhatāṃ gobhir aśvaiḥ | iyaṃ vediḥ svapatyā suvīrā | idaṃ barhir ati barhīṃṣy anyā | imaṃ yajñaṃ viśve avantu devāḥ | bhaga eva bhagavāṃ astu devāḥ | 0 tena vayaṃ bhagavantaḥ syāma |

{ 2.5.5.19} taṃ tvā bhaga sarva it - johavīmi | sa no bhaga puraetā bhava-iha | bhaga praṇetar bhaga satyarādhaḥ | bhaga-imāṃ dhiyam ud avadadan naḥ | bhaga pra ṇo janaya gobhir aśvaiḥ | bhaga pra nṛbhir nṛvantaḥ syāma | śaśvatīḥ samā upayanti lokāḥ | śaśvatīḥ samā upayanty āpaḥ | iṣṭaṃ pūrtaṃ śaśvatīnāṃ samānāṃ śāśvatena | 0 haviṣā-iṣṭvā-anantaṃ lokaṃ param āruroha |

{ 2.5.5.20} iyam eva sā yā prathamā vyaucchat | sā rūpāṇi kurute pañca devī | dve svasārau vayatas tantram etat | sanātanaṃ vitataṃ ṣaṇmayūkham | ava-anyāṃs tantūn kirato dhatto anyān | na-avapṛjyāte na gamāte antam | ā vo yantu-udavāhāso adya | vṛṣṭiṃ ye viśve maruto junanti | ayaṃ yo agnir marutaḥ samiddhaḥ | 0 etaṃ juṣadhvaṃ kavayo yuvānaḥ |

{ 2.5.5.21} dhārāvarā maruto dhṛṣṇu v ojasaḥ {?} | mṛgā na bhīmās taviṣebhir ūrmibhiḥ | agnayo na śuśucānā ṛjīṣiṇaḥ | bhrumiṃ dhamanta upa gā avṛṇvata | vicakrame trir devaḥ | āvedhasaṃ nīlapṛṣṭhaṃ bṛhantam | bṛhaspatiṃ sadane sādayadhvam | sādadyoniṃ dama ādīdivāṃsam | hiraṇyavarṇam aruṣaṃ sapema | 0 sa hi śuciḥ śatapatraḥ sa śundhyūḥ |

{ 2.5.5.22} hiraṇyavāśīr iṣiraḥ suvarṣāḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ sa svāveśa ṛṣvāḥ | pūrū sakhibhya āsutiṃ kariṣṭhaḥ | pūṣaṃs tava vrate vayam | na riṣyema kadā cana | stotāras ta iha smasi | yās te pūṣan nāvo antaḥ samudre | hiraṇyayīr antarikṣe caranti | yābhir yāsi dūtyāṃ sūryasya | 0 kāmena kṛtaḥ śrava icchamānaḥ |

{ 2.5.5.23} araṇyāny araṇyāny asau | yā pra-iva naśyasi | kathā grāmaṃ na pṛcchasi | na tvā bhīr iva vindatī3 | vṛṣāravāya vadate | yad upāvati ciccikaḥ | āghāṭī́bhir iva dhāvayan | araṇyānir mahīyate | uta gāva ivā''dan | 0 uto veśma-iva dṛśyate |

{ 2.5.5.24} uto araṇyāniḥ sāyam | śakaṭī́r iva sarjati | gām aṅga-eṣa āhvayati | dārv aṅga-eṣa upāvardhīt | vasann araṇyānyāṃ sāyam | akrukṣad iti manyate | na vā araṇyānir hanti | anyaś ca-in na-abhigacchati | svādoḥ phalasya jagdhvā | 0 yatra kāmaṃ nipadyate | 1 āñjanagandhīṃ surabhīm | 2 bahvannām akṛṣīvalām | 3 pra-ahaṃ mṛgāṇāṃ mātaram | 4 araṇyānīm aśaṃsiṣam |

anuvāka 6 { 2.5.6.25} vārtrahatyāya śavase | pṛtanāsāhyāya ca | indra tvāvartayāmasi | subrahmāṇaṃ vīravantaṃ bṛhantam | uruṃ gabhīraṃ pṛthubudhnam indra | śrutarṣim ugram abhimātiṣāham | asmabhyaṃ citraṃ vṛṣaṇaṃ rayiṃ dāḥ | kṣetriyai tvā nirṛtyai { niŕ̥ṛtyai} tvā | druho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt | 0 anāgasaṃ brahmaṇe tvā karomi |

{ 2.5.6.26} śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe ime | śaṃ te agniḥ saha-adbhir astu | śaṃ te catasraḥ pradiśo bhavantu | yā daivīś catasraḥ pradiśaḥ | vātapatnīr abhi sūryo vicaṣṭe | tāsāṃ tvā jarasa ādadhāmi | pra yakṣma etu nirṛtiṃ { niŕ̥ṛtiṃ} parācaiḥ | amoci yakṣmād duritād avartyai |

{ 2.5.6.27} druhaḥ pāśān nirṛtyai { niŕ̥ṛtyai} ca-udamoci | ahā avartim avidat syonam | apyabhūd bhadre sukṛtasya loke | sūryam ṛtaṃ tamaso grāhyā yat | devā amuñcann asṛjan vyenasaḥ | evam aham imaṃ kṣetriyāj jāmiśaṃsāt | druho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt | bṛhaspate yuvam indraś ca vasvaḥ | divyasya-iśāthe uta pārthivasya | 0 dhattaṃ rayiṃ stuvate kīraye cit |

{ 2.5.6.28} yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ | devāyudham indram ājohuvānāḥ | viśvāvṛdham abhi ye rakṣamāṇāḥ | yena hatā dīrgham adhvānam āyan | anantam artham nivartsyamānāḥ | yat te sujāte himavatsu bheṣajam | mayobhūḥ śaṃtamā yad dhṛdo 'si | tato no dehi sībale | ado giribhyo adhi yat pradhāvasi | 0 saṃśobhamānā kanyā-ìva śubhre |

{ 2.5.6.29} tāṃ tvā mudgalā haviṣā vardhayanti | sā naḥ sībale rayim ābhājaya-iha | pūrvaṃ devā apareṇa-anupaśyañ janmabhiḥ | janma-anyavaraiḥ parāṇi | vedāni devā ayam asmi-iti mām | ahaṃ hitvā śarīraṃ jarasaḥ parastāt | prāṇāpānau cakṣuḥ śrotram | vācaṃ manasi saṃbhṛtām | hitvā śarīraṃ jarasaḥ parastāt | 0 ā bhūtiṃ bhūtiṃ vayam aśnavāmahai | 1 imā eva tā uṣaso yāḥ prathamā vyaucchan | 2 tā devyaḥ kurvate pañca rūpā | 3 śaśvatīr na-avapṛjyanti | 4 na gamanty antam |

anuvāka 7 { 2.5.7.30} vasūnāṃ tvā-adhītena | rudrāṇāmūrmyā | ādityānāṃ tejasā | viśveṣāṃ devānāṃ kratunā | marutām emnā juhomi svāhā | abhibhūtir aham āgamam | indrasakhā svāyudhaḥ | āssva-āśāsu { āsvāśāsu} duṣṣáhaḥ | idaṃ varco agninā dattam āgāt | 0 yaśo bhargaḥ saha ojo balaṃ ca |

{ 2.5.7.31} dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya | pratigṛbhṇāmi mahate vīryāya | āyur asi viśvāyur asi | sarvāyur asi sarvam āyur asi | sarvaṃ ma āyur bhūyāt | sarvam āyur geṣam | bhūr bhuvaḥ suvaḥ | agnir dharmeṇa-annādaḥ | mṛtyur dharmeṇa-annapatiḥ | 0 brahma kṣatraṃ svāhā |

{ 2.5.7.32} prajāpatiḥ praṇetā | bṛhaspatiḥ puraetā | yamaḥ panthāḥ | candramāḥ punarasuḥ svāhā | agnir annādo 'nnapatiḥ | annādyam asmin yajñe yajamānāya dadātu svāhā | somo rājā rājapatiḥ | rājyam asmin yajñe yajamānāya dadātu svāhā | varuṇaḥ samrāṭ samrāṭpatiḥ | 0 sāmrājyam asmin yajñe yajamānāya dadātu svāhā |

{ 2.5.7.33} mitraḥ kṣatraṃ kṣatrapatiḥ | kṣatram asmin yajñe yajamānāya dadātu svāhā | indro balaṃ balapatiḥ | balam asmin yajñe yajamānāya dadātu svāhā | bṛhaspatir brahma brahmapatiḥ | brahmāsmin yajñe yajamānāya dadātu svāhā | savitā rāṣṭraṃ rāṣṭrapatiḥ | rāṣṭram asmin yajñe yajamānāya dadātu svāhā | pūṣā viśāṃ viṭpatiḥ viśam asmin yajñe yajamānāya dadātu svāhā | 0 sarasvatī puṣṭiḥ puṣṭipatnī | 1 puṣṭim asmin yajñe yajamānāya dadātu svāhā | 2 tvaṣṭā paśūnāṃ mithunānāṃ rūpakṛd rupapatiḥ | 3 rūpeṇa-asmin yajñe yajamānāya paśūn dadātu svāhā |

anuvāka 8 { 2.5.8.34} sa īṃ pāhi ya ṛjīṣī tarutraḥ | yaśśípravān vṛṣabho yo matīnām | yo gotrabhid vajrabhṛd yo hariṣṭhāḥ | sa indra citrāṃ abhitṛndhi vājān | ā te śuṣmo vṛṣabha etu paścāt | ā-uttarād adharāgā purastāt | ā viśvato abhi sametv arvāṅ | indra dyumnaṃ suvarvad dhehy asme | proṣv asmai puroratham | 0 indrāya śūṣam arcata |

{ 2.5.8.35} abhīke cid u lokakṛt | saṅge samatsu vṛtrahā | asmākaṃ bodhi coditā | nabhantām anyakeṣām | jyākā adhi dhanvasu | indraṃ vayaṃ śunāsīram | asmin yajñe havāmahe | ā vājair upa no gamat | indrāya śunāsīrāya | 0 srucā juhuta no haviḥ |

{ 2.5.8.36} juṣatāṃ prati medhiraḥ | pra havyāni ghṛtavanty asmai | haryaśvāya bhartā sajoṣāḥ | indra-ṛtubhir brahmaṇā vāvṛdhānaḥ | śunāsīrī havir idaṃ juṣasva | vayaḥ suparṇā upasedur indram | priyamedhā ṛṣayo nādhamānāḥ | apa dhvāntamūrṇuhi pūrdhi cakṣuḥ | mumugdhy asmān nidhaya-iva baddhān | 0 bṛhad indrāya gāyata |

{ 2.5.8.37} maruto vṛtrahantamam | yena jyotir ajanayann ṛtāvṛdhaḥ | devaṃ devāya jāgṛvi | kā mihaikāḥ ka ime pataṅgāḥ | mānthālāḥ kuli pari mā patanti | anāvṛtā-enān pradhamantu devāḥ | sauparṇaṃ cakṣus tanuvā videya | evā''vandasva varuṇaṃ bṛhantam | namasyā dhīram amṛtasya gopām | 0 sa naḥ śarma trivarūthaṃ viyaṃsat |

{ 2.5.8.38} yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ | nāke suparṇam upa yat patantam | hṛdā venanto abhyacakṣata tvā | hiraṇyapakṣaṃ varuṇasya dūtam | yamasya yonau śakunaṃ bhuraṇyum | śaṃ no devīr abhiṣṭaye | āpo bhavantu pītaye | śaṃ yor abhisravantu naḥ | īśānā vāryāṇām | 0 kṣayantīś carṣaṇīnām |

{ 2.5.8.39} apo yācāmi bheṣajam | apsu me somo abravīt | antar viśvāni bheṣajā | agniṃ ca viśvaśaṃbhuvam | āpaś ca viśvabheṣajīḥ | yad apsu te sarasvati | goṣv aśveṣu yan madhu | tena me vājinīvati | mukham {F aṅgdhi} {ānaṃdā. aṅgdhi} {BI aṅdhi} { aṅgdhi} sarasvati | 0 yā sarasvatī vaiśambhalyā |

{ 2.5.8.40} tasyāṃ me rāsva | tasyās te bhakṣīya | tasyās te bhūyiṣṭhabhājo bhūyāsma | ahaṃ tvad asmi mad asi tvam etat | mama-asi yonis tava yonir asmi | mama-iva san vaha havyāny agne | putraḥ pitre lokakṛt- jātavedaḥ | ihaiva san tatra santaṃ tvā-agne | prāṇena vācā manasā bibharmi | 0 tiro mā santam āyur mā prahāsīt |

{ 2.5.8.41} jyotiṣā tvā vaiśvānareṇa-upatiṣṭhe | ayaṃ te yonir ṛtviyaḥ | yato jāto arocathāḥ | taṃ jānann agna āroha | athā no vardhayā rayim | yā te agne yajñiyā tanūs tayā-ihy {BI tapehy} āroha-ātmānam {ānaṃdā. ārohātmānam} { ārohātmātmānam} {BI ārohātyātmānaṃ} | acchā {F vasūni} {BI vasūti} kṛṇvann asme {BI asmin} naryā purūṇi | yajño bhūtvā yajñam āsīda svāṃ yonim | jātavedo bhuva ājāyamānaḥ sakṣaya ehi | 0 upāvaroha jātavedaḥ punas tvam |

{ 2.5.8.42} devebhyo havyaṃ vaha naḥ prajānan | āyuḥ prajāṃ rayim asmāsu dhehi | ajasro dīdihi no duroṇe | tam indraṃ johavīmi | maghavānam ugram | satrādadhānam apratiṣkutaṃ śavāṃsi | maṃhiṣṭho gīrbhir ā ca yajñiyo 'vavartat { vavartat} | rāye no viśvā supathā kṛṇotu {ānaṃdā. kṛṇota} vajrī | trikadrukeṣu mahiṣo yavāśiraṃ tuviśuṣmas tṛpat | 0 somam apibad viṣṇunā sutaṃ yathā-avaśat | 1 sa īṃ mamāda mahi karma kartave mahām urum |

{ 2.5.8.43} sainaṃ saścad devaṃ devaḥ satyam induṃ satya indraḥ | vidad yatī saramā rugṇam adreḥ | mahi pāthaḥ pūrvyaṃ sadhriyakkaḥ | agraṃ nayat supady akṣarāṇām | acchā ravaṃ prathamā jānatī gāt | vidad gavyaṃ saramā dṛḍhamūrvam | yenā nukaṃ mānusī bhojate viṭ | ā ye viśvā svapatyāni cakruḥ | kṛṇvānāso amṛtatvāya gātum | 0 tvaṃ nṛbhir nṛpate devahūtau |

{ 2.5.8.44} bhūrīṇi vṛtvā haryaśva haṃsi | tvaṃ nidasyuṃ cumurim | dhuniṃ ca-asvāpayo dabhītaye suhantu | evā pāhi pratnathā mandatu tvā | śrudhi brahma vāvṛdhasva-uta gīrbhiḥ | āviḥ sūryaṃ kṛṇuhi pīpihīṣaḥ | jahi śatrūṃr {ānaṃdā. śatrūṃs} abhi gā indra tṛndhi | agne bādhasva vi mṛdho nudasva | apa-amīvā apa rakṣāṃsi sedha | 0 asmāt samudrād bṛhato divo naḥ |

{ 2.5.8.45} apāṃ bhūmānam upa naḥ sṛja-iha | yajña pratitiṣṭha sumatau {F suśevā} {ānaṃdā. suśevā} {BI suśavā} { seśevā} ā tvā | vasūni purudhā viśantu | dīrgham āyur yajamānāya kṛṇvan | {F adhāmṛtena} {ānaṃdā. adhāmṛtena} {BI athāmṛtena} { adhāmṛtena} jaritāram {F aṅgdhi} {ānaṃdā. aṅgdhi} {BI aṅdhi} { aṅgdhi} | indraḥ śunāvad vitanoti sīram | saṃvatsarasya pratimāṇam etat | arkasya jyotis tad id āsa jyeṣṭham | saṃvatsaraṃ śunavat sīram etat | 0 indrasya rādhaḥ prayataṃ puru tmanā | 1 tad arkarūpaṃ vimimānam eti | 2 dvādaśa-are pratitiṣṭhati-id vṛṣā | 3 aśvāyanto gavyanto vājayantaḥ | 4 havāmahe tvā-upaganta vā u | 5 ābhūṣantas tvā sumatau navāyām | 6 vayam indra tvā śunaṃ huvema |







prapāṭhaka: 6 anuvāka 1 { 2.6.1.1} svādvīṃ tvā svādunā | tīvrāṃ tīvreṇa | amṛtām amṛtena | madhumatīṃ madhumatā | sṛjāmi saṃ somena | somo 'sy aśvibhyāṃ pacyasva | sarasvatyai pacyasva | indrāya sutrāmṇe pacyasva | pari-ito ṣiñcatā sutam | 0 somo ya uttamaṃ haviḥ |

{ 2.6.1.2} dadhanvā[dadhanvān?] yo naryo apsv antar ā | suṣava somam adribhiḥ | punātu te parisrutam | somaṃ sūryasya duhitā | vāreṇa śaśvatā tanā | vāyuḥ pūtaḥ pavitreṇa | prāṅ somo atidrutaḥ |

{ 2.6.1.3} indrasya yujyaḥ sakhā | brahma kṣatraṃ pavate teja indriyam | surayā somaḥ suta āsuto madāya | śukreṇa deva devatāḥ pipṛgdhi | rasena-annaṃ yajamānāya dhehi | kuvid aṅga yavamanto yavaṃ cit | yathā dānty anupūrvaṃ viyūya | iha-ihaiṣāṃ kṛṇuta bhojanāni | ye barhiṣo namovṛktiṃ na jagmuḥ | 0 upayāmagṛhīto 'sy aśvibhyāṃ tvā juṣṭaṃ gṛhṇāmi |

{ 2.6.1.4} sarasvatyā indrāya sutrāmṇe | eṣa te yonis tejase tvā | vīryāya tvā balāya tvā | tejo 'si tejo mayi dhehi | vīryam asi vīryaṃ mayi dhehi | balam asi balaṃ mayi dhehi | nānā hi vāṃ devahitaṃ sadaḥ kṛtam | mā saṃsṛkṣāthāṃ parame vyoman | surā tvam asi śuṣmiṇī soma eṣaḥ | 0 mā mā hiṃsīḥ svāṃ yonim āviśan |

{ 2.6.1.5} upayāmagṛhīto 'sy āśvinaṃ tejaḥ | sārasvataṃ vīryam | aindraṃ balam | eṣa te yonir modāya tvā | ānandāya tvā mahase tvā | ojo 'sy ojo mayi dhehi | manyur asi manyuṃ mayi dhehi | maho 'si maho mayi dhehi | saho 'si saho mayi dhehi | 0 yā vyāghraṃ viṣūcikā | 1 ubhau vṛkaṃ ca rakṣati | 2 śyenaṃ patatriṇaṃ siṃham | 3 sā-imaṃ pātv aṃhasaḥ | 4 saṃpṛcaḥ stha {ḍyumoṇṭa saṃpṛca stha} saṃ mā bhadreṇa pṛṅkta | 5 vipṛcaḥ stha {ḍyumoṇṭa vipṛca stha} vi mā pāpmanā pṛṅkta |

anuvāka 2 { 2.6.2.6} somo rājā-amṛtaṃ sutaḥ | ṛjīṣeṇa-ajahān mṛtyum | ṛtena satyam indriyam | vipānaṃ śukram andhasaḥ | indrasya-indriyam | idaṃ payo 'mṛtaṃ madhu | somam adbhyo vyapibat | chandasā haṃsaḥ śuciṣat | ṛtena satyam indriyam | 0 adbhyaḥ kṣīraṃ vyapibat |

{ 2.6.2.7} kruṅṅ āṅgiraso dhiyā | ṛtena satyam indriyam | annāt parisruto rasam | brahmaṇā vyapibat kṣatram | ṛtena satyam indriyam | reto mūtraṃ vijahāti | yoniṃ praviśad indriyam | garbho jarāyuṇā-āvṛtaḥ | ulbaṃ jahāti janmanā | 0 ṛtena satyam indriyam |

{ 2.6.2.8} vedena rūpe vyakarot | satāsatī prajāpatiḥ | ṛtena satyam indriyam | somena somau vyapibat | sutāsutau prajāpatiḥ | ṛtena satyam indriyam | dṛṣṭvā rūpe vyākarot | satyānṛte prajāpatiḥ | aśraddhām anṛte 'dadhāt | 0 śraddhāṃ satye prajāpatiḥ | 1 ṛtena satyam indriyam | 2 dṛṣṭvā parisruto rasam | 3 śukreṇa śukraṃ vyapibat | 4 payaḥ somaṃ prajāpatiḥ | 5 ṛtena satyam indriyam | 6 vipānaṃ śukram andhasaḥ | 7 indrasya-indriyam | 8 idaṃ payo 'mṛtaṃ madhu |


anuvāka 3 { 2.6.3.9} {a} surāvantaṃ barhiṣadaṃ suvīram | yajñaṃ hinvanti mahiṣā namobhiḥ | dadhānāḥ somaṃ divi devatāsu | madema-indraṃ yajamānāḥ svarkāḥ | {b} yas te rasaḥ saṃbhṛta oṣadhīṣu | somasya śuṣmaḥ surayā sutasya | tena jinva yajamānaṃ madena | sarasvatīm aśvināv indram agnim | {c} yam aśvinā namucer āsurād adhi | 0 sarasvaty asanod indriyāya |

{ 2.6.3.10} imaṃ taṃ śukraṃ madhumantam indum | somaṃ rājānam iha bhakṣayāmi | {d} yad atra riptaṃ rasinaḥ sutasya | yad indro apibat-śacībhiḥ | ahaṃ tad asya manasā śivena | somaṃ rājānam iha bhakṣayāmi | {e} pitṛbhyaḥ svadhāvibhyaḥ svadhā namaḥ | pitāmahebhyaḥ svadhāvibhyaḥ svadhā namaḥ | prapitāmahebhyaḥ svadhāvibhyaḥ svadhā namaḥ | 0 akṣan pitaraḥ |

{ 2.6.3.11} amīmadanta pitaraḥ | atītṛpanta pitaraḥ | amīmṛjanta pitaraḥ | pitaraḥ śundhadhvam | {f} punantu mā pitaraḥ somyāsaḥ | punantu mā pitāmahāḥ | punantu prapitāmahāḥ | pavitreṇa śata-āyuṣā | {g} punantu mā pitāmahāḥ | 0 punantu prapitāmahāḥ |

{ 2.6.3.12} pavitreṇa śata-āyuṣā | viśvam āyur vyaśnavai | {h} agna āyūṃṣi pavase, {i} 'gne pavasva | {j} pavamānaḥ suvarjanaḥ, {k} punantu mā devajanāḥ | {l} jātavedaḥ pavitravad, {m} yat te pavitram arciṣi | {n} ubhābhyāṃ deva savitar, {o} vaiśvadevī punatī | {p} ye samānāḥ samanasaḥ | pitaro yamarājye | teṣāṃ lokaḥ svadhā namaḥ | 0 yajño deveṣu kalpatām |

{ 2.6.3.13) {q} ye sajātāḥ samanasaḥ | jīvā jīveṣu māmakāḥ | teṣāṃ śrīr mayi kalpatām | asmin-loke śataṃ samāḥ | {r} dve srutī aśṛṇavaṃ pitṛṇām | ahaṃ devānām uta martyānām | yābhyām {ḍyumoṇṭa tābhyām} idaṃ viśvam ejat sameti | yad antarā pitaraṃ mātaraṃ ca | {s} idaṃ haviḥ prajananaṃ me astu | 0 daśavīraṃ sarvagaṇaṃ svastaye | 1 ātmasani prajāsani | 2 paśusany abhayasani lokasani | 3 agniḥ prajāṃ bahulāṃ me karotu | 4 annaṃ payo reto asmāsu dhatta | 5 rāyas poṣam iṣamūrjam asmāsu dīdharat svāhā |

anuvāka 4 { 2.6.4.14} {a} sīsena tantraṃ manasā manīṣiṇaḥ | ūrṇāsūtreṇa kavayo vayanti | aśvinā yajñaṃ savitā sarasvatī | indrasya rūpaṃ varuṇo bhiṣajyan | {b} tad asya rūpam amṛtaṃ śacībhiḥ | tisro dadhur devatāḥ saṃrarāṇāḥ | lomāni śaṣpair bahudhā na tokmabhiḥ | tvag asya māṃsam abhavanna lājāḥ | {c} tad aśvinā bhiṣajā rudravartanī | 0 sarasvatī vayati peśo antaraḥ {ḍyumoṇṭa antaram cf. { 2.6.4.15} asthi majjānaṃ māsaraiḥ | kārotareṇa dadhato gavāṃ tvaci | {d} sarasvatī manasā peśalaṃ vasu | nāsatyābhyāṃ vayati darśataṃ vapuḥ | rasaṃ parisrutā na rohitam | nagnahur dhīras tasaraṃ na vema | {e} payasā śukram amṛtaṃ janitram | surayā mūtrāj janayanti retaḥ | apa-amatiṃ durmatiṃ bādhamānāḥ | 0 ūvadhyaṃ vātaṃ sabuvaṃ tad ārāt |

{ 2.6.4.16} {f} indraḥ sutrāmā hṛdayena satyam | puroḍāśena savitā jajāna | yakṛt klomānaṃ varuṇo bhiṣajyan {ānaṃdā. bhiṣajyan} | matasne vāyavyair na mināti pittam | {g} āntrāṇi sthālī madhu pinvamānā | gudā {ḍyumoṇṭa gudāḥ} pātrāṇi sudughā na dhenuḥ | śyenasya patraṃ na plīhā śacībhiḥ | āsandī nābhir udaraṃ na mātā | {h} kumbho vaniṣṭhur janitā śacībhiḥ | 0 yasminn agre yonyāṃ garbho antaḥ |

{ 2.6.4.17} plāśīr vyaktaḥ śatadhāra utsaḥ | duhe na kumbhīṃ svadhāṃ pitṛbhyaḥ | {i} mukhaṃ sad asya śira it sadena {ḍyumoṇṭa satena} | jihvā pavitram aśvinā saṃ sarasvatī | capyaṃ { cappaṃ} na pāyur bhiṣag asya vālaḥ | vastir na śepo harasā tarasvī | {j} aśvibhyāṃ cakṣur amṛtaṃ grahābhyām | chāgena tejo haviṣā śṛtena | pakṣmāṇi godhūmaiḥ kvalair utāni | 0 peśo na śuklam asitaṃ vasāte |

{!} { 2.6.4.18} {k} avir na meṣo nasi vīryāya | prāṇasya panthā amṛto grahābhyām | sarasvaty upa vākair vyānam | nasyāni barhir badarairjajāna | {l} indrasya rūpam ṛṣabho balāya | karṇabhyāṃ śrotram amṛtaṃ grahābhyām | yavā na barhir bhruvi kesarāṇi | karkandhu { karkandu} jajñe madhu sāraghaṃ mukhāt | {m} ātmann upasthe na vṛkasya loma | 0 mukhe śmaśrūṇi na vyāghralomam {ḍyumoṇṭa vyāghraloman} |

{ 2.6.6.31} pūtaṃ pavitreṇa-iva-ājyam | āpaḥ śundhantu mā-enasaḥ | {i} ud vayaṃ tamasas pari | paśyanto jyotir uttaram | devaṃ devatrā sūryam | aganma jyotir uttamam | {j} pratiyuto varuṇasya pāśaḥ | pratyasto varuṇasya pāśaḥ | {k} edho 'sy edhiṣīmahi | 0 samid asi |

{ 2.6.6.32} tejo 'si tejo mayi dhehi | {l} apo anvacāriṣam | rasena samasṛkṣmahi | payasvāṃ agna āgamam | taṃ mā saṃsṛja varcasā | prajayā ca dhanena ca | {m} samāvavarti pṛthivī | sam uṣāḥ | sam u sūryaḥ | 0 sam u viśvām idaṃ jagat | 1 vaiśvānarajyotir bhūyāsam | 2 vibhuṃ kāmaṃ vyaśnavai | 3 {n} bhūḥ svāhā |

anuvāka 7 { 2.6.7.33} {a} hotā yakṣat samidhā-indram iḍas pade | nābhā pṛthivyā adhi | divo varṣmant samidhyate | ojiṣṭhaś carṣaṇīsahān {ḍyumoṇṭa carṣanīsaham} | vetv ājyasya hotar yaja | {b} hotā yakṣat tanūnapātam | ūtibhir, jetāram aparājitam | indraṃ devaṃ suvarvidam | pathibhir madhumattamaiḥ | 0 narāśaṃsena tejasā |

{ 2.6.7.34} vetv ājyasya hotar yaja | {c} hotā yakṣad iḍābhir indram īḍitam | ājuhvānam amartyam | devo devaiḥ savīryaḥ | vajrahastaḥ puraṃdaraḥ | vetv ājyasya hotar yaja | {d} hotā yakṣad barhiṣi-indraṃ niṣadvaram | vṛṣabhaṃ naryāpasam | vasubhī rudrair ādityaiḥ | 0 sayugbhir barhir āsadat |

{ 2.6.7.35} vetv ājyasya hotar yaja | {e} hotā yakṣad ojo na vīryam | saho, dvāra indram avardhayan | suprāyaṇā viśrayantām ṛtāvṛdhaḥ | dvāra indrāya mīḍhuṣe | viyantv ājyasya hotar yaja | {f} hotā yakṣad uṣe indrasya dhenū | sudughe mātarau mahī | savātarau {,ānaṃdā. savātarau} na tejasī | 0 vatsam indram avardhatām |

{!} { 2.6.7.36} vītām ājyasya hotar yaja | {g} hotā yakṣad daivayā hotārā | bhiṣajā sakhāyā | haviṣā-indraṃ bhiṣajyataḥ, kavī devau pracetasau | indrāya dhatta indriyam | vītām ājyasya hotar yaja | {h} hotā yakṣat tisro devīḥ | trayas tridhātavo 'pasaḥ | iḍā sarasvatī bhāratī |

{!} { 2.6.7.37} mahī,-indrapatnīr haviṣmatīḥ | viyantv ājyasya hotar yaja | {j} hotā yakṣat tvaṣṭāram indraṃ devam | bhiṣajaṃ suyajaṃ ghṛtaśriyam | pururūpaṃ suretasaṃ maghonim | indrāya tvaṣṭā dadhad indriyāṇi | vetv ājyasya hotar yaja | {j} hotā yakṣad vanaspatim śamitāraṃ śatakratum | dhiyo joṣṭāram indriyam |

{!} { 2.6.7.38} madhvā samañjan pathibhiḥ sugebhiḥ | svadāti havyaṃ madhunā ghṛtena | vetv ājyasya hotar yaja | {k} hotā yakṣad indraṃ svāhā-ājyasya | svāhā medasaḥ | svāhā stokānām | svāhā svāhākṛtīnām | svāhā havyasūktīnām | svāhā devāṃ ājyapān | 0 svāhā,-indraṃ hotrāj juṣāṇāḥ | 1 indra-ājyasya {,ānaṃdā. indra ājyasya} viyantu | 2 hotar yaja |


anuvāka 8 { 2.6.8.39} {a} samiddha indra uṣasām anīke | purorucā pūrvakṛd vāvṛdhānaḥ | tribhir devais triṃśatā vajrabāhuḥ | jaghāna vṛtraṃ vi duro vavāra | {b} narāśaṃsaḥ prati śūro mimānaḥ | tanūnapāt prati yajñasya dhāma | gobhir vapāvān madhunā samañjan | hiraṇyaiś candrī yajati pracetāḥ | {c} īḍito devair harivāṃ abhiṣṭiḥ | 0 ājuhvāno haviṣā śardhamānaḥ |

{ 2.6.8.40} puraṃdaro maghavān vajrabāhuḥ | āyātu yajñam upa no juṣāṇaḥ | {d} juṣāṇo barhir harivān na indraḥ | prācīnaṃ sīdat pradiśā pṛthivyāḥ | uruvyacāḥ prathamānaṃ syonam | ādityair aktaṃ vasubhiḥ sajoṣāḥ | {e} indraṃ duraḥ kavaṣyo dhāvamānāḥ | vṛṣāṇaṃ yantu janayaḥ supatnīḥ | dvāro devīr abhito viśrayantām | 0 suvīrā vīraṃ prathamānā mahobhiḥ |

{ 2.6.8.41} {f} uṣāsānaktā bṛhatī bṛhantam | payasvatī sudughe śūram indram | peśasvatī tantunā saṃvyayantī | devānāṃ devaṃ yajataḥ surukme | {g} daivyā mimānā manasā purutrā | hotārāv indraṃ prathamā suvācā | mūrdhan yajñasya madhunā dadhānā | prācīnaṃ jyotir haviṣā vṛdhātaḥ | {h} tisro devīr haviṣā vardhamānāḥ | 0 indraṃ juṣāṇā vṛṣaṇaṃ na patnīḥ |

{ 2.6.8.42} acchinnaṃ tantuṃ payasā sarasvatī | iḍā devī bhāratī | viśvatūrtiḥ | {i} tvaṣṭā dadhad indrāya śuṣmam | apāko 'rciṣṭur yaśase purūṇi | vṛṣā yajan vṛṣaṇaṃ bhūriretāḥ | mūrdhan yajñasya samanaktu devān | {j} vanaspatir avasṛṣṭo na pāśaiḥ | tmanyā samañjan-śamitā na devaḥ | 0 indrasya havyair jaṭharaṃ pṛṇānaḥ | 1 svadāti havyaṃ madhunā ghṛtena | 2 {k} stokānām induṃ prati śūra indraḥ | 3 vṛṣāyamāṇo vṛṣabhas turāṣāṭ | 4 ghṛtapruṣā madhunā havyam undan | 5 mūrdhan yajñasya juṣatāṃ svāhā |


anuvāka 9 { 2.6.9.43} {a} ā carṣaṇiprā, {b} viveṣa yan mā | {c} taṃ sadhrīcīḥ | {d} satyam it tan na tvāvāṃ anyo asti | indra devo na martyo jyāyān | ahann ahiṃ pariśayānam arṇaḥ | avāsṛjo 'po acchā samudram | {e} prasasāhiṣe puruhūta śatrūn | jyeṣṭhas te śuṣma iha rātir astu | indra-ābhara dakṣiṇenā vasūni | 0 patiḥ sindhūnām asi revatīnām | 1 {f} sa śevṛdham adhidhā dyumnam asme | 2 mahi kṣatraṃ janāṣāḍ indra tavyam | 3 rakṣā ca no maghonaḥ pāhi sūrīn | 4 rāye ca naḥ svapatyā iṣe dhāḥ |

anuvāka 10 { 2.6.10.44} {a} devaṃ barhir indraṃ sudevaṃ devaiḥ | vīravatstīrṇaṃ vedyām avardhayat | vastor vṛtaṃ {ḍyumoṇṭa vṛktaṃ} pra-aktor bhṛtam | rāyā barhiṣmato 'tyagāt | vasuvane vasudheyasya vetu yaja | {b} devīr dvāra indraṃ saṃghāte | viḍvīr yāmann avardhayan | ā vatsena taruṇena kumāreṇa ca mīvitā apa-arvāṇam | reṇukakāṭaṃ nudantām | 0 vasuvane vasudheyasya viyantu yaja |

{ 2.6.10.45} {c} devī uṣāsānaktā | indraṃ yajñe prayaty ahvetām | daivīr viśaḥ prāyāsiṣṭām | suprīte sudhite abhūtām | vasuvane vasudheyasya vītāṃ yaja | {d} devī joṣṭrī vasudhitī | devam indram avardhatām | ayāvy anyā-aghā dveṣāṃsi | ā-anyā-avākṣīd vasu vāryāṇi | 0 yajamānāya śikṣite |

{ 2.6.10.46} vasuvane vasudheyasya vītāṃ yaja | {e} devī ūrjāhutī dughe sudughe | payasā-indram avardhatām | iṣamūrjam anyā-avākṣīt | sagdhiṃ sapītim anyā | navena pūrvaṃ dayamāne | purāṇena navam | adhātāmūrjamūrjāhutī vasu vāryāṇi | yajamānāya śikṣite | 0 vasuvane vasudheyasya vītāṃ yaja |

{ 2.6.10.47} {f} devā daivyā hotārā | devam indram avardhatām | hatāghaśaṃsāv ābhārṣṭāṃ vasu vāryāṇi | yajamānāya śikṣitau | vasuvane vasudheyasya vītāṃ yaja | {g} devīs tisras tisro devīḥ | patim indram avardhayan | aspṛkṣad bhāratī divam | rudrair yajñaṃ sarasvatī | 0 iḍā vasumatī gṛhān |

{ 2.6.10.48} vasuvane vasudheyasya viyantu yaja | {h} deva indro narāśaṃsaḥ | trivarūthas trivandhuraḥ | devam indram avardhayat | śatena śitipṛṣṭhānām āhitaḥ | sahasreṇa pravartate | mitrāvaruṇā-id asya hotram arhataḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ stotram | aśvinā-ādhvaryavam | 0 vasuvane vasudheyasya vetu yaja |

{ 2.6.10.49} {i} deva indro vanaspatiḥ | hiraṇyaparṇo madhuśākhaḥ supippalaḥ | devam indram avardhayat | divam agreṇa-āprāt | ā-antarikṣaṃ pṛthivīm adṛṃhīt | vasuvane {|} vasudheyasya vetu yaja | {j} devaṃ barhir vāritīnām | devam indram avardhayat | svāsastham indreṇa-āsannam | 0 anyā barhīṃṣy abhyabhūt | 1 vasuvane vasudheyasya vetu yaja | 2 {k} devo agniḥ sviṣṭakṛt | 3 devam indram avardhayat | 4 sviṣṭaṃ kurvant sviṣṭakṛt | 5 sviṣṭam adya karotu naḥ | 6 vasuvane vasudheyasya vetu yaja { jaya} |

anuvāka 11 { 2.6.11.50} {a} hotā yakṣat samidhā-agnim iḍas pade | aśvinā-indraṃ sarasvatīm | ajo dhūmo na godhūmaiḥ kvalair bheṣajam | madhu śaṣpair na teja indriyam | payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtaṃ madhu | viyantv ājyasya hotar yaja | {b} hotā yakṣat tanūnapāt-sarasvatī | avir meṣo na bheṣajam | pathā madhumatā-ābharan | 0 aśvinā-indrāya vīryam |

{ 2.6.11.51} badarair upavākābhir bheṣajaṃ tokmabhiḥ | payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtaṃ madhu | viyantv ājyasya hotar yaja | {c} hotā yakṣan narāśaṃsaṃ na nagnahum | patiṃ surāyai bheṣajam | meṣaḥ sarasvatī bhiṣak | ratho na candry aśvinor vapā indrasya vīryam | badarair upavākābhir bheṣajaṃ tokmabhiḥ | payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtaṃ madhu | 0 viyantv ājyasya hotar yaja |

{ 2.6.11.52} {d} hotā yakṣad iḍā-iḍita ājuhvānaḥ sarasvatīm | indraṃ balena vardhayan | ṛsabheṇa gavā-indriyam | aśvinā-indrāya vīryam | yavaiḥ karkandhubhiḥ | madhu lājair na māsaram | payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtaṃ madhu | viyantv ājyasya hotar yaja | {e} hotā yakṣad barhiḥ, suṣṭarīmo 'rṇamradāḥ | 0 bhiṣaṅ, nāsatyā |

{ 2.6.11.53} bhiṣajā-aśvinā,-aśvā śiśumatī | bhiṣag, dhenuḥ sarasvatī | bhiṣag, duha indrāya bheṣajam | payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtaṃ madhu | viyantv ājyasya hotar yaja | {f} hotā yakṣad duro diśaḥ | kavaṣyo na vyacasvatīḥ | aśvibhyāṃ na duro diśaḥ | indro na rodasī dughe | 0 duhe kāmānt sarasvatī |

{ 2.6.11.54} aśvinā-indrāya bheṣajam | śukraṃ na jyotir indriyam | payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtaṃ madhu | viyantv ājyasya hotar yaja | {g} hotā yakṣat supeśasoṣe naktaṃ divā | aśvinā saṃjānāne | samañjāte sarasvatyā | tviṣim indre na bheṣajam | śyeno na rajasā hṛdā | 0 payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtaṃ madhu |

{ 2.6.11.55} viyantv ājyasya hotar yaja | {h} hotā yakṣad daivyā hotārā bhiṣajā-aśvinā | indraṃ na jāgṛvī divā naktaṃ na bheṣajaiḥ | śūṣaṃ sarasvatī bhiṣak | sīsena duha indriyam | payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtaṃ madhu | viyantv ājyasya hotar yaja | {i} hotā yakṣat tisro devīrná bheṣajam | trayas tridhātavo 'pasaḥ | 0 rūpam indre hiraṇyayam |

{ 2.6.11.56} aśvinā-iḍā na bhāratī | vācā sarasvatī | maha, indrāya dadhur indriyam | payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtaṃ madhu | viyantv ājyasya hotar yaja | {j} hotā yakṣat tvaṣṭāram indram aśvinā | bhīṣajaṃ na sarasvatīm | ojo na jūtir indriyam | vṛko na rabhaso bhiṣak | 0 yaśaḥ surayā bheṣajam |

{ 2.6.11.57} śriyā na māsaram | payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtaṃ madhu | viyantv ājyasya hotar yaja | {k} hotā yakṣad vanaspatim | śamitāraṃ śatakratum | bhīmaṃ na manyuṃ rājānaṃ vyāghraṃ namasośvinā, bhāmam {|} sarasvatī bhiṣak | indrāya duha indriyam | payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtaṃ madhu viyantv ājyasya hotar yaja |

{ 2.6.11.58} {l} hotā yakṣad agniṃ svāhā-ājyasya stokānām | svāhā medasāṃ pṛthak | svāhā chāgam aśvibhyām | svāhā meṣaṃ sarasvatyai | svāhā-ṛṣabham indrāya siṃhāya sahasā-indriyam | svāhā-agniṃ na bheṣajam | svāhā somam indriyam | svāhā-indraṃ sutrāmānaṃ savitāraṃ varuṇaṃ bhiṣajāṃ patim | svāhā vanaspatiṃ priyaṃ pātho na bheṣajam | 0 svahā devāṃ ājyapān |

{ 2.6.11.59} svāhā-agniṃ hotrāj juṣāṇo agnir bheṣajam | payaḥ somaḥ parisrutā ghṛtaṃ madhu | viyantv ājyasya hotar yaja | {m} hotā yakṣad aśvinā sarasvatīm indraṃ sutrāmāṇam | ime somāḥ surāmāṇaḥ | chāgair na meṣair ṛṣabhaiḥ sutāḥ | śaṣpair na tokmabhiḥ | lājair mahasvantaḥ | madā māsareṇa pariṣkṛtāḥ | 0 śukrāḥ payasvanto 'mṛtāḥ | 1 prasthitā vo madhuścutaḥ tān aśvinā sarasvatī-indraḥ sutrāmā vṛtrahā | 2 juṣantāṃ saumyaṃ madhu | 3 pibantu madantu viyantu somam | 4 hotar yaja |

anuvāka 12 { 2.6.12.60} {a} samiddho agnir aśvinā | tapto gharmo virāṭ sutaḥ | duhe dhenuḥ sarasvatī | somaṃ śukram iha-indriyam | {b} tanūpā bhiṣajā sute | aśvinā-ubhā sarasvatī | madhvā rajāṃsi-indriyam | indrāya pathibhir vahān | indrāya-induṃ sarasvatī | 0 narāśaṃsena nagnahuḥ {ḍyumoṇṭa nagnahum} |

{ 2.6.12.61} adhātām aśvinā madhu | bheṣajaṃ bhiṣajā sute | {d} ājuhvānā sarasvatī | indrāya-indriyāṇi vīryam | iḍābhir aśvināv {ḍyumoṇṭa aśvināv} iṣam | samūrjaṃ saṃ rayiṃ dadhuḥ | {e} aśvinā namuceḥ sutam | somaṃ śukraṃ parisrutā | sarasvatī tam ābharat | 0 barhiṣā-indrāya pātave |

{ 2.6.12.62} {f} kavaṣyo na vyacasvatīḥ | aśvibhyāṃ na duro diśaḥ | indro na rodasī dughe | duhe kāmānt sarasvatī | {g} uṣāsā naktam aśvinā | divā-indraṃ sāyam indriyaiḥ | saṃjānāne supeśasā | samañjāte sarasvatyā {h} pātaṃ no aśvinā divā | pāhi naktaṃ sarasvati |

{ 2.6.12.63} daivyā hotārā bhiṣajā | pātam indraṃ sacā sute | {i} tisras tredhā sarasvatī | aśvinā bhārati-iḍā | tīvraṃ parisrutā somam | indrāya suṣavur madam | {j} aśvinā {ḍyumoṇṭa aśvinā} bheṣajaṃ madhu | bheṣajaṃ naḥ sarasvatī | indre tvaṣṭā yaśaḥ śriyam | 0 rūpaṃ-rūpam adhuḥ sute | 1 {k} ṛtuthā-indro vanaspatiḥ | 2 śaśamānaḥ parisrutā | 3 kīlālam aśvibhyāṃ madhu | 4 duhe dhenuḥ sarasvatī | 5 {l} gobhir na somam aśvinā {ḍyumoṇṭa aśvinā} | 6 māsareṇa pariṣkṛtā {ḍyumoṇṭa parisrutā} | 7 samadhātāṃ sarasvatyā | 8 svāhā-indre sutaṃ madhu |

anuvāka 13 { 2.6.13.64} {a} aśvinā havir indriyam | namucer dhiyā sarasvatī | ā śukram āsurād vasu | magham indrāya jabhrire | {b} yam aśvinā sarasvatī | haviṣā-indram avardhayan | sa bibheda balaṃ magham {ḍyumoṇṭa suggests makham} | namucāv āsure sacā | {c} tam indraṃ paśavaḥ sacā | 0 aśvinā-ubhā sarasvatī

{ 2.6.13.65} dadhānā abhyanūṣata | haviṣā yajñam indriyam | {d} ya indra indriyaṃ dadhuḥ | savitā varuṇo bhagaḥ | sa sutrāmā haviṣpatiḥ | yajamānāya saścata | {e} savitā varuṇo dadhat | yajamānāya dāśuṣe | ādatta namucer vasu | 0 sutrāmā balam indriyam |

{ 2.6.13.66} {f} varuṇaḥ kṣatram indriyam | bhagena savitā śriyam | sutrāmā yaśasā balam | dadhānā yajñam āśata {ānaṃdā. āsata} | aśvinā {ḍyumoṇṭa aśvinā} gobhir indriyam | aśvebhir vīryaṃ balam | haviṣā-indraṃ sarasvatī | yajamānam avardhayan | {h} tā nāsatyā supeśasā | 0 hiraṇyavartanī narā | 1 sarasvatī haviṣmatī | 2 indra karmasu no 'vata | 3 tā bhiṣajā sukarmaṇā | 4 sā sudughā sarasvatī | 5 sa vṛtrahā śatakratuḥ | 6 indrāya dadhur indriyam

anuvāka 14 { 2.6.14.67} {a} devaṃ barhiḥ sarasvatī | sudevam indre aśvinā | tejo na cakṣur akṣyoḥ | barhiṣā dadhur indriyam | vasuvane vasudheyasya viyantu yaja | {b} devīr dvāro aśvinā | bhiṣajā-indre sarasvatī | prāṇaṃ na vīryaṃ nasi | dvāro dadhur indriyam | 0 vasuvane vasudheyasya viyantu yaja

{ 2.6.14.68} {c} devī uṣāsāv aśvinā | bhiṣajā-indre sarasvatī | balaṃ na vācam āsye | uṣābhyāṃ dadhur indriyam | vasumane vasudheyasya viyantu yaja | {d} devī joṣṭrī aśvinā sutrāmā-indre sarasvatī | śrotraṃ na karṇayor yaśaḥ | joṣṭrībhyāṃ dadhur indriyam | vasuvane vasudheyasya viyantu yaja |

{ 2.6.14.69} {e} devī ūrjāhutī dughe sudughe | payasā-indraṃ sarasvaty aśvinā bhiṣajā-avata | śukraṃ na jyotiḥ stanayor āhutī dhatta indriyam | vasuvane vasudheyasya viyantu yaja | {f} devā devānāṃ bhiṣajā | hotārāv indram aśvinā | vaṣaṭkāraiḥ sarasvatī | tviṣiṃ na hṛdaye matim | hotṛbhyāṃ dadhur indriyam | 0 vasuvane vasudheyasya viyantu yaja |

{ 2.6.14.70} {g} devīs tisras tisro devīḥ | sarasvaty aśvinā bhāratī-iḍā | śūṣaṃ na madhye nābhyām | indrāya dadhur indriyam | vasuvane vasudheyasya viyantu yaja | {h} deva indro narāśaṃsaḥ | trivarūthaḥ sarasvatyā-aśvibhyām īyate rathaḥ | reto na rūpam amṛtaṃ janitram | indrāya tvaṣṭā dadhad inriyāṇi vasuvane vasudheyasya viyantu yaja |

{ 2.6.14.71} {i} deva indro vanaspatiḥ | hiraṇyaparṇo aśvibhyām | sarasvatyāḥ {ḍyumoṇṭa sarasvatyā} supippalaḥ | indrāya pacyate madhu | ojo na jūtim ṛṣabho na bhāmam | vanasapatir no dadhad indriyāṇi | vasuvane vasudheyasya viyantu yaja | {j} devaṃ barhir vāritīnām | adhvare stīrṇam aśvibhyām | 0 ūrṇamradāḥ sarasvatyāḥ |

{ 2.6.14.72} syonam indra te sadaḥ | īśāyai manyuṃ rājānaṃ barhiṣā dadhur indriyam | vasuvane vasudheyasya viyantu yaja | {k} devo agniḥ sviṣṭakṛt | devān yakṣad yathā-yatham | hotārāv indram aśvinā | vācā vācaṃ sarasvatīm | agniṃ somaṃ sviṣṭakṛt | sviṣṭa indraḥ sutrāmā savitā varuṇo bhiṣak | 0 iṣṭo devo vanasapatiḥ | 1 sviṣṭā devā ājyapāḥ | 2 iṣṭo agniragninā | 3 hotā hotre sviṣṭakṛt | 4 yaśo na dadhad indriyam | 5 ūrjam apacitiṃ svadhām | 6 vasuvane vasudheyasya viyantu yaja |

prapāṭhaka: 7 anuvāka 1 { 2.7.1.1} trivṛt stomo bhavati | brahmavarcasaṃ vai trivṛt | brahmavarcasam eva-avarundhe | agniṣṭomaḥ somo bhavati | brahmavarcasaṃ vā agniṣṭomaḥ | brahmavarcasam eva-avarundhe | rathaṃtaraṃ sāma bhavati | brahmavarcasaṃ vai rathaṃtaram | brahmavarcasam eva-avarundhe | 0 parisrajī hotā bhavati |

{ 2.7.1.2} aruṇo mirmiras triśukraḥ | etad vai brahmavarcasasya rūpam | rūpeṇaiva brahmavarcasam avarundhe | bṛhaspatir akāmayata devānāṃ purodhāṃ gaccheyam iti | sa etaṃ bṛhaspatisavam apaśyat | tam āharat | tena-ayajata | tato vai sa devānāṃ purodhām agacchat | yaḥ purodhākāmaḥ syāt | 0 sa bṛhaspatisavena yajeta |

{ 2.7.1.3} purodhām eva gacchati | tasya prātaḥsavane sanneṣu nārāśaṃseṣu | ekādaśa dakṣiṇā nīyante | ekādaśa mādhyaṃdine savane sanneṣu nārāśaṃseṣu | ekādaśa tṛtīyasavane sanneṣu nārāśaṃseṣu | trayastriṃśat saṃpadyante | trayastriṃśad vai devatāḥ | devatā eva-avarundhe | aśvaś catustriṃśaḥ | 0 prājāpatyo vā aśvaḥ |

{ 2.7.1.4} prajāpatiś catustriṃśo devatānāṃ | yāvatīr eva devatāḥ | tā eva-avarundhe | kṛṣṇājine 'bhiṣiñcati | brahmaṇo vā etad rūpam | yat kṛṣṇājinam | brahmavarcasena-evainaṃ samardhayati | ājyena-abhiṣiñcati | tejo vā ājyam | 0 teja eva-asmin dadhāti |

anuvāka 2 { 2.7.2.5} yad āgneyo bhavati | agnimukhā hy {BI agnimukhād dhy} ṛddhiḥ | atha yat pauṣṇaḥ | puṣṭir vai pūṣā | puṣṭir vaiśyasya | puṣṭim eva-avarundhe | prasavāya sāvitraḥ | atha yat tvāṣṭraḥ | tvaṣṭā hi rūpāṇi vikaroti | 0 nirvaruṇatvāya vāruṇaḥ |

{ 2.7.2.6} atho ya eva kaś ca santsūyate | sa hi vāruṇaḥ | atha yad vaiśvadevaḥ | vaiśvadevo hi vaiśyaḥ | atha yan mārutaḥ | māruto hi vaiśyaḥ | sapta-etāni havīṃṣi bhavanti | saptagaṇā vai marutaḥ | pṛśniḥ paṣṭhauhī mārutyā-ālabhyate | 0 viḍ vai marutaḥ | 1 viśa evaitan madhyato 'bhiṣicyate | 2 tasmād vā eṣa viśaḥ priyaḥ | 3 viśo hi madhyato 'bhiṣicyate | 4 ṛṣabhacarme 'dhyabhiṣiñcati | 5 sa hi prajanayitā | 6 dadhnā-abhiṣiñcati | 7 ūrg vā annādyaṃ dadhi | 8 ūrjā-evainam annādyena samardhayati |

anuvāka 3 { 2.7.3.7} yad āgneyo bhavati | āgneyo vai brāhmaṇaḥ | atha yat saumyaḥ | saumyo hi brāhmaṇaḥ | prasavāya-eva sāvitraḥ | atha yad bārhaspatyaḥ | etad vai brāhmaṇasya vākpatīyam | atha yad agnīṣomīyaḥ | āgneyo vai brāhmaṇaḥ | 0 tau yadā saṃgacchete |

{ 2.7.3.8} atha vīryāvattaro bhavati | atha yat sārasvataḥ | etad dhi pratyakṣaṃ brāhmaṇasya vākpatīyam | nirvaruṇatvāya-eva vāruṇaḥ | atho ya eva kaś ca santsūyate | sa hi vāruṇaḥ | atha yad dyāvāpṛthivyaḥ | indro vṛtrāya vajram udayacchat | taṃ dyāvāpṛthivī na-anvamanyetām | 0 tam etenaiva bhāgadheyena-anvamanyetām |

{ 2.7.3.9} vajrasya { vajrasya} vā eṣo 'numānāya | anumatavajraḥ sūyātā iti | aṣṭāv etāni havīṃṣi bhavanti | aṣṭa-akṣarā gāyatrī | gāyatrī brahmavarcasam | gāyatriyā-eva brahmavarcasam avarundhe | hiraṇyena ghṛtam utpunāti | tejasa eva ruce | kṛṣṇājine 'bhiṣiñcati | 0 brahmaṇo vā etad ṛksāmayo rūpam | 1 yat kṛṣṇājinam | 2 brahmann evainam ṛksāmayor adhy abhiṣiñcati | 3 ghṛtena-abhiṣiñcati | 4 tathā vīryāvattaro { vīryavattaro} bhavati |

anuvāka 4 { 2.7.4.10} na vai somena somasya savo 'sti | hato hy eṣaḥ | abhiṣuto hy eṣaḥ | na hi hataḥ sūyate | saumīṃ sūtavaśām ālabhate | somo vai retodhāḥ | reta eva tad dadhāti | saumyā-ṛcā-abhiṣiñcati | retodhā hy eṣā | 0 retaḥ somaḥ | 1 reta evāsmin dadhāti | 2 yat kiṃ ca rājasūyam ṛte somam | 3 tat sarvaṃ bhavati | 4 aṣāḍhaṃ yutsu pṛtanāsu paprim | 5 suvarṣām apsvāṃ vṛjanasya gopām | 6 bhareṣujāṃ sukṣitiṃ suśravasam | 7 jayantaṃ tvām anu madema soma |

anuvāka 5 { 2.7.5.11} yo vai somena sūyate | sa devasavaḥ | yaḥ paśunā sūyate | sa devasavaḥ | ya iṣṭyā sūyate | sa manuṣyasavaḥ | etaṃ vai pṛthaye devāḥ prāyacchan | tato vai so 'py āraṇyānāṃ paśūnām asūyata | yāvatīḥ kiyatīś ca prajā vācaṃ vadanti | 0 tāsāṃ sarvāsāṃ sūyate |

{ 2.7.5.12} ya etena yajate | ya u cainam evaṃ veda | nārāśaṃsyā-ṛcā-abhiṣiñcati | manuṣyā vai narāśaṃsaḥ | nihnútya vāva-etat | atha-abhiṣiñcati | yat kiṃ ca rājasūyam anuttaravedīkam | tat sarvaṃ bhavati | ye me pañcāśataṃ daduḥ | 0 aśvānāṃ sadhastutiḥ | 1 dyumad agne mahi śravaḥ | 2 bṛhat kṛdhi maghonām | 3 nṛvad amṛta nṛṇām |

anuvāka 6 { 2.7.6.13} eṣa gosavaḥ | ṣaṭtriṃśa ukthyo bṛhatsāmā | pavamāne kaṇvarathaṃtaraṃ bhavati | yo vai vājapeyaḥ | sa samrāṭtsavaḥ | yo rājasūyaḥ | sa varuṇasavaḥ | prajāpatiḥ svārājyaṃ parameṣṭhī | svārājyaṃ gaur eva | 0 gaur iva bhavati |

{ 2.7.6.14} ya etena yajate | ya u cainam evaṃ veda | ubhe bṛhadrathaṃtare bhavataḥ | tad dhi svārājyam | ayutaṃ dakṣiṇāḥ | tad dhi svārājyam | pratidhuṣā-abhiṣiñcati | tad dhi svārājyam | anuddhate vedyai dakṣiṇata āhavanīyasya bṛhatastotraṃ praty abhiṣiñcati | iyaṃ vāva rathaṃtaram |

{ 2.7.6.15} asau bṛhat | anayor evainam anantarhitam abhiṣiñcati | paśustomo vā eṣaḥ | tena gosavaḥ | ṣaṭtriṃśaḥ sarvaḥ | revaj jātaḥ sahasā vṛddhaḥ | kṣatrāṇāṃ kṣatrabhṛttamo vayodhāḥ | mahān mahitve tastabhānaḥ | kṣatre rāṣṭre ca jāgṛhi | prajāpates tvā parameṣṭhinaḥ svārājyenā-abhiṣiñcāmi-ity āha | svārājyam evainaṃ gamayati |

anuvāka 7 { 2.7.7.1} siṃhe vyāghra uta yā pṛdākau | tviṣir agnau brāhmaṇe sūrye yā | indraṃ yā devī subhagā jajāna | sā na āgan varcasā saṃvidānā | yā rājanye dundubhāv āyatāyām | aśvasya krandye puruṣasya māyau | indraṃ yā devī subhagā jajāna | sā na āgan varcasā saṃvidānā | yā hastini dvipini yā hiraṇye | tviṣir aśveṣu puruṣeṣu goṣu |

{ 2.7.7.2} indraṃ yā devī subhagā jajāna | sā na āgan varcasā saṃvidānā | rathe akṣeṣu vṛṣabhasya vāje | vāte parjanye varuṇasya śuṣme | indraṃ yā devī subhagā jajāna | sā na āgan varcasā saṃvidānā | rāḍ asi virāḍ asi | samrāḍ asi svarāḍ asi | indrāya tvā tejasvate tejasvantaṃ śrīṇāmi | indrāya tvā-ojasvata ojasavantaṃ śrīṇāmi |

{ 2.7.7.3} indrāya tvā payasvate payasvantaṃ śrīṇāmi | indrāya tvā-āyuṣmata āyuṣmantaṃ śrīṇāmi | tejo 'si | tat te prayacchāmi | tejasvad astu me mukham | tejasvat-śiro astu me | tejasvān viśvataḥ pratyaṅ | tejasā saṃpipṛgdhi mā | ojo 'si | tat te prayacchāmi |

{ 2.7.7.4} ojasvad astu me mukham | ojasvat-śiro astu me | ojasvān viśvataḥ pratyaṅ | ojasā saṃpipṛgdhi mā | payo 'si | tat te prayacchāmi | payasvad astu me mukham | payasvat-śiro astu me | payasvān viśvataḥ pratyaṅ | payasā saṃpipṛgdhi mā |

{ 2.7.7.5} āyur asi | tat te prayacchāmi | āyuṣmad astu me mukham | āyuṣmat-śiro astu me āyuṣmān viśvataḥ pratyaṅ | āyuṣā saṃpipṛgdhi mā | imam agna āyuṣe varcase kṛdhi | priyaṃ reto varuṇa soma rājan | māteva-asmā adite śarma yaccha | viśve devā jaradaṣṭir yathā sat |

{ 2.7.7.6} āyur asi viśvāyur asi | sarvāyur asi sarvam āyur asi | yato vāto manojavāḥ | yataḥ kṣaranti sindhavaḥ | tāsāṃ tvā sarvāsāṃ rucā | abhiṣiñcāmi varcasā | samudra iva-asi gahmanā | soma iva-asy adābhyaḥ | agnir iva viśvataḥ pratyaṅ | sūrya iva jyotiṣā vibhūḥ |

{ 2.7.7.7} apāṃ yo dravaṇe rasaḥ | tam aham asmā āmuṣyāyaṇāya | tejase brahmavarcasāya gṛhṇāmi | apāṃ ya ūrmau rasaḥ | tam aham asmā āmuṣyāyaṇāya | ojase vīryāya gṛhṇāmi | apāṃ yo madhyato rasaḥ | tam aham asmā āmuṣyāyaṇāya | puṣṭyai prajananāya gṛhṇāmi | apāṃ yo yajñiyo rasaḥ | tam aham asmā āmuṣyāyaṇāya | āyuṣe dīrghāyutvāya gṛhṇāmi |

anuvāka 8 { 2.7.8.1} abhiprehi vīrayasva | ugraś cettā sapatnahā | ātiṣṭha mitravardhanaḥ | tubhyaṃ devā adhibruvan | aṅkau nyaṅkāv abhita ātiṣṭha vṛtrahan ratham | ātiṣṭhantaṃ pari viśve abhūṣan | śriyaṃ vasānaś carati svarocāḥ | mahat tad asya-asurasya nāma | ā viśvarūpo amṛtāni tasthau | anu tvā-indro madatv anu bṛhaspatiḥ |

{ 2.7.8.2} anu somo anv agnir āvīt | anu tvā viśve devā avantu | anu sapta rājāno ya uta-abhiṣiktāḥ | anu tvā mitrāvaruṇāv iha-avatam | anu dyāvāpṛthivī viśvaśaṃbhū | sūryo ahobhir anu tvā-avatu | candramā nakṣatrair anu tvā-avatu | dyauś ca tvā pṛthivī ca pracetasā | śukro bṛhad dakṣinā tvā pipartu | anu svadhā cikitāṃ somo agniḥ | ā-ayaṃ pṛṇaktu rajasī {ānaṃdā. rajasi} upastham |

anuvāka 9 { 2.7.9.1} prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata | tā asmāt sṛṣṭāḥ parācīr āyan | sa etaṃ prajāpatir odanam apaśyat | so 'nnaṃ bhūto 'tiṣṭhat | tā anyatra-annādyam avittvā | prajāpatiṃ prajā upāvartanta | annam evainaṃ bhūtaṃ paśyantīḥ prajā upāvartante | ya etena yajate | ya u cainam evaṃ veda | sarvāṇy annāni bhavanti |

{ 2.7.9.2} sarve puruṣāḥ | sarvāṇy eva-annāny avarundhe | sarvān puruṣān | rāḍ asi virāḍ asi-ity āha | svārājyam evainaṃ gamayati | yad dhiraṇyaṃ dadāti | tejas tena-avarundhe | yat tisṛdhanvam | vīryaṃ tena | yad aṣṭrām |

{ 2.7.9.3} puṣṭiṃ tena | yat kamaṇḍalum | āyuṣṭena | yad dhiraṇyam ābadhnāti | jyotir vai hiraṇyam | jyotir evāsmin dadhāti | atho tejo vai hiraṇyam | teja evā''tman dhatte | yad odanaṃ prāśnāti | etad eva sarvam avarudhya |

{ 2.7.9.4} tad asminn ekadhā-adhāt | rohiṇyāṃ kāryaḥ | yad brāhmaṇa eva rohiṇī | tasmād eva | atho varṣma-evainaṃ samānānāṃ karoti | udyatā sūryeṇa kāryaḥ | udyantaṃ vā etaṃ sarvāḥ prajāḥ pratinandanti | didṛkṣeṇyo darśanīyo bhavati | {not in } ya evaṃ veda | brahmavādino vadanti |

{ 2.7.9.5} avetyo 'vabhṛthā3 nā3 iti | yad darbhapuñjīlaiḥ pavayati | tat svid eva-avaiti | tan na-avaiti | tribhiḥ pavayati | traya ime lokāḥ | ebhir evainaṃ lokaiḥ pavayati | atho apāṃ vā etat tejo varcaḥ | yad darbhāḥ | yad darbhapuñjīlaiḥ pavayati | apām evainaṃ tejasā varcasobhiṣiñcati |

anuvāka 10 { 2.7.10.1} prajāpatir akāmayata bahor bhūyānt syām iti | sa etaṃ pañcaśāradīyam apaśyat | tam āharat | tena-ayajata | tato vai sa bahor bhūyān abhavat | yaḥ kāmayeta bahor bhūyānt syām iti | sa pañcaśāradīyena yajeta | bahor eva bhūyān bhavati | marutstomo vā eṣaḥ | maruto hi devānāṃ bhūyiṣṭhāḥ |

{ 2.7.10.2} bahur bhavati | ya etena yajate | ya u cainamevaṃ veda | pañcaśāradīyo bhavati | pañca vā ṛtavaḥ saṃvatsaraḥ | ṛtuṣv eva saṃvatsare pratitiṣṭhati | atho pañca.akṣarā paṅktiḥ | pāṅkto yajñaḥ | yajñam eva-avarundhe | saptadaśaṃ stomā na-atiyanti | saptadaśaḥ prajāpatiḥ | prajāpater āptyai |

anuvāka 11 { 2.7.11.11} agastyo marudbhya ukṣṇaḥ praukṣat | tān indra ādatta | ta enaṃ vajram udyatya-abhyāyanta | tān agastyaś caiva-indraśca kayāśubhīyena-aśamayatām | tāṇ-śāntān upāhvayata | yat kayāśubhīyaṃ bhavati śāntyai | tasmād eta aindrā mārutā ukṣāṇaḥ savanīyā bhavanti | trayaḥ prathame 'hann ālabhyante | evaṃ dvitīye | evaṃ tṛtīye |

{ 2.7.11.2} evaṃ caturthe | pañca-uttame 'hann ālabhyante | varṣiṣṭham iva hy etad ahaḥ | varṣiṣṭhaḥ samānānāṃ bhavati | ya etena yajate | ya u cainam evaṃ veda | svārājyaṃ vā eṣa yajñaḥ | etena vā ekayāvā kāndamaḥ svārājyam agacchat | svārājyaṃ gacchati | ya etena yajate |

{ 2.7.11.3} ya u cainam evaṃ veda | māruto vā eṣa stomaḥ | etena vai maruto devānāṃ bhūyiṣṭhā abhavan | bhūyiṣṭhaḥ samānānāṃ bhavati | ya etena yajate | ya u cainam evaṃ veda | pañcaśāradīyo vā eṣa yajñaḥ | ā pañcamāt puruṣād annam atti | ya etena yajate | ya u cainam evaṃ veda | saptadaśaṃ stomā na-atiyanti | saptadaśaḥ prajāpatiḥ | prajāpater eva na-eti |

anuvāka 12 { 2.7.12.1} asya-ajarāso damā maritrāḥ | arcaddhūmāso agnayaḥ pāvakāḥ | śvicīcayaḥ śvātrāso bhuraṇyavaḥ | vanarṣado vāyavo na somāḥ | yajā no mitrāvaruṇā | yajā devāṃ ṛtaṃ bṛhat | agne yakṣi svaṃ damam | aśvinā pibataṃ sutam | dīdyagnī śucivratā | ṛtunā yajñavāhasā |

{ 2.7.12.2} dve virūpe carataḥ { virūpe caratas} svarthe | anya-anyā vatsam upadhāpayete | harir anyasyāṃ bhavati svadhāvān | śukro anyasyāṃ dadṛśe suvarcāḥ | pūrvāparaṃ carato māyayā-etau | śiśū krīḍantau pariyāto adhvaram | viśvāny anyo bhuvanā-abhicaṣṭe | ṛtūn anyo vidadhaj jāyate punaḥ | trīṇi śatā trī- sahasrāṇy agnim | triṃśat- ca devā nava ca-asaparyan |

{ 2.7.12.3} aukṣan ghṛtair āstṛṇan barhir asmai | ād id hotāraṃ nyaṣādayanta | agninā-agniḥ samidhyate | kavir gṛhapatir yuvā | havyavāḍ juhvāsyaḥ | agnir devānāṃ jaṭharam | pūtadakṣaḥ kavikratuḥ | devo devebhir āgamat | agniśriyo maruto viśvakṛṣṭayaḥ | 0 ā tveṣam ugram ava īmahe vayam |

{ 2.7.12.4} te svāmino rudriyā varṣanirṇijaḥ | siṃhā na heṣakratavaḥ sudānavaḥ | yad uttame maruto madhyame vā | yad vā-avame subhagāso diviṣṭha { diviṣṭha} {?} | tato no rudrā uta vā-anvasya | agne vittād dhaviṣo yad yajāmaḥ | īḍe agniṃ svavasaṃ namobhiḥ | iha prasapto vicayat kṛtaṃ naḥ | rathair iva prabhare vājapadbhiḥ { vājapadbhiḥ} | pradakṣiṇin marutāṃ stomam ṛdhyām |

{ 2.7.12.5} śrudhi { śrudhi {?}} śrutkarṇa vahnibhiḥ | devair agne sayāvabhiḥ | āsīdantu barhiṣi | mitro varuṇo aryamā | prātaryāvāṇo adhvaram | viśveṣām aditir yajñiyānām | viśveṣām { viśveṣām} atithir mānuṣāṇām | agnir devānām ava āvṛṇānaḥ | sumṛḍīko bhavatu viśvavedāḥ | tve agne sumatiṃ bhikṣamānāḥ |

{ 2.7.12.6} divi śravo dadhire yajñiyāsaḥ | naktā ca cakrur uṣasā virūpe | kṛṣṇaṃ { kṛṣṇaṃ} ca varṇam aruṇaṃ ca saṃdhuḥ | tvām agna ādityāsa āsyam | tvāṃ jihvāṃ śucayaś cakrire kave | tvāṃ rātiṣāco adhvareṣu saścire | tve devā havir adanty āhutam | ni tvā yajñasya sādhanam | agne hotāram ṛtvijam | vanuṣvad deva dhīmahi pracetasam | jīraṃ dūtam amartyam |

anuvāka 13 { 2.7.13.1} tiṣṭhā harī ratha ā yujyamānā yāhi | vāyur na niyuto no accha | pibāsy andho abhisṛṣṭo asme | indra svāhā rarimā te madāya | kasya vṛṣā sute sacā | niyutvān vṛṣabho raṇat | vṛtrahā somapītaye | indraṃ vayaṃ mahādhane | indram arbhai havāmahe | yujaṃ vṛtreṣu vajriṇam |

{ 2.7.13.2} dvitā yo vṛtrahantamaḥ | vida indraḥ śatakratuḥ | upa no haribhiḥ sutam | sa sūra ā janayañ jyotir indram | ayā dhiyā taraṇir adribarhāḥ | ṛtena śuṣmīn avamāno arkaiḥ | vyusridho asro adrir bibheda | uta tyad āśvaśviyam | yad indra nāhuṣīṣv ā | agre vikṣu pratīdayat |

{ 2.7.13.3} bhareṣv indraṃ suhavaṃ havāmahe | aṃhomucaṃ sukṛtaṃ daivyaṃ janam | agniṃ mitraṃ varuṇaṃ sātaye bhagam | dyāvāpṛthivī marutaḥ svastaye | mahi kṣetraṃ puruś candraṃ vividvān | ādit sakhibhyaś ca rathaṃ samairat | indro nṛbhir ajanad dīdyānaḥ sākam | sūryam uṣasaṃ gātum agnim | uruṃ no lokam anuneṣi vidvān | suvarvaj jyotir abhayaṃ svasti |

{ 2.7.13.4} ṛṣvā ta indra sthavirasya bāhū | upastheyāma śaraṇā bṛhantā | ā no viśvābhir ūtibhiḥ sajoṣāḥ | brahma juṣāṇo haryaśva yāhi | varīvṛjat sthavirebhiḥ suśipra | asme dadhad vṛṣaṇaṃ śuṣmam indra | indrāya gāva āśiram | duduhre vajriṇe madhu | yat sīm upahvare 'vidat | tās te vajrin dhenavo jojayur naḥ |

{ 2.7.13.5} gabhastayo niyuto viśvavārāḥ | ahar-ahar bhūya ij joguvānāḥ | pūrṇā indra kṣumato bhojanasya | imāṃ te dhiyaṃ prabhare maho mahīm | asya stotre dhiṣaṇā yat ta ānaje | tam utsave ca prasave ca sāsahim | indraṃ devāsaḥ śavasā madann anu |

anuvāka 14 { 2.7.14.1} prajāpatiḥ paśūn asṛjata | te 'smāt sṛṣṭāḥ parāñca āyan | tān agniṣṭomena na-āpnot | tān ukthyena na-āpnot | tān ṣoḍaśinā na-āpnot | tān rātriyā na-āpnot | tānt saṃdhinā na-āpnot | so 'gnim abravīt | imān ma īpsa-iti | tān agnis trivṛtā stomena na-āpnot |

{ 2.7.14.2} sa indram abravīt | imān ma īpsa-iti {ānaṃdā. īpsateti} | tān indraḥ pañcadaśena stomena na-āpnot | sa viśvān devān abravīt | imān ma īpsata-iti | tān viśve devāḥ saptadaśena stomena na-āpnuvan | sa viṣṇum abravīt | imān ma īpsa-iti | tān viṣṇur ekaviṃśena stomena-āpnot | vāravantīyena-avārayata |

{ 2.7.14.3} idaṃ viṣṇur vicakrama iti vyakramata | yasmāt paśavaḥ pra pra-iva bhraṃśeran | sa etena yajeta | yad āpnot | tad aptoryāmasya-āptoryāmatvam | etena vai devā jaitvāni jitvā | yaṃ kāmam akāmayanta tam āpnuvan | yaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayate | tam etena-āpnoti |

anuvāka 15 { 2.7.15.1} vyāghrò 'yam agnau carati praviṣṭaḥ | ṛṣīṇāṃ putro abhiśastipā ayam | namaskāreṇa | namasā te juhomi | mā devānāṃ mithuyā karma {?} bhāgam | sāvīrhi deva prasavāya pitre | varṣmāṇam asmai varimāṇam asmai | atha-asmabhyaṃ savitaḥ sarvatātā | dive diva āsuvā bhūripaśvaḥ | bhūto bhuteṣu carati praviṣṭaḥ | sa bhūtānām adhipatir babhūva |

{ 2.7.15.2} tasya mṛtyau carati rājasūyam | sa rājā rājyam anumanyatām idam | yebhiś śilpaiḥ paprathānām adṛṃhat | yebhir dyām abhyapiṃśat prajāpatiḥ | yebhir vācaṃ viśvarūpāṃ samavyayat | tena-imam agna iha varcasā samaṅgdhi | yebhir ādityas tapati praketubhiḥ | yebhiḥ sūryo dadṛśe citrabhānuḥ | yebhir vācaṃ puṣkalebhir avyayat | tena-imam agna iha varcasā samaṅgdhi |

{ 2.7.15.3} ā-ayaṃ bhātu śavasā pañca kṛṣṭīḥ | indra iva jyeṣṭho bhavatu prajāvān | asmā astu puṣkalaṃ citrabhānu | ā-ayaṃ pṛṇaktu rajasī upastham | yat te śilpaṃ kaśyapa rocanāvat | indriyāvat puṣkalaṃ citrabhānu | yasmint sūryā arpitāḥ sapta sākam | tasmin rājānam adhiviśraya-imam | dyaur asi pṛthivyasi | vyāghro vaiyāghre 'dhi |

{ 2.7.15.4} viśrayasva diśo mahīḥ | viśas tvā sarvā vāñchantu | mā tvad rāṣṭram adhibhraśat | yā divyā āpaḥ payasā saṃbabhūvuḥ | yā antarikṣa uta pārthivīr yāḥ | tāsāṃ tvā sarvāsāṃ rucā | abhiṣiñcāmi varcasā | abhi tvā varcasā sicaṃ divyena | payasā saha | yathā-āsā rāṣṭravardhanaḥ |

{ 2.7.15.5} tathā tvā savitā karat | indraṃ viśvā avīvṛdhan | samudravyacasaṃ giraḥ | rathītamaṃ rathīnām | vājānāṃ satpatiṃ patim | vasavas tvā purastād abhiṣiñcantu gāyatreṇa chandasā | rudrās tvā dakṣiṇato 'bhiṣiñcantu traiṣṭubhena chandasā | ādityās tvā paścād abhiṣiñcantu jāgatena chandasā | viśve tvā devā uttarato 'bhiṣiñcantv ānuṣṭubhena chandasā | bṛhaspatis tvā-upariṣṭād abhiṣiñcatu pāṅktena chandasā |

{ 2.7.15.6} aruṇaṃ tvā vṛkam ugraṃ khajaṃkaram | rocamānaṃ marutām agre arciṣaḥ | sūryavantaṃ maghavānaṃ viṣāsahim | indram ukthyeṣu nāmahūtamaṃ huvema | pra bāhavā sisṛtaṃ jīvase naḥ | ā no gavyūtim ukṣataṃ ghṛtena | ā no jane śravayataṃ yuvānā | śrutaṃ me mitrāvaruṇā havemā | indrasya te vīryakṛtaḥ | bāhū upāvaharāmi |

anuvāka 16 { 2.7.16.1} abhi prehi vīrayasva | ugraś cettā sapatnahā | ātiṣṭha vṛtrahantamaḥ | tubhyaṃ devā adhibruvan | aṅkau nyaṅkāv abhito rathaṃ yau | dhvāntaṃ vātāgram anusaṃcarantau | dūrehetir indriyāvān patatrī | te no 'gnayaḥ paprayaḥ pārayantu | namas ta ṛṣe gada | avyathāyai {ānaṃdā. avyadhāyai} tvā svadhāyai tvā |

{ 2.7.16.2} mā na indra-abhitas tvad ṛṣva-ariṣṭāsaḥ | evā brahman tava-id astu | tiṣṭhā rathe adhi yad vajrahastaḥ | ā raśmīn deva yuvase svaśvaḥ | ātiṣṭha vṛtrahann ātiṣṭhantaṃ pari | anu tvā-indro madatv anu tvā mitrāvaruṇau | dyauś ca tvā pṛthivī ca pracetasā | śukro bṛhad dakṣiṇā tvā pipartu | anu svadhā cikitāṃ somo agniḥ | anu tvā-avatu savitā savena |

{ 2.7.16.3} indraṃ viśvā avīvṛdhan | samudravyācasaṃ giraḥ | rathītamaṃ {ānaṃdā. rathītataṃ} rathīnām | vājānāṃ satpatiṃ patim | pari mā senyā ghoṣāḥ | jyānāṃ vṛñjantu gṛdhnavaḥ {ānaṃdā. gṛvadhnaḥ?} methiṣṭhāḥ pinvamānā iha | māṃ gopatim abhisaṃviśantu | tan me 'numatir anumanyatām | tan mātā pṛthivī tat pitā dyauḥ |

{ 2.7.16.4} tad grāvāṇaḥ somasuto mayobhuvaḥ | tad aśvinā śṛṇutaṃ saubhagā yuvam | avate heḍa ud uttamam | enā vyāghraṃ pariṣasvajānāḥ | siṃhaṃ hinvanti {ānaṃdā. hinvati} mahate saubhagāya | samudraṃ na suhavaṃ tasthivāṃsam | marmṛjyante dvīpinam apsv antaḥ | ud asāv etu sūryaḥ | ud idaṃ māmakaṃ vacaḥ | udihi deva sūrya | saha vagnunā mama | ahaṃ vāco vivācanam | mayi vāg astu dharṇasiḥ | yantu nadayo varṣantu parjanyāḥ | supippalā oṣadhayo bhavantu | annavatām odanavatām āmikṣavatām | eṣāṃ rājā bhūyāsam |

anuvāka 17 { 2.7.17.1} ye keśinaḥ prathamāḥ sattram āsata | yebhir ābhṛtaṃ yad idaṃ virocate | tebhyo juhomi bahudhā ghṛtena | rāyas poṣeṇa-imaṃ varcasā saṃsṛjātha | na-ṛte brahmaṇas tapaso vimokaḥ | dvināmnī dīkṣā vaśinī hy ugrā | pra keśāḥ suvate kāṇḍíno bhavanti | teṣāṃ brahma-id īśe vapanasya na-anyaḥ | āroha proṣṭhaṃ viṣahasva śatrūn | avāsrāg dīkṣā vaśinī hy ugrā |

{ 2.7.17.2} dehi dakṣiṇāṃ pratirasva-āyuḥ | athā mucyasva varuṇasya pāśāt | yena-avapat savitā kṣureṇa | somasya rājño varuṇasya vidvān | tena brahmāṇo vapata-idam asya-ūrjā-imam | rayyā varcasā saṃsṛjātha | mā te keśān anu gād varca etat | tathā dhātā karotu te | tubhyam indro bṛhaspatiḥ | savitā varca ādadhāt |

{ 2.7.17.3} tebhyo nidhānaṃ bahudhā vyaicchan | antarā dyāvāpṛthivī apaḥ suvaḥ | darbhastambe vīryakṛte nidhāya | pauṃsyena-imaṃ varcasā saṃsṛjātha | balaṃ te bāhuvoḥ savitā dadhātu | somas tvā-anaktu payasā ghṛtena | strīṣu rūpam aśvinā-etan nidhattam | pauṃsyena-imaṃ { poṃsyenemaṃ} varcasā saṃsṛjātha | yat sīmantaṃ kaṅkatas te lilekha | yad vā kṣuraḥ parivavarja vapaṃs te | strīṣu rūpam aśvinā-etan nidhattam | pauṃsyena-imaṃ saṃsṛjātho vīryeṇa |

anuvāka 18 { 2.7.18.1} indraṃ vai svā viśo maruto na-apācāyan | so 'napacāyyamāna etaṃ vighanam apaśyat | tam āharat | tena-ayajata | tena-evā''sāṃ taṃ saṃstambhaṃ vyahan | yad vyahan | tad vighanasya vighanatvam | vi pāpmānaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ hate | ya etena yajate | ya u cainam evaṃ veda |

{ 2.7.18.2} yaṃ rājānaṃ viśo na-apacāyeyuḥ | yo vā brāhmaṇas tamasā pāpmanā prāvṛtaḥ syāt | sa etena yajeta | vighanena-evainad vihatya | viśām ādhipatyaṃ gacchati | tasya dve dvādaśe stotre bhavataḥ | dve caturviṃśe | audbhidyam eva tat | etad vai kṣatrasya-audbhidyam | yad asmai svā viśo baliṃ haranti |

{ 2.7.18.3} haranty asmai viśo balim | ā-enam apratikhyātaṃ gacchati | ya evaṃ veda | prabāhug vā agre kṣatrāṇy ātepuḥ | teṣām indraḥ kṣatrāṇy ādatta | na vā imāni kṣatrāṇy abhūvann iti | tan nakṣatrāṇāṃ nakṣatratvam | ā śreyaso bhrātṛvyasya teja indriyaṃ datte | ya etena yajate | ya u cainam evaṃ veda |

{ 2.7.18.4} tad yathā ha vai sacākriṇau kaplakāv upāvahitau syātām | evam etau yugmantau stomau | ayukṣu stomeṣu kriyete | pāpmano 'pahatyai | apa pāpmānaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ hate | ya etena yajate | ya u cainam evaṃ veda | tad yathā ha vai sūtagrāmaṇyaḥ | evaṃ chandāṃsi | teṣv asāv ādityo bṛhatīr abhyūḍhaḥ {ānaṃdā. abhyūḍaḥ} |

{ 2.7.18.5} satobṛhatīṣu stuvate sato bṛhan | prajayā paśubhir asāni-ity eva | vyatiṣaktābhiḥ stuvate | vyatiṣaktaṃ vai kṣatraṃ viśā | viśā-evainaṃ kṣatreṇa vyatiṣajati | vyatiṣaktābhiḥ stuvate | vyatiṣakto vai grāmaṇīḥ sajātaiḥ | sajātair evainaṃ vyatiṣajati | vyatiṣaktābhiḥ stuvate | vyatiṣakto vai puruṣaḥ pāpmabhiḥ | vyatiṣaktābhir evāsya pāpmano nudate |



prapāṭhaka: 8 anuvāka 1 { 2.8.1.1} pīvonnāṃ {ḍyumoṇṭa pīvoannāṃ} rayivṛdhaḥ sumedhāḥ | śvetaḥ siṣakti niyutām abhiśrīḥ | te vāyave samanaso vitasthuḥ | viśvā-it- naraḥ svapatyāni cakruḥ | rāye 'nu yaṃ jajñatū rodasī ubhe | rāye devī dhiṣaṇā dhāti devam | adhā vāyuṃ niyutaḥ saścata svāḥ | uta śvetaṃ vasudhitiṃ nireke | {c} ā vāyo, {d} pra yābhiḥ | 0 {d} pra vāyum acchā bṛhatī manīṣā |

{ 2.8.1.2} bṛhadrayiṃ viśvavārāṃ {ḍyumoṇṭa viśvavāraṃ} rathaprām | dyutadyāmā niyutaḥ patyamānaḥ | kaviḥ kavim iyakṣasi prayajyo | ā no niyudbhiḥ śatinībhir adhvaram | sahasriṇībhir upa yāhi yajñam | vāyo asmin haviṣi mādayasva | yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ | prajāpate na tvad etāny anyaḥ | viśvā jātāni pari tā babhūva | yatkāmās te juhumas tan no astu |

{ 2.8.1.3} vayaṃ syāma patayo rayīṇām | rayīṇāṃ patiṃ yajataṃ bṛhantam | asmin bhare nṛtamaṃ vājasātau | prajāpatiṃ prathamajām ṛtasya | yajāma devam adhi no bravītu | prajāpate tvaṃ nidhipāḥ purāṇaḥ | devānāṃ pitā janitā prajānām | patir viśvasya jagataḥ paraspāḥ | havir no deva vihave juṣasva | tava-ime lokāḥ pradiśo diśaś ca |

{ 2.8.1.4} parāvato nivata udvataś ca | prajāpate viśvasṛj jīvadhanya idaṃ no deva | pratiharya havyam | prajāpatiṃ prathamaṃ yajñiyānām | devānām agre yajataṃ yajadhvam | sa no dadātu draviṇaṃ {ānaṃdā. draviṇaṃ} suvīryam | rāyas poṣaṃ viṣyatu nābhim asme | yo rāya īśe śatadāya ukthyaḥ | yaḥ paśūnāṃ rakṣitā viṣṭhitānām | prajāpatiḥ prathamajā ṛtasya |

{ 2.8.1.5} sahasradhāmā juṣatāṃ havir naḥ | somāpūṣaṇā- imau devau | somāpūṣaṇā rajaso vimānam | saptacakraṃ ratham aviśvaminvam | viṣūvṛtaṃ manasā yujyamānam | taṃ jinvatho vṛṣaṇā pañcaraśmim | divyanyaḥ sadanaṃ cakra uccā | pṛthivyām anyo adhy antarikṣe | tāv asmabhyaṃ puruvāraṃ purukṣum | rāyas poṣaṃ viṣyatāṃ nābhim asme |

{ 2.8.1.6} dhiyaṃ pūṣā jinvatu viśvaminvaḥ | rayiṃ somo rayipatir dadhātu | avatu devy aditir anarvā | bṛhad vadema vidathe suvīrāḥ | viśvāny anyo bhuvanā jajāna | viśvam anyo abhicakṣāṇa eti | somāpūṣaṇāv avataṃ dhiyaṃ me | yuvabhyāṃ viśvāḥ pṛtanā jayema | ud uttamaṃ varuṇa- astabhnād dyām {so tai.saṃ.1.8.2.2; ,āṣaḥ yām} | yat kiṃ ca-idaṃ kitavāsaḥ | ava te heḍas tat tvā yāmi | ādityānām avasā na dakṣiṇā | dhārayanta ādityāsas tisro bhūmīr dhārayan | yajño devānāṃ śucir apaḥ |

anuvāka 2 { 2.8.2.7} te śukrāsaḥ śucayo raśmivantaḥ | sīdann ādityā adhi barhiṣi priye | kāmena devāḥ sarathaṃ divo naḥ | āyāntu yajñam upa no juṣāṇāḥ | te sūnavo aditeḥ pīvasām iṣam | ghṛtaṃ pinvat pratiharyann ṛtejāḥ | pra yajñiyā yajamānāya yemure | ādityāḥ kāmaṃ pitumantam asme | ā naḥ putrā aditer yāntu yajñam | ādityāsaḥ pathibhir devayānaiḥ |

{ 2.8.2.2} asme kāmaṃ dāśuṣe sannamantaḥ | puroḍāśaṃ ghṛtavantaṃ juṣantām | skabhāyata nirṛtiṃ sedhata-āmatim | pra raśmibhir yatamānā amṛdhrāḥ | ādityāḥ kāma prayatāṃ vaṣaṭkṛtim | juṣadhvaṃ no havyadātiṃ yajatrāḥ | ādityān kāmam avase huvema | ye bhūtāni janayanto vicikhyuḥ | sīdantu putrā aditer upastham | stīrṇaṃ barhir haviradyāya devāḥ |

{ 2.8.2.3} stīrṇaṃ barhiḥ sīdatā yajñe asmin | dhrājāḥ sedhanto amatiṃ durevām | asmabhyaṃ putrā aditeḥ prayaṃsata | ādityāḥ kāma haviṣo juṣāṇāḥ | agne naya supathā rāye asmān | viśvāni deva vayunāni vidvān | yuyodhy asmaj juhurāṇam enaḥ | bhūyiṣṭhāṃ te namauktiṃ vidhema | pra vaḥ śukrāya bhānave bharadhvam | havyaṃ matiṃ ca-agnaye supūtam |

{ 2.8.2.4} yo daivyāni mānuṣā janūṃṣi | antar viśvāni vidmanā jigāti | acchā giro matayo devayantīḥ | agniṃ yanti draviṇaṃ bhikṣamāṇāḥ | susaṃdṛśaṃ supratīkaṃ svañcam | havyavāham aratiṃ mānuṣāṇām | agne tvam asmad yuyodhy amīvāḥ | anagnitrā abhyamanta kṛṣṭīḥ | punar asmabhyaṃ suvitāya deva | kṣāṃ viśvebhir ajarebhir yajatra |

{ 2.8.2.5} agne tvaṃ pārayā navyo asmān | svastibhir ati durgāṇi viśvā | pūś ca pṛthvī bahulā na urvī | bhavā tokāya tanayāya śaṃ yoḥ | pra kāravo mananā vacyamānāḥ | devadrīcīṃ nayatha devayantaḥ | dakṣiṇāvāḍ vājinī prācy eti | havir bharanty agnaye ghṛtācī | indraṃ naro, yuje ratham, | jagṛbhṇā {ḍyumoṇṭa jagṛbhmā} te dakṣiṇām indra hastam |

{ 2.8.2.6} vasūyavo vasupate vasūnām | vidmā hi tvā gopatiṃ śūra gonām | asmabhyaṃ citraṃ vṛṣaṇaṃ rayiṃ dāḥ | tava-idaṃ viśvam abhitaḥ paśavyam | yat paśyasi cakṣasā sūryasya | gavām asi gopatir eka indra | bhakṣīmahi te prayatasya vasvaḥ | sam indra ṇo manasā neṣi gobhiḥ | saṃ sūribhir maghavant saṃ svastyā | saṃ brahmaṇā devakṛtaṃ yad asti |

{ 2.8.2.7} saṃ devānāṃ sumatyā yajñiyānām | ārāt-śatrum apa bādhasva dūram | ugro yaḥ śambaḥ puruhūta tena | asme dhehi yavamad gomad indra | kṛdhī dhiyaṃ jaritre vājaratnām | ā vedhasaṃ, sa hi śuciḥ, | bṛhaspatiḥ prathamaṃ jāyamānaḥ | maho jyotiṣaḥ parame vyoman | saptāsyas tuvijāto raveṇa | vi saptaraśmir adhamat tamāṃsi |

{ 2.8.2.8} bṛhaspatiḥ samajayad vasūni | maho vrajān gomato deva eṣaḥ | apaḥ siṣāsant suvar apratīttaḥ | bṛhaspatir hanty amitram arkaiḥ | bṛhaspate pary, evā pitre, | ā no divaḥ, pāvīravī, | imā juhvānā, yas te stanaḥ, | sarasvaty abhi no neṣi, | iyaṃ śuṣmebhir bisakhā iva-arujat | sānu girīṇāṃ taviṣebhir ūrmibhiḥ | pārāvadaghnīm {ḍyumoṇṭa pārāvataghīm} avase suvṛktibhiḥ | sarasvatīm āvivāsema dhītibhiḥ |

anuvāka 3 { 2.8.3.1} somo dhenuṃ somo arvantam āśum | somo vīraṃ karmaṇyaṃ dadātu | sādanyaṃ vidathyaṃ sabheyam | pituḥśravaṇaṃ yo dadāśad asmai | aṣāḍhaṃ yutsu, tvaṃ soma kratubhiḥ, | yā te dhāmāni haviṣā yajanti, | tvam imā oṣadhīḥ soma viśvāḥ | tvam apo ajanayas tvaṃ gāḥ | tvamātatantha-urv antarikṣam | tvaṃ jyotiṣā vi tamo vavartha |

{ 2.8.3.2} yā te dhāmāni divi yā pṛthivyām | yā parvateṣv oṣadhīṣv apsu | tebhir no viśvaiḥ sumanā aheḍan | rājant soma prati havyā gṛbhāya | viṣṇor nu kaṃ, tad asya priyam, | pra tad viṇuḥ, | paro mātrayā tanuvā vṛdhāna | na te mahitvam anvaśnuvanti | ubhe te vidma rajasī pṛthivyā viṣṇo deva tvam | paramasya vitse |

{ 2.8.3.3} vicakrame, trir devaḥ, | ā te maho, yo jāta eva. | abhi gotrāṇi. | ābhiḥ spṛdho mithatīr ariṣaṇyan | amitrasya vyathayā manyum indra ābhir viśvā abhiyujo viṣūcīḥ | āryāya viśo 'vatarīr dāsīḥ | ayaṃ śṛṇve adha jayann uta ghnan | ayam uta prakṛṇute yudhā gāḥ | yadā satyaṃ kṛṇute manyum indraḥ |

{ 2.8.3.4} viśvaṃ dṛḍhaṃ bhayata ejad asmāt | anu svadhām akṣarann āpo asya | avardhata madhya ā nāvyānām | sadhrīcīnena manasā tam indra ojiṣṭhena | hanmanā-ahann abhi dyūn | marutvantaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ vāvṛdhānam | akavāriṃ divyaṃ śāsam indram | viśvāsāham avase nūtanāya | ugraṃ sahodām iha taṃ huvema | janiṣṭhā ugraḥ sahase turāya |

{ 2.8.3.5} mandra ojiṣṭho bahulābhimānaḥ | avardhann indraṃ marutaś cid atra | mātā yad vīraṃ dadhanad dhaniṣṭhā | kva syā vo marutaḥ svadhā-āsīt | yan mām ekaṃ samadhattā-ahihatye | ahaṃ hy ugras taviṣas tuviṣmān | viśvasya śatror anamaṃ vadhasnáiḥ | vṛtrasya tvā śvasathād īṣamāṇāḥ | viśve devā ajahur ye sakhāyaḥ | marudbhir indra sakhyaṃ te astu |

{ 2.8.3.6} atha-imā viśvāḥ pṛtanā jayāsi | vadhīṃ { vardhīṃ ?} vṛtraṃ maruta indriyeṇa | svena bhāmena taviṣo babhūvān | aham etā manave viśvaścandrāḥ | sugā apaś cakara vajrabāhuḥ | sa yo vṛṣā vṛṣṇiyebhiḥ samokāḥ | maho divaḥ pṛthivyāś ca samrāṭ | satīnasattvā {ḍyumoṇṭa satīnasatvā} havyo bhareṣu | marutvān no bhavatv indra ūtī | indro vṛtram atarad vṛtratūrye |

{ 2.8.3.7} anādhṛṣyo maghavā śūra indraḥ | anv enaṃ viśo amadanta pūrviḥ | ayaṃ rājā jagataś carṣaṇīnām | sa eva vīraḥ sa u vīryāvān | sa ekarājo jagataḥ paraspāḥ | yadā vṛtram atarat-śūra indraḥ | atha-abhavad damitā-abhikratūnām | indro yajñaṃ vardhayan viśvavedāḥ | puroḍāśasya juṣatāṃ havir naḥ | vṛtraṃ tīrtvā dānavaṃ vajrabāhuḥ |

{ 2.8.3.8} diśo 'dṛṃhad dṛṃhitā dṛṃhaṇena | imaṃ yajñaṃ vardhayan viśvavedāḥ | puroḍāśaṃ pratigṛbhṇātv indraḥ | yadā vṛtram atart-śūra indraḥ | atha-ekarājo abhavaj janānām | indro devān-śambarahatya āvat | indro devānām abhavat purogāḥ | indro yajñe haviṣā vāvṛdhānaḥ | vṛtratūr ṇo abhayaṃ śarma yaṃsat | yaḥ sapta sindhūṃr adadhāt pṛthivyām | yaḥ sapta lokān akṛṇod diśaś ca | indro haviṣmānt sagaṇo marudbhiḥ | vṛtratūr ṇo yajñam iha-upayāsat |

anuvāka 4 { 2.8.4.1} indras tarasvān abhimātihā-ugraḥ | hiraṇyavāśīr iṣiraḥ suvarṣāḥ |tasya vayaṃ sumatau yajñiyasya | api bhadre saumanase syāma | hiraṇyavarṇo abhayaṃ kṛṇotu | abhimātihā-indraḥ pṛtanāsu jiṣṇuḥ | sa naḥ śarma trivarūthaṃ viyaṃsat | yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ | indraṃ stuhi vajriṇaṃ stomapṛṣṭham {ḍyumoṇṭa somapṛṣṭham} | puroḍāśasya juṣatāṃ havir naḥ |

{ 2.8.4.2} hatvā-abhimātīḥ pṛtanāḥ sahasvān | atha-abhayaṃ kṛṇuhi { athābhayaṃ kṛṇuhi} viśvato naḥ | stuhi śūraṃ vajriṇam apratītam | abhimātihanaṃ puruhūtam indram | ya eka it-śatapatir janeṣu | tasmā indrāya havir ājuhota | indro devānām adhipāḥ purohitaḥ | diśāṃ patir abhavad vājinīvān | abhimātihā taviṣas tuviṣmān | asmabhyaṃ citraṃ vṛṣaṇaṃ rayiṃ dāt |

{ 2.8.4.3} ya ime dyāvāpṛthivī mahitvā | balena-adṛṃhad abhimātihā-indraḥ | sa no haviḥ pratigṛbhṇātu rātaye | devānāṃ devo nidhipā no avyāt | anavas te rathaṃ, vṛṣṇe yat te. | indrasya nu vīryāṇy, ahann ahim, | indro yāto 'vasitasya rājā | śamasya ca śṛṅgiṇo vajrabāhuḥ | sa-id u rājā kṣeti carṣaṇīnām | arān na nemiḥ pari tā babhūva |

{ 2.8.4.4} abhi sidhmo ajigād asya śatrūn | vi tigmena vṛṣabheṇā puro 'bhet | saṃ vajreṇa-asṛjad vṛtram indraḥ | pra svāṃ matim atirat-śāśadānaḥ | viṣṇuṃ devaṃ varuṇamūtaye bhagam | medasā devā vapayā yajadhvam | tā no yajñam āgataṃ viśvadhenā | prajāvad asme draviṇā-iha dhattam | medasā devā vapayā yajadhvam | viṣṇuṃ ca devaṃ varuṇaṃ ca rātim |

{ 2.8.4.5} tā no amīvā apabādhamānau | imaṃ yajñaṃ juṣamāṇāv upetam | viṣṇūvaruṇā yuvam adhvarāya naḥ | viśe janāya mahi śarma yacchatam | dīrghaprayajyū haviṣā vṛdhānā | jyotiṣā-arātīr dahataṃ tamāṃsi | yayor ojasā skabhitā rajāṃsi | vīryebhir vīratamā śaviṣṭhā | yā patyete apratītā śahobhiḥ | viṣṇū agan varuṇā pūrvahūtau |

{ 2.8.4.6} viṣṇūvaruṇāv abhiśastipā vām | devā yajanta haviṣā ghṛtena | apa-amīvāṃ sedhataṃ rakṣasaś ca | athā dhattaṃ yajamānāya śaṃ yoḥ | aṃhomucā vṛṣabhā supratūrtī | devānāṃ devatamā śaviṣṭhā | viṣṇūvaruṇā pratiharyataṃ naḥ | idaṃ narā {ḍyumoṇṭa narā} prayatamūtaye haviḥ | mahī nu dyāvāpṛthivī iha jyeṣṭhe | rucā bhavatāṃ śucayadbhir arkaiḥ |

{ 2.8.4.7} yat sīṃ variṣṭhe bṛhatī viminvan | nṛvadbhyo {ḍyumoṇṭa ruvad dho} 'kṣā paprathānebhir evaiḥ | pra pūrvaje pitarā navyasībhiḥ | gīrbhiḥ kṛṇudhvaṃ sadane ṛtasya | ā no dyāvāpṛthivī daivyena | janena yātaṃ mahi vāṃ varūtham | sa it svapā bhuvaneṣv āsa | ya ime dyāvāpṛthivī jajāna | urvī gabhīre rajasī sumeke | avaṃśe dhīraḥ śacyā samairat |

{ 2.8.4.8} bhūriṃ dve acarantī carantam | padvantaṃ garbham apadī dadhāte | nityaṃ na sūnuṃ pitror upasthe | taṃ pipṛtaṃ rodasī satyavācam | idaṃ dyāvāpṛthivī satyam astu | pitar mātar yad iha-upabruve vām | bhūtaṃ devānām avame avobhiḥ | vidyāma-iṣaṃ vṛjanaṃ jīradānum | urvī pṛthvī bahule dūre-ante | upabruve namasā yajñe asmin | dadhāte ye subhage supratūrtī | dyāvā rakṣataṃ pṛthivī no abhvāt | yā jātā oṣadhayo, 'ti viśvāḥ pariṣṭhāḥ, | yā oṣadhayaḥ somarājñīr, aśvāvatīṃ somavatīm, | oṣadhīr iti mātaro, 'nyā vo anyām avatu, |

anuvāka 5 { 2.8.5.5} śuciṃ nu stomaṃ, śnathad vṛtram, | ubhā vām indrāgnī, pra carṣaṇibhyaḥ, | ā vṛtrahaṇā, gīrbhir vipraḥ, | brahmaṇaspate tvam asya yantā | sūktasya bodhi tanayaṃ ca jinva | viśvaṃ tad bhadraṃ yad avanti devāḥ | bṛhad vadema vidathe suvīrāḥ | sa īṃ satyebhiḥ sakhibhiḥ śucadbhiḥ | godhāyasaṃ vi dhanasair atardat | brahmaṇaspatir vṛṣabhir varāhaiḥ |

{ 2.8.5.2} gharmasvedobhir draviṇaṃ vyānaṭ | brahmaṇaspater abhavad yathāvaśam | satyo manyur mahi karmā kariṣyataḥ | yo gā udājat sa dive vi ca-abhajat | mahi-iva rītiḥ śavasā sarat {ḍyumoṇṭa śavasā-'sarat} pṛthak | indhāno agniṃ vanavad vanuṣyataḥ | kṛtabrahmā śūśuvad rātahavya it | jātena jātam ati sṛt prasṛṃsate {ḍyumoṇṭa ati sa prasarsr̥te, probably based upon ṛ.ve..2.25.1} | yaṃ-yaṃ yujaṃ kṛṇute brahmaṇaspatiḥ | brahmaṇaspate suyamasya viśvahā |

{ 2.8.5.3} rāyaḥ syāma rathyo vivasvataḥ | vīreṣu vīrāṃ upapṛṅgdhi nas tvam | yad īśāno brahmaṇā veṣi me havam | sa ij janena sa viśā sa janmanā | sa putrair vājaṃ bharate dhanā nṛbhiḥ | devānāṃ yaḥ pitaram āvivāsati | śraddhāmanā haviṣā brahmaṇaspatim | yas te pūṣan nāvo antaḥ, | śukraṃ te anyat, pūṣā-imā āśāḥ, | prapathe pathām ajaniṣṭa pūṣā |

{ 2.8.5.4} prapathe divaḥ prapathe pṛthivyāḥ | ubhe abhi priyatame sadhasthe | ā ca parā ca carati prajānan | pūṣā subandhur diva ā pṛthivyāḥ | iḍas patir maghavā dasmavarcāḥ | taṃ devāso adaduḥ sūryāyai | kāmena kṛtaṃ tavasaṃ svañcam | ajāśvaḥ paśupā vājavastyaḥ | dhiyaṃjinvo viśve bhuvane arpitaḥ | aṣṭrāṃ pūṣā śithirām udvarīvṛjat |

{ 2.8.5.5} saṃcakṣāṇo bhuvanā deva īyate | śucī vo havyā marutaḥ śucīnām | śuciṃ hinomy adhvaraṃ śucibhyaḥ | ṛtena { ṛtena} satyam ṛtasāpa āyan | śucijanmānaḥ śucayaḥ pāvakāḥ | pra citram arkaṃ gṛṇate turāya | mārutāya svatavase bharadhvam | ye sahāṃsi sahasā sahante | rejate agne pṛthivī makhebhyaḥ | aṃseṣv ā marutaḥ khādayo vaḥ |

{ 2.8.5.6} vakṣaḥsu rukmā upaśiśriyāṇāḥ | vi vidyuto na vyr̥ṣṭibhī rucānāḥ | anu svadhām āyudhair yat-śamānāḥ | yā vaḥ śarma śaśamānāya santi | tridhātūni dāśuṣe yacchata-adhi | asmabhyaṃ tāni maruto viyanta | rayiṃ no dhatta vṛṣaṇaḥ suvīram | ime turaṃ maruto rāmayanti | ime sahaḥ sahasa ānamanti | ime śaṃsaṃ vanuṣyato nipānti |

{ 2.8.5.7} guru dveṣo araruṣe dadhanti | arā iva-id acaramā aha-iva | pra prajāyante akavā mahobhiḥ | pṛśneḥ putrā upamāso rabhiṣṭhāḥ | svayā matyā marutaḥ saṃmimikṣuḥ | anu te dāyi maha indriyāya | satrā te viśvam anu vṛtrahatye | anu kṣattram anu saho yajatra | indra devebhir anu te nṛṣahye | ya indra śuṣmo maghavan te asti |

{ 2.8.5.8} śikṣā sakhibhyaḥ puruhūta nṛbhyaḥ | tvaṃ hi dṛḍhā maghavan vicetāḥ | apāvṛdhi parivṛtiṃ na rādhaḥ | indro rājā jagataś carṣaṇīnām | adhi kṣami viṣurūpaṃ yad asti | tato dadātu dāśuṣe vasūni | codad rādha upastutaś cid arvāk | tam u ṣṭuhi yo abhibhūtyojāḥ | vanvann avātaḥ puruhūta indraḥ | aṣāḍham ugraṃ sahamānam ābhiḥ |

{ 2.8.5.9} gīrbhir vardha vṛṣabhaṃ carṣaṇīnām | sthūrasya rāyo bṛhato ya īśe | tam u ṣṭavāma vidatheṣv indram | yo vāyunā jayati gomatīṣu | pra dhṛṣṇuyā nayati vasyo accha | ā te śuṣmo vṛṣabha etu paścāt | ā-uttarād adharāg ā purastāt | ā viśvato abhi sametv arvāṅ | indra dyumnaṃ suvarvad dhehy asme |

anuvāka 6 { 2.8.6.1} ā devo yātu savitā suratnaḥ | antarikṣaprā vahamāno aśvaiḥ | haste dadhāno naryā purūṇi | niveśayan ca prasuvan ca bhūma | abhīvṛtaṃ kṛśanair viśvarūpam | hiraṇyaśamyaṃ yajato bṛhantam | āsthād rathaṃ savitā citrabhānuḥ | kṛṣṇā rajāṃsi taviṣīṃ dadhānaḥ | sa ghā no devaḥ savitā savāya | āsāviṣad vasupatir vasūni |

{ 2.8.6.2} viśrayamāṇo amatim {ḍyumoṇṭa amatim} urūcīm | martabhojanam adha rāsate na {ḍyumoṇṭa naḥ} | vi janān-śyāvāḥ śitipādo akhyan | rathaṃ hiraṇyapraugaṃ vahantaḥ | śaśvad diśaḥ savitur daivyasya | upasthe viśvā bhuvanāni tasthuḥ | vi suparṇo antarikṣāṇy akhyat | gabhīravepā asuraḥ sunīthaḥ | kva-ìdānīṃ sūryaḥ kaś ciketa | katamāṃ dyāṃ raśmir asya-ātatāna |

{ 2.8.6.3} bhagaṃ dhiyaṃ vājayantaḥ purandhim | narāśaṃso gnāspatir no avyāt | ā 'ye {ḍyumoṇṭa āye} vāmasya saṃgathe rayīṇām | priyā devasya savituḥ syāma | ā no viśve askrā gamantu devāḥ | mitro aryamā varuṇaḥ sajoṣāḥ | bhuvan yathā no viśve vṛdhāsaḥ | karant suṣahā vithuraṃ na śavaḥ | śaṃ no devā viśvadevā bhavantu | śaṃ sarasvatī saha dhībhir astu |

{ 2.8.6.4} śam abhiṣācaḥ śam u rātiṣācaḥ | śaṃ no divyāḥ pārthivāḥ śaṃ no apyāḥ | ye savituḥ satyasavasya viśve | mitrasya vrate varuṇasya devāḥ | te saubhagaṃ vīravad gomad apnaḥ | dadhātana draviṇaṃ citram asme | agne yāhi dūtyaṃ vāriṣeṇyaḥ {ḍyumoṇṭa mā viṣaṇyaḥ} | devāṃ acchā brahmakṛtā gaṇena | sarasvatīṃ maruto aśvinā-apaḥ | yakṣi devān ratnadheyāya viśvān |

{ 2.8.6.5} dyauḥ pitaḥ pṛthivi mātar adhruka | agne bhrātarvasavo mṛḍatā naḥ | viśva ādityā ādite sajoṣāḥ | asmabhyaṃ śarma bahulaṃ viyanta | viśve devāḥ śṛṇuta-imaṃ havaṃ me | ye antarikṣe ya upa dyavi ṣṭha | ye agnijihvā uta vā yajatrāḥ | āsadya-asmin barhiṣi mādayadhvam | ā vāṃ mitrāvaruṇā havyajuṣṭim | namasā devāvav avasā vavṛtyām |

{ 2.8.6.6} asmākaṃ brahma pṛtanāsu sahyā asmākam | {ḍyumoṇṭa suggests not to put |} vṛṣṭir divyā supārā | yuvaṃ vastrāṇi pīvasā vasāthe | yuvor acchidrā mantavo ha sargāḥ | avātiratam anṛtāni viśvā | ṛtena mitrāvaruṇā sacethe | tat su vāṃ mitrāvaruṇā mahitvam | īrmā tasthuṣīr ahabhir duduhre | viśvāḥ pinvatha svasarasya dhenāḥ anu vām ekaḥ pavir āvavarti |

{ 2.8.6.7} yad baṃhiṣṭhaṃ na-ativide {ḍyumoṇṭa nātividhe} sudānū | acchidraṃ śarma bhuvanasya gopā | tato {ḍyumoṇṭa tena} no mitrāvaruṇāv avīṣṭam | siṣāsanto jīgivāṃsaḥ syāma | ā no mitrāvaruṇā havyadātim | ghṛtair gavyūtim ukṣatam iḍābhiḥ | prati vām atra varam ā janāya | pṛṇītam udgó divyasyaṃ cāroḥ | pra bāhavā sisṛtaṃ jīvase naḥ | ā no gavyūtim ukṣataṃ ghṛtena |

{ 2.8.6.8} ā no jane śravayataṃ yuvānā | śrutaṃ me mitrāvaruṇā hava-imā | imā rudrāya sthiradhanvane giraḥ | kṣipreṣave devāya svadhāmne | aṣāḍhāya sahamānāya mīḍhuṣe | tigmāyudhāya bharatā śṛṇotana {ḍyumoṇṭa śṛṇotu noḥ} | tvā dattebhī rudra śaṃtamebhiḥ | śataṃ himā aśīya bheṣajebhiḥ | vyasmad dveṣo vitaraṃ vy aṃhaḥ | vy amīvāṃś cātayasvā viṣūcīḥ |

{ 2.8.6.9} arhan bibharṣi, mā nas toke, | ā te pitar marutāṃ sumnam etu | mā naḥ sūryasya saṃdṛśo yuyothāḥ | abhi no vīro arvati kṣameta | prajāyemahi rudra prajābhiḥ | evā babhro vṛṣabha cekitāna | yathā deva na hṛṇīṣe na haṃsi | hāvanaśrūr no rudra-iha bodhi | bṛhad vadema vidathe suvīrāḥ | pari ṇo rudrasya hetiḥ, stuhi śrutam, | mīḍhuṣṭama-, arhan bibharṣi, | tvam agne rudra, ā vo rājānam, |

anuvāka 7 { 2.8.7.1} sūryo devīm uṣasaṃ rocamānā {ḍyumoṇṭa rocamānām} maryaḥ {ḍyumoṇṭa maryo} | na yoṣām abhyeti paścāt | yatrā naro devayanto yugāni | vitanvate prati bhadrāya bhadram | bhadrā aśvā haritaḥ sūryasya | citrā edagvā {ḍyumoṇṭa etagvā} anumādyāsaḥ | namasyanto diva ā pṛṣṭham asthuḥ | pari dyāvāpṛthivī yanti sadyaḥ | tat sūryasya devatvaṃ tan mahitvam | madhyā kartor vitataṃ saṃjabhāra |

{ 2.8.7.2} yadā-id ayukta haritaḥ sadhasthāt | ād rātrī vāsas tanute simasmai | tan mitrasya varuṇasya-abhicakṣe | sūryo rūpaṃ kṛṇute dyor upasthe | anantam anyad druśad asya pājaḥ | kṛṣṇam anyad dharitaḥ saṃbharanti | adyā devā uditā sūryasya | nir aṃhasaḥ pipṛtān niravadyāt | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām | aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ |
