{ 2.8.7.3} divo rukma urucakṣā udeti | dūre-arthas taraṇir bhrājamānaḥ | nūnaṃ janāḥ sūryeṇa prasūtāḥ | āyann arthāni kṛṇavann apāṃsi | śaṃ no bhava cakṣasā śaṃ no ahnā | śaṃ bhānunā śaṃ himā śaṃ ghṛṇena | yathā śam asmai {ḍyumoṇṭa adhvān-} śam asad duroṇe | tat sūrya draviṇaṃ dhehi citram | citraṃ devānām udagād anīkam | cakṣur mitrasya varuṇasya-agneḥ |

{ 2.8.7.4} āprā dyāvāpṛthivī antarikṣam | sūrya ātmā jagatas tasthuṣaś ca | tvaṣṭā dadhat, tan nas turīpam, | tvaṣṭā vīraṃ, piśaṅgarūpaḥ, | daśa-imaṃ tvaṣṭur janayanta garbham | atandrāso yuvatayo bibhartram {ḍyumoṇṭa vibhṛtram} | tigmānīkaṃ svayaśasaṃ janeṣu | virocamānaṃ pari ṣīṃ nayanti | āviṣṭyo vardhate cārur āsu | jihmānāmūrdhvaḥ svayaśā upasthe |

{ 2.8.7.5} ubhe tvaṣṭur bibhyatur jāyamānāt | pratīcī siṃhaṃ pratijoṣayete | mitro janān, {p} pra sa mitra, | ayaṃ mitro namasyaḥ suśevaḥ | rājā sukṣatro ajaniṣṭa vedhāḥ | tasya vayaṃ sumatau yajñiyasya api bhadre saumanase syāma | anamīvāsaṃ iḍayā madantaḥ | mitajmavo {ḍyumoṇṭa mitajñavo} varimann ā pṛthivyāḥ | ādityasya vratam upakṣyantaḥ {ḍyumoṇṭa upakṣiyantaḥ} |

{ 2.8.7.6 } vayaṃ mitrasya sumatau syāma | mitraṃ na īṃ śimyā goṣu gavyavat {ḍyumoṇṭa gavyavaḥ} | svādhiyo vidathe apsv ajījanan | arejayatāṃ {aṃ} rodasī pājasā girā | prati priyaṃ yajataṃ januṣām avaḥ | mahāṃ ādityo namasopasadyah | yātayajjano gṛṇate suśevaḥ | tasmā etat panyatamāya juṣṭam | agnau mitrāya havir ājuhota | ā vāṃ ratho rodasī badbadhānaḥ |

{ 2.8.7.7} hiraṇyayo vṛṣabhir yātv aśvaiḥ | ghṛtavartaniḥ pavibhī rucānaḥ | iṣāṃ voḍhā nṛpatir vājinīvān | sa paprathāno abhi pañca bhūma | trivandhuro manasā-āyātu yuktaḥ | viśo yena gacchatho devayantīḥ | kutrācid yāmam aśvinā dadhānā | svaśvā yaśasā-āyātam arvāk | dasrā nidhiṃ madhumantaṃ pibāthaḥ | vi vāṃ ratho vadhvā yādamānaḥ |

{ 2.8.7.8} antān divo bādhate vartanibhyām | yuvoḥ śriyaṃ pari yoṣā vṛṇīta | sūro duhitā paritakmiyāyām {ḍyumoṇṭa paritakmyāyām} | yad devayantam avathaḥ śacībhiḥ | parighraṃsa vāṃ manā vāṃ vayo gām {ḍyumoṇṭa pari ghraṃsam omanā vāṃ vayo gāt} | yo ha sya vāṃ rathirā vasta usrāḥ | ratho yujānaḥ pariyāti vartiḥ | tena naḥ śaṃ yor uṣaso vyuṣṭau | ny aśvinā vahataṃ yajñe asmin | yuvaṃ bhujyum avaviddhaṃ samudre |

{ 2.8.7.9} udūhathur arṇaso asridhānaiḥ | patatribhir aśramair avyathibhiḥ | daṃsanābhir aśvinā pārayantām | agnīṣomā yo adya vām | idaṃ vacaḥ saparyati | tasmai dhattaṃ suvīryam | gavāṃ poṣaṃ svaśviyaṃ | yo agnīṣomā haviṣā saparyāt | devadrīcā manasā yo ghṛtena | tasya vrataṃ rakṣataṃ pātam aṃhasaḥ |

{ 2.8.7.10} viśe janāya mahi śarma yacchatam | agnīṣomā ya āhutim | yo vāṃ dāśād dhaviṣkṛtim | sa prajayā suvīryam | viśvam āyur vyaśnavat | agnīṣomā ceti tad vīryaṃ vām | yad amuṣṇītam avasaṃ paṇiṃ goḥ | avātirataṃ prathayasya śeṣaḥ avindataṃ jyotir ekaṃ bahubhyaḥ | agnīṣomāv imaṃ su me, 'gnīṣomā haviṣaḥ prasthitasya, |

anuvāka 8 { 2.8.8.1} aham asmi prathamajā ṛtasya | pūrvaṃ devebhyo amṛtasya nābhiḥ | yo mā dadāti sa id eva mā-āvāḥ | aham annam annam adantam admi | pūrvam agner api dahaty annam | yattau ha-āsāte {ḍyumoṇṭa hāsāte} ahamuttareṣu | vyāttam asya paśavaḥ sujambham | paśyanti dhīrāḥ pracaranti pākāḥ | jahāmy anyaṃ na jahāmy anyam | aham annaṃ vaśam it-carāmi |

{ 2.8.8.2} samānam arthaṃ paryemi bhuñjat | ko mām annaṃ manuṣyo dayeta | parāke annaṃ nihitaṃ loka etat | viśvair devaiḥ pitṛbhir guptam annam | yad adyate lupyate yat paropyate | śatatamī sā tanūr me babhūva | mahāntau carū sakṛddugdhena paprau | divaṃ ca pṛśni pṛthivīṃ ca sākam | tat saṃpibanto na minanti vedhasaḥ | na-etad bhūyo bhavati no kanīyaḥ |

{ 2.8.8.3} annaṃ prāṇam annam apānam āhuḥ | annaṃ mṛtyuṃ tam u jīvātum āhuḥ | annaṃ brahmāṇo jarasaṃ vadanti | annam āhuḥ prajananaṃ prajānām | mogham annaṃ vindate apracetāḥ | satyaṃ bravīmi vadha it sa tasya | na-aryamaṇaṃ puṣyati no sakhāyam | kevalāgho bhavati kevalādī | ahaṃ meghaḥ stanayan varṣann asmi | mām adanty aham admy anyān |

{ 2.8.8.4} ahaṃ sad amṛto bhavāmi | mad ādityā adhi sarve tapanti | devīṃ vācam ajanayanta, yad vāg vadanti, | anantām antād adhi nirmitāṃ mahīm | yasyāṃ devā adadhur bhojanāni | ekākṣarāṃ dvipadāṃ ṣaṭpadāṃ ca | vācaṃ devā upajīvanti viśve | vācaṃ devā upajīvanti viśve | vācaṃ gandharvāḥ paśavo manuṣyāḥ | vāci-imā viśvā bhuvanāny arpitā |

{ 2.8.8.5} sā no havaṃ juṣatām indrapatnī | vāg akṣaraṃ prathamajā ṛtasya | vedānāṃ mātā-amṛtasya nābhiḥ | sā no juṣāṇo 'payajñam āgāt | avantī devī suhavā me astu | yām ṛṣayo mantrakṛto manīṣiṇaḥ | anvaicchan devās tapasā śrameṇa | tāṃ devīṃ vācaṃ haviṣā yajāmahe | sā no dadhātu sukṛtasya loke | catvāri vāk parimitā padāni |

{ 2.8.8.6} tāni vidur brāhmaṇā ye manīṣiṇaḥ | guhā trīṇi nihitā na-iṅgayanti | turīyaṃ vāco manuṣyā vadanti | śraddhayā-agniḥ samidhyate | śraddhayā vindate {ḍyumoṇṭa hūyate} haviḥ | śraddhāṃ bhagasya mūrdhani | vacasā-āvedayāmasi | priyaṃ śraddhe dadataḥ | priyaṃ śraddhe didāsataḥ | priyaṃ bhojeṣu yajvasu |

{ 2.8.8.7} idaṃ ma uditaṃ kṛdhi | yathā devā asureṣu | śraddhām ugreṣu cakrire | evaṃ bhojeṣu yajvasu | asmākam uditaṃ kṛdhi | śraddhāṃ devā {ḍyumoṇṭa devā} yajamānāḥ | vāyugopā upāsate | śraddhāṃ hṛdayyayā-ākūtyā | śraddhayā hūyate haviḥ {ḍyumoṇṭa vindate vasu ?} | 0 śraddhāṃ prātar havāmahe |

{ 2.8.8.8} śraddhāṃ madhyaṃdinaṃ pari | śraddhāṃ sūryasya nimruci | śraddhe śraddhāpaya-iha mā | śraddhā devān adhivaste | śraddhā viśvam idaṃ jagat | śraddhāṃ { śraddhā} kāmasya mātaram | haviṣā vardhayāmasi | brahma jajñānaṃ prathamaṃ purastāt | vi sīmataḥ suruco vena āvaḥ | sa budhniyā upamā asya viṣṭhāḥ |

{ 2.8.8.9} sataś ca yonim asataś ca vivaḥ | pitā virājām ṛṣabho rayīṇām | antarikṣaṃ viśvarūpa āviveśa | tam arkair abhyarcanti vahsám | brahma santaṃ brahmaṇā vardhayantaḥ | brahma devān ajanayat | brahma viśvam idaṃ jagat | brahmaṇaḥ kṣatraṃ nirmitam | brahma brāhmaṇa ātmanā | 0 antar asminn ime lokāḥ |

{ 2.8.8.10} antar viśvam idaṃ jagat | brahma-eva bhūtānāṃ jyeṣṭham | tena ko 'rhati spardhitum | brahman devās trayastriṃśat | brahmann indraprajāpatī | brahman ha viśvā bhūtāni | nāvi-iva-antaḥ samāhitā | catasra āśāḥ pracarantv agnayaḥ | imaṃ no yajñaṃ nayatu prajānan | ghṛtaṃ pinvann ajaraṃ suvīram |

{ 2.8.8.11} brahma samid bhavaty āhutīnām | ā gāvo agmann uta bhadram akran | sīdantu goṣṭhe raṇayantv asme | prajāvatīḥ pururūpā iha syuḥ | indrāya pūrvīr uṣaso duhānāḥ | indro yajvane pṛṇate ca śikṣati | upa-id dadāti na svaṃ muṣāyati | bhūyo-bhūyo rayim id asya vardhayan | abhinne khille nidadhāti devayum | na tā naśanti, na tā arvā, |

{ 2.8.8.12} gāvo bhago gāva indro me acchāt {ḍyumoṇṭa acchān}, | gāvaḥ somasya prathamasya bhakṣaḥ | imā yā gāvaḥ sa janāsa indraḥ | icchāmi-id dhṛdā manasā cid indram | yūyaṃ gāvo medayathā kṛśaṃ cit | aślīlaṃ {ḍyumoṇṭa aśrīraṃ} cit kṛṇuthā supratīkam | bhadraṃ gṛhaṃ kṛṇutha bhadravācaḥ | bṛhad vo vaya ucyate sabhāsu | prajāvatīḥ sūyavasaṃ riśantīḥ {ānaṃdā. ruśantīḥ} | śuddhā apa suprapāṇe pibantīḥ | mā vaḥ stena īśata mā-aghaśaṃsaḥ | pari vo hetī rudrasya vṛñjyāt {ḍyumoṇṭa vṛjyāḥ} | upa-idam upaparcanam | āsu goṣu-upapṛcyatām | upa- ṛṣabhasya retasi | upa-indra tava vīrye |

anuvāka 9 { 2.8.9.1} tā sūryācandramasā viśvabhṛttamā {ḍyumoṇṭa |} mahat | tejo vasumad rājato divi | sāmātmānā carataḥ sāmacāriṇā | yayor vrataṃ na mame jātu devayoḥ | ubhāv antau pariyāta armyā {ḍyumoṇṭa ūrmyā?[sic.]} | divo na raśmīṃs tanuto vy arṇave | ubhā bhuvantī bhuvanā kavikratū | sūryā na candrā carato hatāmatī | patī dyumad viśvavidā ubhā divaḥ | sūryā ubhā candramasā vicakṣaṇā |

{ 2.8.9.2} viśvavārā virivo-bhā vareṇyā {ḍyumoṇṭa vareṇyau} | tā no 'vataṃ matimantā mahivratā | viśvavaparī prataraṇā tarantā | suvarvidā dṛśaye bhūriraśmī | sūryā hi candrā vasu tveṣa darśatā | manasvinā-ubhā-anucarato nu saṃ divam | asya śravo nadyaḥ sapta bibhrati | dyāvākṣāmā pṛthivī darśataṃ vapuḥ | asme sūryācandramasobhicakṣe | śraddhe kam indra carato vicarturam |

{ 2.8.9.3} pūrvāparaṃ carato māyayā-etau | śiśū krīḍantau pariyāto adhvaram | viśvāny anyo bhuvanā-abhicaṣṭe | ṛtūn anyo vidadhaj jāyate punaḥ | hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucayaḥ pāvakā, yāsāṃ rājā, | yāsāṃ devāḥ, śivena mā cakṣuṣā paśyata, | āpo bhadrā, ād it paśyāmi, | na-asad āsīn no sad āsīt tadānīm | na-āsīd rajo no vyoma-aparo yat | kim āvarīvaḥ kuha kasya śarman |

{ 2.8.9.4} ambhaḥ kim āsīd gahanaṃ gabhīram | na mṛtyur amṛtaṃ tarhi {ḍyumoṇṭa na mṛtyur āsīd amṛtam na tarhi,} na { haḥ | here} rātriyā ahna āsīt praketaḥ | ānīd avātaṃ svadhayā tad ekam | tasmād dha-anyaṃ na paraḥ kiṃ cana-āsa | tama āsīt tamasā gūḍham agre {ḍyumoṇṭa puts "," here} praketam {ḍyumoṇṭa apraketaṃ} | salilaṃ sarvam ā idam | tucchenābhv {ḍyumoṇṭa tucchena bhv} apihitaṃ yad āsīt | tamasas {ḍyumoṇṭa tapasas} tan mahinā'jāyata-ekam | kāmas tad agre sam avartata-adhi |

{ 2.8.9.5} manaso retaḥ prathamaṃ yad āsīt | sato bandhum asati niravindan | hṛdi pratīṣyā kavayo manīṣā | tiraścīno vitato raśmir eṣām | adhaḥ svid {ānaṃdā. adhasvid} āsī3d upari svid āsī3t | retodhā āsan mahimāna āsan | svadhā avastāt prayatiḥ parastāt | ko addhā veda ka iha pravocat | kuta ājātā kuta iyaṃ visṛṣṭiḥ | arvāg devā asya visarjanāya {ḍyumoṇṭa visarjanena} |

{ 2.8.9.6} athā ko veda yata ābabhūva | iyaṃ visṛṣṭir yata ābabhūva | yadi vā dadhe yadi vā na | yo asyā-adhyakṣaḥ parame vyoman | so aṅga veda yadi vā na veda | kiṃ svid vanaṃ ka u sa vṛkṣa āsīt | yato dyāvāpṛthivī niṣṭatakṣuḥ | manīṣiṇo manasā pṛcchata-id u tat | yad adhyatiṣṭhad bhuvanāni dhārayan | brahma vanaṃ brahma sa vṛkṣa āsīt |

{ 2.8.9.7} yato dyāvāpṛthivī niṣṭatakṣuḥ | manīṣiṇo manasā vibravīmi vaḥ | brahmā-adhyatiṣṭhad bhuvanāni dhārayan | prātar agniṃ prātar indraṃ havāmahe | prātar mitrāvaruṇā prātar aśvinā | prātar bhagaṃ pūṣaṇaṃ brahmaṇaspatim | prātaḥ somam uta rudraṃ huvema | prātar jitaṃ bhagam ugraṃ huvema | vayaṃ putram aditer yo vidhartā | ādhraś cid yaṃ manyamānas turaś cit |

{ 2.8.9.8} rājā cid yaṃ bhagaṃ bhakṣi-ity āha | bhaga praṇetar bhaga satyarādhaḥ | bhaga-imāṃ dhiyam udava dadan naḥ | bhaga pra ṇo janaya gobhir aśvaiḥ | bhaga pra nṛbhir nṛvantaḥ syāma | uta-idānīṃ bhagavantaḥ syāma | uta prapitva uta madhye ahnām | uta-uditā maghavant sūryasya | vayaṃ devānāṃ sumatau syāma | bhaga eva bhagavāṃ astu devāḥ |

{ 2.8.9.9} tena vayaṃ bhagavantaḥ syāma | taṃ tvā bhaga sarva it- johavīmi {ḍyumoṇṭa johavīti} | sa no bhaga pura etā bhava-iha | sam adhvarāya-uṣaso namanta | dadhikrāvā-iva śucaye padāya | arvācīnaṃ vasuvidaṃ bhagaṃ naḥ | ratham iva-aśvā vājina āvahantu | aśvāvatīr gomatīr na uṣāsaḥ | vīravatīḥ sadam ucchantu bhadrāḥ | ghṛtaṃ duhānā viśvataḥ prapīnāḥ | yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ |









prapāṭhaka: 1 anuvāka 1 { 3.1.1.1} agnir naḥ pātu kṛttikāḥ | nakṣatraṃ devam indriyam | idam āsāṃ vicakṣaṇam | havir āsaṃ juhotana | yasya bhānti raśmayo yasya ketavaḥ | yasya-imā viśvā bhuvanāni sarvā | sa kṛttikābhir abhi saṃvasānaḥ | agnir no devaḥ suvite dadhātu | prajāpate rohiṇī vetu patnī | viśvarūpā bṛhatī citrabhānuḥ |

{ 3.1.1.2} sā no yajñasya suvite dadhātu | yathā jīvema śaradaḥ savīrāḥ | rohiṇī devy udagāt purastāt | viśvā rūpāṇi pratimodamānā | prajāpatiṃ haviṣā vardhayantī | priyā devānām upayātu yajñam | somo rājā mṛgaśīrṣeṇa āgann | śivaṃ nakṣatraṃ priyam asya dhāma | āpyāyamāno bahudhā janeṣu | retaḥ prajāṃ yajamāne dadhātu |

{ 3.1.1.3} yat te nakṣatraṃ mṛgaśīrṣam asti | priyaṃ rājan priyatamaṃ priyāṇām | tasmai te soma haviṣā vidhema | śaṃ na edhi dvipade śaṃ catuṣpade | - ārdrayā rudraḥ prathamāna eti | śreṣṭho devānāṃ patir aghniyānām | nakṣatram asya haviṣā vidhema | mā naḥ prajāṃ rīriṣan mā-uta vīrān | hetī rudrasya pari ṇo vṛṇaktu | ārdrā nakṣatraṃ juṣatāṃ havir naḥ |

{ 3.1.1.4} pramuñcamānau duritāni viśvā | apa-aghaśaṃsaṃ nudatām arātim | punar no devy aditiḥ spṛṇotu | punarvasū naḥ punar etāṃ yajñam | punar no devā abhiyantu sarve | punaḥ-punar vo haviṣā yajāmaḥ | evā na {ḍyumoṇṭa evā na} devy aditir anarvā | viśvasya bhartrī jagataḥ pratiṣṭhā | punarvasū haviṣā vardhayantī | priyaṃ devānām apyetu pāthaḥ |

{ 3.1.1.5} bṛhaspatiḥ prathamaṃ jāyamānaḥ | tiṣyaṃ nakṣatram abhi saṃbabhūva | śreṣṭho devānāṃ pṛtanāsu jiṣṇuḥ | diśo 'nu sarvā abhayaṃ no astu | tiṣyaḥ purastād uta madhyato naḥ | bṛhaspatir naḥ paripātu paścāt | bādhetāṃ dveṣo abhayaṃ kṛṇutām | suvīryasya patayaḥ syāma | idaṃ sarpebhyo havir astu juṣṭam | āśreṣā yeṣām anuyanti cetaḥ |

{ 3.1.1.6} ye antarikṣaṃ pṛthivīṃ kṣiyanti | te naḥ sarpāso havam āgamiṣṭhāḥ | ye rocane sūryasya-api sarpāḥ | ye divaṃ devīm anu saṃcaranti | yeṣām āśreṣā anuyanti kāmam | tebhyaḥ sarpebhyo madhumat- juhomi | upahūtāḥ pitaro ye maghāsu | manojavasaḥ sukṛtaḥ sukṛtyāḥ | te no nakṣatre havam āgamiṣṭhāḥ | svadhābhir yajñaṃ prayataṃ juṣantām |

{ 3.1.1.7} ye agnidagdhā ye 'nāgnidagdhāḥ | ye 'muṃ lokaṃ pitaraḥ kṣiyanti | yāṃś ca vidma yāṃ u ca na pravidma | maghāsu yajñaṃ sukṛtaṃ juṣantām | gavāṃ patiḥ phalgunīnām asi tvam | tad aryaman varuṇa mitra cāru | taṃ tvā vayaṃ sanitāraṃ sanīnām | jīvā jīvantam upa saṃviśema | yena-imā viśvā bhuvanāni saṃjitā | yasya devā anusaṃyanti cetaḥ |

{ 3.1.1.8} aryamā rājā-ajaras tuviṣmān | phalgunīnām ṛṣabho roravīti | śreṣṭho devānāṃ bhagavo bhaga-asi | tat tvā viduḥ phalgunīs tasya vittāt | asmabhyaṃ kṣatram ajaraṃ suvīryam | gomad aśvavad upa saṃnuda-iha | bhago ha dātā bhaga it pradātā | bhago devīḥ phalgunīr āviveśa | bhagasya-it taṃ prasavaṃ gamema | yatra devaiḥ sadhamādaṃ madema |

{ 3.1.1.9} āyātu devaḥ savitā-upayātu | hiraṇyayena suvṛtā rathena | vahan hastaṃ subhagaṃ vidmanāpasam | prayacchantaṃ papuriṃ puṇyam accha | hastaḥ prayacchatv amṛtaṃ vasīyaḥ | dakṣiṇena pratigṛbhṇīma enat | dātāram adya savitā videya | yo no hastāya prasuvāti yajñam | tvaṣṭā nakṣatram abhyeti citrām | subhaṃsasaṃ yuvatiṃ rocamānām |

{ 3.1.1.10} niveśayann amṛtān martyāṃś ca | rūpāṇi piṃśan bhuvanāni viśvā | tan nas tvaṣṭā tad u citrā vicaṣṭām | tan nakṣatraṃ bhūridā astu mahyam | tan naḥ prajāṃ vīravatīṃ sanotu | gobhir no aśvaiḥ samanaktu yajñam | vāyur nakṣatram abhyeti niṣṭyām | tigmaśṛṅgo vṛṣabho roruvāṇaḥ | samīrayan bhuvanā mātariśvā | apa dveṣāṃsi nudatām arātīḥ |

{ 3.1.1.11} tan no vāyus tad u niṣṭyā śṛṇotu | tan nakṣattraṃ bhūridā astu mahyam | tan no devāso anujānantu kāmam | yathā tarema duritāni viśvā | dūram asmat-śatravo yantu bhītāḥ | tad indrāgnī kṛṇutāṃ tad viśākhe | tan no devā anumadantu yajñam | paścāt purastād abhayaṃ no astu | nakṣatrāṇām adhipatnī viśākhe | śreṣṭhāv indrāgnī bhuvanasya gopau |

{ 3.1.1.12} viṣūcaḥ śatrūn apabādhamānau | apa kṣudhaṃ nudatām arātim | pūrṇā paścād uta pūrṇā purastāt | ut-madhyataḥ paurṇamāsī jigāya | tasyāṃ devā adhi saṃvasantaḥ | uttame nāka iha mādayantām | pṛthvī suvarcā yuvatiḥ sajoṣāḥ | paurṇamāsy udagāt-śobhamānā | āpyāyayantī duritāni viśvā | uruṃ duhāṃ {ḍyumoṇṭa duhāṃ} yajamānāya yajñam |

anuvāka 2 { 3.1.2.1} ṛdhyāsma havyair namasopasadya | mitraṃ devaṃ mitradheyaṃ no astu | anūrādhān haviṣā vardhayantaḥ | śataṃ jīvema śaradaḥ savīrāḥ | citraṃ nakṣatram udagāt purastāt | anūrādhāsa iti yad vadanti | tan mitra eti pathibhir devayānaiḥ | hiraṇyayair vitatair antarikṣe | indro jyeṣṭhām anu nakṣatram eti | yasmin vṛtraṃ vṛtratūrye tatāra |

{ 3.1.2.2} tasmin vayam amṛtaṃ duhānāḥ | kṣudhaṃ tarema duritiṃ duriṣṭim | purandarāya vṛṣabhāya dhṛṣṇave | aṣāḍhāya sahamānāya mīḍhuṣe | indrāya jyeṣṭhā madhumad duhānā | uruṃ kṛṇotu yajamānāya lokam | mūlaṃ prajāṃ vīravatīṃ videya | parācy etu nirṛtiḥ parācā | gobhir nakṣatraṃ paśubhiḥ samaktam | ahar bhūyād yajamānāya mahyam |

{ 3.1.2.3} ahar no adya suvite dadhātu | mūlaṃ nakṣatram iti yad vadanti | parācīṃ vācā nirṛtiṃ nudāmi | śivaṃ prajāyai śivam astu mahyam | yā divyā āpaḥ payasā saṃbabhūvuḥ | yā antarikṣa uta pārthivīr yāḥ | yāsām aṣāḍhā anuyanti kāmam | tā na āpaḥ śaṃ syonā bhavantu | yāś ca kūpyā yāś ca nādyāḥ samudriyāḥ | yāś ca vaiśantīr uta prāsacīr yāḥ |

{ 3.1.2.4} yāsām aṣāḍhā madhu bhakṣayanti | tā na āpaḥ śaṃ syonā bhavantu | tan no viśve upaśṛṇvantu devāḥ | tad aṣāḍhā abhi saṃyantu yajñam | tan nakṣatraṃ prathatāṃ paśubhyaḥ | kṛṣir vṛṣṭir yajamānāya kalpatām | śubhrāḥ kanyā yuvatayaḥ supeśasaḥ | karmakṛtaḥ sukṛto vīryāvatīḥ | viśvān devān haviṣā vardhayantīḥ | aṣāḍhāḥ kāmam upayāntu yajñam |

{ 3.1.2.5} yasmin brahmābhyajayat sarvam etat | amuṃ ca lokam idamū ca sarvam | tan no nakṣatram abhijid vijitya | śriyaṃ dadhātv ahṛṇīyamānam | ubhau lokau brahmaṇā saṃjitemau | tan no nakṣatram abhijid vicaṣṭām | tasmin vayaṃ pṛtanāḥ saṃjayema | taṃ no devāso anujānantu kāmam | śṛṇvanti śroṇām amṛtasya gopām | puṇyām asyā upaśṛṇomi vācam |

{ 3.1.2.6} mahīṃ devīṃ viṣṇupatnīm ajūryām | pratīcīm enāṃ haviṣā yajāmaḥ | tredhā viṣṇur urugāyo vicakrame | mahīṃ divaṃ pṛthivīm antarikṣam | tat-śroṇā-eti śrava icchamānā | puṇyaṃ ślokaṃ yajamānāya kṛṇvatī | aṣṭau devā vasavaḥ somyāsaḥ | catasro devīr ajarāḥ śraviṣṭhāḥ | te yajñaṃ pāntu rajasaḥ parastāt | saṃvatsarīṇam amṛtaṃ svasti |

{ 3.1.2.7} yajñaṃ naḥ pāntu vasavaḥ purastāt | dakṣiṇato 'bhiyantu śraviṣṭhāḥ | puṇyaṃ nakṣatram abhi saṃviśāma | mā no arātir aghaśaṃsā-āgann | kṣatrasya rājā varuṇo 'dhirājaḥ | nakṣatrāṇāṃ śatabhiṣag vasiṣṭhaḥ | tau devebhyaḥ kṛṇuto dīrgham āyuḥ | ' śataṃ sahasrā bheṣajāni dhattaḥ | yajñaṃ no rājā varuṇa upayātu | tan no viśve abhi saṃyantu devāḥ |

{ 3.1.2.8} tan no nakṣatraṃ śatabhiṣag juṣāṇam { śatabhigjuṣāṇam} | dīrgham āyuḥ pratirad bheṣajāni | aja ekapād udagāt purastāt | viśvā bhūtāni pratimodamānaḥ | tasya devāḥ prasavaṃ yanti sarve | proṣṭhapadāso amṛtasya gopāḥ | vibhrājamānaḥ samidhāna ugraḥ | ā-antarikṣam aruhad agaṃ dyām | taṃ sūryaṃ devam ajam ekapādam | proṣṭhapadāso anuyanti sarve |

{ 3.1.2.9} ahir budhniyaḥ prathamāna eti | śreṣṭho devānām uta mānuṣāṇām | taṃ brāhmaṇāḥ somapāḥ somyāsaḥ | proṣṭhapadāso abhirakṣanti sarve | catvāra ekam abhi karma devāḥ | proṣṭhapadāsa iti yān vadanti | te budhniyaṃ pariṣadyaṃ stuvantaḥ | ahiṃ rakṣanti namasopasadya | pūṣā revaty anveti panthām | puṣṭipatī paśupā vājabastyau |

{ 3.1.2.10} imāni havyā prayatā juṣāṇā | sugair no yānair upayātāṃ yajñam | kṣudrān paśūn rakṣatu revatī naḥ | gāvo no aśvāṃ anvetu pūṣā | annaṃ rakṣantau bahudhā virūpam | vājaṃ sanutāṃ yajamānāya yajñam | tad aśvināv aśvayujā-upayātām | śubhaṃ gamiṣṭhau suyamebhir aśvaiḥ | svaṃ nakṣatraṃ haviṣā yajantau | madhvā saṃpṛktau yajuṣā samaktau |

{ 3.1.2.11} yau devānāṃ bhiṣajau havyavāhau | viśvasya dūtāv amṛtasya gopau | tau nakṣatraṃ jujuṣāṇā-upayātām | namo 'śvibhyāṃ kṛṇumo 'śvayugbhyām | apa pāpmānaṃ bharaṇīr bharantu | tad yamo rājā bhagavān vicaṣṭām | lokasya rājā mahato mahān hi | sugaṃ naḥ panthām abhayaṃ kṛṇotu | yasmin nakṣatre yama eti rājā | yasminn enam abhyaṣiñcanta devāḥ | tad asya citraṃ haviṣā yajāma | apa pāpmānaṃ bharaṇīr bharantu | niveśanī yat te devā adadhuḥ |

anuvāka 3 { 3.1.3.1} navo-navo bhavati jāyamāno {tai.saṃ.2.4.14.} yam ādityā aṃśum āpyāyayanti {tai.saṃ.2.4.14.} | ye virūpe samanasā saṃvyayantī | samānaṃ tantuṃ paritā tanota | vibhū prabhū anubhū viśvato huve | te no nakṣatre havam āgametam | vayaṃ devī brahmaṇā saṃvidānāḥ | suratnāso devavītiṃ dadhānāḥ | ahorātre haviṣā vardhayantaḥ | ati pāpmānam atimuktyā gamema | praty uv adṛśy āyatī (pratyuvadṛśyāyatī) |

{ 3.1.3.2} vyucchantī duhitā divaḥ | apo mahī vṛṇute cakṣuṣā | tamo jyotiṣkṛṇoti sūnarī | ud usriyāḥ sacate sūryaḥ | sacā udyan nakṣatram arcimat | tava-id uṣo vyuṣi sūryasya ca | saṃ bhaktena gamemahi | tan no nakṣatram arcimat | bhānumat teja uccarat | upa yajñam iha-āgamat |

{ 3.1.3.3} pra nakṣatrāya devāya | indrāya-induṃ havāmahe | sa naḥ savitā suvat sanim | puṣṭidāṃ vīravattamam | ud u tyaṃ citram | aditir na uruṣyatu mahīmū ṣu mātaram | idaṃ viṣṇuḥ pra tad viṣṇuḥ | agnir mūrdhā bhuvaḥ | anu no 'dya-anumatir anv id anumate tvam | havyavāhaṃ sviṣṭam |

anuvāka 4 { 3.1.4.1} agnir vā akāmayata | annādo devānāṃ syām iti | sa etam agnaye kṛttikābhyaḥ puroḍāśam aṣṭākapālaṃ niravapat | tato vai so 'nnādo devānām abhavat | agnir vai devānām annādaḥ | yathā ha vā agnir devanām annādaḥ | evaṃ ha vā eṣa manuṣyāṇāṃ bhavati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | agnaye svāhā kṛttikābhyaḥ svāhā | ambāyai svāhā dulāyai svāhā | nitatnyái svāhā-abhrayantyai svāhā | meghayantyai svāhā varṣayantyai svāhā | cupuṇīkāyai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.4.2} prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata | tā asmāt sṛṣṭāḥ parācīr āyann | tāsāṃ rohiṇīm abhyadhyāyat | so 'kāmayata | upa mā-āvarteta {,ānaṃdā. mā varteta) | sam enayā gaccheya-iti | sa etaṃ prajāpataye rohiṇyai caruṃ niravapat | tato vai sā tam upāvartata | sam enayā-agacchata { enayā'gacchata} | upa ha vā enaṃ priyam āvartate | saṃ priyeṇa gacchate | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | prajāpataye svāhā rohiṇyai svāhā rocamānāyai svāhā prajābhyaḥ svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.4.3} somo vā akāmayata | oṣadhīnāṃ rājyam abhijayeyam iti | sa etaṃ somāya mṛgaśīrṣāya śyāmākaṃ caruṃ payasi niravapat | tato vai sa oṣadhīnāṃ rājyam abhyajayat | samānānāṃ ha vai rājyam abhijayati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | somāya svāhā mṛgaśīrṣāya svāhā | invakābhyaḥ svāhā-oṣadhībhyaḥ svāhā | rājyāya svāhā-abhijityai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.4.4} rudro vā akāmayata | paśumānt syām iti | sa etaṃ rudrāya-ārdrāyai praiyaṃgavaṃ { praiyyaṅgavaṃ} caruṃ payasi niravapat | tato vai sa paśumān abhavat | paśumān ha vai bhavati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | rudrāya svāhā-ārdrāyai svāhā | pinvamānāyai svāhā paśubhyaḥ svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.4.5} ṛkṣā vā iyam alomakā-āsīt | sokāmayata | oṣadhībhir vanaspatibhiḥ prajāyeya-iti | sa-etam adityai punarvasubhyāṃ caruṃ niravapat | tato vā iyam oṣadhībhir vanaspatibhiḥ prājāyata | prajāyate ha vai prajayā paśubhiḥ | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti adityai svāhā punarvasubhyām {,ānaṃdā. |} svāhā-ābhūtyai svāhā prajātyai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.4.6} bṛhaspatir vā akāmayata | brahmavarcasī syām iti | sa etaṃ bṛhaspataye tiṣyāya naivāraṃ caruṃ payasi niravapat | tato vai sa brahmavarcasy abhavat | brahmavarcasī ha vai bhavati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | bṛhaspataye svāhā tiṣyāya svāhā | brahmavarcasāya svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.4.7} devāsurāḥ saṃyattā āsann | te devāḥ sarpebhya āśreṣābhya ājye karambhaṃ niravapann | tan etābhir eva devatābhir upānayan | etābhir ha vai devatābhir dviṣantaṃ bhrātṛvyam upanayati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u ca-enad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | sarpebhyaḥ svāhā ''śreṣābhyaḥ svāhā | dandaśūkebhyaḥ svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.4.8} pitaro vā akāmayanta | pitṛloka ṛdhnuyāma-iti | ta etaṃ pitṛbhyo maghābhyaḥ puroḍāśaṃ ṣaṭkapālaṃ niravapann | tato vai te pitṛloka ārdhnuvan | pitṛloke ha vā ṛdhnoti | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | pitṛbhyaḥ svāhā maghābhyaḥ {,ānaṃdā. |} svāhā-anaghābhyaḥ svāhā-agadābhyaḥ {,ānaṃdā. |} svāhā-arundhatībhyaḥ svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.4.9} aryamā vā akāmayata | paśumānt syām iti | sa etam aryamṇe phalgunībhyāṃ caruṃ niravapat | tato vai sa paśumān abhavat | paśumān ha vai bhavati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | aryamṇe svāhā phalgunībhyāṃ svāhā | paśubhyaḥ svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.4.10} bhago vā akāmayata | bhagī śreṣṭhī devānāṃ syām iti | sa etaṃ bhagāya phalgunībhyāṃ caruṃ niravapat | tato vai sa bhagī śreṣṭhī devānām abhavat | bhagī ha vai śreṣṭhī samānānāṃ bhavati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | bhagāya svāhā phalgunībhyāṃ svāhā | śreṣṭhyāya svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.4.11} savitā vā akāmayata śran me devā dadhīrann | savitā syām iti | sa etaṃ savitre hastāya puroḍāśaṃ dvādaśakapālaṃ niravapad āśūnāṃ vrīhīṇām | tato vai tasmai śrad devā adadhata | savitā-abhavat | śraddha vā asmai manuṣyā dadhate | savitā samānānāṃ bhavati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | savitre svāhā hastāya {,ānaṃdā. |} svāhā dadate {ḍyumoṇṭa dadate} svāhā pṛṇate {,ānaṃdā. |} svāhā prayacchate svāhā pratigṛbhṇate svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.4.12} tvaṣṭā vā akāmayata | citraṃ prajāṃ vindeya-iti | sa etaṃ tvaṣṭre citrāyai puroḍāśam aṣṭākapālaṃ niravapat | tato vai sa citraṃ prajām avindata | citraṃ ha vai prajāṃ vindate | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | tvaṣṭre svāhā citrāyai svāhā | caitrāya svāhā prajāyai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.4.13} vāyur vā akāmayata | kāmacāram eṣu lokeṣv abhijayeyam iti | sa etad vāyave niṣṭyāyai gṛṣṭyai dugdhaṃ payo niravapat | tato vai sa kāmacāram eṣu lokeṣv abhyajayat | kāmacāraṃ ha vā eṣu lokeṣv abhijayati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | vāyave svāhā niṣṭyāyai svāhā | kāmacārāya svāhā-abhijityai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.4.14} indrāgnī vā akāmayetām | śreṣṭhyaṃ devānām abhijayeva-iti | tāv etam indrāgnibhyāṃ viśākhābhyāṃ puroḍāśam ekādaśakapālaṃ niravapatām tato vai tau śreṣṭhyaṃ devānām abhyajayatām | śreṣṭhyaṃ ha vai samānānām abhijayati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | indrāgnibhyāṃ svāhā viśākhābhyāṃ svāhā | śreṣṭhyāya svāhā-abhijityai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.4.15} atha-etat paurṇamāsyā ājyaṃ nirvapati | kāmo vai paurṇamāsī | kāma ājyam | kāmena-eva kāmaṃ samardhayati | kṣipram enaṃ sa kāma upanamati | yena kāmena yajate | so 'tra juhoti | paurṇamāsyai svāhā kāmāya svāhā-āgatyai svāhā-iti |

anuvāka 5 { 3.1.5.1} mitro vā akāmayata | mitradheyam eṣu lokeṣv abhijayeyam iti | sa etaṃ mitrāya-anūrādhebhyaś caruṃ niravapat | tato vai sa mitradheyam eṣu lokeṣv abhyajayat | mitradheyaṃ ha vā eṣu lokeṣv abhijayati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | mitrāya svāhā-anūrādhebhyaḥ svāhā | mitradheyāya svāhā-abhijityai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.5.2} indro vā akāmayata | jyaiṣṭhyaṃ devānām abhijayeyam iti | sa etam indrāya jyeṣṭhāyai puroḍāśam ekādaśakapālaṃ niravapan mahāvrīhīṇām | tato vai sa jyaiṣṭhyaṃ devānām abhyajayat | jyaiṣṭhyaṃ ha vai samānānām abhijayati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | indrāya svāhā jyeṣṭhāyai svāhā jyaiṣṭhyāya svāhā-abhijityai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.5.3} prajāpatir vā akāmayata | mūlaṃ prajāṃ vindeya-iti | sa etaṃ prajāpataye mūlāya caruṃ niravapat | tato vai sa mūlaṃ prajām avindata | mūlaṃ ha vai prajāṃ vindate | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | prajāpataye svāhā mūlāya svāhā | prajāyai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.5.4} āpo vā akāmayanta | samudraṃ kāmam abhijayema-iti | tā etam adbhyo 'ṣāḍhābhyaś caruṃ niravapann | tato vai tāḥ samudraṃ kāmam abhyajayan | samudraṃ ha vai kāmam abhijayati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | adbhyaḥ svāhā-aṣāḍhābhyaḥ svāhā | samudrāya svāhā kāmāya svāhā | abhijityai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.5.5} viśve vai devā akāmayanta | anapajayyaṃ jayema-iti | ta etaṃ viśvebhyo devebhyo 'ṣāḍhābhyaś caruṃ niravapan | tato vai te 'napajayyam ajayann | anapajayyaṃ ha vai jayati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | viśvebhyo devebhyaḥ svāhā-aṣāḍhābhyaḥ svāhā | anapajayyāya svāhā jityai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.5.6} brahma vā akāmayata { akāmaya} | brahmalokam abhijayeyam iti | tad etaṃ brahmaṇe 'bhijite caruṃ niravapat | tato vai tad brahmalokam abhyajayat | brahmalokaṃ ha vā abhijayati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | brahmaṇe svāhā-abhijite svāhā | brahmalokāya svāhā-abhijityai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.5.7} viṣṇur vā akāmayata | puṇyaṃ ślokaṃ śṛṇvīya | na mā pāpī kīrtir āgacched iti | sa etaṃ viṣṇave śroṇāyai puroḍāśaṃ trikapālaṃ niravapat | tato vai sa puṇyaṃ ślokam aśṛṇuta | na-enaṃ pāpī kīrtir āgacchat | puṇyaṃ ha vai ślokaṃ śṛṇute | na-enaṃ pāpī kīrtir āgacchati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | viṣṇave svāhā śroṇāyai svāhā | ślokāya svāhā śrutāya svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.5.8} vasavo vā akāmayanta | agraṃ devatānāṃ parīyāma-iti | ta etaṃ vasubhyaḥ śraviṣṭhābhyaḥ puroḍāśam aṣṭākapālaṃ niravapan | tato vai te 'graṃ devatānāṃ paryāyann | agraṃ ha vai samānānāṃ paryeti | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | vasubhyaḥ svāhā śraviṣṭhābhyaḥ svāhā | agrāya svāhā parītyai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.5.9} indro vā akāmayata | dṛḍho 'śithilaḥ syām iti | sa etaṃ varuṇāya śatabhiṣaje bheṣajebhyaḥ puroḍāśaṃ daśakapālaṃ niravapat kṛṣṇānāṃ vrīhīṇām | tato vai sa dṛḍho 'śithilo 'bhavat | dṛḍho ha vā aśithilo bhavati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | varuṇāya svāhā śatabhiṣaje svāhā | bheṣajebhyaḥ svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.5.10} ajo vā ekapād akāmayata | tejasvī brahmavarcasī syām iti | sa etam ajāya-ekapade proṣṭhapadebhyaś caruṃ niravapat | tato vai sa tejasvī brahmavarcasy abhavat | tejasvī ha vai brahmavarcasī bhavati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | ajāya-ekapade svāhā proṣṭhapadebhyaḥ svāhā | tejase svāhā brahmavarcasāya svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.5.11} ahir vai budhniyo 'kāmayata | imāṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ vindeya-iti | sa etam ahaye budhniyāya proṣṭhapadebhyaḥ puroḍāśaṃ bhūmikapālaṃ niravapat | tato vai sa imāṃ pratiṣṭhām avindata | imāṃ ha vai pratiṣṭhāṃ vindate | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | ahaye budhniyāya svāhā proṣṭhapadebhyaḥ svāhā | pratiṣṭhāyai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.5.12} pūṣā vā akāmayata | paśumānt syām iti | sa etaṃ pūṣṇe revatyai caruṃ niravapat | tato vai sa paśumān abhavat | paśumān ha vai bhavati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | pūṣṇe svāhā revatyai svāhā | paśubhyaḥ svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.5.13} aśvinau vā akāmayetām | śrotrasvināv abadhirau syāva-iti | tav etam aśvibhyām aśvayugbhyāṃ puroḍāśaṃ dvikapālaṃ niravapatām | tato vai tau śrotrasvināv abadhirāv abhavatām | śrotrasvī ha vā abadhiro bhavati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | aśvibhyāṃ svāhā-aśvayugbhyāṃ svāhā | śrotrāya svāhā śrutyai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.5.14} yamo vā akāmayata | pitṛṇāṃ rājyam abhijayeyam iti | sa etaṃ yamāyā-apabharaṇībhyaś caruṃ niravapat | tato vai sa pitṛṇāṃ rājyam abhyajayat | samānānāṃ ha vai rājyam abhijayati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | yamāya svāhā-apabharaṇībhyaḥ svāhā | rājyāya svāhā-abhijityai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.5.15} athaitad amāvāsyāyā ājyaṃ nirvapati | kāmo vā amāvāsyā | kāma ājyam | kāmena-eva kāmaṃ samardhayati | kṣipram enaṃ sa kāma upanamati | yena kāmena yajate | so 'tra juhoti | amāvāsyāyai svāhā kāmāya svāhā-āgatyai svāhā-iti |

anuvāka 6 { 3.1.6.1} candramā vā akāmayata | ahorātrān ardhamāsān māsān ṛtūnt saṃvatsaram āptvā | candramasaḥ sāyujyaṃ salokatām āpnuyām iti | sa etaṃ candramase pratīdṛśyāyai puroḍāśaṃ pañcadaśakapālaṃ niravapat | tato vai so 'horātrān ardhamāsān māsān ṛtūnt saṃvatsaram āptvā | candramasaḥ sāyujyaṃ salokatām āpnot | ahorātrān ha vā ardhamāsān māsān ṛtūnt saṃvatsaram āptvā | candramasaḥ sāyujyaṃ salokatām āpnoti | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | candramase svāhā pratīdṛśyāyai svāhā | ahorātrebhyaḥ svāhā-ardhamāsebhyaḥ svāhā | māsebhyaḥ svāhā-ṛtubhyaḥ svāhā | saṃvatsarāya svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.6.2} ahorātre vā akāmayetām | aty ahorātre mucyevahi | na nāv ahorātre āpnuyātām iti | te etam ahorātrābhyāṃ caruṃ niravapatām | dvayānāṃ vrīhīṇām | śuklānāṃ ca kṛṣṇānāṃ ca | savātyor dugdhe | śvetāyai ca kṛṣṇāyai ca | tato vai te aty ahorātre amucyete | na-ene ahorātre āpnutām | ati ha vā ahorātre mucyate | na-enam ahorātre āpnutaḥ | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | ahne svāhā rātriyai svāhā | atimuktyai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.6.3} uṣā vā akāmayata | priyā-ādityasya subhagā syām iti | sa-etam uṣase caruṃ niravapat | tato vai sā priyā-ādityasya subhagā-abhavat | priyo ha vai samānānāṃ subhago bhavati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | uṣase svāhā vyuṣṭyai svāhā | vyūṣuṣyai svāhā vyucchantyai svāhā | vyuṣṭāyai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.6.4} atha-etasmai nakṣatrāya caruṃ nirvapati | yathā tvaṃ devānām asi | evam ahaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ bhūyāsam iti | yathā ha vā etad devānām | evaṃ ha vā eṣa manuṣyāṇāṃ bhavati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | nakṣatrāya svāhā-udeṣyate svāhā | udyate svāhā-uditāya svāhā | harase svāhā bharase svāhā | bhrājase svāhā tejase svāhā | tapase svāhā brahmavarcasāya svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.6.5} sūryo vā akāmayata | nakṣatrāṇāṃ pratiṣṭhā syām iti | sa etaṃ sūryāya nakṣatrebhyaś caruṃ niravapat | tato vai sa nakṣatrāṇāṃ pratiṣṭhā-abhavat | pratiṣṭhā ha vai samānānāṃ bhavati | ya etena haviṣā yajate | ya u cainad evaṃ veda | so 'tra juhoti | sūryāya svāhā nakṣatrebhyaḥ svāhā | pratiṣṭhāyai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.6.6} atha-etam adityai caruṃ nirvapati | iyaṃ vā aditiḥ | asyām eva pratitiṣṭhati | so 'tra juhoti | adityai svāhā pratiṣṭhāyai svāhā-iti |

{ 3.1.6.7} atha-etaṃ viṣṇave caruṃ nirvapati | yajño vai viṣṇuḥ | yajña eva-antataḥ pratitiṣṭhati | so 'tra juhoti | viṣṇave svāhā yajñāya svāhā | pratiṣṭhāyai svāhā-iti |







prapāṭhaka: 2 anuvāka 1 { 3.2.1.1} tṛtīyasyām ito divi soma āsīt | taṃ gāyatry āharat | tasya parṇam acchidyata | tat parṇo 'bhavat | tat parṇasya parṇatvam | brahma vai parṇaḥ | yat parṇaśākhayā vatsān apākaroti | brahmaṇā-evainān apākaroti | gāyatro vai parṇaḥ | gāyatrāḥ paśavaḥ |

{ 3.2.1.2} tasmāt trīṇi-trīṇi parṇasya palāśāni | tripadā gāyatrī | yat parṇaśākhayā gāḥ prārpayati | svayā-evainā devatayā prārpayati | yaṃ kāmayeta-apaśuḥ syād iti | aparṇāṃ tasmai śuṣkāgrām āharet | apaśur eva bhavati | yaṃ kāmayeta paśumānt syād iti | bahuparṇāṃ tasmai bahuśākhām āharet | paśumantam evainaṃ karoti |

{ 3.2.1.3} yat prācīm āharet | devalokam abhijayet | yad udīcīṃ manuṣyalokam | prācīm udīcīm āharati | ubhayor lokayor abhijityai | iṣe tvā-ūrje tvā-ity āha | iṣam eva-ūrjaṃ yajamāne dadhāti | vāyavaḥ stha-ity āha | vāyur vā antarikṣasya-adhyakṣāḥ | antarikṣadevatyāḥ khalu vai paśavaḥ |

{ 3.2.1.4} vāyava evainān paridadāti | pra vā enān etad ākaroti | yad āha | vāyavaḥ stha-ity upāyavaḥ stha-ity āha | yajamānāya-eva paśūn upahvayate | devo vaḥ savitā prārpayatv ity āha prasūtyai | śreṣṭhatamāya karmaṇa ity āha | yajño hi śreṣṭhatamaṃ karma | tasmād evam āha | āpyāyadhvam aghniyā devabhāgam ity āha |

{ 3.2.1.5} vatsebhyaś ca vā etāḥ purā manuṣyebhyaś ca-āpyāyanta | devebhya evainā indrāya-āpyāyati | ūrjasvatīḥ payasvatīr ity āha | ūrjaṃ hi payaḥ saṃbharanti | prajāvatīr anamīvā ayakṣmā ity āha prajātyai | mā vaḥ stena {ānaṃdā. va stena} īśata mā-aghaśaṃsa ity āha guptyai | rudrasya hetiḥ pari vo vṛṇaktv ity āha | rudrād evainās trāyate | dhruvā asmin gopatau syāta bahvīr ity āha | dhruvā evāsmin bahvīḥ karoti |

{ 3.2.1.6} yajamānasya paśūn pāhi-ity āha | paśunāṃ gopīthāya | tasmāt sāyaṃ paśava upa samāvartante | anadhaḥ sādayati | garbhāṇāṃ dhṛtyā aprapādāya | tasmād garbhāḥ prajānām aprapādukāḥ | upari-iva nidadhāti | upari-iva hi suvargo lokaḥ | suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai |

anuvāka 2 { 3.2.2.1} devasya tvā savituḥ prasava ity aśvaparśum ādatte prasūtyai | aśvinor bāhubhyām ity āha | aśvinau hi devānām adhvaryū āstām | pūṣṇo hastābhyām ity āha yatyai | yo vā oṣadhīḥ parvaśo veda | na-enāḥ sa hinasti | prajāpatir vā oṣadhīḥ parvaśo veda | sa enā na hinasti aśvaparśvā barhir acchaiti | prājāpatyo vā aśvaḥ sayonitvāya |

{ 3.2.2.2} oṣadhīnām ahiṃsāyai | yajñasya ghoṣad asi-ity āha | yajamāna eva rayiṃ dadhāti | pratyuṣṭaṃ rakṣaḥ pratyuṣṭā arātaya ity āha | rakṣasām apahatyai | pra-iyam agād dhiṣaṇā barhir acchā-ity āha | vidyā vai dhiṣaṇā | vidyayā-evainad acchaiti | manunā kṛtā svadhayā vitaṣṭa-ity āha | mānavī hi parśuḥ svadhākṛtā |

{ 3.2.2.3} ta āvahanti kavayaḥ purastād ity āha | śuśruvāṃso vai kavayaḥ | yajñaḥ purastāt | mukhata eva yajñam ārabhate | atho yad etad uktvā yataḥ kutaś ca-āharati | tat prācyā eva diśo bhavati | devebhyo juṣṭam iha barhir āsada ity āha | barhiṣaḥ samṛddhyai | karmaṇo 'naparādhāya | devānāṃ pariṣūtam asi-ity āha |

{ 3.2.2.4} yad vā idaṃ kiṃ ca | tad devānāṃ pariṣūtam | atho yathā vasyase pratiprocya-āha-idaṃ kariṣyāmi-iti | evam eva tad adhvaryur devebhyaḥ pratiprocya barhir dāti | ātmano 'hiṃsāyai | yāvataḥ stambān paridiśet | yat teṣām ucchiṃṣyāt | ati tad yajñasya recayet | ekaṃ ṃ stambaṃ paridiśet | taṃ sarvaṃ dāyāt |

{ 3.2.2.5} yajñasya-anatirekāya | varṣavṛddham asi-ity āha | varṣavṛddhā vā oṣadhayaḥ | devabarhir ity āha | devebhya eva-enat karoti | mā tvā-anvaṅ mā tiryag ity āha-ahiṃsāyai | parva te rādhyāsam ity āha-ṛdhyai | ācchettā te mā riṣam ity āha | na-asya-ātmano mīyate | ya evaṃ veda |

{ 3.2.2.6} deva barhiś śatavalśaṃ viroha-ity āha | prajā vai barhiḥ | prajānāṃ prajananāya | sahasravalśā vi vayaṃ ruhema-ity āha | āśiṣam eva-etām āśāste | pṛthivyāḥ saṃpṛcaḥ pāhi-ity āha pratiṣṭhityai | ayuṃ gāyuṃ gān {ḍyumoṇṭa ayuṅgāyuṅgān} muṣṭīn-lunoti | mithunatvāya prajātyai | susaṃbhṛtā tvā saṃbharāmi-ity āha | brahmaṇā-evainat saṃbharati |

{ 3.2.2.7} adityai rāsnā-asi-ity āha | iyaṃ vā aditiḥ | asyā evainad rāsnāṃ karoti | indrāṇyai saṃnahanam ity āha | indrāṇī vā agre devatānāṃ samanahyata | sordhnot | ṛddhyai saṃnahyati | prajā vai barhiḥ | prajānām aparāvāpāya | tasmāt snāvasaṃtatāḥ prajā jāyante

{ 3.2.2.8} pūṣā te granthiṃ grathnātv ity āha | puṣṭim eva yajamāne dadhāti | sa te mā sthād ity āha-ahiṃsāyai |paścāt prāñcam upagūhati | paścād vai prācīnaṃ reto dhīyate | paścād evāsmai prācīnaṃ reto dadhāti | indrasya tvā bāhubhyām udyaccha ity āha | indriyam eva yajamāne dadhāti | bṛhaspater mūrdhnā harāmi-ity āha | brahma vai devānāṃ bṛhaspatiḥ |

{ 3.2.2.9} brahmaṇā-evainad dharati | urv antarikṣam anvihītyāha gatyai | devaṃgamam asīty āha | devān eva-enad gamayati | anadhaḥ sādayati | garbhāṇāṃ dhṛtyā aprapādāya | tasmād garbhāḥ prajānām aprapādukāḥ | uparīva nidadhāti | uparīva hi suvargo lokaḥ | suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai |

anuvāka 3 { 3.2.3.1} pūrvedyur idhmābarhiḥ karoti | yajñam evā''rabhya gṛhītvā-upavasati | prajāpatir yajñam asṛjata | tasya-ukhe asraṃsetām | yajño vai prajāpatiḥ | yat sāṃnāyya-ukhe bhavataḥ | yajñasyaiva tad ukhe upadadhāty aprasraṃsāya | śundhadhvaṃ daivyāya karmaṇe devayajyāyā ity āha | devayajyāyā eva-enāni śundhati | mātariśvano gharmo 'si-ity āha |

{ 3.2.3.2} antarikṣaṃ vai mātariśvano gharmaḥ | eṣāṃ lokānāṃ vidhṛtyai | dyaur asi pṛthivyasīty āha | divaś ca hy eṣā pṛthivyāś ca saṃbhṛtā | yad ukhā | tasmād evam āha | viśvadhāyā asi parameṇa dhāmnā-ity āha | vṛṣṭir vai viśvadhāyāḥ | vṛṣṭim eva-avarundhe | dṛṃhasva mā hvār ity āha dhṛtyai |

{ 3.2.3.3} vasūnāṃ pavitram asīty āha | prāṇā vai vasavaḥ | teṣāṃ vā etad bhāgadheyam | yat pavitram | tebhya eva-enat karoti | śatadhāraṃ sahasradhāram ity āha | prāṇeṣv evāyur dadhāti sarvatvāya | trivṛt palāśaśākhāyāṃ darbhamayaṃ bhavati | trivṛd vai prāṇaḥ | trivṛtam eva prāṇaṃ madhyato yajamāne dadhāti |

{ 3.2.3.4} saumyaḥ parṇaḥ sayonitvāya | sākṣāt pavitraṃ darbhāḥ | prāk sāyam adhinidadhāti | tat prāṇāpānayo rūpam | tiryak prātaḥ | tad darśasya rūpam | dārśyaṃ hy etad ahaḥ | annaṃ vai candramāḥ | annaṃ prāṇāḥ | ubhayam eva-upaity ajāmitvāya |

{ 3.2.3.5} tasmād ayaṃ sarvataḥ pavate | hutaḥ stoko huto drapsa ity āha pratiṣṭhityai | haviśo 'skandāya | na hi hutaṃ svāhākṛtaṃ skandati | divi nāko nāmāgniḥ | tasya vipruṣo bhāgadheyam | agnaye bṛhate nākāya-ity āha | nākam evāgniṃ bhāgadheyena samardhayati | svāhā dyāvāpṛthivībhyām ity āha | dyāvāpṛthivyor eva-enat pratiṣṭhāpayati |

{ 3.2.3.6} pavitravaty ānayati | apāṃ caiva-oṣadhīnāṃ ca rasaṃ saṃsṛjati | atho oṣadhīṣv eva paśūn pratiṣṭhāpayati | anvārabhya vācaṃ yacchati | yajñasya dhṛtyai | dhārayann āste | dhārayanta iva hi duhanti | kām adhukṣa ity āha-ā tṛtīyasyai | traya ime lokāḥ | imān eva lokān yajamāno duhe |

{ 3.2.3.7} amūm iti nāma gṛhṇāti | bhadram evāsāṃ karmāviṣkaroti | sā viśvāyuḥ sā viśvavyacāḥ sā viśvakarma-ity āha | iyaṃ vai viśvāyuḥ | antarikṣaṃ viśvavyacāḥ | asau viśvakarmā | imān eva-etābhir lokān yathāpūrvaṃ duhe | atho yathā pradātre puṇyam āśāste | evam eva-enā etad upastauti | tasmāt prādād ity unnīya vandamānā upastuvantaḥ paśūn duhanti |

{ 3.2.3.8} bahu dugdhīndrāya devebhyo havir iti vācaṃ visṛjate | yathādevatam eva prasauti | daivyasya ca mānuṣasya ca vyāvṛttyai | trir āha | triṣatyā hi devāḥ | avācaṃyamo 'nanvārabhya-uttarāḥ | aparimitam evāvarundhe | na dārupātreṇa duhyāt | agnivad vai dārupātram | yad dārupātreṇa duhyāt |

{ 3.2.3.9} yātayāmnā haviṣā yajeta | atho khalv āhuḥ | puroḍāśamukhāni vai havīṃṣi | na-itaitaḥ puroḍāśaṃ haviṣo yāmo 'stīti | kāmam eva dārupātreṇa duhyāt | śūdra eva na duhyāt | asato vā eṣa saṃbhūtaḥ | yat-śūdraḥ | ahavir eva tad ity āhuḥ | yat-śūdro dogdhīti |

{ 3.2.3.10} agnihotram eva na duhyāt-śūdraḥ | tad dhi na-utpunanti | yadā khalu vai pavitram atyeti | atha tad dhavir iti | saṃpṛcyadhvam ṛtāvarīr ity āha | apāṃ caiva-oṣadhīnāṃ ca rasaṃ saṃsṛjati | tasmād apāṃ ca-oṣadhīnāṃ ca rasam upajīvāmaḥ | mandrā dhanasya sātaya ity āha | puṣṭim eva yajamāne dadhāti | somena tvātanacmīndrāya dadhīty āha |

{ 3.2.3.11} somam eva-enat karoti | yo vai somaṃ bhakṣayitvā | saṃvatsaraṃ somaṃ na pibati | punarbhakṣyo 'sya somapītho bhavati | somaḥ khalu vai sāṃnāyyam | ya evaṃ vidvānt sāṃnāyyaṃ pibati | apunarbhakṣyo 'sya somapītho bhavati | na mṛnmayenāpidadhyāt | yan mṛnmayenāpidadhyāt | pitṛdevatyaṃ syāt |

{ 3.2.3.12} ayaspātreṇa vā dārupātreṇa vā-apidadhāti | tad dhi sadevam | udanvad bhavati | āpo vai rakṣoghnīḥ | rakṣasām apahatyai | adastam asi viṣṇave tvā-ity āha | yajño vai viṣṇuḥ | yajñāya-eva-enad adastaṃ karoti | viṣṇo havyaṃ rakṣasva-ity āha guptyai | anadhaḥ sādayati | garbhāṇāṃ dhṛtyā aprapādāya | tasmād garbhāḥ prajānām aprapādukāḥ | uparīva nidadhāti | uparīva hi suvargo lokaḥ | suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai |

anuvāka 4 { 3.2.4.1} karmaṇe vāṃ devebhyaś śakeyam ity āha śaktyai | yajñasya vai saṃtatim anu prajāḥ paśavo yajamānasya saṃtāyante | yajñasya vicchittim anu prajāḥ paśavo yajamānasya vicchidyante | yajñasya saṃtatir asi yajñasya tvā saṃtatyai stṛṇāmi saṃtatyai tvā yajñasya-ity ā-āhavanīyāt saṃtanoti | yajamānasya prajāyai paśūnāṃ saṃtatyai | apaḥ praṇayati | śraddhā vā āpaḥ śraddhām evā''rabhya praṇīya pracarati | apaḥ praṇayati | yajño vā āpaḥ |

{ 3.2.4.2} yajñam evā''rabhya praṇīya pracarati | apaḥ praṇayati | vajro vā āpaḥ | vajram eva bhrātṛvyebhyaḥ prahṛtya praṇīya pracarati | apaḥ praṇayati | āpo vai rakṣoghnīḥ | rakṣasām apahatyai | apaḥ praṇayati | āpo vai devānāṃ priyaṃ dhāma | devānām eva priyaṃ dhāma praṇīya pracarati |

{ 3.2.4.3} apaḥ praṇayati | āpo vai sarvā devatāḥ | devatā evārabhya praṇīya pracarati | veṣāya tvā-ity āha | veṣāya hy enad ādatte | pratyuṣṭaṃ rakṣaḥ pratyuṣṭā arātaya ity āha | rakṣasām apahatyai | dhūr asīty āha | eṣa vai dhuryo 'gniḥ | taṃ yad anupaspṛśyātīyāt |

{ 3.2.4.4} adhvaryuṃ ca yajamānaṃ ca pradahet | upaspṛśyātyeti | adhvaryoś ca yajamānasya cāpradāhāya | dhūrva {ānaṃdā. dhūrvan} taṃ yo 'smān dhūrvati taṃ dhūrva yaṃ vayaṃ dhūrvāma ity āha | dvau vāva puruṣau | yaṃ caiva dhūrvati | yas cainaṃ dhūrvati | tāv ubhau śucā-arpayati | tvaṃ devānām asi sasnitamaṃ papritamaṃ juṣṭatamaṃ vahnitamaṃ devahūtamam ity āha | yathāyajur eva-etat |

{ 3.2.4.5} ahrutam asi havirdhānam ity āha-anārtyai | dṛṃhasva mā hvār ity āha dhṛtyai | mitrasya tvā cakṣuṣā prekṣa ity āha mitratvāya | mā bher mā saṃvikthā mā tvā hiṃsiṣam ity āhāhiṃsāyai | yad vai kiṃ ca vāto na-abhivāti | tat sarvaṃ varuṇadevatyam | uru vātāya-ity āha | avāruṇam eva-enat karoti | devasya tvā savituḥ prasava ity āha prasūtyai | aśvinor bāhubhyām ity āha |

{ 3.2.4.6} aśvinau hi devānām adhvaryū āstām | pūṣṇo hastābhyām ity āha | yatyai | agnaye juṣṭaṃ nirvapāmīty āha | agnaya eva-enāñ juṣṭaṃ nirvapati | trir yajuṣā | traya ime lokāḥ | eṣāṃ lokānām āptyai | tūṣṇīṃ caturtham | aparimitam eva-avarundhe | sa evam eva-anupūrvaṃ havīṃṣi nirvapati |

{ 3.2.4.7} idaṃ devānām idam u naḥ saha-ity āha vyāvṛttyai | sphātyai tvā na-arātyā ity āha guptyai | tamasīva vā eṣo 'ntaś carati | yaḥ parīṇahi | suvar abhi vikhyeṣaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ jyotir ity āha | suvar eva-abhi vipaśyati vaiśvānaraṃ jyotiḥ | dyāvāpṛthivī haviṣi gṛhīta udavepetām | dṛṃhantāṃ duryā dyāvāpṛthivyor ity āha | gṛhāṇāṃ dyāvāpṛthivyor dhṛtyai | urv antarikṣam anvihīty āha gatyai | adityās tvā-upasthe sādayāmīty āha | iyaṃ vā aditiḥ | asyā eva-enad upasthe sādayati | agne havyaṃ rakṣasva-ity āha guptyai |

anuvāka 5 { 3.2.5.1} indro vṛtram ahann | so 'paḥ | abhyamriyata | tāsāṃ yan medhyaṃ yajñiyaṃ sadevam āsīt | tad apodakrāmat | te darbhā abhavann | yad darbhair apa utpunāti | yā eva medhyā yajñiyāḥ sadevā āpaḥ | tābhir eva-enā utpunāti | dvābhyām utpunāti |

{ 3.2.5.2} dvipād yajamānaḥ pratiṣṭhityai | devo vaḥ savitā-utpunātv ity āha | savitṛprasūta eva-enā utpunāti | acchidreṇa pavitreṇā-ity āha | asau vā adityo 'cchidraṃ pavitram | tena-eva-enā utpunāti | vasoḥ sūryasya raśmibhir ity āha | prāṇā vā āpaḥ { |} prāṇā vasavaḥ | prāṇā raśmayaḥ |

{ 3.2.5.3} prāṇair eva prāṇānt saṃpṛṇakti | sāvitriyā-ṛcā | savitṛprasūtaṃ me karmāsad iti | savitṛprasūtam eva-asya karma bhavati | paccho gāyatriyā triṣṣamṛddhatvāya | āpo devīr agrepuvo agreguva ity āha | rūpam evā''sām etan mahimānaṃ vyācaṣṭe | agra imaṃ yajñaṃ nayatāgre yajñapatim ity āha | agre eva yajñaṃ nayanti | agre yajñapatim |

{ 3.2.5.4} yuṣmān indro 'vṛṇīta vṛtratūrye yūyam indram avṛṇīdhvaṃ vṛtratūrya ity āha | vṛtraṃ ha haniṣyann indra āpo vavre | āpo ha-indraṃ vavrire | saṃjñām evā''sām etat sāmānaṃ vyācaṣṭe | prokṣitāḥ stha-ity āha | tenāpaḥ prokṣitāḥ | agnaye vo juṣṭaṃ prokṣāmy agnīṣomābhyām ity āha | yathādevatam eva-enān prokṣati | triḥ prokṣati | tryāvṛd dhi yajñaḥ |

{ 3.2.5.5} atho rakṣasām apahatyai | śundhadhvaṃ daivyāya karmaṇe devayajyāyā ity āha | devayajyāyā eva-enāni śundhati | triḥ prokṣati | tryāvṛd dhi yajñaḥ | atho medhyatvāya | avadhūtaṃ rakṣo 'vadhūtā arātaya ity āha | rakṣasām apahatyai | adityās tvag asīty āha | iyaṃ vā aditiḥ |

{ 3.2.5.6} asyā eva-enat tvacaṃ karoti | prati tvā pṛthivī vettv {ānaṃdā. vetv} ity āha pratiṣṭhityai | purastāt pratīcīnagrīvam uttaraloma-upastṛṇāti medhyatvāya | tasmāt purastāt pratyañcaḥ paśāvo medham upatiṣṭhante | tasmāt prajā mṛgaṃ grāhukāḥ | yajño devebhyo nilāyata | kṛṣṇo rūpaṃ kṛtvā | yat kṛṣṇājine havir adhyavahanti | yajñād eva tad yajñaṃ prayuṅkte | haviṣo 'skandāya |

{ 3.2.5.7} adhiṣavaṇam asi vānaspatyam ity āha | adhiṣavaṇam eva-enat karoti | prati tvā-ādityās tvag vettv {ānaṃdā. vetv} ity āha sayatvāya | agnes tanūr asīty āha | agner vā eṣā tanūḥ | yad oṣadhayaḥ | vāco visarjanam ity āha | yadā hi prajā oṣadhīnām aśnanti | atha vācaṃ visṛjante | devavītaye tvā gṛhṇāmīty āha |

{ 3.2.5.8} devatābhir eva-enat samardhayati | adrir asi vānaspatya ity āha | grāvāṇam eva-enat karoti | sa idaṃ devebhyo havyaṃ suśami śamiṣva-ity āha śāntyai | haviṣkṛd ehīty āha | ya eva devānāṃ haviṣkṛtaḥ | tān hvyati | trir hvayati | triṣatyā hi devāḥ | iṣam āvada-ūrjam āvada-ity āha |

{ 3.2.5.9} iṣam eva-ūrjaṃ yajamāne dadhāti | dyumad vadata vayaṃ saṃghātaṃ jeṣma-ity āha bhrātṛvyābhibhūtyai | manoḥ śraddhādevasya yajamānasya-asuraghnī vāk | yajñāyudheṣu praviṣṭāsīt | te 'surā yāvanto yajñāyudhānām udvadatām upāśṛṇvann | te parābhavan | tasmāt svānāṃ madhye 'vasāya yajeta | yāvanto 'sya bhrātṛvyā yajñāyudhānām udvadatām upaśṛṇvanti | te parābhavanti | uccaiḥ samāhantavā āha vijityai |

{ 3.2.5.10} vṛṅkta eṣām indriyaṃ vīryam | śreṣṭha eṣāṃ bhavati | varṣavṛddham asi prati tvā varṣavṛddhaṃ vettv {ānaṃdā. vetv} ity āha | varṣavṛddhā vā oṣadhayaḥ | varṣavṛddhā iṣīkāḥ samṛddhyai | yajñaṃ rakṣāṃsy anu prāviśan | tāny asnā paśubhyo niravādayanta | tuṣair oṣadhībhyaḥ | parāpūtaṃ rakṣaḥ parāpūtā arātaya ity āha | rakṣasām apahatyai |

{ 3.2.5.11} rakṣasāṃ bhāgo 'sīty āha | tuṣair eva rakṣāṃsi niravadayate | apa upaspṛśati medhyatvāya | vāyur vo vivinaktv ity āha | pavitraṃ vai vāyuḥ | punāty eva-enān | antarikṣād iva vā ete praskandanti | ye śūrpāt | devo vaḥ savitā hiraṇyapāṇiḥ pratigṛhṇātv ity āha pratiṣṭhityai | haviṣo 'skandāya | triṣ phalīkartavā āha | tryāvṛd dhi yajñaḥ | atho medhyatvāya |

anuvāka 6 { 3.2.6.1} avadhūtaṃ rakṣo 'vadhūtā arātaya ity āha | rakṣasām apahatyai | adityās tvag asīty āha | iyaṃ vā aditiḥ | asyā eva-enat tvacaṃ karoti | prati tvā pṛthivī vettv {ānaṃdā. vetv} ity āha pratiṣṭhityai | purastāt pratīcīnagrīvam uttaraloma-upastṛṇāti medhyatvāya | tasmāt purastāt pratyañcaḥ paśavo medham upatiṣṭhante | tasmāt prajā mṛgaṃ grāhukāḥ | yajño devebhyo nilāyata |

{ 3.2.6.2} kṛṣṇo rūpaṃ kṛtvā | yat kṛṣṇājine havir adhipinaṣṭi | yajñād eva tad yajñaṃ prayuṅkte | haviṣo 'skandāya | dyāvāpṛthivī saha-āstām | te śamyāmātram ekam ahar vyaitāṃ śamyāmātram ekam ahaḥ | divaḥ skambhanir asi prati tvā-ādityās tvag vettv {ānaṃdā. vetv} ity āha | dyāvāpṛthivyor vītyai | dhiṣaṇāsi parvatyā prati tvā divaḥ skambhanir vettv {ānaṃdā. vetv} ity āha | dyāvāpṛthivyor vidhṛtyai |

{ 3.2.6.3} dhiṣaṇāsi pārvateyī prati tvā parvatir vettv {ānaṃdā. vetv} ity āha | dyāvāpṛthivyor dhṛtyai | devasya tvā savituḥ prasava ity āha prasūtyai | aśvinor bāhubhyām ity āha | aśvinau hi devānām adhvaryū āstām | pūṣṇo hastābhyām ity āha yatyai | adhivapāmīty āha | yathādevatam eva-enān adhivapati | dhānyam asi dhinuhi devān ity āha | etasya yajuṣo vīryeṇa |

{ 3.2.6.4} yāvad ekā devatā kāmayate yāvad ekā | tāvad āhutiḥ prathate | na hi tad asti | yat tāvad eva syāt | yāvaj juhoti | prāṇāya tvā-apānāya tvā-ity āha | prāṇān eva yajamāne dadhāti | dīrghām anu prasitim āyuṣe dhām ity āha | āyur eva-asmin dadhāti | antarikṣād iva vā etāni praskandanti | yāni dṛṣadaḥ | devo vaḥ savitā hiraṇyapāniḥ prati gṛhṇātv ity āha pratiṣṭhityai | haviṣo 'skandāya | asaṃvapantī piṃṣā-aṇū́ni kurutād ity āha medhyatvāya |

anuvāka 7 { 3.2.7.1} dhṛṣṭir asi brahma yaccha-ity āha dhṛtyai | apāgne 'gnim āmādaṃ jahi niṣkravyādaṃ sedha-ā devayajaṃ vaha-ity āha | ya evā''māt kravyāt | tam apahatya | medhye 'gnau kapālam upadadhāti | nirdagdhaṃ rakṣo nirdagdhā arātaya ity āha | rakṣāṃsy eva nirdahati | agnivatyupadadhāti | asminn eva loke jyotir dhatte | aṅgāram adhivartayati |

{ 3.2.7.2} antarikṣa eva jyotir dhatte | ādityam eva-amuṣmin loke jyotir dhatte | jyotiṣmanto 'smā ime lokā bhavanti | ya evaṃ veda | dhruvam asi pṛthivīṃ dṛṃha-ity āha | pṛthivīm eva-etena dṛṃhati | dhartram asy antarikṣaṃ dṛṃha-ity āha | antarikṣam eva-etena dṛṃhati | dharuṇam asi divaṃ dṛṃha-ity āha | divam eva-etena dṛṃhati |

{ 3.2.7.3} dharmāsi diśo dṛṃha-ity āha | diśa eva-etena dṛṃhati | imān eva-etair lokān dṛṃhati | dṛṃhante 'smā ime lokāḥ prajayā paśubhiḥ | ya evaṃ veda | trīṇy agre kapālāny upadadhāti | traya ime lokāḥ | eṣāṃ lokānām āptyai | ekam agre kapālam upadadhāti | ekaṃ vā agre kapālaṃ puruṣasya saṃbhavati |

{ 3.2.7.4} atha dve | atha trīṇi | atha catvāri | atha-aṣṭau | tasmād aṣṭākapālaṃ puruṣasya śiraḥ | yad evaṃ kapālāny upadadhāti | yajño vai prajāpatiḥ | yajñam eva prajāpatiṃ saṃskaroti | ātmānam eva tat saṃskaroti | taṃ saṃskṛtam ātmānam |

{ 3.2.7.5} amuṣmin-loke 'nu paraiti | yad aṣṭāv upadadhāti | gāyatriyā tat saṃmitam | yan nava | trivṛtā tat | yad daśa | virājā tat | yad ekādaśa | triṣṭubhā tat | yad dvādaśa |

{ 3.2.7.6} jagatyā tat | chandaḥsaṃmitāni sa upadadhat kapālāni | imān lokān anupūrvaṃ diśo vidhṛtyai dṛṃhati | atha-āyuḥ prāṇān prajāṃ paśūn yajamāne dadhāti | sajātān asmā abhito bahulān karoti | citaḥ stha-ity āha | yathāyajur eva-etat | bhṛgūṇām aṅgirasāṃ tapasā tapyadhvam ity āha | devatānām eva-enāni tapasā tapati | tāni tataḥ saṃsthite | yāni gharme kapālāny upacinvanti vedhasa iti catuṣpadayā-ṛcā vimuñcati | catuṣpādaḥ paśavaḥ | paśuṣv eva-upariṣṭāt pratitiṣṭhati |

anuvāka 8 { 3.2.8.1} devasya tvā savituḥ prasava ity āha prasūtyai | aśvinor bāhubhyām ity āha | aśvinau hi devānām adhvaryū āstām | pūṣṇo hastābhyām ity āha yatyai | saṃvapāmīty āha | yathādevatam eva-enāni saṃvapati | sam āpo adbhir agmata sam oṣadhayo rasena-ity āha | āpo vā oṣadhīr jinvanti | oṣadhayo 'po jinvanti | anyā vā etāsām anyā jinvanti |

{ 3.2.8.2} tasmād evam āha | saṃ revatīr jagatībhir madhumatīr madhumatībhiḥ sṛjyadhvam ity āha | āpo vai revatīḥ | paśavo jagatīḥ | oṣadhayo madhumatīḥ | āpa oṣadhīḥ paśūn | tān eva-asmā ekadhā saṃsṛjya | madhumataḥ karoti | adbhyaḥ pari prajātāḥ stha samadbhiḥ pṛcyadhvam iti paryāplāvayati | yathā suvṛṣṭa imām anuvisṛtya |

{ 3.2.8.3} āpa oṣadhīr mahayanti | tādṛg eva tat | janayatyai tvā saṃyaumīty āha | prajā eva-etena dādhāra | agnaye tvā-agnīṣomābhyām ity āha vyāvṛttyai | makhasya śiro 'sīty āha | yajño vai makhaḥ | tasyaitat-śiraḥ | yat puroḍāśaḥ | tasmād evam āha |

{ 3.2.8.4} gharmo 'si viśvāyur ity āha | viśvam eva-āyur yajamāne dadhāti | uru prathasva-uru te yajñapatiḥ prathatām ity āha | yajamānam eva prajayā paśubhiḥ prathayati | tvacaṃ gṛhṇīṣva-ity āha | sarvam eva-enaṃ satanuṃ karoti | athāpa ānīya pari mārṣṭi | māṃsa eva tat tvacaṃ dadhāti | tasmāt tvacā māṃsaṃ channam | gharmo vā eṣo 'śāntaḥ |

{ 3.2.8.5} ardhamāse-'rdhamāse pravṛjyate | yat puroḍāśaḥ | sa īśvaro yajamānaṃ śucā pradahaḥ | paryagni karoti | paśum eva-enam akaḥ | śāntyā apradāhāya | triḥ paryagni karoti | tryāvṛd dhi yajñaḥ | atho rakṣasām apahatyai | antaritaṃ rakṣo 'ntaritā arātaya ity āha |

{ 3.2.8.6} rakṣasām antarhityai | puroḍāśaṃ vā adhiśritaṃ rakṣāṃsy ajighāṃsan | divi nāko nāmāgnī rakṣohā | sa eva-asmād rakṣāṃsy apāhann | devas tvā savitā śrapayatv ity āha | savitṛprasūta eva-enaṃ śrapayati | varṣiṣṭhe adhi nāka ity āha | rakṣasām apahatyai | agnis te tanuvaṃ mā-atidhāg ity āhānatidāhāya | agne havyaṃ rakṣasva-ity āha guptyai |

{ 3.2.8.7} avidahantaś śrapayata-iti vācaṃ visṛjate | yajñam eva havīṃśy abhivyāhṛtya pratanute | purorucam avidāhāya śṛtyai karoti | mastiṣko vai puroḍāśaḥ | taṃ yan na-abhivāsayet | āvir mastiṣkaḥ syāt | abhivāsayati | tasmād guhā mastiṣkaḥ | bhasmanā-abhivāsayati | tasmān māṃsenāsthi channam |

{ 3.2.8.8} vedenābhivāsayati | tasmāt keśaiḥ śiraś channam | akhalatibhāvuko bhavati | ya evaṃ veda | paśor vai pratimā puroḍāśaḥ | sa na-ayajuṣkam abhivāsyaḥ | vṛthā-iva syāt | īśvarā yajamānasya paśavaḥ prametoḥ | saṃ brahmaṇā pṛcyasva-ity āha | prāṇā vai brahma |

{ 3.2.8.9} prāṇāḥ paśavaḥ | prāṇair eva paśūnt saṃpṛṇakti | na pramāyukā bhavanti | yajamāno vai puroḍāśaḥ | prajā paśavaḥ purīṣam | yad evam abhivāsayati | yajamānam eva prajayā paśubhiḥ samardhayati | devā vai havir bhṛtvā {ḍyumoṇṭa mṛtvā?}-abruvann | kasminn idaṃ mrakṣyāmaha iti | so 'gnir abravīt |

{ 3.2.8.10} mayi tanūḥ saṃ nidhaddhvam { nidhadhvam} | ahaṃ vas taṃ janayiṣyāmi | yasmin mrakṣyadhva iti | te devā agnau tanūḥ saṃ nyadadhata | tasmād āhuḥ | agniḥ sarvā devatā iti | so 'ṅgāreṇāpaḥ | abhyapātayat | tata ekato 'jāyata | sa dvitīyam abhyapātayat |

{ 3.2.8.11} tato dvito 'jāyata | sa tṛtīyam abhyapātayat | tatas trito 'jāyata | yad adbhyo 'jāyanta | tad āpyānām āpyatvam | yad ātmabhyo 'jāyanta | tad ātmyānām ātmyatvam | te devā āpyeṣv amṛjata | āpyā amṛjata sūryābhyudite | sūryābhyuditaḥ sūryābhinimrukte |

{ 3.2.8.12} sūryābhinimruktaḥ kunakhini | kunakhī śyāvadati | śyāvadann agradidhiṣau | agradidhiṣuḥ parivitte | parivitto vīrahaṇi | vīrahā brahmahaṇi tad brahmahaṇaṃ na-atyacyavata | antarvedi ninayaty avarudhyai | ulmukena-abhigṛhṇāti śṛtatvāya | śṛtakāmā iva hi devāḥ |

anuvāka 9 { 3.2.9.1} devasya tvā savituḥ prasava iti sphyam ādatte prasūtyai | aśvinor bāhubhyām ity āha | aśvinau hi devānām adhvaryū āstām | pūṣṇo hastābhyām ity āha yatyai | ādada indrasya bāhur asi dakṣiṇa ity āha | indriyam eva yajamāne dadhāti | sahasrabhṛṣṭiḥ śatatejā ity āha | rūpam eva-asyaitan mahimānaṃ vyācaṣṭe | vāyur asi tigmatejā ity āha | tejo vai vāyuḥ |

{ 3.2.9.2} teja eva-asmin dadhāti | viṣād vai nāma-āsura āsīt | so 'bibhet | yajñena mā devā abhibhaviṣyantīti | sa pṛthivīm abhyavamīt | somedhyā-abhavat | atho yad indro vṛtram ahan | tasya lohitaṃ pṛthivīm anu vyadhāvat | somedhyā-abhavat | pṛthivi devayajanīty āha |

{ 3.2.9.3} medhyām eva-enāṃ devayajanīṃ karoti | oṣadhyās te mūlaṃ mā hiṃsiṣam ity āha | oṣadhīnām ahiṃsāyai | vrajaṃ gaccha gosthānam ity āha | chandāṃsi vai vrajo gosthānaḥ | chandāṃsy eva-asmai vrajaṃ gosthānaṃ karoti | varṣatu te dyaur ity āha | vṛṣṭir vai dyauḥ | vṛṣṭim eva-avarundhe | badhāna deva savitaḥ paramasyāṃ parāvatīty āha |

{ 3.2.9.4} dvau vāva puruṣau | yaṃ caiva dveṣṭi | yaś cainaṃ dveṣṭi | tāv ubhau badhnāti paramasyāṃ parāvati śatena pāśaiḥ | yo 'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣmas tam ato mā maug ity āhānimruktyai {ḍyumoṇṭa āhānirmuktyai} | ararur vai nāma-āsura āsīt | sa pṛthivyām upamlupto 'śayat | taṃ devā apahato 'raruḥ pṛthivyā iti pṛthivyā apāghnann | bhrātṛvyo vā araruḥ | apahato 'raruḥ pṛthivyā iti yad āha |

{ 3.2.9.5} bhrātṛvyam eva pṛthivyā apahanti | te 'manyanta | divaṃ vā ayam itaḥ patiṣyatīti | tam araruste divaṃ mā skān iti divaḥ paryabādhanta | bhrātṛvyo vā araruḥ | ararus te divaṃ mā skān iti yad āha | bhrātṛvyam eva divaḥ paribādhate | stambayajur harati | pṛthivyā eva bhrātṛvyam apahanti | dvitīyaṃ harati |

{ 3.2.9.6} antarikṣād eva-enam apahanti | tṛtīyaṃ harati | diva eva-enam apahanti | tūṣṇīṃ caturthaṃ harati | aparimitād eva-enam apahanti | asurāṇāṃ vā iyam agra āsīt | yāvad āsīnaḥ parāpaśyati | tāvad devānām | te devā abruvan | astv eva no 'syām apīti |

{ 3.2.9.7} kyan no dāsyatha-iti | yāvat svayaṃ parigṛhṇītha-iti | te vasavas tvā-iti dakṣiṇataḥ paryagṛhṇann | rudrās tvā-iti paścāt | ādityās tvā-ity uttarataḥ | te 'gninā prāñco 'jayann | vasubhir dakṣiṇā | rudraiḥ pratyañcaḥ | ādityair udañcaḥ | yasyaivaṃ viduṣo vediṃ parigṛhṇanti |

{ 3.2.9.8} bhavaty ātmanā | parāsya bhrātṛvyo bhavati | devasya savituḥ sava ity āha prasūtyai | karma kṛṇvanti vedhasa ity āha | iṣitaṃ hi karma kriyate | pṛthivyai medhyaṃ cāmedhyaṃ ca vyudakrāmatām | prācīnam udīcīnaṃ medhyam | pratīcīnaṃ dakṣiṇā-amedhyam | prācīm udīcīṃ pravaṇāṃ karoti | medhyām eva-enāṃ devayajanīṃ karoti |

{ 3.2.9.9} prāñcau vedyaṃsāv unnayati | āhavanīyasya parigṛhītyai | pratīcī śroṇī | gārhapatyasya parigṛhītyai | atho mithunatvāya | uddhanti | yad evāsyā amedhyam | tad apahanti | uddhanti tasmād oṣadhayaḥ parābhavanti |

{ 3.2.9.10} mūlaṃ cchinatti | bhrātṛvyasyaiva mūlaṃ cchinatti | mūlaṃ vā atitiṣṭhad rakṣāṃsy anu-utpipate | yad dhastena chindyāt | kunakhinīḥ prajāḥ syuḥ | sphyena cchinatti | vajro vai sphyaḥ | vajreṇaiva yajñād rakṣāṃsy apahanti | pitṛdevatyā-atikhātā | iyatīṃ khanati |

{ 3.2.9.11} prajāpatinā yajñamukhena saṃmitām | vedir devebhyo nilāyata | tāṃ caturaṅgule 'nvavindann | tasmāc caturaṅgulaṃ kheyā | caturaṅgulaṃ khanati | caturaṅgule hy oṣadhayaḥ pratitiṣṭhanti | ā pratiṣṭhāyai khanati | yajamānam eva pratiṣṭhāṃ gamayati | dakṣiṇato varṣīyasīṃ karoti | devayajanasyaiva rūpam akaḥ |

{ 3.2.9.12} purīṣavatīṃ karoti | prajā vai paśavaḥ purīṣam | prajayā-evainaṃ paśubhiḥ purīṣavantaṃ karoti | uttaraṃ parigrāhaṃ parigṛhṇāti | etāvatī vai pṛthivī | yāvatī vediḥ | tasyā etāvata eva bhrātṛvyaṃ nirbhajya | ātmana uttaraṃ parigrāhaṃ parigṛhṇāti | ṛtam asy ṛtasadanam asy ṛtaśrīr asīty āha | yathāyajur evaitat |

{ 3.2.9.13} krūram iva vā etat karoti | yad vediṃ karoti | dhā asi svadhā asīti yoyupyate śāntyai | urvī cāsi vasvī cāsīty āha | urvīm evaināṃ vasvīṃ karoti | purā krūrasya visṛpo virapśinn ity āha medhyatvāya | udādāya pṛthivīṃ jīradānur yām airayan candramasi svadhābhir ity āha | yad evāsyā amedhyam | tad apahatya | medhyāṃ devayajanīṃ kṛtvā |

{ 3.2.9.81} yad adaś candramasi medhyam | tad asyām erayati | tāṃ dhīrāso anudṛśya yajanta ity āhānukhyātyai | {j} prokṣaṇīr āsādaya | idhmābarhir upasādaya | sruvaṃ ca srucaś ca saṃmṛḍḍhi | patnīṃ saṃnahya | ājyena-udehīty āhānupūrvatāyai | prokṣaṇīr āsādayati | āpo vai rakṣoghnīḥ |

{ 3.2.9.15} rakṣasām apahatyai | sphyasya vartmant sādayati | yajñasya saṃtatyai | uvāca hāsito daivalaḥ | etāvatīr vā amuṣmin-loka āpa āsann | yāvatīḥ prokṣaṇīr iti | tasmād bahvīr āsādyāḥ | sphyam udasyan | yaṃ dviṣyāt taṃ dhyāyet | śucā-evainam arpayati |

anuvāka 10 { 3.2.10.1} vajro vai sphyaḥ | yad anvañcaṃ dhārayet | vajre 'dhvaryuḥ kṣaṇvīta | purastāt tiryañcaṃ dhārayati | varjo vai sphyaḥ | vajreṇaiva yajñasya dakṣiṇato rakṣāṃsy apahanti | agnibhyāṃ prācaś ca pratīcaś ca | sphyena-udīcaś ca-adharācaś ca | sphyena vā eṣa vajreṇāsyai pāpmānaṃ bhrātṛvyam apahatya | utkare 'dhi pravṛścati |

{ 3.2.10.2} yathā-upadhāya vṛścanty evam | hastāv avanenikte | ātmānam eva pavayate | sphyaṃ prakṣālayati medhyatvāya | atho pāpmana eva bhrātṛvyasya nyaṅgaṃ cchinatti | idhmābarhir upasādayati yuktyai | yajñasya mithunatvāya | atho purorucam evaitāṃ dadhāti | uttarasya karmaṇo 'nukhyātyai | na purastāt pratyag upasādayet |

{ 3.2.10.3} yat purastāt pratyag upasādayet | anyatra-āhutipathād idhmaṃ pratipādayet | prajā vai barhiḥ | aparādhnuyād barhiṣā prajānāṃ prajananam | paścāt prāg upasādayati | āhutipathena-idhmaṃ pratipādayati | saṃpraty eva barhiṣā prajānāṃ prajananam upaiti | dakṣiṇam idhmam | uttaraṃ barhiḥ | ātmā vā idhmaḥ | prajā barhiḥ | prajā hy ātmana uttaratarā tīrthe | tato medham upanīya | yathādevatam evainat pratiṣṭhāpayati | pratitiṣṭhati prajayā paśubhir yajamānaḥ |







prapāṭhaka: 3 anuvāka 1 { 3.3.1.1} pratyuṣṭaṃ rakṣaḥ pratyuṣṭā arātaya ity āha | rakṣasām apahatyai | agner vas tejiṣṭhena tejasā niṣṭapāmīty āha medhyatvāya | srucaḥ saṃmārṣṭi | sruvam agre | pumāṃsam eva-abhyaḥ saṃśyati mithunatvāya | atha juhūm | atha-upabhṛtam | atha dhruvām | asau vai juhūḥ |

{ 3.3.1.2} antarikṣam upabhṛt | pṛthivī dhruvā | ime vai lokāḥ srucaḥ | vṛṣṭiḥ saṃmārjanāni | vṛṣṭir vā imān- lokān anupūrvaṃ kalpayati | te tataḥ klṛptāḥ samedhante | samedhante 'smā ime lokāḥ prajayā paśubhiḥ | ya evaṃ veda | yadi kāmayeta vaṣukaḥ parjanyaḥ syād iti | agrataḥ saṃmṛjyāt |

{ 3.3.1.3} vṛṣṭim eva niyacchati | arvācīnāgrā { avācīnāgrā} hi vṛṣṭiḥ | yadi kāmayetāvarṣukaḥ syād iti | mūlataḥ saṃmṛjyāt | vṛṣṭim eva-udyacchati | tad u vā āhuḥ | agrata eva-upariṣṭāt saṃmṛjyāt | mūlato 'dhastāt | tad anupūrvaṃ kalpate | varṣuko bhavatīti |

{ 3.3.1.4} prācīm abhyākāram | agrair antarataḥ | evam iva hy annam adyate | atho agrād vā oṣadhīnāmūrjaṃ prajā upajīvanti | ūrja eva-annādyasyāvarudhyai {ānaṃdā. -āvaruddhyai} | adhastāt pratīcīm | daṇḍam uttamataḥ | mūlena mūlaṃ pratiṣṭhityai | tasmād aratnau prāñcy upariṣṭāl lomāni | pratyañcy adhastāt |

{ 3.3.1.5} srug ghy eṣā | prāṇo vai sruvaḥ | juhūr dakṣiṇo hastaḥ | upabhṛt savyaḥ | ātmā dhruvā | annaṃ saṃmārjanāni | mukhato vai prāṇo 'pāno bhūtvā | ātmānam annaṃ praviśya | bāhyatas tanuvaṃ śubhayati | tasmāt sruvam eva-agre saṃmārṣṭi | mukhato hi prāṇo 'pāno bhūtvā | ātmānam annam āviśati | tau prāṇāpāṇau | avyardhukaḥ prāṇāpānābhyāṃ bhavati | ya evaṃ veda |

anuvāka 2 { 3.3.2.1} divaḥ śilpam avatatam | pṛthivyāḥ kakubhi śritam | tena vayaṃ sahasravalśena | sapatnaṃ nāśayāmasi svāhā-iti sruksaṃmārjanāny agnau praharati | āpo vai darbhāḥ | rūpam eva-eṣām etan mahimānaṃ vyācaṣṭe | anuṣṭubhā-ṛcā | ānuṣṭubhaḥ prajāpatiḥ | prājāpatyo vedaḥ | vedasyāgraṃ sruksaṃmārjanāni |

{ 3.3.2.2} svena-eva-enāni chandasā | svayā devatayā samardhayati | atho ṛg vāva yoṣā | darbho vṛṣā | tan mithunam | mithunam eva-asya tad yajñe karoti prajananāya | prajāyate prajayā paśubhir yajamānaḥ | tāny eke vṛthā-evāpāsyanti | tat tathā na kāryam | ārabdhasya yajñiyasya karmaṇaḥ sa vidohaḥ |

{ 3.3.2.3} yady enāni paśavo 'bhitiṣṭheyuḥ | na tat paśubhyaḥ kam | adbhir mārjayitvā-utkare nyasyet | yad vai yajñiyasya karmaṇo 'nyatra-āhutībhyaḥ saṃtiṣṭhate | utkaro vāva tasya pratiṣṭhā | etāṃ hi tasmai pratiṣṭhāṃ devāḥ samabharan | yad adbhir mārjayati | tena śāntam | yad utkare nyasyati | pratiṣṭhām eva-enāni tad gamayati |

{ 3.3.2.4} pratitiṣṭhati prajayā paśubhir yajamānaḥ | atho stambasya vā etad rūpam | yat sruksaṃmārjanāni | stambaśo vā oṣadhayaḥ | tāsāṃ jaratkakṣe paśavo na ramante | apriyo hy eṣāṃ jaratkakṣaḥ | yāvadapriyo ha vai jaratkakṣaḥ paśūnām | tāvadapriyaḥ paśūnāṃ bhavati | yasyaitāny anyatrāgner dadhati | navadāvyāsu vā oṣadhīṣu paśavo ramante |

{ 3.3.2.5} navadāvo hy eṣāṃ priyaḥ | yāvatpriyo ha vai navadāvaḥ paśūnām | tāvatpriyaḥ paśūnāṃ bhavati | yasyaitāny agnau praharanti | tasmād etāny agnāv eva praharet | yatarasmint saṃmṛjyāt | paśūnāṃ dhṛtyai | yo bhūtānām adhipatiḥ | rudras tanticaro vṛṣā | paśūn asmākaṃ mā hiṃsīḥ | etad astu hutaṃ tava svāhā-ity agnisaṃmārjanāny agnau praharati | eṣā vā eteṣāṃ yoniḥ | eṣā pratiṣṭhā | svām eva-enāni yonim | svāṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ gamayati | pratitiṣṭhati prajayā paśubhir yajamānaḥ |

anuvāka 3 { 3.3.3.1} ayajño vā eṣaḥ | yo 'patnīkaḥ | na prajāḥ prajāyeran | patny anvāste | yajñam eva-akaḥ | prajānāṃ prajananāya | yat tiṣṭhantī saṃnahyeta | priyaṃ jñātiṃ rundhyāt | āsīnā saṃnahyate | āsīnā hy eṣā vīryaṃ karoti |

{ 3.3.3.2} yat paścāt prācy anvāsīta | anayā samadaṃ dadhīta | devānāṃ patniyā samadaṃ dadhīta | deśād dakṣiṇata udīcy anvāste | ātmano gopīthāya | āśāsānā saumanasam ity āha | medhyām eva-enāṃ kevalīṃ kṛtvā | āśiṣā samardhayati | agner anuvratā bhūtvā saṃnahye sukṛtāya kam ity āha | etad vai patniyai vrata-upanayanam |

{ 3.3.3.3} tena-evaināṃ vratam upanayati | tasmād āhuḥ | yaś caivaṃ veda yaś cana | yoktram eva yute | yam anvāste | tasyāmuṣmin-loke bhavatīti yoktreṇa | yad yoktram | sa yogaḥ | yad āste | sa kṣemaḥ |

{ 3.3.3.4} yogakṣemasya klṛptyai | yuktaṃ kriyātā āśīḥ kāme yujyātā iti | āśiṣaḥ samṛddhyai | granthiṃ grathnāti | āśiṣa eva-asyāṃ parigṛhṇāti | pumān vai granthiḥ | strī patnī | tan mithunam | mithunam eva-asya tad yajñe karoti prajananāya | prajāyate prajayā paśubhir yajamānaḥ |

{ 3.3.3.5} atho ardho vā eṣa ātmanaḥ | yat patnī | yajñasya dhṛtyā aśithilaṃbhāvāya | suprajasas tvā vayaṃ supatnīr upasedima-ity āha | yajñam eva tan mithunī karoti | ūne 'tiriktaṃ dhīyātā iti prajātyai | mahīnāṃ payo 'sy oṣadhīnāṃ rasa ity āha | rūpam evāsyaitan mahimānaṃ vyācaṣṭe | tasya te 'kṣīyamāṇasya nirvapāmi devayajyāyā ity āha | āśiṣam evaitām āśāste |

anuvāka 4 { 3.3.4.1} ghṛtaṃ ca vai madhu ca prajāpatir āsīt | yato madhv āsīt {,ānaṃdā. āsīt} | tataḥ prajā asṛjata | tasmān madhuṣi prajananam ivāsti | tasmān madhuṣā na pracaranti | yātayāma hi | ājyena pracaranti | yajño vā ājyam | yajñena-eva yajñaṃ pracaranty ayātayāmatvāya | patny avekṣate |

{ 3.3.4.2} mithunatvāya prajātyai | yad vai patnī yajñasya karoti | mithunaṃ tat | atho patniyā eva-eṣa yajñasyānvārambho 'navacchittyai | amedhyaṃ vā etat karoti | yat patny avekṣate | gārhapatye 'dhiśrayati medhyatvāya | āhavanīyam abhyuddravati | yajñasya saṃtatyai | tejo 'si tejo 'nu prehīty āha |

{ 3.3.4.3} tejo vā agniḥ | teja ājyam | tejasā-eva tejaḥ samardhayati | agnis te tejo mā vinaid ity āha-ahiṃsāyai | sphyasya vartmant sādayati | yajñasya saṃtatyai | agner jihvā-asi subhūr devānām ity āha | yathāyajur eva-etat | dhāmne-dhāmne devebhyo yajuṣe-yajuṣe bhava-ity āha | āśiṣam eva-etām āśāste |

{ 3.3.4.4} tad vā ataḥ pavitrābhyām eva-utpunāti | yajamāno vā ājyam | prāṇāpānau pavitre | yajamāna eva prāṇāpānau dadhāti | punarāhāram | evam iva hi prāṇāpānau saṃcarataḥ | śukram asi jyotir asi tejo 'sīty āha | rūpam eva-asyaitan mahimānaṃ vyācaṣṭe | trir yajuṣā | traya ime lokāḥ |

{ 3.3.4.5} eṣāṃ lokānām āptyai | triḥ | tryāvṛd dhi yajñaḥ | atho medhyatvāya | atha-ājyavatībhyām apaḥ | rūpam evāsām etad varṇaṃ dadhāti | api vā uta-āhuḥ | yathā ha vai yoṣā suvarṇaṃ hiraṇyaṃ peśalaṃ bibhratī rūpāṇy āste | evam etā etarhīti | āpo vai sarvā devatāḥ |

{ 3.3.4.6} eṣā hi viśveṣāṃ devānāṃ tanūḥ | yad ājyam | tatra-ubhayor mīmāṃsā | jāmiḥ { jāmi} syāt | yad yajuṣā-ājyaṃ yajuṣā-apa utpunīyāt | chandasopa utpunāty ajāmitvāya | atho mithunatvāya | sāvitriyā-ṛcā | savitṛprasūtaṃ me karmāsad iti | savitṛprasūtam evāsya karma bhavati | paccho gāyatriyā triḥṣamṛddhatvāya | adbhir eva-oṣadhīḥ saṃnayati | oṣadhībhiḥ paśūn | paśubhir yajamānam | śukraṃ tvā śukrāyāṃ jyotis tvā jyotiṣy arcis tvā-arciṣīty āha sarvatvāya | paryāptyā anantarāyāya |

anuvāka 5 { 3.3.5.1} devāsurāḥ saṃyāttā āsan | sa etam indra ājyasyāvakāśam apaśyat | tenāvaikṣata | tato devā abhavan | parā-asurāḥ | ya evaṃ vidvān ājyam avekṣate | bhavaty ātmanā | parā-asya bhrātṛvyo bhavati | brahmavādino vadanti | yadājyenānyāni havīṃṣy abhighārayati |

{ 3.3.5.2} atha kena-ājyam iti | satyena-iti brūyāt | cakṣur vai satyam | satyenaivainad abhighārayati | īśvaro vā eṣo 'ndho bhavitoḥ | yaś cakṣuṣā-ājyam avekṣate | nimīlyāvekṣeta | dādhāra-ātman cakṣuḥ | abhy ājyaṃ ghārayati | ājyaṃ gṛhṇāti |

{ 3.3.5.3} chandāṃsi vā ājyam | chandāṃsy eva prīṇāti | catur juhvāṃ gṛhṇāti | catuṣpādaḥ paśavaḥ | paśūn eva-avarundhe | aṣṭāv upabhṛti | aṣṭākṣarā gāyatrī | gāyatraḥ prāṇaḥ | prāṇam eva paśuṣu dadhāti | catur dhruvāyām |

{ 3.3.5.4} catuṣpādaḥ paśavaḥ | paśuṣv eva-upariṣṭāt pratitiṣṭhati | yajamānadevatyā vai juhūḥ | bhrātṛvyadevatyā-upabhṛt | catur juhvāṃ gṛhṇan bhūyo gṛhṇīyāt | aṣṭāv upabhṛti gṛhṇan kanīyaḥ | yajamānāya-eva bhrātṛvyam upastiṃ karoti | gaur vai srucaḥ | catur juhvāṃ gṛhṇāti | tasmāc catuṣpadī |

{ 3.3.5.5} aṣṭāv upabhṛti | tasmād aṣṭāśaphā | catur dhruvāyām | tasmāc catustanā | gām eva tat saṃskaroti | sosmai saṃskṛteṣamūrjaṃ duhe | yaj juhvāṃ gṛhṇāti | prayājebhyas tat | yad upabhṛti | prayājānūyājebhyas tat | sarvasmai vā etad yajñāya gṛhyate | yad dhruvāyām ājyam |

anuvāka 6 { 3.3.6.1} āpo devīr agrepuvo agreguva ity āha | rūpam evā''sām etan mahimānaṃ vyācaṣṭe | agra imaṃ yajñaṃ nayatāgre yajñapatim ity āha | agra eva yajñaṃ nayanti | agre yajñapatim | yuṣmān indro 'vṛṇīta vṛtratūrye yūyam indram avṛṇīdhvaṃ vṛtratūrya ity āha | vṛtraṃ ha haniṣyann indra āpo vavre | āpo ha-indraṃ vavrire | saṃjñām evā''sām etat sāmānaṃ vyācaṣṭe | prokṣitāḥ stha-ity āha |

{ 3.3.6.2} tena-āpaḥ prokṣitāḥ | agnir devebhyo nilāyata | kṛṣṇo rūpaṃ kṛtvā | sa vanaspatīn prāviśat | kṛṣṇo 'sy ākhareṣṭho 'gnaye tvā svāhā-ity āha | agnaya eva-enaṃ juṣṭaṃ karoti | atho agner eva medham avarundhe | vedir asi barhiṣe tvā svahā-ity āha | prajā vai barhiḥ | pṛthivī vediḥ |

{ 3.3.6.3} prajā eva pṛthivyāṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati | barhir asi srugbhyas tvā svāhā-ity āha | prajā vai barhiḥ | yajamānaḥ srucaḥ | yajamānam eva prajāsu pratiṣṭhāpayati | dive tvā-antarikṣāya tvā pṛthivyai tvā-iti barhir āsādya prokṣati | ebhya evainaṃ-lokebhyaḥ prokṣati | atha tataḥ saha srucā purastāt pratyañcaṃ granthiṃ pratyukṣati | prajā vai barhiḥ | yathā sūtyai kāla āpaḥ purastād yanti |

{ 3.3.6.4} tādṛg eva tat | svadhā pitṛbhya ity āha | svadhākāro hi pitṛṇām | ūrgbhava barhiṣadbhya iti dakṣiṇāyai śroṇer ā-uttarasyai ninayati saṃtatyai | māsā vai pitaro barhiṣadaḥ | māsān eva prīṇāti | māsā vā oṣadhīr vardhayanti | māsāḥ pacanti samṛddhyai | anatiskandan ha parjanyo varṣati | yatra-etad evaṃ kriyate |

{ 3.3.6.5} ūrjā pṛthivīṃ gacchata-ity āha | pṛthivyām eva-ūrjaṃ dadhāti | tasmāt pṛthivyā ūrjā bhuñjate | granthiṃ visraṃsayati | prajanayaty eva tat | ūrdhvaṃ prāñcam udgūḍhaṃ pratyañcam āyacchati | tasmāt prācīnaṃ reto dhīyate | pratīcīḥ prajā jāyante | viṣṇoḥ stūpo 'sīty āha | yajño vai viṣṇuḥ |

{ 3.3.6.6} yajñasya dhṛtyai | purastāt prastaraṃ gṛhṇāti | mukhyam evainaṃ karoti | iyantaṃ gṛhṇāti | prajāpatinā yajñamukhena saṃmitam | iyantaṃ gṛhṇāti | yajñaparuṣā saṃmitam | iyantaṃ gṛhṇāti | etāvad vai puruṣe vīryam | vīryasaṃmitam |

{ 3.3.6.7} aparimitaṃ gṛhṇāti | aparimitasyāvaruddhyai | tasmin pavitre apisṛjati | yajamāno vai prastaraḥ | prāṇāpānau pavitre | yajamāna eva prāṇāpānau dadhāti | ūrṇāmradasaṃ tvā stṛṇāmīty āha | yathāyajur evaitat | svāsasthaṃ devebhya ity āha | devebhya evainat svāsasthaṃ karoti |

{ 3.3.6.8} barhiḥ stṛṇāti | prajā vai barhiḥ | pṛthivī vediḥ | prajā eva pṛthivyāṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati | anatidṛśnaṃ stṛṇāti | prajaya-evainaṃ paśubhir anatidṛśnaṃ karoti | dhārayan prastaraṃ paridhīn paridadhāti | yajamāno vai prastaraḥ | yajamāna eva tat svayaṃ paridhīn paridadhāti | gandharvo 'si viśvāvasur ity āha |

{ 3.3.6.9} viśvam eva-āyur yajamāne dadhāti | indrasya bāhurasi dakṣiṇa ity āha | indriyam eva yajamāne dadhāti | mitrāvaruṇau tvā-uttarataḥ paridhattām ity āha | prāṇāpānau mitrāvaruṇau | prāṇāpānāv evāsmin dadhāti | sūryas tvā purastāt pātv ity āha | rakṣasām apahatyai | kasyāś cid abhiśas tyā ity āha | aparimitād evainaṃ pāti |

{ 3.3.6.10} vītihotraṃ tvā kava ity āha | agnim eva hotreṇa samardhayati | dyumantaṃ samidhīmahīty āha samiddhyai | agne bṛhantam adhvara ity āha vṛddhyai | viśo yantre stha ity āha | viśāṃ yatyai | udīcīnāgre nidadhāti pratiṣṭhityai | vasūnāṃ rudrāṇām ādityānāṃ sadasi sīda-ity āha | devatānām eva sadane prastaraṃ sādayati | juhūr asi ghṛtācī nāmnā-ity āha |

{ 3.3.6.11} asau vai juhūḥ | antarikṣam upabhṛt | pṛthivī dhruvā | tāsām etad eva priyaṃ nāma | yad ghṛtācīti | yad ghṛtācīty āha | priyeṇaivainā nāmnā sādayati | etā asadant sukṛtasya loka ity āha | satyaṃ vai sukṛtasya lokaḥ | satya evaināḥ sukṛtasya loke sādayati | tā viṣṇo pāhīty āha | yajño vai viṣṇuḥ | yajñasya dhṛtyai | pāhi yajñaṃ pāhi yajñapatiṃ pāhi māṃ yajñaniyam ity āha | yajñāya yajamānāya-ātmane | tebhya evā''śiṣam āśāste 'nārtyai |

anuvāka 7 { 3.3.7.1} agninā vai hotrā | devā asurān abhyabhavan | agnaye samidhyamānāyānubrūhīty āha bhrātṛvyābhibhūtyai | ekaviṃśatim idhmadārūṇi bhavanti | ekaviṃśo vai puruṣaḥ | puruṣasya-āptyai | pañcadaśa-idhmadārūṇy abhyādadhāti | pañcadaśa vā ardhamāsasya rātrayaḥ | ardhamāsaśaḥ saṃvatsara āpyate | trīn paridhīn paridadhāti |

{ 3.3.7.2} ūrdhve samidhāv ādadhāti | anūyājebhyaḥ samidhamatiśinaṣṭi | ṣaṭ saṃpadyante | ṣaḍ vā ṛtavaḥ | ṛtūn eva prīṇāti | vedena-upavājayati | prājāpatyo vai vedaḥ | prājāpatyaḥ prāṇaḥ | yajamāna āhavanīyaḥ | yajamāna eva prāṇaṃ dadhāti |

{ 3.3.7.3} trir upavājayati | trayo vai prāṇāḥ | prāṇān eva-asmin dadhāti | vedena-upayatya sruveṇa prājāpatyam āghāram āghārayati | yajño vai prajāpatiḥ | yajñam eva prajāpatiṃ mukhata ārabhate | atho prajāpatiḥ sarvā devatāḥ | sarvā eva devatāḥ prīṇāti | agnim agnīt tris-triḥ saṃmṛḍḍhīty { saṃmṛḍhḍhīty} āha | tryāvṛd dhi yajñaḥ |

{ 3.3.7.4} atho rakṣasām apahatyai | paridhīnt saṃmārṣṭi | punāty evainān | tris-triḥ saṃmārṣṭi | tryāvṛd dhi yajñaḥ | atho medhyatvāya | atho ete vai devāśvāḥ | devāśvān eva tat saṃmārṣṭi | suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai | āsīno 'nyam āghāram āghārayati |

{ 3.3.7.5} tiṣṭhann anyam | yathā-ano vā rathaṃ vā yuñjyāt | evam eva tad adhvaryur yajñaṃ yunakti | suvargasya lokasyābhyūḍhyai | vahanty enaṃ grāmyāḥ paśavaḥ | ya evaṃ veda | bhuvanam asi viprathasva-ity āha | yajño vai bhuvanam | yajña eva yajamānaṃ prajayā paśubhiḥ prathayati | agne yaṣṭar idaṃ nama ity āha |

{ 3.3.7.6} agnir vai devānāṃ yaṣṭā | ya eva devānāṃ yaṣṭā | tasmā eva namaskaroti | juhv ehy agnis tvā hvayati devayajyāyā upabhṛd ehi devas tvā savitā hvayati devayajyāyā ity āha | āgneyī vai juhūḥ | sāvitry upabhṛt | tābhyām evaine prasūta ādatte | agnāviṣṇū mā-āvām avakramiṣam ity āha | agniḥ purastāt | viṣṇur yajñaḥ paścāt |

{ 3.3.7.7} tābhyām eva pratiprocyātyākrāmati | vijihāthāṃ mā mā saṃtāptam ity āhāhiṃsāyai | lokaṃ me lokakṛtau kṛṇutam ity āha | āśiṣam evaitām āśāste | viṣṇoḥ sthānam asīty āha | yajño vai viṣṇuḥ | etat khalu vai devānām aparājitam āyatanam | yad yajñaḥ | devānām eva-aparājita āyatane tiṣṭhati | ita indro akṛṇod vīryāṇīty āha |

{ 3.3.7.8} indriyam eva yajamāne dadhāti | samārabhya-ūrdhvo adhvaro divispṛśam ity āha vṛddhyai | āghāram āghāryamāṇam anu samārabhya | etasmin kāle devāḥ suvargaṃ lokam āyan | sākṣād eva yajamānaḥ suvargaṃ lokam eti | atho samṛddhenaiva yajñena yajamānaḥ suvargaṃ lokam eti | ahruto {BI ahnuto} yajño yajñapater ity āhānārtyai | indrāvānt svāhā-ity āha | indriyam eva yajamāne dadhāti | bṛhad bhā ity āha |

{ 3.3.7.9} suvargo vai loko bṛhad bhāḥ | suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai yajamānadevatyā vai juhūḥ | bhrātṛvyadevatyā-upabhṛt | prāṇa āghāraḥ | yat saṃsparśayet | bhrātṛvye 'sya prāṇaṃ dadhyāt | asaṃsparśayann atyākrāmati | yajamāna eva prāṇaṃ dadhāti | pāhi mā-agne duścaritād ā mā sucarite bhaja-ity āha |

{ 3.3.7.10} agnir vāva pavitram | vṛjinam anṛtaṃ duścaritam | ṛjukarmaṃ satyaṃ sucaritam | agnir evainaṃ vṛjinād anṛtād duścaritāt pāti | ṛjukarme satye sucarite bhajati tasmād evam āśāste | ātmano gopīthāya | śiro vā etad yajñasya | yad āghāraḥ | ātmā dhruvā |

{ 3.3.7.11} āghāram āghārya dhruvāṃ samanakti | ātmann eva yajñasya śiraḥ pratidadhāti | dviḥ samanakti | dvau hi prāṇāpānau | tad āhuḥ | trir eva samañjyāt | tridhātu hi śira iti | śira iva-etad yajñasya | atho trayo vai prāṇāḥ | prāṇān evāsmin dadhāti | makhasya śiro 'si saṃ jyotiṣā jyotir aṅktām ity āha | jyotir evāsmā upariṣṭād dadhāti | suvargasya lokasyānukhyātyai |

anuvāka 8 { 3.3.8.1} dhiṣṇiyā vā ete nyupyante | yad brahmā | yad hotā | yad adhvaryuḥ | yad agnīt | yad yajamānaḥ | tān yad antareyāt | yajamānasya prāṇānt saṃkarṣet | pramāyukaḥ syāt | puroḍāśam apagṛhya saṃcaraty adhvaryuḥ |

{ 3.3.8.2} yajamānāya-eva tal lokaṃ śiṃṣati | na-asya prāṇānt saṃkarṣati | na pramāyuko bhavati | purastāt pratyaṅṅ āsīnaḥ | iḍāyā iḍām ādadhāti | hastyāṃ hotre | paśavo vā iḍā | paśavaḥ puruṣaḥ | paśuṣv eva paśūn pratiṣṭhāpayati | iḍāyai vā eṣā prajātiḥ |

{ 3.3.8.3} tāṃ prajātiṃ yajamāno 'nu prajāyate | dvir aṅgulāv anakti parvaṇoḥ | dvipād yajamānaḥ pratiṣṭhityai | sakṛd upastṛṇāti | dvir ādadhāti | sakṛd abhighārayati | catuḥ saṃpadyate | catvāri vai paśoḥ pratiṣṭhānāni | yāvān eva paśuḥ | tam upahvayate |

{ 3.3.8.4} mukham iva praty upahvayeta | saṃmukhān eva paśūnupahvayate | paśavo vā iḍā | tasmāt sonvārabhyā | adhvaryuṇā ca yajamānena ca | upahūtaḥ paśumān asānīty āha | upa hy enau hvayate hotā | iḍāyai devatānām upahave | upahūtaḥ paśumān bhavati | ya evaṃ veda |

{ 3.3.8.5} yāṃ vai hastyām iḍām ādadhāti | vācaḥ sā bhāgadheyam | yām upahvayate | prāṇānāṃ sā | vācaṃ caiva prāṇāṃś cāvarundhe | atha vā etarhy upahūtāyām iḍāyām | puroḍāśasyaiva barhiṣado mīmāṃsā | yajamānaṃ devā abruvan | havir no nirvapa-iti | na-aham abhāgo nirvapsyāmīty abravīt |

{ 3.3.8.6} na mayā-abhāgayā-anuvakṣyatha-iti vāg abravīt | na-aham abhāgā puro'nuvākyā bhaviṣyāmīti puro'nuvākyā | na-aham abhāgā yājyā bhaviṣyāmīti yājyā | na mayā-abhāgena vaṣaṭkariṣyatha-iti vaṣaṭkāraḥ | yad yajamānabhāgaṃ nidhāya puroḍāśaṃ barhiṣadaṃ karoti | tān eva tad bhāginaḥ karoti | caturdhā karoti | catasro diśaḥ | dikṣv eva pratitiṣṭhati | barhiṣadaṃ karoti |

{ 3.3.8.7} yajamāno vai puroḍāśaḥ | prajā barhiḥ | yajamānameva prajāsu pratiṣṭhāpayati | tasmād asthnā'nyāḥ prajāḥ pratitiṣṭhanti | māṃsenānyāḥ | atho khalv āhuḥ | dakṣiṇā vā etā haviryajñasyāntarvedy avarudhyante | yat puroḍāśaṃ barhiṣadaṃ karotīti | caturdhā karoti | catvāro hy ete haviryajñasya-ṛtvijaḥ |

{ 3.3.8.8} brahmā hotā-adhvaryur agnīt | tam abhimṛśet | idaṃ brahmaṇaḥ | idaṃ hotuḥ | idam adhvaryoḥ | idam agnīdha iti | yathā-evādaḥ saumye 'dhvare | ādeśam ṛtvigbhyo dakṣiṇā nīyante | tādṛg eva tat | agnīdhe prathamāyā-ādadhāti |

{ 3.3.8.9} agnimukhā hy ṛddhiḥ | agnimukhām eva-ṛddhiṃ yajamāna ṛdhnoti | sakṛd upastīrya dvir ādadhat | upastīrya dvir abhighārayati | ṣaṭ saṃpadyante | ṣaḍ vā ṛtavaḥ | ṛtūn eva prīṇāti | vedena brahmaṇe brahmabhāgaṃ pariharati | prājāpatyo vai vedaḥ | prājāpatyo brahmā |

{ 3.3.8.10} savitā yajñasya prasūtyai | atha kāmam anyena | tato hotre | madhyaṃ vā etad yajñasya | yad hotā | madhyata eva yajñaṃ prīṇāti | athādhvaryave | pratiṣṭhā vā eṣā yajñasya | yad adhvaryuḥ | tasmād haviryajñasyaitām evā''vṛtam anu |

{ 3.3.8.11} anyā dakṣiṇā nīyante | yajñasya pratiṣṭhityai | agnim agnīt sakṛt-sakṛt saṃmṛḍhḍhīty āha | parāṅ iva hy etarhi yajñaḥ | iṣitā daivyā hotāra ity āha | iṣitaṃ hi karma kriyate | bhadravācyāya preṣito mānuṣaḥ sūktavākāya sūktā brūhīty āha | āśiṣam evaitām āśāste | svagā daivyā hotṛbhya ity āha | yajñam eva tat svagā karoti | svastir mānuṣebhya ity āha | āśiṣam evaitām āśāste | śaṃyor brūhīty āha | śaṃyum eva bārhaspatyaṃ bhāgadheyena samardhayati |

anuvāka 9 { 3.3.9.1} atha srucāv anuṣṭugbhyāṃ vājavatībhyāṃ vyūhati | pratiṣṭhā vā anuṣṭuk | annaṃ vājaḥ pratiṣṭhityai | annādyasyāvarudhyai | prācīṃ juhūmūhati | jātān eva bhrātṛvyān praṇudate | pratīcīm upabhṛtam | janiṣyamāṇān eva pratinudate | sa viṣūca eva-apohya sapatnān yajamānaḥ | asmin loke pratitiṣṭhati |

{ 3.3.9.2} dvābhyām | dvipratiṣṭho hi | vasubhyas tvā rudrebhyas tvā-ādityebhyas tvā-ity āha | yathāyajur evaitat | srukṣu prastaram anakti | ime vai lokāḥ srucaḥ | yajamānaḥ prastaraḥ | yajamānam eva tejasonakti | tredhā-anakti | traya ime lokāḥ |

{ 3.3.9.3} ebhya evainaṃ lokebhyo 'nakti | abhipūrvam anakti | abhipūrvam eva yajamānaṃ tejasānakti | aktaṃ rihāṇā ity āha | tejo vā ājyam | yajamānaḥ prastaraḥ | yajamānam eva tejasānakti | viyantu vaya ity āha | vaya evainaṃ kṛtvā | suvargaṃ lokaṃ gamayati |
