{ 3.8.3.5} tasmād aśvamedhayājī sarvāṇi bhūtāny abhibhavati | bhuvanam asīty āha | bhūmānam eva-upaiti | yantā-asīty āha | yantāram evainaṃ karoti | dhartā-asīty āha | dhartāram evainaṃ karoti | so 'gniṃ vaiśvānaram ity āha | agnāv evainaṃ vaiśvānare juhoti | saprathasam ity āha |

{ 3.8.3.6} prajaya-evainaṃ paśubhiḥ prathayati | svāhākṛta ity āha | homa evāsyaiṣaḥ | pṛthivyām ity āha | asyām eva-enaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati | yantā rāḍ yantā-asi yamano dhartā-asi dharuṇa ity āha | rūpam evāsyaitan mahimānaṃ vyācaṣṭe | kṛṣyai tvā kṣemāya tvā rayyai tvā poṣāya tvā-ity āha | āśiṣam eva-etām āśāste | svagā tvā devebhya ity āha | devebhya eva-enaṃ svagā karoti | svāhā tvā prajāpataya ity āha | prājāpatyo vā aśvaḥ | yasyā eva devatāyā ālabhyate | tayā-eva-enaṃ samardhayati |

anuvāka 4 { 3.8.4.1} yaḥ pitur anujāyāḥ putraḥ | sa purastān nayati | yo mātur anujāyāḥ putraḥ | sa paścān nayati | viṣvañcam evāsmāt pāpmānaṃ vivṛhataḥ | yo arvantaṃ jighāṃsati tam abhyamīti varuṇa iti śvānaṃ caturakṣaṃ prasauti | paro martaḥ paraḥ śvā-iti śunaś caturakṣasya prahanti | śvā-iva vai pāpmā bhrātṛvyaḥ | pāpmānam evāsya bhrātṛvyaṃ hanti | saidhrakaṃ musalaṃ bhavati |

{ 3.8.4.2} karma-karma-evāsmai sādhayati | pauṃścaleyo hanti | puṃścalvāṃ vai devāḥ śucaṃ nyadadhuḥ | śucā-evāsya śucaṃ hanti | pāpmā vā etam īpsatīty āhuḥ | yo 'śvamedhena yajata iti | aśvasyādhaspadam upāsyati | vajrī vā aśvaḥ prājāpatyaḥ | vajreṇaiva pāpmānaṃ bhrātṛvyam avakrāmati | dakṣiṇā-apaplāvayati |

{ 3.8.4.3} pāpmānam evāsmāt-śamalam apaplāvayati | aiṣīka udūho bhavati | āyur vā iṣīkāḥ | āyur evāsmin dadhati | amṛtaṃ vā iṣīkāḥ | amṛtam evāsmin dadhati | vetasaśākhā-upasaṃbaddhā bhavati | apsuyonir vā aśvaḥ | apsujo vetasaḥ | svād eva-enaṃ yonir nirmimīte | purastāt pratyañcam abhyudūhati | purastād evāsmin pratīcy amṛtaṃ dadhāti | ahaṃ ca tvaṃ ca vṛtrahann iti brahmā yajamānasya hastaṃ gṛhṇāti | brahmakṣattre eva saṃdadhāti | abhi kratvā-indra bhūr adha jmann ity adhvaryur yajamānaṃ vācayaty abhijityai |

anuvāka 5 { 3.8.5.1} catvāra ṛtvijaḥ samukṣanti | ābhya evainaṃ catasṛbhyo digbhyo 'bhisamīrayanti | śatena rājaputraiḥ sahādhvaryuḥ | purastāt pratyaṅ tiṣṭhan prokṣati | anenāśvena medhyena-iṣṭvā | ayaṃ rājā vṛtraṃ vadhyād iti | rājyaṃ vā adhvaryuḥ | kṣattraṃ {= kṣatraṃ } rājaputraḥ | rājyena-evāsmin kṣattraṃ {= kṣatraṃ } dadhāti | śatenārājabhir ugraiḥ saha brahmā |

{ 3.8.5.2} dakṣiṇata udaṅ tiṣṭhan prokṣati | anenāśvena medhyena-iṣṭvā | ayaṃ rājāpratidhṛṣyo 'stv iti | balaṃ vai brahmā | balam arājā-ugraḥ | balena-evāsmin balaṃ dadhāti | śatena sūtagrāmaṇibhiḥ saha hotā | paścāt prāṅ tiṣṭhan prokṣati | anenāśvena medhyena-iṣṭvā | ayaṃ rājā-asyai viśaḥ |

{ 3.8.5.3} bahugvai bahvaśvāyai bahvajāvikāyai | bahuvrīhiyavāyai bahumāṣatilāyai | bahuhiraṇyāyai bahuhastikāyai | bahudāsapūruṣāyai rayimatyai puṣṭimatyai | bahurāyaspoṣāyai rājā-astv iti | bhūmā vai hotā | bhūmā sūtagrāmaṇyaḥ | bhūmnā-evāsmin bhūmānaṃ dadhāti | śatena kṣattasaṃgrahītṛbhiḥ saha-udgātā | uttarato dakṣiṇā tiṣṭhan prokṣati |

{ 3.8.5.4} anenāśvena medhyena-iṣṭvā | ayaṃ rājā sarvam āyur etv iti | āyur vā udgātā | āyuḥ kṣattasaṃgrahītāraḥ | āyuṣā-evāsminn āyur dadhāti | śataṃ-śataṃ bhavanti | śatāyuḥ puruṣaḥ śata-indriyaḥ | āyuṣy eva-indriye pratitiṣṭhati catuḥśatā bhavanti | catasro diśaḥ | dikṣv eva pratitiṣṭhati |

anuvāka 6 { 3.8.6.1} yathā vai haviṣo gṛhītasya skandati | evaṃ vā etad aśvasya skandati | yan niktam anālabdham utsṛjanti | yat stokyā anvāha | sarvahutam evainaṃ karoty aksandāya | askannaṃ hi tat | yad dhutasya skandati | sahasram anvāha | sahasrasaṃmitaḥ suvargo lokaḥ | suvargasya lokasyābhijityai |

{ 3.8.6.2} yatparimitā anubrū́yāt | parimitam avarundhīta | aparimitā anvāha | aparimitaḥ suvargo lokaḥ | suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai | stokyā juhoti | yā eva varṣyā āpaḥ | tā avarundhe | asyāṃ juhoti | iyaṃ vā agnir vaiśvānaraḥ |

{ 3.8.6.3} asyām eva-enāḥ pratiṣṭhāpayati | uvāca ha prajāpatiḥ | stokyāsu vā aham aśvamedhaṃ saṃsthāpayāmi | tena tataḥ saṃsthitena carāmīti | agnaye svāhā-ity āha | agnaya evainaṃ juhoti | somāya svāhā-ity āha | somāya-evainaṃ juhoti | savitre svāhā-ity āha | savitra evainaṃ juhoti |

{ 3.8.6.4} sarasvatyai svāhā-ity āha | sarasvatyā evainaṃ juhoti | pūṣṇe svāhā-ity āha | pūṣṇa evainaṃ juhoti | bṛhaspataye svāhā-ity āha | bṛhaspataya evainaṃ juhoti | apāṃ modāya svāhā-ity āha | adbhya evainaṃ juhoti | vāyave svāhā-ity āha | vāyava evainaṃ juhoti |

{ 3.8.6.5} mitrāya svāhā-ity āha | mitrāya-evainaṃ juhoti | varuṇāya svāhā-ity āha | varuṇāya-evainaṃ juhoti | etābhya evainaṃ devatābhyo juhoti | daśa-daśa saṃpādaṃ juhoti | daśākṣarā virāṭ | annaṃ virāṭ | virājā-evānnādyam avarundhe | pra vā eṣo 'smāl lokāc cyavate | yaḥ parācīr āhutīr juhoti | punaḥ-punar abhyāvartaṃ juhoti | asminn eva loke pratitiṣṭhati | etāṃ ha vāva so 'śvamedhasya saṃsthitim uvācāskandāya | askannaṃ hi tat | yad yajñasya saṃsthitasya skandati |

anuvāka 7 { 3.8.7.1} prajāpataye tvā juṣṭaṃ prokṣāmīti purastāt pratyaṅ tiṣṭhan prokṣati | prajāpatir vai devānām annādo vīryāvān | annādyam evāsmin vīryaṃ dadhāti | tasmād aśvaḥ paśūnām annādo vīryāvattamaḥ | indrāgnibhyāṃ tvā-iti dakṣiṇataḥ | indrāgnī vai devānām ojiṣṭhau baliṣṭhau | oja evāsmin balaṃ dadhāti | tasmād aśvaḥ paśūnām ojiṣṭho baliṣṭhaḥ | vāyave tvā-iti paścāt | vāyur vai devānām āśuḥ sārasāritamaḥ |

{ 3.8.7.2} javam evāsmin dadhāti | tasmād aśvaḥ | paśūnām āśuḥ sārasāritamaḥ | viśvebhyas tvā devebhya ity uttarataḥ | viśve vai devā devānāṃ yaśasvitamāḥ | yaśa evāsmin dadhāti | tasmād aśvaḥ paśūnāṃ yaśasvitamaḥ | devebhyas tvā-ity adhastāt | devā vai devānām apacitatamāḥ | apacitim evāsmin dadhāti | tasmād aśvaḥ paśūnām apacitatamaḥ |

{ 3.8.7.3} sarvebhyas tvā devebhya ity upariṣṭāt | sarve vai devās tviṣimanto harasvinaḥ | tviṣim evāsmin haro dadhāti | tasmād aśvaḥ paśūnāṃ tviṣimān harasvitamaḥ | dive tvā-antarikṣāya tvā pṛthivyai tvā-ity āha | ebhya evainaṃ lokebhyaḥ prokṣati | sate tvā-asate tvā-adbhyas tvā-oṣadhībhyas tvā viśvebhyas tvā bhutebhya ity āha | tasmād aśvamedhayājinaṃ sarvāṇi bhūtāny upajīvanti | brahmavādino vadanti | yat prājāpatyo 'śvaḥ | atha kasmād enam anyābhyo devatābhyo 'pi prokṣatīti | aśve vai sarvā devatā anvāyattāḥ | taṃ yad viśvebhyas tvā bhūtebhya iti prokṣati | devatā evāsminn anvāyātayati | tasmād aśve sarvā devatā anvāyattāḥ |

anuvāka 8 { 3.8.8.1} yathā vai haviṣo gṛhītasya skandati | evaṃ vā etad aśvasya skandati | yat prokṣitam anālabdham utsṛjanti | yad aśvacaritāni juhoti | sarvahutam evainaṃ karoty askandāya | askannaṃ hi tat | yad dhutasya skandati | īṃkārāya svāhā-ī́ṃkṛtāya svāhā-ity āha | etāni vā aśvacaritāni | caritair evainaṃ samardhayati |

{ 3.8.8.2} tad āhuḥ | anāhutayo vā aśvacaritāni | na-etā hotavyā iti | atho khalv āhuḥ | hotavyā eva | atra vāva-evaṃ vidvān aśvamedhaṃ saṃsthāpayati | yad aśvacaritāni juhoti | tasmād dhotavyā iti | bahirdhā vā enam etad āyatanād dadhāti | bhrātṛvyam asmai janayati |

{ 3.8.8.3} yasyānāyatane 'nyatrāgner āhutīr juhoti | sāvitriyā iṣṭyāḥ purastāt sviṣṭakṛtaḥ | āhavanīye 'śvacaritāni juhoti | āyatana evāsya-āhutīr juhoti | nāsmai bhrātṛvyaṃ janayati | tad āhuḥ | yajñamukhe-yajñamukhe hotavyāḥ | yajñasya klṛptyai | suvargasya lokasyānukhyātyā iti | atho khalv āhuḥ |

{ 3.8.8.4} yad yajñamukhe-yajñamukhe juhuyāt | paśubhir yajamānaṃ vyardhayet | ava suvargāl lokāt padyeta | pāpīyānt syād iti | sakṛd eva hotavyāḥ | na yajamānaṃ paśubhir vyardhayati | abhi suvargaṃ lokaṃ jayati | na pāpīyān bhavati | aṣṭācatvāriṃśatam aśvarūpāṇi juhoti | aṣṭācatvāriṃśadakṣarā jagatī | jāgato 'śvaḥ prājāpatyaḥ samṛddhyai | ekam atiriktaṃ juhoti | tasmād ekaḥ prajāsv ardhukaḥ |

anuvāka 9 { 3.8.9.1} vibhūr mātrā prabhūḥ pitrā-ity āha | iyaṃ vai mātā | asau pitā | ābhyām evainaṃ paridadāti | aśvo 'si hayo 'sīty āha | śāsty evainam etat | tasmāt-śiṣṭāḥ prajā jāyante | atyo 'sīty āha | tasmād aśvaḥ sarvān paśūn atyeti | 0 tasmād aśvaḥ sarveṣāṃ paśūnāṃ śreṣṭhyaṃ gacchati |

{ 3.8.9.33} pra yaśaḥ śreṣṭhyam āpnoti | ya evaṃ veda | naro 'sy arvā-asi saptir asi vājy asīty āha | rūpam evāsyaitan māhimānaṃ vyācaṣṭe | yayur nāmāsīty āha | etad vā aśvasya priyaṃ nāmadheyam | priyeṇaivainaṃ nāmadheyenābhivadati | tasmād apy ā-amitrau saṃgatya | nāmnā ced dhvayete { cedhvayete} | mitram eva bhavataḥ |

{ 3.8.9.3} ādityānāṃ patvā-anvihīty āha | ādityān evainaṃ gamayati | agnaye svāhā svāhā-indrāgnibhyām iti pūrvahomāñ juhoti | pūrva eva dviṣantaṃ bhrātṛvyam atikrāmati | bhūr asi bhuve tvā bhavyāya tvā bhaviṣyate tvā-ity utsṛjati sarvatvāya | devā āśāpālā etaṃ devebhyo 'śvaṃ medhāya prokṣitaṃ gopāyata-ity āha | śataṃ vai talpyā rājaputrā devā āśāpālāḥ | tebhya evainaṃ paridadāti | īśvaro vā aśvaḥ pramuktaḥ parāṃ parāvataṃ gantoḥ | iha dhṛtiḥ svāhā-iha vidhṛtiḥ svāhā-iha rantiḥ svāhā-iha ramatiḥ svāhā-iti catṛṣu patsu juhoti |

{ 3.8.9.4} etā vā aśvasya bandhanam | tābhir evainaṃ badhnāti | tasmād aśvaḥ pramukto bandhanam āgacchati | tasmād aśvaḥ pramukto bandhanaṃ na jahāti | rāṣṭraṃ vā aśvamedhaḥ | rāṣṭre khalu vā ete vyāyacchante | ye 'śvaṃ medhyaṃ rakṣanti | teṣāṃ ya udṛcaṃ gacchanti | rāṣṭrād eva te rāṣṭraṃ gacchanti | atha ya udṛcaṃ na gacchanti | { 3.8.9.5} rāṣṭrād eva te vyavacchidyante | parā vā eṣa sicyate | yo 'balo 'śvamedhena yajate | yad amitrā aśvaṃ vinderan | hanyetāsya yajñaḥ | catuśśatā rakṣanti | yajñasyāghātāya | athānyam ānīya prokṣeyuḥ | sā-eva tataḥ prāyaścittiḥ |

anuvāka 10 { 3.8.10.1} prajāpatir akāmayatāśvamehena yajeya-iti | sa tapo 'tapyata | tasya tepānasya | sapta-ātmano devatā udakrāman | sā dīkṣā-abhavat | sa etāni vaiśvadevāny apaśyat | tāny ajuhot | tair vai sa dīkṣām avārundha | yad vaiśvadevāni juhoti | dīkṣām eva tair yajamāno 'varundhe |

{ 3.8.10.2} sapta juhoti | sapta hi tā devatā udakrāman | anvahaṃ juhoti | anvaham eva dīkṣām avarundhe | trīṇi vaiśvadevāni juhoti | catvāry audgrahaṇāni | sapta saṃpadyante | sapta vai śīrṣaṇyāḥ prāṇāḥ | prāṇā dīkṣā | prāṇair eva prāṇān dīkṣām avarundhe |

{ 3.8.10.3} ekaviṃśatiṃ vaiśvadevāni juhoti | ekaviṃśatir vai devalokāḥ | dvādaśa māsāḥ pañca-ṛtavaḥ | traya ime lokāḥ | asāv āditya ekaviṃśaḥ | eṣa suvargo lokaḥ | tad daivyaṃ kṣattram { kṣatram} | sā śrīḥ | tad bradhnasya viṣṭapam | tat svārājyam ucyate |

{ 3.8.10.4} triṃśatam audgrahaṇāni juhoti | triṃśadakṣarā virāṭ | annaṃ virāṭ | virājā-evānnādyam avarundhe | tredhā vibhajya devatāṃ juhoti | tryāvṛto vai devāḥ | tryāvṛta ime lokāḥ | eṣāṃ lokānām āptyai | eṣāṃ lokānāṃ klṛptyai | apa vā etasmāt prāṇāḥ krāmanti |

{ 3.8.10.5} yo dīkṣām atirecayati | saptāhaṃ pracaranti | sapta vai śīrṣanyāḥ prāṇaḥ | prāṇā dīkṣā | prāṇair eva prāṇān dīkṣām avarundhe | pūrṇāhutim uttamāṃ juhoti | sarvaṃ vai pūrṇāhutiḥ | sarvam evā''pnoti | atho iyaṃ vai pūrṇāhutiḥ | asyām eva pratitiṣṭhati |

anuvāka 11 { 3.8.11.1} prajāpatir aśvamedham asṛjata | taṃ sṛṣṭaṃ na kiṃ cana-udayacchat | taṃ vaiśvadevāny eva-udayacchan | yad vaiśvadevāni juhoti | yajñasya-udyatyai | svāhā-ādhim ādhītāya svāhā | svāhā-ādhītaṃ manase svāhā | svāhā manaḥ prajāpataye svāhā | kāya svāha kasmai svāhā katamasmai svāhā-iti prājāpatye mukhye bhavataḥ | prajāpatimukhābhir evainaṃ devatābhir udyacchate | { 3.8.11.2} adityai svāhā-adityai mahyai svāhā-adityai sumṛḍīkāyai svāhā-ity āha | iyaṃ vā aditiḥ | asyā evainaṃ pratiṣṭhāya-udyacchate | sarasvatyai svāhā sarasvatyai bṛhatyai svāhā sarasvatyai pāvakāyai svāhā-ity āha | vāg vai sarasvatī | vācā-evainam udyacchate | pūṣṇe svāhā pūṣṇe prapathyāya svāhā pūṣṇe naraṃdhiṣāya svahā-ity āha | paśavo vai pūṣā | paśubhir evainam udyacchate | tvaṣṭre svāhā tvaṣṭre turīpāya svāhā tvaṣṭre pururūpāya svāhā-ity āha | tvaṣṭā vai paśūnāṃ mithunānāṃ rūpakṛt | rūpam eva paśuṣu dadhāti | atho rūpair evainam udyacchate | viṣṇave svāhā viṣṇave nikhuryapāya svahā viṣṇave nibhūyapāya svāhā-ity āha | yajño vai viṣṇuḥ | yajñāya-evainam udyacchate | pūrṇāhutim uttamāṃ juhoti | pratyuttabdhyai sayatvāya |

anuvāka 12 { 3.8.12.1} sāvitram aṣṭākapālaṃ prātar nirvapati | aṣṭākṣarā gāyatrī | gāyatraṃ prātaḥsavanam | prātaḥsavanād evainaṃ gāyatriyāś chandaso 'dhi nirmimīte | atho prātaḥsavanam eva tena-āpnoti | gāyatrīṃ chandaḥ | savitre prasavitra ekādaśakapālaṃ madhyaṃdine | ekādaśākṣarā triṣṭup | traiṣṭubhaṃ mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam | mādhyaṃdinād evainaṃ savanāt triṣṭubhaś chandaso 'dhi nirmimīte |

{ 3.8.12.2} atho mādhyaṃdinam eva savanaṃ tena-āpnoti | triṣṭubhaṃ chandaḥ | savitra āsavitre dvādaśakapālam aparāhṇe | dvādaśākṣarā jagatī | jāgataṃ tṛtīyasavanam | tṛtīyasavanād evainaṃ jagatyāś chandaso 'dhi nirmimīte | atho tṛtīyasavanam eva tena-āpnoti | jagatīṃ chandaḥ | īśvaro vā aśvaḥ pramuktaḥ parāṃ parāvataṃ gantoḥ | iha dhṛtiḥ svāhā-iha vidhṛtiḥ svāhā-iha rantiḥ svāhā-iha rantiḥ svāhā-iti catasra āhutīr juhoti |

{ 3.8.12.3} catasro diśaḥ | digbhir evainaṃ parigṛhṇāti | āśvattho vrajo bhavati | prajāpatir devebhyo nilāyata | aśvo rūpaṃ kṛtvā | so 'śvatthe saṃvatsaram atiṣṭhat | tad aśvatthasyāśvatthatvam | yad āśvattho vrajo bhavati | sva evainaṃ yonau pratiṣṭhāpayati |

anuvāka 13 { 3.8.13.1} ā brahman brāhmaṇo brahmavarcasī jāyatām ity āha | brāhmaṇa eva brahmavarcasaṃ dadhāti | tasmāt purā brāhmaṇo brahmavarcasy ajāyata | ā-asmin rāṣṭre rājanya iṣavyaḥ śūro mahāratho jāyatām ity āha | rājanya eva śauryaṃ mahimānaṃ dadhāti | tasmāt purā rājanya iṣavyaḥ śūro mahāratho 'jāyata | dogdhrī dhenur ity āha | dhenvām eva payo dadhāti | tasmāt purā dogdhrī dhenur ajāyata | voḍhā-anaḍvān iy āha |

{ 3.8.13.2} anaḍuhy eva vīryaṃ dadhāti | tasmāt purā voḍhā-anaḍvān ajāyata | āśuḥ saptir ity āha | aśva eva javaṃ dadhāti | tasmāt purā-āśur aśvo 'jāyata | puraṃdhir yoṣā-ity āha | yoṣity eva rūpaṃ dadhāti | tasmāt strī yuvatiḥ priyā bhāvukā | jiṣṇū ratheṣṭhā ity āha | ā ha vai tatra jiṣṇū ratheṣṭhā jāyate |

{ 3.8.13.3} yatra-etena yajñena yajante | sabheyo yuvā-ity āha | yo vai pūrvavayasī | sa sabheyo yuvā | tasmād yuvā pumān priyo bhāvukaḥ | ā-asya yajamānasya vīro jāyatām ity āha | ā ha vai tatra yajamānasya vīro jāyate | yatra-etena yajñena yajante | nikāme-nikāme naḥ parjanyo varṣatv ity āha | nikāme-nikāme ha vai tatra parjanyo varṣati | yatra-etena yajñena yajante | phalinyo na oṣadhayaḥ pacyantām ity āha | phalinyo ha vai tatra-oṣadhayaḥ pacyante | yatra-etena yajñena yajante | yogakṣemo naḥ kalpatām ity āha | kalpate ha vai tatra prajābhyo yogakṣemaḥ | yatra-etena yajñena yajante |

anuvāka 14 { 3.8.14.1} prajāpatir devebhyo yajñān vyādiśat | sa ātmann aśvamedham adhatta | taṃ devā abruvan | eṣa vāva yajñaḥ | yad aśvamedhaḥ | apy eva no 'trāstv iti | tebhya etān annahomān prāyacchat | tān ajuhot | tair vai sa devān aprīṇāt | yad annahomāñ juhoti |

{ 3.8.14.2} devān eva tair yajamānaḥ prīṇāti | ājyena juhoti | agner vā etad rūpam | yad ājyam | yad ājyena juhoti | agnim eva tat prīṇāti | madhunā juhoti | mahatyai vā etad devatāyai rūpam | yan madhu | yan madhunā juhoti |

{ 3.8.14.3} mahatīm eva tad devatāṃ prīṇāti | taṇḍulair juhoti | vasūnāṃ vā etad rūpam | yat taṇḍulāḥ | yat taṇḍulair juhoti | vasūn eva tat prīṇāti | pṛthukair juhoti | rudrāṇāṃ vā etad rūpam | yat pṛthukāḥ | yat pṛthukair juhoti |

{ 3.8.14.4} rudrān eva tat prīṇāti | lājair juhoti | ādityānāṃ vā etad rūpam | yal lājāḥ | yal lājair juhoti | ādityān eva tat prīṇāti | karambair juhoti | viśveṣāṃ vā etad devānāṃ rūpam | yat karambāḥ | yat karambair juhoti |

{ 3.8.14.5} viśvān eva tad devān prīṇāti | dhānābhir juhoti | nakṣatrāṇāṃ vā etad rūpam | yad dhānāḥ | yad dhānābhir juhoti | nakṣatrāṇy eva tat prīṇāti | saktubhir juhoti | prajāpater vā etad rūpam | yat saktavaḥ | yat saktubhir juhoti |

{ 3.8.14.6} prajāpatim eva tat prīṇāti | masūsyair juhoti | sarvāsāṃ vā etad devatāṃ rūpam | yan masūsyāni | yan masūsyair juhoti | sarvā eva tad devatāḥ prīṇāti | priyaṅgutaṇḍulair juhoti | priyāṅgā ha vai nāmaite | etair vai devā aśvasyāṅgāni samadadhuḥ | yat priyaṅgutaṇḍulair juhoti | aśvasyaivāṅgāni saṃdadhāti | daśānnāni juhoti | daśākṣarā virāṭ | virāṭ kṛtsnasyānnādyasyāvaruddhyai |

anuvāka 15 { 3.8.15.1} prajāpatir aśvamedham asṛjata | taṃ sṛṣṭaṃ rakṣāṃsy ajighāṃsan | sa etān prajāpatir naktaṃhomān apaśyat | tān ajuhoti | tair vai sa yajñād rakṣāṃsy apāhan | yan naktaṃhomāñ juhoti | yajñād eva tair yajamāno rakṣāṃsy apahanti | ājyena juhoti | vajro vā ājyam | vajreṇaiva yajñād rakṣāṃsy apahanti |

{ 3.8.15.2} ājyasya pratipadaṃ karoti | prāṇo vā ājyam | mukhata evāsya prāṇaṃ dadhāti | annahomāñ juhoti | śarīravad evāvarundhe | vyatyāsaṃ juhoti | ubhayasyāvarudhyai | naktaṃ juhoti | rakṣasām apahatyai | ājyenāntato juhoti |

{ 3.8.15.3} prāṇo vā ājyam | ubhayata evāsya prāṇaṃ dadhāti | purastāc copariṣṭāc ca | ekasmai svāhā-ity āha | asminn eva loke pratitiṣṭhati | dvābhyāṃ svāhā-ity āha | amuṣminn eva loke pratitiṣṭhati | ubhayor eva lokayoḥ pratitiṣṭhati | asmiṃś cāmuṣmiṃś ca | śatāya svāhā-ity āha | śatāyur vai puruṣaḥ śatavīryaḥ | āyur eva vīryam avarundhe | sahasrāya svāhā-ity āha | āyur vai sahasram | āyur evāvarundhe | sarvasmai svāhā-ity āha | aparimitam evāvarundhe |

anuvāka 16 { 3.8.16.1} prajāpatiṃ vā eṣa īpsatīty āhuḥ | yo 'śvamedhena yajata iti | atho āhuḥ | sarvāṇi bhūtānīti | ekasmai svāhā-ity āha | prajāpatir vā ekaḥ | tam evā''pnoti | ekasmai svāhā dvābhyāṃ svāhā-ity abhipūrvam āhutīr juhoti | abhipūrvam eva suvargaṃ lokam eti | 0 ekottaraṃ juhoti |

{ 3.8.16.2} ekavad eva suvargaṃ lokam eti | saṃtataṃ juhoti | suvargasya lokasya saṃtatyai | śatāya svāhā-ity āha | śatāyur vai puruṣaḥ śatavīryaḥ | āyur eva vīryam avarundhe | sahasrāya svāhā-ity āha | āyur vai sahasram | āyur evāvarundhe | ayutāya svāhā niyutāya svāhā prayutāya svāhā-ity āha |

{ 3.8.16.3} traya ime lokāḥ | imān eva lokān avarundhe | arbudāya svāhā-ity āha | vāg vā arbudam | vācam evāvarundhe | nyarbudāya svāhā-ity āha | yo vai vāco bhūmā | tan nyarbudam | vāca eva bhūmānam avarundhe | samudrāya svāhā-ity āha |

{ 3.8.16.4} samudram evā''pnoti | madhyāya svāhā-ity āha | madhyam evā''pnoti | antāya svāhā-ity āha | antam eva-āpnoti | parārdhāya svāhā-ity āha | parārdham evā''pnoti | uṣase svāhā vyuṣṭyai svāhā-ity āha | rātir vā uṣāḥ | ahar vyuṣṭiḥ | ahorātre evāvarundhe | atho ahorātrayor eva pratitiṣṭhati | tā yad ubhayīr divā vā naktaṃ vā juhuyāt | ahorātre mohayet | uṣase svāhā vyuṣṭyai svāhā-udeṣyate svāhā-udyate svāhā-ity anudite juhoti | uditāya svāhā suvargāya svāhā lokāya svāhā-ity udite juhoti | ahorātrayor avyatimohāya |

anuvāka 17 { 3.8.17.1} vibhūr mātrā prabhūḥ pitrā-ity aśvanāmāni juhoti | ubhayor evainaṃ lokayor nāmadheyaṃ gamayati | āyanāya svāhā prāyaṇāya svāhā-ity uddrāvāñ juhoti | sarvam evainam askannaṃ suvargaṃ lokaṃ gamayati | agnaye svāhā somāya svāhā-iti pūrvahomāñ juhoti | pūrva eva dviṣantaṃ bhrātṛvyam atikrāmati | pṛthivyai svāhā-antarikṣāya svāhā-ity āha | yathāyajur eva-etat | agnaye svāhā somāya svāhā-iti pūrvadīkṣā juhoti | pūrva eva dviṣantaṃ bhrātṛvyam atikrāmati |

{ 3.8.17.2} pṛthivyai svāhā-antarikṣāya svāhā-ity ekaviṃśinīṃ dīkṣāṃ juhoti | ekaviṃśatir vai devalokāḥ | dvādaśa māsāḥ pañca-ṛtavaḥ | traya ime lokāḥ | asāv āditya ekaviṃśaḥ | eṣa suvargo lokaḥ | suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai | bhuvo devānāṃ karmaṇā-ity ṛtudīkṣā juhoti | ṛtūn evāsmai kalpayati | agnaye svāhā vāyave svāhā-iti juhoty anantarityai |

{ 3.8.17.3} arvāṅ yajñaḥ saṃkrāmatv ity āptīr juhoti | suvargasya lokasya-āptyai | bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyad iti paryāptīr juhoti | suvargasya lokasya paryāptyai | ā me gṛhā bhavantv ity ābhūr juhoti | suvargasya lokasya-ābhūtyai | agninā tapo 'nvabhavad ity anubhūr juhoti | suvargasya lokasya-anubhūtyai | svāhā-ādhim ādhītāya svāhā-iti samastāni vaiśvadevāni juhoti | samastam eva dviṣantaṃ bhrātṛvyam atikrāmati |

{ 3.8.17.4} dadbhyaḥ svāhā hanūbhyāṃ svāhā-ity aṅgahomāñ juhoti | aṅge-aṅge vai puruṣasya pāpmā-upaśliṣṭaḥ | aṅgād-aṅgād evainaṃ pāpmanas tena muñcati | añjyetāya svāhā kṛṣṇāya svāhā śvetāya svāhā-ity aśvarūpāṇi juhoti | rūpair evainaṃ samardhayati | oṣadhībhyaḥ svāhā mūlebhyasḥ svāhā-ity oṣadhihomāñ juhoti | dvayyó vā oṣadhayaḥ | puṣpebhyo 'nyāḥ phalaṃ gṛhṇanti | mūlebhyo 'nyāḥ | 0 tā eva-ubhayīr avarundhe |

{ 3.8.17.5} vanaspatibhyaḥ svāhā-iti vanaspatihomāñ juhoti | āraṇyasyānnādyasyāvarudhyai | meṣas tvā pacatair avatv ity apāvyāni juhoti | prāṇā vai devā apāvyāḥ | prāṇān evāvarundhe | kūpyābhyaḥ svāhā-adbhyaḥ svāhā-ity apāṃ homāñ juhoti | apsu vā āpaḥ | annaṃ vā āpaḥ | adbhyo vā annaṃ jāyate | yad evādbhyo 'nnaṃ jāyate | tad avarundhe |

anuvāka 18 { 3.8.18.1} ambhāṃsi juhoti | ayaṃ vai loko 'mbhāṃsi | tasya vasavo 'dhipatayaḥ | agnir jyotiḥ | yad ambhāṃsi juhoti | imam eva lokam avarundhe | vasūnāṃ sāyujyaṃ gacchati | agniṃ jyotir avarundhe | nabhāṃsi juhoti | antarikṣaṃ vai nabhāṃsi |

{ 3.8.18.2} tasya rudrā adhipatayaḥ | vāyur jyotiḥ | yan nabhāṃsi juhoti | antarikṣam evāvarundhe | rudrāṇāṃ sāyujyaṃ gacchati | vāyuṃ jyotir avarundhe | mahāṃsi juhoti | asau vai loko mahāṃsi | tasya-ādityā adhipatayaḥ | sūryo jyotiḥ |

{ 3.8.18.3} yan mahāṃsi juhoti | amum eva lokam avarundhe | ādityānāṃ sāyujyaṃ gacchati | sūryaṃ jyotir avarundhe | namo rājñe namo varuṇaya-iti yavyāni juhoti | annādyasyāvarudhyai | mayobhūr vāto abhi vātūsrā iti gavyāni juhoti | paśūnām avarudhyai | prāṇāya svāhā vyānāya svāhā-iti saṃtatihomañ juhoti | suvargasya lokasya saṃtatyai |

{ 3.8.18.4} sitāya svāhā-asitāya svāhā-iti pramuktīr juhoti | suvargasya lokasya pramuktyai | pṛthivyai svāhā-antarikṣāya svāhā-ity āha | yathāyajur evaitat | datvate svahā-adantakāya svāhā-iti śarīrahomāñ juhoti | pitṛlokam eva tair yajamāno 'varundhe | kas tvā yunakti sa tvā yunaktv iti paridhīn yunakti | ime vai lokāḥ paridhayaḥ | imān evāsmai lokān yunakti | suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai |

{ 3.8.18.5} yaḥ prāṇato ya ātmadā iti mahimānau juhoti | suvargo vai loko mahaḥ | suvargam eva tābhyāṃ lokaṃ yajamāno 'varundhe | ā brahman brāhmaṇo brahmavarcasī jāyatām iti samastāni brahmavarcasāni juhoti | brahmavarcasam eva tair yajamāno 'varundhe | jajñi bījam iti juhoty anantarityai | agnaye samanamat pṛthivyai samanamad iti saṃnatihomāñ juhoti | suvargasya lokasya saṃnatyai | bhūtāya svāhā bhaviṣyate svāhā-iti bhūtābhavyau homau juhoti | ayaṃ vai loko bhūtam |

{ 3.8.18.6} asau bhaviṣyat | anayor eva lokayoḥ pratitiṣṭhati | sarvasya-āptyai | sarvasya-avaruddhyai | yad akrandaḥ prathamaṃ jāyamāna ity aśvastomīyaṃ juhoti | sarvasya-āptyai | sarvasya jityai | sarvam eva tena-āpnoti | sarvaṃ jayati | yo 'śvamedhena yajate |

{ 3.8.18.7} ya u cainam evaṃ veda | yajñaṃ rakṣāṃsy ajighāṃsan | sa etān prajāpatir naktaṃhomān apaśyat | tān ajuhot | tair vai sa yajñād rakṣāṃsy apāhan | yan naktaṃhomāñ juhoti | yajñād eva tair yajamāno rakṣāṃsy apahanti | uṣase svāhā vyuṣṭyai svāhā-ity antato juhoti | suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai |

anuvāka 19 { 3.8.19.1} ekayūpo vā-ekādaśinī vā | anyeṣāṃ yajñānāṃ yūpā bhavanti | ekaviṃśiny aśvamedhasya | svargasya lokasyābhijityai | bailvo vā khādiro vā pālāśo vā | anyeṣāṃ yajñakratūnāṃ yūpā bhavanti | rājjudāla ekaviṃśatyaratnir aśvamedhasya | suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai | nānyeṣāṃ paśūnāṃ tejanyā {ḍyumoṇṭa tedanyā} avadyanti | avadyanty aśvasya |

{ 3.8.19.2} pāpmā vai tejanī {ḍyumoṇṭa tedanī} | pāpmano 'pahatyai | plakṣaśākhāyām anyeṣāṃ paśūnām avadyanti | vetasaśākhāyām aśvasya | apsuyonir vā aśvaḥ | apsujo vetasaḥ | sva evāsya yonāv avadyati | yūpeṣu grāmyān paśūn niyuñjanti | ārokeṣv āraṇyān dhārayanti | paśūnāṃ vyāvṛttyai | ā grāmyān paśūn-labhante | pra-āraṇyānt sṛjanti | pāpmano 'pahatyai |

anuvāka 20 { 3.8.20.1} rājjudālam agniṣṭhaṃ minoti | bhrūṇahatyāyā apahatyai | pautudravāv abhito bhavataḥ | puṇyasya gandhasyāvarudhyai | bhrūṇahatyām evāsmād apahatya | puṇyena gandhena-ubhayataḥ parigṛhṇāti | ṣaḍ bailvā bhavanti | bramavarcasasyāvaruddhyai | ṣaṭ khādirāḥ | tejaso 'varudhyai |

{ 3.8.20.2} ṣaṭ pālāśāḥ | somapīthasyāvarudhyai | ekaviṃśatiḥ saṃpadyante | ekaviṃśatir vai devalokāḥ | dvādaśa māsāḥ pañca-ṛtavaḥ | traya ime lokāḥ | asāv āditya ekaviṃśaḥ | eṣa suvargo lokaḥ | suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai | śataṃ paśavo bhavanti |

{ 3.8.20.3} śatāyuḥ puruṣaḥ śata-indriyaḥ | āyuṣy eva-indriye pratitiṣṭhati | sarvaṃ vā aśvamedhy āpnoti | aparimitā bhavanti | aparimitasyāvaruddhyai | brahmavādino vadanti | kasmāt satyāt | dakṣiṇato 'nyeṣāṃ paśūnām avadyanti | uttarato 'śvasya-iti | vāruṇo vā aśvaḥ |

{ 3.8.20.4} eṣā vai varuṇasya dik | svāyām evāsya diśy avadyati | yad itareṣāṃ paśūnām avadyati | śatadevatyaṃ tenāvarundhe | cite 'gnāv adhi vaitase kaṭa 'śvaṃ cinoti | apsuyonir vā aśvaḥ apsujo vetasaḥ | sva eva-enaṃ yonau pratiṣṭhāpayati | purastāt pratyañcaṃ tūparaṃ cinoti | paścāt prācīnaṃ gomṛgam |

{ 3.8.20.5} prāṇāpānāv evāsmint samyañcau dadhāti | aśvaṃ tūparaṃ gomṛgam iti sarvahuta etāñ juhoti | eṣāṃ lokānām abhijityai | ātmanā-abhijuhoti | sātmānam eva-enaṃ satanuṃ karoti | sātmā-amuṣmin-loke bhavati | ya evaṃ veda | atho vasor eva dhārāṃ tenāvarundhe | iluvardāya svāhā balivardāya svāhā-ity āha | saṃvatsaro vā iluvardaḥ | parivatsaro balivardaḥ | saṃvatsarād eva parivatsarād āyur avarundhe | āyur evāsmin dadhāti | tasmād aśvamedhayājī jarasā visrasomuṃ lokam eti |

anuvāka 21 { 3.8.21.1} ekaviṃśo 'gnir bhavati | ekaviṃśaḥ stomaḥ | ekaviṃśatir yūpāḥ | yathā vā aśvā varṣabhā vā vṛṣāṇaḥ saṃsphureran | evam etat stomāḥ saṃsphurante | yad ekaviṃśāḥ | te yat samṛccheran | hanyetāsya yajñaḥ | dvādaśa evāgniḥ syād ity āhuḥ | dvādaśaḥ stomaḥ |

{ 3.8.21.2} ekādaśa yupāḥ | yad dvādaśo 'gnir bhavati | dvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ | saṃvatsareṇaivāsmā annam avarundhe | yad daśa yūpā bhavanti | daśākṣarā virāṭ | annaṃ virāṭ | virājā-evānnādyam avarundhe | ya {ānaṃdā. sa} ekādaśaḥ | stana evāsyai saḥ |

{ 3.8.21.3} duha eva-enāṃ tena | tad āhuḥ | yad dvādaśo 'gniḥ syād dvādaśaḥ stoma ekādaśa yūpāḥ | yathā sthuriṇā yāyāt | tādṛk tat | ekaviṃśa evāgniḥ syād ity āhuḥ | ekaviṃśaḥ stomaḥ | ekaviṃśatir yūpāḥ | yathā praṣṭibhir yāti | tādṛg eva tat |

{ 3.8.21.4} yo vā aśvamedhe tisraḥ kakubho veda | kakud dha rājñāṃ bhavati | ekaviṃśo 'nir bhavati | ekaviṃśaḥ stomaḥ | ekaviṃśatir yūpāḥ | etā vā aśvamedhe tisraḥ kakubhaḥ | ya evaṃ veda | kakud dha rājñāṃ bhavati | yo vā aśvamedhe trīṇi śīrṣāṇi veda | śiro ha rājñāṃ bhavati | ekaviṃśo 'gnir bhavati | ekaviṃśaḥ stomaḥ | ekaviṃśatir yūpāḥ | etāni vā aśvamedhe trīṇi śīrṣāṇi | ya evaṃ veda | śiro ha rājñāṃ bhavati |

anuvāka 22 { 3.8.22.1} devā vā aśvamedhe pavamāne | suvargaṃ lokaṃ na prājānan | tam aśvaḥ prājānāt | yad aśvamedhe 'śvèna medhyena-udañco bahiṣpavamānaṃ sarpanti | suvargasya lokasya prajñātyai | na vai manuṣyaḥ suvargaṃ lokam añjasā veda | aśvo vai suvargaṃ lokam añjasā veda | yad udgātā-udgāyet | yathā-akṣetrajño 'nyena pathā pratipādayet | tādṛk tat |

{ 3.8.22.2} udgātāram aparuddhya | aśvam udgīthāya vṛṇīte | yathā kṣetrajño 'ñjasā nayati | evam evainam aśvaḥ suvargaṃ lokam añjasā nayati | puccham anvārabhante | suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai | hiṃ karoti | sāmaivākaḥ | hiṃ karoti | udgītha evāsya saḥ |

{ 3.8.22.3} vaḍabā uparundhanti | mithunatvāya prajātyai | atho yathā-upagātāra upagāyanti | tādṛg eva tat | udagāsīd aśvo medhya ity āha | prājāpatyo vā aśvaḥ | prajāpatir udgīthaḥ | udgītham evāvarundhe | atho ṛksāmayor eva pratitiṣṭhati | hiraṇyena-upākaroti | jyotir vai hiraṇyam | jyotir eva mukhato dadhāti | yajamāne ca prajāsu ca | atho hiraṇyajyotir eva yajamānaḥ suvargaṃ lokam eti |

anuvāka 23 { 3.8.23.1} puruṣo vai yajñaḥ | yajñaḥ prajāpatiḥ | yad aśve paśūn niyuñjanti | yajñād eva tad yajñaṃ prayuṅkte | aśvaṃ tūparaṃ gomṛgam | tān agniṣṭha ālabhate | senāmukham eva tat saṃśyati | tasmād rājamukhaṃ bhīṣmaṃ bhāvukam | āgneyaṃ kṛṣṇagrīvaṃ purastāl lalāṭe | pūrvāgnim eva taṃ kurute |

{ 3.8.23.2} tasmāt pūrvāgniṃ purastāt sthāpayanti | pauṣṇam anvañcam | annaṃ vai pūṣā | tasmāt pūrvāgnāv āhāryam āharanti | aindrāpauṣṇam upariṣṭāt | aindro vai rājanyo 'nnaṃ pūṣā | annādyena-evainam ubhayataḥ parigṛhṇāti | tasmād rājanyo 'nnādo bhāvukaḥ | āgneyau kṛṣṇagrīvau bāhuvoḥ | bāhuvor eva vīryaṃ dhatte |

{ 3.8.23.3} tasmād rājanyo bāhubalī bhāvukaḥ | tvāṣṭrau lomaśasakthau sakthyoḥ | sakthyor eva vīryaṃ dhatte | tasmād rājanya ūrubalī bhāvukaḥ | śitipṛṣṭhau bārhaspatyau pṛṣṭhe | brahmavarcasam eva-upariṣṭād dhatte | atho kavace evaite abhitaḥ paryūhate | tasmād rājanyaḥ saṃnaddho vīryaṃ karoti | dhātre pṛṣa-udaram adhastāt | pratiṣṭhām evaitāṃ kurute | atho iyaṃ vai dhātā | asyām eva pratitiṣṭhati | sauryaṃ balakṣaṃ pucche | utsedham eva taṃ kurute | tasmād utsedhaṃ bhaye prajā abhisaṃśrayanti |







prapāṭhaka: 9 anuvāka 1 { 3.9.1.1} prajāpatir aśvamedham asṛjata | so 'smāt sṛṣṭo 'pākrāmat | tam aṣṭādaśibhir anuprāyuṅkta | tam āpnot | tam āptvā-aṣṭādaśibhir avārundha | yad aṣṭādaśina ālabhyante | yajñam eva tair āptvā yajamāno 'varundhe | saṃvatsarasya vā eṣā pratimā | yad aṣṭādaśinaḥ | 0 dvādaśa māsāḥ pañc-ṛtavaḥ |

{ 3.9.1.2} saṃvatsaro 'ṣṭādaśaḥ | yad aṣṭādaśina ālabhyante | saṃvatsarem eva tair āptvā yajamāno 'varundhe | agniṣṭhè 'nyān paśūn upākaroti | itareṣu yūpeṣv aṣṭādaśino 'jāmitvāya | nava-nava-ālabhyante savīryatvāya | yad āraṇyaiḥ saṃsthāpayet | vyavasyetāṃ pitāputrau | vy adhvānaḥ krāmeyuḥ | vidūraṃ grāmayor grāmāntau syātām |

{ 3.9.1.3} ṛkṣīkāḥ puruṣavyāghrāḥ parimoṣiṇi āvyādhinīs taskarā araṇyeṣv ājāyeran | tad āhuḥ | apaśavo vā ete | yad āraṇyāḥ | yad āraṇyaiḥ saṃsthāpayet | kṣipre yajamānam araṇyaṃ mṛtaṃ hareyuḥ | araṇyāyatanā hy āraṇyāḥ paśava iti | yat paśūn na-ālabheta | anavaruddhā asya paśavaḥ syuḥ | yat paryagnikṛtān utsṛjet |

{ 3.9.1.4} yajñavaiśasaṃ { yajñaveśasaṃ} kuryāt | yat paśūn ālabhate | tena-eva paśūn avarundhe | yat paryagnikṛtān utsṛjaty ayajñavaiśasāya { ayajñaveśasāya} | avaruddhā asya paśavo bhavanti | na yajñavaiśasaṃ { yajñaveśasaṃ} bhavati | na yajamānam araṇyaṃ mṛtaṃ haranti | grāmyaiḥ saṃsthāpayati | ete vai paśavaḥ kṣemo nāma | saṃ pitāputrāv avasyataḥ | sam adhvānaḥ krāmanti | samantikaṃ grāmayor grāmāntau bhavataḥ | na-ṛkṣīkāḥ puruṣavyāghrāḥ parimoṣiṇa āvyādhinīs taskarā araṇyeṣv ājāyante |

anuvāka 2 { 3.9.2.1} prajāpatir akāmayata-ubhau lokāv avarundhīya-iti | sa etān ubhayān paśūn apaśyat | grāmyāṃś ca-āraṇyāṃś ca | tān ālabhata | tair vai sa ubhau lokāv avārundha | grāmyair eva paśubhir imaṃ lokam avārundha | āraṇyair amum | yad grāmyān paśūn ālabhate | imam eva tair lokam avarundhe | yad āraṇyān |

{ 3.9.2.2} amuṃ taiḥ | anavaruddho vā etasya saṃvatsara ity āhuḥ | ya ita-itaś cāturmāsyāni saṃvatsaraṃ prayuṅkta iti | etāvān vai saṃvatsaraḥ | yac cāturmāsyāni | yad ete cāturmāsyāḥ paśava ālabhyante | pratyakṣam eva taiḥ saṃvatsaraṃ yajamāno 'varundhe | vi vā eṣa prajayā paśubhir ṛdhyate | yaḥ saṃvatsaraṃ prayuṅkte | saṃvatsaraḥ suvargo lokaḥ |

{ 3.9.2.3} suvargaṃ tu lokaṃ nāparādhnoti | prajā vai paśava ekādaśinī | yad eta aikādaśināḥ paśava ālabhyante | sākṣād eva prajāṃ paśūn yajamāno 'varundhe | prajāpatir virājam asṛjata | sā sṛṣṭā-aśvamedhaṃ praviśat | tāṃ daśibhir anu prāyuṅkta | tām āpnot | tām āptvā daśibhir avārundha | yad daśina ālabhyante |

{ 3.9.2.4} virājam eva tair āptvā yajamāno 'varundhe | ekādaśa daśata ālabhyante | ekādaśākṣarā triṣṭup | traiṣṭubhāḥ paśavaḥ | paśūn evāvarundhe | vaiśvadevo vā aśvaḥ | nānādevatyāḥ paśavo bhavanti | aśvasya sarvatvāya | nānārūpā bhavanti | tasmān nānārūpāḥ paśavaḥ | bahurūpā bhavanti | tasmād bahurūpāḥ paśavaḥ samṛddhyai |

anuvāka 3 { 3.9.3.1} asmai vai lokāya grāmyāḥ paśava ālabhyante | amuṣmā āraṇyāḥ | yad grāmyān paśūn ālabhate | imam eva tair lokam avarundhe | yad āraṇyān | amuṃ taiḥ | ubhayān paśūn ālabhate | grāmyāṃś ca-āraṇyāṃś ca | ubhayor lokayor avaruddhyai | ubhayān paśūn ālabhate |

{ 3.9.3.2} grāmyāṃś ca-āraṇyāṃś ca | ubhayasyānnādyasyāvaruddhyai | ubhayān paśūn ālabhate | grāmyāṃś ca-āraṇyāṃś ca | ubhayeṣāṃ paśūnām avaruddhyai | trayas-trayo bhavanti | traya ime lokāḥ | eṣāṃ lokānām āptyai | brahmavādino vadanti | kasmāt satyāt |

{ 3.9.3.3} asmin- loke bahavaḥ kāmā iti | yat samānībhyo devatābhyo 'nye'-nye paśava ālabhyante | asminn eva tal loke kāmān dadhāti | tasmād asmin- loke bahavaḥ kāmāḥ | trayāṇāṃ-trayāṇāṃ saha vapā juhoti | tryāvṛto vai devāḥ | tryāvṛta ime lokāḥ | eṣāṃ lokānām āptyai | eṣāṃ lokānāṃ klṛptyai | paryagnikṛtān āraṇyān utsṛjanty ahiṃsāyai |

anuvāka 4 { 3.9.4.1} yuñjanti bradhnam ity āha | asau vā ādityo bradhnaḥ | ādityam evāsmai yunakti | aruṣam ity āha | agnir vā aruṣaḥ | agnim evāsmai yunakti | carantam ity āha | vāyur vai caran | vāyum evāsmai yunakti | paritasthuṣa ity āha |

{ 3.9.4.2} ime vai lokāḥ paritasthuṣaḥ | iman evāsmai lokān yunakti | rocante rocanā divīty āha | nakṣatrāṇi vai rocanā divi | nakṣatrāṇy evāsmai rocayati | yuñjanty asya kāmyā-ity āha | kāmān evāsmai yunakti | harī vipakṣasā-ity āha | ime vai harī vipakṣasā | ime evāsmai yunakti |

{ 3.9.4.3} śoṇā dhṛṣṇū nṛvāhasā-ity āha | ahorātre vai nṛvāhasā | ahorātre evāsmai yunakti | etā evāsmai devatā yunakti | suvargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai | ketuṃ kṛṇvann aketava iti dhvajaṃ pratimuñcati | yaśa eva-enaṃ rājñāṃ gamayati | jīmūtasya-iva bhavati pratīkam ity āha | yathāyajur eva-etat | ye te panthānaḥ savitaḥ pūrvyāsa ity adhvaryur yajamānaṃ vācayaty abhijityai |

{ 3.9.4.4} parā vā etasya yajña eti | yasya paśur upākṛto 'nyatra vedyā eti | etaṃ stotar etena pathā punar aśvam āvartayāsi na ity āha | vāyur vai stotā | vāyum evāsya parastād dadhāty āvṛttyai | yathā vai haviṣo gṛhītasya skandati | evaṃ vā etad aśvasya skandati | yad asya-upākṛtasya lomāni śīyante | yad vāleṣu kācān āvayanti | lomāny evāsya tat saṃbharanti |

{ 3.9.4.5} bhūr bhuvaḥ suvar iti prājāpatyābhir āvayanti | prājāpatyo vā aśvaḥ | svayā-evainaṃ devatayā samardhayanti | bhūr iti mahiṣī | bhuva iti vāvātā | suvar iti parivṛktī́ | eṣāṃ lokānām abhijityai | hiraṇyayāḥ kācā bhavanti | jyotir vai hiraṇyam | rāṣṭram aśvamedhaḥ |

{ 3.9.4.6} jyotiś caivāsmai rāṣṭraṃ ca samīcī dadhāti | sahasraṃ bhavanti | sahasrasaṃmitaḥ suvargo lokaḥ | suvargasya lokasyābhijityai | apa vā etasmāt teja indriyaṃ paśavaḥ śrīḥ krāmanti | yo 'śvamedhena yajate | vasavas tvā-añjantu gāyatreṇa chandasā-iti mahiṣy abhyanakti | tejo vā ājyam | tejo gāyatrī | tejasā-evāsmai tejo 'varundhe |

{ 3.9.4.7} rudrās tvā-añjantu traiṣṭubhena chandasā-iti vāvātā | tejo vā ājyam | indriyaṃ triṣṭup | tejasā-evāsmā indriyam avarundhe | ādityās tvā-añjantu jāgatena chandasā-iti parivṛktī́ | tejo vā ājyam | paśavo jagatī | tejasā-evāsmai paśūn avarundhe | patnayo 'bhyañjanti | śriyā vā etad rūpam |

{ 3.9.4.8} yat patnayaḥ | śriyam evāsmin tad dadhati | nāsmāt teja indriyaṃ paśavaḥ śrī̀r apakāmanti | lājī3n- śācī3n yaśo mamā3ṃ { mamā4ṃ} ity atiriktam annam aśvāya-upāharanti | prajām evānnādīṃ kurvate | etad devā annam atta-etad annam addhi prajāpata ity āha | prajāyām evānnādyaṃ dadhate | yadi nāvajighret | agniḥ paśur āsīd ity avaghrāpayet | ava haiva jighrati | ākrān vājī kramair atyakramīd vājī dyaus te pṛṣṭhaṃ pṛthivī sadhastham ity aśvam anumantrayate | eṣāṃ lokānām abhijityai | samiddho añjan kṛdaraṃ matīnām ity aśvasya-āpriyo bhavanti sarūpatvāya |

anuvāka 5 { 3.9.5.1} tejasā vā eṣa brahmavarcasena vyṛdhyate | yo 'śvamedhena yajate | hotā ca brahmā ca brahmodyaṃ vadataḥ | tejasā caivainaṃ brahmavarcasena ca samardhayataḥ | dakṣiṇato brahmā bhavati | dakṣiṇataāyatano vai brahmā | bārhaspatyo vai brahmā | brahmavarcasam evāsya dakṣiṇato dadhāti | tasmād dakṣiṇo 'rdho brahmavarcasitaraḥ | uttarato hotā bhavati |

{ 3.9.5.2} uttarataāyatano vai hotā | āgneyo vai hotā | tejo vā agniḥ | teja evāsya-uttarato dadhāti | tasmād uttaro 'rdhas tejasvitaraḥ | yūpam abhito vadataḥ | yajamānadevatyo vai yūpaḥ | yajamānam eva tejasā ca brahmavarcasena ca samardhayataḥ | kiṃ svid āsīt pūrvacittir ity āha | dyaur vai vṛṣṭiḥ pūrvacittiḥ |

{ 3.9.5.3} divam eva vṛṣṭim avarundhe | kiṃ svid āsīd bṛhad vaya ity āha | aśvo vai bṛhad vayaḥ | aśvam evāvarundhe | kiṃ svid āsīt piśaṅgilā-ity āha | rātrir vai piśaṅgilā | rātrim evāvarundhe | kiṃ svid āsīt pilippilā-ity āha | śrīr vai pilippilā | annādyam evāvarundhe |

{ 3.9.5.4} kaḥ svid ekākī caratīty āha | asau vā āditya ekākī carati | teja evāvarundhe | ka u svij jāyate punar ity āha | candramā vai jāyate punaḥ | āyur evāvarundhe | kiṃ svid dhimasya bheṣajam ity āha | agnir vai himasya bheṣajam | brahmavarcasam evāvarundhe | kiṃ svid āvapanaṃ mahad ity āha |

{ 3.9.5.5} ayaṃ vai loka āvapanaṃ mahat | asminn eva loke pratitiṣṭhati | pṛcchāmi tvā param antaṃ pṛthivyā ity āha | vedir vai paro 'ntaḥ pṛthivyāḥ | vedim evāvarundhe | pṛcchāmi tvā bhuvanasya nābhim ity āha | yajño vai bhuvanasya nābhiḥ | yajñam evāvarundhe | pṛcchāmi tvā vṛṣṇo aśvasya reta ity āha | somo vai vṛṣṇo aśvasya retaḥ | somapītham evāvarundhe | pṛcchāmi vācaḥ paramaṃ vyoma-ity āha | brahma vai vācaḥ paramaṃ vyoma | brahamvarcasam evāvarundhe |

anuvāka 6 { 3.9.6.1} apa vā etasmāt prāṇāḥ krāmanti | yo 'śvamedhena yajate | prāṇāya svāhā vyānāya svāhā-iti saṃjñapyamāna āhutīr juhoti | prāṇān evāsmin dadhāti | nāsmāt prāṇā apakrāmanti | avantīḥ sthāvantīs tvāvantu | priyaṃ tvā priyāṇām | varṣiṣṭham āpyānām | nidhīnāṃ tvā nidhipatiṃ havāmahe vaso mama-ity āha | apa-evāsmai tad dhnuvate |

{ 3.9.6.2} atho dhuvanty eva-enam | atho ny evāsmai hnuvate | triḥ pariyanti traya ime lokāḥ | ebhya eva-enaṃ lokebhyo dhuvate | triḥ punaḥ pariyanti | ṣaṭ saṃpadyante | ṣaḍ vā ṛtavaḥ | ṛtubhir evainaṃ dhuvate | apa vā etebhyaḥ prāṇāḥ krāmanti |

{ 3.9.6.3} ye yajñe dhuvanaṃ tanvate | navakṛtvaḥ pariyanti | nava vai pūruṣe prāṇāḥ | prāṇān evā''tman dadhate | na-ebhyaḥ prāṇā apakrāmanti | ambe ambāly ambika iti patnīm udānayati | ahvata-evainām | subhage kāmpīlavāsinī-ity āha | tapa evainām upanayati | suvarge loke saṃ prorṇvāthām ity āha |

{ 3.9.6.4} suvargam eva-enāṃ lokaṃ gamayati | ā-aham ajāni garbhadham ā tvam ajāsi garbhadham ity āha | prajā vai paśavo garbhaḥ | prajām eva paśūn ātman dhatte | devā vā aśvamedhe pavamāne | suvargaṃ lokaṃ na prājānan | tam aśvaḥ prājānāt | yat sūcībhir asipathān kalpayanti | suvargasya lokasya prajñātyai | gāyatrī triṣṭub jagatī-ity āha |

{ 3.9.6.5} yathāyajur eva-etat | trayyaḥ sūcyò bhavanti | ayasmayyo rajatā hariṇyaḥ | asya vai lokasya rūpam ayasmayyaḥ | antarikṣasya rajatāḥ | divo hariṇyaḥ | diśo vā ayasmayyaḥ | avāntaradiśā rajatāḥ | ūrdhvā hariṇyaḥ | diśa evāsmai kalpayati | kas tvā chyati kas tvā viśāstīty āhāhiṃsāyai |

anuvāka 7 { 3.9.7.1} apa vā etasmāt-śrī rāṣṭraṃ krāmati | yo 'śvamedhena yajate | ūrdhvām enām ucchrayatād ity āha | śrīr vai rāṣṭram aśvamedhaḥ | śriyam evāsmai rāṣṭramūrdhvam ucchrayati | veṇubhāraṃ girāv iva-ity āha | rāṣṭraṃ vai bhāraḥ | rāṣṭram evāsmai paryūhati | athāsyā madhyam edhatām ity āha | śrīr vai rāṣṭrasya madhyam |

{ 3.9.7.2} śriyam evāvarundhe | śīte vāte punann iva-ity āha | kṣemo vai rāṣṭrasya śīto vātaḥ | kṣemam evāvarundhe | yad dhariṇī yavam attīty āha | viḍ vai hariṇī | rāṣṭraṃ yavaḥ | viśaṃ caivāsmai rāṣṭraṃ ca samīcī dadhāti | na puṣṭaṃ paśu manyata ity āha | tasmād rājā paśūn na puṣyati |

{ 3.9.7.3} śūdrā yad aryajārā na poṣāya dhanāyatīty āha | tasmād vaiśīpūtraṃ nābhiṣiñcante | iyaṃ yakā śakuntikā-ity āha | viḍ vai śakuntikā | rāṣṭram aśvamedhaḥ | viśaṃ caivāsmai | rāṣṭraṃ ca samīcī dadhāti | āhalam iti sarpatīty āha | tasmād rāṣṭrāya viśaḥ sarpanti | āhataṃ gabhe pasa ity āha | viḍ vai gabhaḥ |

{ 3.9.7.4} rāṣṭraṃ pasaḥ | rāṣṭram eva viśy āhanti | tasmād rāṣṭraṃ viśaṃ ghātukam | mātā ca te pitā ca ta ity āha | iyaṃ vai mātā | asau pitā | ābhyām evainaṃ paridadāti | agraṃ vṛkṣasya rohata ity āha | śrīr vai vṛkṣasyāgram | śriyam evāvarundhe |

{ 3.9.7.5} prasulāmīti te pitā gabhe muṣṭim ataṃsayad ity āha | viḍ vai gabhaḥ | rāṣṭraṃ muṣṭiḥ | rāṣṭram eva viśy āhanti | tasmād rāṣṭraṃ viśa ghātukam | apa vā etebhyaḥ prāṇāḥ krāmanti | ye yajñe 'pūtaṃ vadanti | dadhikrāvṇo akāriṣam iti surabhimatīm ṛcaṃ vadanti | prāṇā vai surabhayaḥ | prāṇān evā''tman dadhate | na-ebhyaḥ prāṇā apakrāmanti | āpo hi ṣṭhā mayobhuva ity adbhir mārjayante | āpo vai sarvā devatāḥ | devatābhir evā''tmānaṃ pavayante |

anuvāka 8 { 3.9.8.1} prajāpatiḥ prajāḥ sṛṣṭvā preṇā-anuprāviśat | tābhyaḥ punaḥ saṃbhavituṃ nāśaknot | so 'bravīt | ṛdhnavad it saḥ | yo me 'taḥ punaḥ saṃbharad iti | taṃ devā aśvamedhena-eva samabharan | tato vai ta ārdhnuvan | yo 'śvamedhena yajate | prajāpatim eva sambharaty ṛdhnoti | puruṣam ālabhate |

{ 3.9.8.2} vairājo vai puruṣaḥ | virājam evā''labhate | atho annaṃ vai virāṭ | annam evāvarundhe | aśvam ālabhate | prājāpatyo vā aśvaḥ | prajāpatim evā''labhate | atho śrīr vā ekaśapham | śriyam evāvarundhe | gām ālabhate |

{ 3.9.8.3} yajño vai gauḥ | yajñam evā''labhate | atho annaṃ vai gauḥ | annam evāvarundhe | ajāvī ālabhate bhūmne | atho puṣṭir vai bhūmā | puṣṭim evāvarundhe | paryagnikṛtaṃ puruṣaṃ ca-āraṇyāṃś ca-utsṛjanty ahiṃsāyai | ubhau vā etau paśū ālabhyete | yaś cāvamo yaś ca paramaḥ | te 'sya-ubhaye yajñe baddhāḥ | abhīṣṭā abhiprītāḥ | abhijitā abhihutā bhavanti | na-enaṃ daṃkṣṇavaḥ paśavo yajñe baddhāḥ | abhīṣṭā abhiprītāḥ | abhijitā abhihutā hiṃsanti | yo 'śvamedhena yajate | ya u cainam evaṃ veda |

anuvāka 9 { 3.9.9.1} prathamena vā eṣa stomena rādhvā | catuṣṭomena kṛtenāyānām uttare 'han | ekaviṃśe pratiṣṭhāyāṃ pratitiṣṭhati | ekaviṃśāt pratiṣṭhāyā ṛtūn anvārohati | ṛtavo vai pṛṣṭhāni | ṛtavaḥ saṃvatsaraḥ | ṛtuṣv eva saṃvatsare pratiṣṭhāya | devatā abhyārohati | śakvarayaḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ bhavanty anyad-anyac chandaḥ | anye-'nye vā ete paśava ālabhyante |

{ 3.9.9.2} uta-iva grāmyāḥ | uta-ivā''raṇyāḥ | ahar eva rūpeṇa samardhayati | atho ahna eva-eṣa balir hriyate | tad āhuḥ | apaśavo vā ete | yad ajāvayaś ca-āraṇyāś ca | ete vai sarve paśavaḥ | yad gavyā iti | gavyān paśūn uttame 'hann ālabhate |

{ 3.9.9.3} tena-eva-ubhayān paśūn avarundhe | prājāpatyā bhavanti | anabhijitasyābhijityai | saurīr nava śvetā vaśā anūbandhā̀ bhavanti | antata eva brahmavarcasam avarundhe | somāya svarājñe 'novāhāv anaḍvāhāv iti dvandvínaḥ paśūn ālabhate | ahorātrāṇām abhijityai | paśubhir vā eṣa vyṛdhyate | yo 'śvamedhena yajate | chagalaṃ kalmāṣaṃ kikidīviṃ vidīgayam iti tvāṣṭrān paśūn ālabhate | paśubhir evā''tmānaṃ samardhayati | ṛtubhir vā eṣa vyṛdhyate | yo 'śvamedhena yajate | piśaṅgās trayo vāsantā ity ṛtupaśūn ālabhate | ṛtubhir evā''tmānaṃ samardhayati | ā vā eṣa paśubhyo vṛścyate | yo 'śvamedhena yajate | paryagnikṛtā utsṛjanty anāvraskāya |

anuvāka 10 { 3.9.10.1} prajāpatir akāmayata mahān annādaḥ syām iti | sa etāv aśvamedhe mahimānāv apaśyat | tāv agṛhṇīta | tato vai sa mahān annādo 'bhavat | yaḥ kāmayeta mahān annādaḥ syām iti | sa etāv aśvamedhe mahimānau gṛhṇīta | mahān evānnādo bhavati | yajamānadevatyā vai vapā | rājā mahimā | yad vapāṃ mahimnā-ubhayataḥ pariyajati | yajamānam eva rājyena-ubhayataḥ parigṛhṇāti | purastātsvāhākārā vā anye devāḥ | upariṣṭātsvāhākārā anye | te vā ete 'śva eva medhya ubhaye 'varudhyante | yad vapāṃ mahimnā-ubhayataḥ pariyajati | tān eva-ubhayān prīṇāti |

anuvāka 11 { 3.9.11.1} vaiśvadevo vā aśvaḥ | taṃ yat prājāpatyaṃ kuryāt | yā devatā apibhāgāḥ | tā bhāgadheyena vyardhayet | devatābhyaḥ samadaṃ dadhyāt | stegān daṃṣṭrābhyāṃ maṇḍūkāñ jambhyebhir iti | ājyam avadānaṃ kṛtvā pratisaṃkhyāyam āhutīr juhoti | yā eva devatā apibhāgāḥ | tā bhāgadheyena samardhayati | na devatābhyaḥ samadaṃ dadhāti |

{ 3.9.11.2} caturdaśa-etān anuvākāñ juhoty anantarityai | prayāsāya svāhā-iti pañcadaśam | pañcadaśa vā ardhamāsasya rātrayaḥ | ardhamāsaśaḥ saṃvatsara āpyate | devāsurāḥ saṃyattā āsan | te 'bruvann agnayaḥ sviṣṭakṛtaḥ | aśvasya medhyasya vayam uddhāram uddharāmahai | atha-etān abhibhavāma-iti | te lohitam udaharanta | tato devā abhavan |

{ 3.9.11.3} parā-asurāḥ | yat sviṣṭakṛdbhyo lohitaṃ juhoti bhrātṛvyābhibhūtyai | bhavaty ātmanā | parā-asya bhrātṛvyo bhavati | gomṛgakaṇṭhena prathamām āhutiṃ juhoti | paśavo vai gomṛgaḥ | rudro 'gniḥ sviṣṭakṛt | rudrād eva paśūn antardadhāti | atho yatra-eṣā-āhutir hūyate | na tatra rudraḥ paśūn abhimanyate |

{ 3.9.11.4} aśvaśaphena dvitīyām āhutiṃ juhoti | paśavo vā ekaśapham | rudro 'gniḥ sviṣṭakṛt | rudrād eva paśūn antardadhāti | atho yatra-eṣā-āhutir hūyate | na tatra rudraḥ paśūn abhimanyate | ayasmayena kamaṇḍalunā tṛtīyām āhutiṃ juhoty āyāsyo vai prajāḥ | rudro 'gniḥ sviṣṭakṛt |rudrād eva prajā antardadhāti | atho yatra-eṣā-āhutir hūyate | na tatra rudraḥ prajā abhimanyate |

anuvāka 12 { 3.9.12.1} aśvasya vā ālabdhasya medha udakrāmat | tad aśvastomīyam abhavat | yad aśvastomīyaṃ juhoti | samedham evainam ālabhate | ājyena juhoti | medho vā ājyam | medho 'śvastomīyam | medhena-evāsmin medhaṃ dadhāti | ṣaṭtriṃśataṃ juhoti | ṣaṭtriṃśad akṣarā bṛhatī |

{ 3.9.12.2} bārhatāḥ paśavaḥ | sā paśūnāṃ mātrā | paśūn eva mātrayā samardhayati | tā yad bhūyasīr vā kanīyasīr vā juhuyāt | paśūn matrayā vyardhayet | ṣaṭtriṃśataṃ juhoti | ṣaṭtriṃśadakṣarā bṛhatī | bārhatāḥ paśavaḥ | sā paśūnāṃ mātrā | paśūn eva mātrayā samardhayati |

{ 3.9.12.3} aśvastomīyaṃ hutvā dvipadā juhoti | dvipād vai puruṣo dvipratiṣṭhaḥ | tad enaṃ pratiṣṭhayā samardhayati | tad āhuḥ | aśvastomīyaṃ pūrvaṃ hotavyā3ṃ dvipadā3 iti | aśvo vā aśvastomīyam | puruṣo dvipadāḥ | aśvastomīyaṃ hutvā dvipadā juhoti | tasmād dvipāc catuṣpādam atti | atho dvipady eva catuṣpadaḥ pratiṣṭhāpayati | dvipadā hutvā | nānyām uttarām āhutiṃ juhuyāt | yad anyām uttarām āhutiṃ juhuyāt | pra pratiṣṭhāyāś cyaveta | dvipadā antato juhoti pratiṣṭhityai |

anuvāka 13 { 3.9.13.1} prajāpatir aśvamedham asṛjata | so 'smāt sṛṣṭo 'pākrāmat | taṃ yajñakratubhir anvaicchat | taṃ yajñakratubhir nānvavindat | tamiṣṭibhir anvaicchat | tam iṣṭibhir anvavindat | tad iṣṭīnām iṣṭitvam | yat saṃvatsaram iṣṭibhir yajate | aśvam eva tad anvicchati | sāvitriyo bhavanti |

{ 3.9.13.2} iyaṃ vai savitā | yo vā asyāṃ naśyati yo nilayate | asyāṃ vāva taṃ vindanti | na vā imām kaś cana-ity āhuḥ | tiryaṅ na-ūrdhvo 'tyetum arhatīti | yat sāvitriyo bhavanti | savitṛprasūta eva-enam icchati | īśvaro vā aśvaḥ pramuktaḥ parāṃ parāvataṃ gantoḥ | yat sāyaṃ dhṛtīr juhoti | aśvasya yatyai dhṛtyai |

{ 3.9.13.3} yat prātar iṣṭibhir yajate | aśvam eva tad anvicchati | yat sāyaṃ dhṛtīr juhoti | aśvasyaiva yatyai dhṛtyai | tasmāt sāyaṃ prajāḥ kṣemyā bhavanti | yat prātar iṣṭibhir yajate | aśvam eva tad anvicchati | tasmād divā naṣṭaiṣa eti | yat prātar iṣṭibhir yajate sāyaṃ dhṛtīr juhoti | ahorātrābhyām evainam anvicchati | atho ahorātrābhyām evāsmai yogakṣemaṃ kalpayati |

anuvāka 14 { 3.9.14.1} apa vā etasmāt-śrī rāṣṭraṃ krāmati | yo 'śvamedhena yajate | brāhmaṇau vīṇāgāthinau gāyataḥ | śriyā vā etad rūpam | yad vīṇā | śriyam evāsmin tad dhattaḥ | yadā khalu vai puruṣaḥ śriyam aśnute | vīṇā-asmai vādyate | tad āhuḥ | yad ubhau brāhmaṇau gāyetām |

{ 3.9.14.2} prabhraṃśukā-asmāt-śrīḥ syāt | na vai brāhmaṇe śrī ramata iti | brāhmaṇo 'nyo gāyet | rājanyo 'nyaḥ | brahma vai brāhmaṇaḥ | kṣatraṃ rājanyaḥ | tathā hāsya brahmaṇā ca kṣatreṇa cobhayataḥ śrīḥ parigṛhītā bhavati | tad āhuḥ | yad ubhau divā gāyetām | apāsmād rāṣṭraṃ krāmet |

{ 3.9.14.3} na vā brāhmaṇe rāṣṭraṃ ramata iti | yadā khalu vai rājā kāmayate | atha brāhmaṇaṃ jināti | divā brāhmaṇo gāyet | naktaṃ rājanyaḥ | brahmaṇo vai rūpam ahaḥ | kṣatrasya rātriḥ | tathā hāsya brahmaṇā ca kṣatreṇa cobhayato rāṣṭraṃ parigṛhītaṃ bhavati | ity adadā ity ayajathā ity apaca iti brāhmaṇo gāyet | iṣṭāpūrtaṃ vai brāhmaṇasya |

{ 3.9.14.4} iṣṭāpūrtena-evainaṃ sa samardhayati | ity ajinā ity ayudhyathā ity amuṃ saṃgrāmam ahann iti rājanyaḥ | yuddhaṃ vai rājanyasya | yuddhena-evainaṃ sa samardhayati | aklṛptā vā etasya-ṛtava ity āhuḥ | yo 'śvamedhena yajata iti | tisro 'nyo gāyati tisro 'nyaḥ | ṣaṭt sampadyante | ṣaḍ vā ṛtavaḥ | ṛtūn evāsmai kalpayataḥ | tābhyāṃ saṃsthāyām | anoyukte ca śate ca dadāti | śatāyuḥ puruṣaḥ śata-indriyaḥ | āyuṣy eva-indriye pratitiṣṭhati |

anuvāka 15 { 3.9.15.1} sarveṣu vā eṣu lokeṣu mṛtyavo 'nvāyattāḥ | tebhyo yad āhutīr na juhuyāt | loke-loka enaṃ mṛtyur vindet | mṛtyave svāhā mṛtyave svāhā-ity abhipūrvam āhutīr juhoti | lokāl-lokād eva mṛtyum avayajate | na-enaṃ loke-loke mṛtyur vindati | yad amuṣmai svāhā-amuṣmai svāhā-iti juhvat saṃcakṣīta | bahuṃ mṛtyum amitraṃ kurvīta | mṛtyave svāhā-ity ekasmā eva-ekāṃ juhuyāt | eko vā amuṣmin- loke mṛtyuḥ |

{ 3.9.15.2} aśanayāmṛtyur eva | tam evāmuṣmin-loke 'vayajate | bhrūṇahatyāyai svāhā-ity avabhṛtha āhutiṃ juhoti | bhrūṇahatyām evāvayajate | tad āhuḥ | yad bhrūṇahatyā-apātryātha | kasmād yajñe 'pi kriyata iti | amṛtyur vā anyo bhrūṇahatyāyā ity āhuḥ | bhrūṇahatyā vāva mṛtyur iti | yad bhrūṇahatyāyai svāhā-ity avabhṛtha āhutiṃ juhoti |

{ 3.9.15.3} mṛtyum eva-āhutyā tarpayitvā paripāṇaṃ kṛtvā | bhrūṇaghne bheṣajaṃ karoti | etāṃ ha vai muṇḍibha audanyavaḥ | {ḍyumoṇṭa suggests to take out daṇḍa} bhrūṇahatyāyai prāyaścittiṃ vidāṃ cakāra | yo hāsyāpi prajāyāṃ brāhmaṇaṃ hanti | sarvasmai tasmai bheṣajaṃ karoti | jumbakāya svāhā-ity avabhṛtha uttamām āhutiṃ juhoti | varuṇo vai jumbakaḥ | antata eva varuṇam avayajate | khalater viklidhasya śuklasya piṅgākṣasya mūrdhan juhoti | etad vai varuṇasya rūpaṃ | rūpeṇaiva varuṇam avayajate |

anuvāka 16 { 3.9.16.1} vāruṇo vā aśvaḥ | taṃ devatayā vyardhayati | yat prājāpatyaṃ karoti | namo rājñe namo varuṇāya-ity āha | vāruṇo vā aśvaḥ | svayā-evainaṃ devatayā samardhayati | namo 'śvāya namaḥ prajāpataya ity āha | prājāpatyo vā aśvaḥ | svayā-evainaṃ devatayā samardhayati | namo 'dhipataya ity āha |

{ 3.9.16.2} dharmo vā adhipatiḥ | dharmam evāvarundhe | adhipatir asy adhipatiṃ mā kurv adhipatir ahaṃ prajānāṃ bhūyāsam ity āha | adhipatim evainaṃ samānānāṃ karoti | māṃ dhehi mayi dhehīty āha | āśiṣam evaitām āśāste | upākṛtāya svāhā-ity upākṛte juhoti | ālabdhāya svāhā-iti niyukte juhoti | hutāya svāhā-iti hute juhoti | eṣāṃ lokānām abijityai |

{ 3.9.16.3} pra vā eṣa ebhyo lokebhyaś cyavate | yo 'śvamedhena yajate | āgneyam aindrāgnam āśvinam | tān paśūn ālabhate pratiṣṭhityai | yad āgneyo bhavati | agniḥ sarvā devatāḥ | devatā evāvarundhe | brahma vā agniḥ | kṣatram indraḥ | yad aindrāgno bhavati |

{ 3.9.16.4} brahmakṣatre evāvarundhe | yad āśvino bhavati | āśiṣām avaruddhyai | trayo bhavanti | traya ime lokāḥ | eṣv eva lokeṣu pratitiṣṭhati | agnaye 'ṃhomuce 'ṣṭākapāla iti daśahaviṣam iṣṭiṃ nirvapati | daśākṣarā virāṭ | annaṃ virāṭ | virājā-evānnādyam avarundhe | agner manve prathamasya pracetasa iti yājyānuvākyā bhavanti sarvatvāya |

anuvāka 17 { 3.9.17.1} yady aśvam upatapad vindet | āgneyam aṣṭākapālaṃ nirvapet | saumyaṃ carum | sāvitram aṣṭākapālam | yad āgneyo bhavati | agniḥ sarvā devatāḥ | devatābhir evainaṃ bhiṣajyati | yat saumyo bhavati | somo vā oṣadhīnāṃ rājā | yābhya evainaṃ vindati |

{ 3.9.17.2} tābhir evainaṃ bhiṣajyati | yat sāvitro bhavati | savitṛprasūta evainaṃ bhiṣajyati | etābhir evainaṃ devatābhir bhiṣajyati | agado haiva bhavati | pauṣṇaṃ caruṃ nirvapet | yadi śloṇaḥ syāt | pūṣā vai śloṇyasya bhiṣak | sa evainaṃ bhiṣajyati | aśloṇo haiva bhavati |

{ 3.9.17.3} raudraṃ caruṃ nirvapet | yadi mahatī devatā-abhimanyeta | etaddevatyo vā aśvaḥ | svayā-evainaṃ devatayā bhiṣajyati | agado haiva bhavati | vaiśvānaraṃ dvādaśakapālaṃ nirvapen mṛgākhare yadi na-āgacchet | iyaṃ vā agnir vaiśvānaraḥ | iyam evainam arcibhyāṃ parirodham ānayati | ā haiva sutyam ahar gacchati | yady adhīyāt |

{ 3.9.17.4} agnaye 'ṃhomuce 'ṣṭākapālaḥ | sauryaṃ payaḥ | vāyavya ājyabhāgaḥ | yajamāno vā aśvaḥ | aṃhasā vā eṣa gṛhītaḥ | yasyāśvo medhāya prokṣito 'dhyeti | yad aṃhomuce nirvapati | aṃhasa eva tena mucyate | yajamāno vā aśvaḥ | retasā vā eṣa vyṛdhyate |

{ 3.9.17.5} yasyāśvo medhāya prokṣito 'dhyeti | sauryaṃ retaḥ | yat sauryaṃ payo bhavati | retasā-evainaṃ sa samardhayati | yajamāno vā aśvaḥ | garbhair vā eṣa vyṛdhyate | yasyāśvo medhāya prokṣito 'dhyeti | vāyavyā garbhāḥ | yad vāyavya ājyabhāgo bhavati | garbhair evainaṃ sa samardhayati | atho yasyaiṣāśvamedhe prāyaścitiḥ kriyate | iṣṭvā vasīyān bhavati |

anuvāka 18 { 3.9.18.1} tad āhuḥ | dvādaśa brahmaudanānt saṃsthite nirvapet | dvādaśabhir veṣṭibhir yajeta-iti | yad iṣṭibhir yajeta | upanāmuka enaṃ yajñaḥ syāt | pāpīyāṃs tu syāt | āptāni vā etasya chandāṃsi | ya ījānaḥ | tāni ka etāvadāśu punaḥ prayuñjīta-iti | sarvā vai saṃsthite yajñe vāg āpyate |

{ 3.9.18.2} sā-āptā bhavati yātayāmnī | krūrīkṛteva hi bhavaty aruṣkṛtā | sā na punaḥ prayujyā-ity āhuḥ | dvādaśa-eva brahmaudanānt saṃsthite nirvapet | prajāpatir vā odanaḥ | yajñaḥ prajāpatiḥ | upanāmuka enaṃ yajño bhavati | na pāpīyān bhavati | dvādaśa bhavanti | 0 dvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ | 1 saṃvatsara eva pratitiṣṭhati |

anuvāka 19 { 3.9.19.3} eṣa vai vibhūr nāma yajñaḥ | sarvaṃ ha vai tatra vibhu bhavati | yatra-etena yajñena yajante | eṣa vai prabhūr nāma yajñaḥ | sarvaṃ ha vai tatra prabhu bhavati | yatra-etena yajñena yajante | eṣa vā ūrjasvān nāma yajñaḥ | sarvaṃ ha vai tatra-ūrjasvadbhavati | yatra-etena yajñena yajante | eṣa vai payasvān nāma yajñaḥ |

{ 3.9.19.4} sarvaṃ ha vai tatra payasvad bhavati | yatra-etena yajñena yajante | eṣa vai vidhṛto nāma yajñaḥ | sarvaṃ ha vai tatra vidhṛtaṃ bhavati | yatra-etena yajñena yajante | eṣa vai vyāvṛtto nāma yajñaḥ | sarvaṃ ha vai tatra vyāvṛttaṃ bhavati | yatra-etena yajñena yajante | eṣa vai pratiṣṭhito nāma yajñaḥ | sarvaṃ ha vai tatra pratiṣṭhitaṃ bhavati |

{ 3.9.19.5} yatra-etena yajñena yajante | eṣa vai tejasvī nāma yajñaḥ | sarvaṃ ha vai tatra tejasvi bhavati | yatra-etena yajñena yajante | eṣa vai brahmavarcasī nāma yajñaḥ | ā ha vai tatra brāhmaṇo brahmavarcasī jāyate | yatra-etena yajñena yajante | eṣa vā ativyādhī nāma yajñaḥ | ā ha vai tatra rājanyo 'tivyādhī jāyate | yatra-etena yajñena yajante | eṣa vai dīrgho nāma yajñaḥ | dīrghāyuṣo ha vai tatra manuṣyā bhavanti | yatra-etena yajñena yajante | eṣa vai klṛpto nāma yajñaḥ | kalpate ha vai tatra prajābhyo yogakṣemaḥ | yatra-etena yajñena yajante |

anuvāka 20 { 3.9.20.1} tārpyeṇāśvaṃ saṃjñapayanti | yajño vai tārpyam | yajñena-evainaṃ samardhayanti | yāmena sāmnā prastotā-anūpatiṣṭhate | yamalokam evainaṃ gamayati | tārpye ca kṛtyadhīvāse cāśvaṃ saṃjñapayanti | etad vai paśūnāṃ rūpam | rūpeṇaiva paśūn avarundhe | hiraṇyakaśipu bhavati | tejaso 'varuddhyai |

{ 3.9.20.2} rukmo bhavati | suvargasya lokasyānukhyātyai | aśvo bhavati | prajāpater āptyai | asya vai lokasya rūpaṃ tārpyam | antarikṣasya kṛtyadhīvāsaḥ | divo hiraṇyakaśipu | ādityasya rukmaḥ | prajāpater aśvaḥ | imam eva lokaṃ tārpyeṇa-āpnoti |

{ 3.9.20.3} antarikṣaṃ kṛtyadhīvāsena | divaṃ hiraṇyakaśipunā | ādityaṃ rukmeṇa | aśvena-eva medhyena prajāpateḥ sāyujyaṃ salokatām āpnoti | etāsām eva devatānāṃ sāyujyam | sārṣṭitāṃ samānalokatām āpnoti | yo 'śvamedhena yajate | ya u cainam evaṃ veda |

anuvāka 21 { 3.9.21.1} ādityāś cāṅgirasaś ca suvarge loke 'spardhanta | te 'ṅgirasa ādityebhyaḥ | amum ādityamaśvaṃ śvetaṃ bhūtaṃ dakṣiṇām anayan | te 'bruvan | yaṃ no 'neṣṭa | sa varyo 'bhūd iti | tasmād aśvaṃ savarya-ity āhvayanti | tasmād yajñe varo dīyate | yat prajāpatir ālabdho 'śvo 'bhavat | tasmād aśvo nāma |

{ 3.9.21.2} yat-śvayad arur āsīt | tasmād arvā nāma | yat sadyo vājant samajayat | tasmād vājī nāma | yad asurāṇāṃ lokān ādatta | tasmād ādityo nāma | agnirvā aśvamedhasya yonir āyatanam | sūryo 'gner yonir āyatanam | yad aśvamedhe 'gnau citya uttaravedim upavapati | yonimantam evainam āyatanavantaṃ karoti |

{ 3.9.21.78} yonimān āyatanavān bhavati | ya {ānaṃdā. sa} evaṃ veda | prāṇāpānau vā etau devānām | yad arkāśvamedhau | prāṇāpānāv evāvarundhe | ojo balaṃ vā etau devānām | yad arkāśvamedhau | ojo balam evāvarundhe | agnir vā aśvamedhasya yonir āyatanam | sūryo 'gner yonir āyatanam | yad aśvamedhe 'gnau citya uttaravediṃ cinoti | tāv arkāśvamedhau | arkāśvamedhāv evāvarundhe | atho arkāśvamedhayor eva pratitiṣṭhati |

anuvāka 22 { 3.9.22.1} prajāpatiṃ vai devāḥ pitaram | paśuṃ bhūtaṃ medhāya-ālabhanta | tam ālabhya-upāvasan | prātar yaṣṭāsmaha iti | ekaṃ vā etad devānām ahaḥ | yat saṃvatsaraḥ | tasmād aśvaḥ purastāt saṃvatsara ālabhyate | yat prajāpatir ālabdho 'śvo 'bhavat | tasmād aśvaḥ | yat sadyo medho 'bhavat |

{ 3.9.22.2} tasmād aśvamedhaḥ | veduko 'śvam āśuṃ bhavati | ya evaṃ veda | yad vai tat prajāpatir ālabdho 'śvo 'bhavat | tasmād aśvaḥ prajāpateḥ paśūnām anurūpatamaḥ | ā-asya putraḥ pratirūpo jāyate | ya evaṃ veda | sarvāṇi bhūtāni sambhṛtya-ālabhate | sam enaṃ devās tejase brahmavarcasāya bharanti | yo 'śvamedhena yajate |

{ 3.9.22.3} ya u cainam evaṃ veda | etad vai tad devā etāṃ devatām | paśuṃ bhūtaṃ medhāya-ālabhanta | yajñam eva | yajñena yajñam ayajanta devāḥ | kāmapraṃ yajñam akurvata | te 'mṛtatvam akāmayanta | te 'mṛtatvam agacchan | yo 'śvamedhena yajate | devānām eva-āyanena-eti |

{ 3.9.22.4} prājāpatyena-eva yajñena yajate kāmapreṇa | apunarmāram eva gacchati | etasya vai rūpeṇa purastāt prājāpatyam ṛṣabhaṃ tūparaṃ bahurūpam ālabhate | sarvebhyaḥ kāmebhyaḥ | sarvasya-āptyai | sarvasya jityai | sarvam eva tena-āpnoti | sarvaṃ jayati | yo 'śvamedhena yajate | ya u cainam evaṃ veda |

anuvāka 23 { 3.9.23.83} yo vā aśvasya medhyasya lomanī veda | aśvasyaiva medhyasya lomaṃllomañjuhoti | ahorātre vā aśvasya medhyasya lomanī | yat sāyamprātar juhoti | aśvasyaiva medhyasya lomaṃllomañ juhoti | etadanukṛti ha sma vai purā | aśvasya medhyasya lomaṃllomañ juhvati | yo vā aśvasya medhyasya pade veda | aśvasyaiva medhyasya padepade juhoti | darśapūrṇamāsau vā aśvasya medhyasya pade |

{ 3.9.23.2} yad darśapūrṇamāsau yajate | aśvasyaiva medhyasya padepade juhoti | etadanukṛti ha sma vai purā | aśvasya medhyasya padepade juhvati | yo vā aśvasya medhyasya vivartanaṃ veda | aśvasyaiva medhyasya vivartane vivartane juhoti | asau vā ādityo 'śvaḥ | sa āhavanīyam āgacchati | tad vivartate | yad agnihotraṃ juhoti | aśvasyaiva medhyasya vivartanevivartane juhoti | etadanukṛti ha sma vai purā | aśvasya medhyasya vivartanevivartane juhvati |







prapāṭhaka: 10 anuvāka 1 sāvitracayanam { 3.10.1.1} saṃjñānaṃ vijñānaṃ prajñānaṃ jānad abhijānat | saṃkalpamānaṃ prakalpamānam upakalpamānam upaklṛptaṃ klṛptam | śreyo vasīya āyat sambhūtaṃ bhūtam | citraḥ ketuḥ prabhān ābhānt saṃbhān | jyotiṣmāṃs tejasvān ātapaṃs tapann abhitapan | rocano rocamānaḥ śobhanaḥ śobhamānaḥ kalyāṇaḥ | darśā dṛṣṭā darśatā viśvarūpā sudarśanā | āpyāyamānā pyāyamānā pyāyā sūnṛterā | āpūryamāṇā pūryamāṇā pūrayantī pūrṇā paurṇamāsī | dātā pradātā-ānando modaḥ pramodaḥ |

{ 3.10.1.2} āveśayan niveśayant saṃveśanaḥ saṃśāntaḥ śāntaḥ | ābhavan prabhavant saṃbhavant saṃbhūto bhūtaḥ | prastutaṃ viṣṭutaṃ saṃstutaṃ kalyāṇaṃ viśvarūpam | śukram amṛtaṃ tejasvi tejaḥ samiddham | aruṇaṃ bhānuman marīcimad abhitapat tapasvat | savitā prasavitā dīpto dīpayan dīpyamānaḥ | jvalañ jvalitā tapan vitapant saṃtapan | rocano rocamānaḥ śumbhūḥ śumbhamāno vāmaḥ | sutā sunvatī prasutā sūyamānā-abhiṣūyamāṇā | pītī prapā saṃpā tṛptis tarpayantī |

{ 3.10.1.3} kāntā kāmyā kāmajātā-āyuṣmatī kāmadughā | abhiśāstā-anumantā-ānando modaḥ pramodaḥ | āsādayan niṣādayant saṃsādanaḥ saṃsannaḥ sannaḥ | ābhūr vibhūḥ prabhūḥ śambhūr bhuvaḥ | pavitraṃ paviyiṣyan pūto medhyaḥ | yaśo yaśasvān āyur amṛtaḥ | jīvo jīviṣyant svargo lokaḥ | sahasvānt sahīyān ojasvānt sahamānaḥ | jayann abhijayant sudraviṇo draviṇodāḥ | ārdrapavitro harikeśo modaḥ pramodaḥ |

{ 3.10.1.4} aruṇo 'ruṇarajāḥ puṇḍarīko viśvajid abhijit | ārdraḥ pinvamāno 'nnavān rasavān irāvān | sarvauṣadhaḥ sambharo mahasvān | ejatkā jovatkāḥ | kṣullakāḥ śipiviṣṭakāḥ | sarisrarāḥ suśeravaḥ | ajirāso gamiṣṇavaḥ | idānīṃ tadānīm etarhi kṣipram ajiram | āśur nimeṣaḥ phaṇo dravann atidravan | tvaraṃs tvaramāṇa āśur āśīyāñ javaḥ {ḍyumoṇṭa javaḥ [?]} | agniṣṭoma ukthyo 'tirātro dvirātras trirātraś catūrātraḥ | agnir ṛtuḥ sūrya ṛtuś candramā ṛtuḥ | prajāpatiḥ saṃvatsaro mahān kaḥ |

anuvāka 2 sāvitracayanam { 3.10.2.1} bhūr agniṃ ca pṛthivīṃ ca māṃ ca | trīṃś ca lokānt saṃvatsaraṃ ca | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda | bhuvo vāyuṃ cāntarikṣaṃ ca māṃ ca | trīṃśca lokānt saṃvatsaraṃ ca | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda | svar ādityaṃ ca divaṃ ca māṃ ca | trīṃś ca lokānt saṃvatsaraṃ ca | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda | bhūr bhuvaḥ svaś candramasaṃ ca diśaś ca māṃ ca | trīṃś ca lokānt saṃvatsaraṃ ca | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

anuvāka 3 sāvitracayanam { 3.10.3.1} tvam eva tvāṃ vettha yo 'si so 'si | tvam eva tvām acaiṣīḥ | citaś cāsi saṃcitaś cāsy agne | etāvāṃś cāsi bhūyāṃś cāsy agne | yat te agne nyūnaṃ yad u te 'tiriktam | ādityās tad aṅgirasaś cinvantu | viśve te devāś citim āpūrayantu | citaś cāsi saṃcitaś cāsy agne | etāvāṃś cāsi bhūyāṃś cāsy agne | mā te agne 'cayena mā-aticayena-āyur āvṛkṣi | sarveṣāṃ jyotiṣāṃ jyotir yad adāv udeti | tapaso jātam anibhṛṣṭam ojaḥ | tat te jyotir iṣṭake | tena me tapa | tena me jvala | tena me dīdihi | yāvad devāḥ | yāvad asāti sūryaḥ | yāvad utāpi brahma |

anuvāka 4 sāvitracayanam { 3.10.4.1} saṃvatsaro 'si parivatsaro 'si | idāvatsaro 'sīduvatsaro 'si | idvatsaro 'si vatsaro 'si | tasya te vasantaḥ siraḥ | grīṣmo dakṣiṇaḥ pakṣaḥ | varṣāḥ puccham | śarad uttaraḥ pakṣaḥ | hemanto madhyam | pūrvapakṣāś citayaḥ | aparapakṣāḥ purīṣam |

{ 3.10.4.2} ahorātrāṇīṣṭakāḥ | ṛṣabho 'si svargo lokaḥ | yasyāṃ diśi mahīyase | tato no maha āvaha | vāyur bhūtvā sarvā diśa āvāhi | sarvā diśo 'nuvivāhi | sarvā diśo 'nusaṃvāhi | cittyā { cityā} citim āpṛṇa | acittyā { acityā} citim āpṛṇa | cid asi samudrayoniḥ |

{ 3.10.4.3} indur dakṣaḥ syena ṛtāvā | hiraṇyapakṣaḥ śakuno bhuraṇyuḥ | mahānt sadhasthe dhruva āniṣattaḥ | namas te astu mā mā hiṃsīḥ | ā-iti pra-iti vīti sam ity ud iti | divaṃ me yaccha | antarikṣaṃ me yaccha | pṛthivīṃ me yaccha | pṛthivīṃ me yaccha | antarikṣaṃ me yaccha | divaṃ me yaccha | ahnā prasāraya | rātryā samaca | rātryā prasāraya ahnā samaca | kāmaṃ prasāraya | kāmaṃ samaca |

anuvāka 5 sāvitracayanam { 3.10.5.1} bhūr bhuvaḥ svaḥ | ojo balam | brahma kṣatram | yaśo mahat | satyaṃ tapo nāma | rūpam amṛtam | cakṣuḥ srotram | mana āyuḥ | viśvaṃ yaśo mahaḥ | samaṃ tapo haro bhāḥ | jātavedā yadi vā pāvako 'si | vaiśvānaro yadi vā vaidyuto 'si | śaṃ prajābhyo yajamānāya lokam | ūrjaṃ puṣṭiṃ dadad abhyāvavṛtsva {ānaṃdā. abhyāvabhṛtsva) |

anuvāka 6 sāvitracayanam { 3.10.6.1} rājñī virājñī | samrājñī svarājñī | arciḥ śociḥ | tapo haro bhāḥ | agnir indro bṛhaspatiḥ | viśve devā bhuvanasya gopāḥ | te mā sarve yaśasā saṃsṛjantu |

anuvāka 7 sāvitracayanam { 3.10.7.1} asave svāhā | vasave svāhā | vibhuve svāhā | vivasvate svāhā | abhibhuve svāhā-adhipataye svāhā | divāṃ pataye svāhā-aṃhaspatyāya svāhā | cākṣuṣmatyāya svāhā jyotiṣmatyāya svāhā | rājñe svāhā virājñe svāhā | saṃrājñe svāhā | svarājñe svāhā | śūṣāya svāhā sūryāya svāhā | candramase svāhā jyotiṣe svāhā | saṃsarpāya svāhā | kalyāṇāya svāhā | arjunāya svāhā |

anuvāka 8 sāvitracayanam { 3.10.8.1} vipaścite pavamānāya gāyata | mahī na dhārā-aty andho arṣati | ahir ha jīrṇām atisarpati tvacam | atyo na krīḍann asarad vṛṣā hariḥ | upayāmagṛhīto 'si mṛtyave tvā juṣṭaṃ gṛhṇāmi | eṣa te yonir mṛtyave tvā | apa mṛtyum apa kṣudham | apa-itaḥ śapathaṃ jahi | adhā { athā} no agna āvaha | rāyaspoṣaṃ sahasriṇam |

{ 3.10.8.2} ye te sahasram ayutaṃ pāśāḥ | mṛtyo martyāya hantave | tān yajñasya māyayā | sarvān avayajāmahe | bhakṣo 'sy amṛtabhakṣaḥ | tasya te mṛtyupītasyāmṛtavataḥ | svagākṛtasya madhumataḥ | upahūtasya-upahūto bhakṣayāmi | mandrā-abhibhūtiḥ ketur yajñānāṃ vāk | asāv ehi |

{ 3.10.8.3} andho jāgṛviḥ prāṇa | asāv ehi | badhira ākrandayitar apāna | asāv ehi | ahasto 'stā { 'stvā} cakṣuḥ | asāv ehi | apādāśo manaḥ | asāv ehi | kave vipracitte śrotra | asāv ehi |

{ 3.10.8.4} suhastaḥ suvāsāḥ | śūṣo nāmāsy amṛto martyeṣu | taṃ tvā-ahaṃ tathā veda | asāv ehi | agnir me vāci śritaḥ | vāg hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | aham amṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi | vāyur me prāṇe śritaḥ |

{ 3.10.8.5} prāṇo hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | aham amṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi | sūryo me cakṣuṣi śritaḥ | cakṣur hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | aham amṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi | candramā me manasi śritaḥ |

{ 3.10.8.6} mano hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | aham amṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi | diśo me śrotre śritāḥ | śrotraṃ hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | aham amṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi | āpo me retasi śritāḥ |

{ 3.10.8.7} reto hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | aham amṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi | pṛthivī me śarīre śritā | śarīraṃ hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | aham amṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi | oṣadhivanaspatayo me lomasu śritāḥ |

{ 3.10.8.8} lomāni hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | aham amṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi | indro me bale śritaḥ | balaṃ hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | aham amṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi | parjanyo me mūrdhni śritaḥ |

{ 3.10.8.9} mūrdhā hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | aham amṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi | īśāno me manyau śritaḥ | manyur hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | aham amṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi | ātmā ma ātmani śritaḥ |

{ 3.10.8.10} ātmā hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | aham amṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi | punar ma ātmā punar āyur āgāt | punaḥ prāṇaḥ punar ākūtam āgāt | vaiśvānaro raśmibhir vāvṛdhānaḥ | antas tiṣṭhatv amṛtasya gopāḥ |

anuvāka 9 sāvitracayanam { 3.10.9.1} prajāpatir devān asṛjata | te pāpmanā saṃditā ajāyanta | tān vyadyat | yad vyadyat | tasmād vidyut | tam avṛścat | yad avṛścat | tasmād vṛṣṭiḥ | tasmād yatra-ete devate abhiprāpnutaḥ { abhiyāpnutaḥ} | vi ca haivāsya tatra pāpmānaṃ dyataḥ |

{ 3.10.9.24} vṛścataś ca | sā-eṣā mīmāṃsognihotra eva saṃpannā {,ānaṃdā. saṃpannā} | atho āhuḥ | sarveṣu yajñakratuṣv iti | hoṣyann apa upaspṛśet | vidyud asi vidya me pāpmānam iti | atha hutvā-upaspṛśet | vṛśṭir asi vṛśca me pāpmānam iti | yakṣyamāṇo vā-iṣṭvā vā | vi ca haivāsyaite devate pāpmānaṃ dyataḥ |

{ 3.10.9.3} vṛścataś ca | atyaṃho ha-āruṇiḥ | brahmacāriṇe praśnān procya prajighāya | parehi | plakṣaṃ daiyāmpātiṃ pṛccha | vettha sāvitrā3n { sāvitrā3ṃ} na vetthā3 iti | tam āgatya papraccha | ācāryo mā prā haiṣīt | vettha sāvitrā3n { sāvitrā3ṃ} na vetthā3 iti | sa ha-uvāca veda-iti |

{ 3.10.9.4} sa kasmin pratiṣṭhita iti | parorajasīti | kas tad yat parorajā iti | eṣa vāva sa parorajā iti ha-uvāca | ya eṣa tapati | eṣo 'rvāgrajā iti | sa kasmin tv eṣa iti | satya iti | kiṃ tat satyam iti | tapa iti |

{ 3.10.9.5} kasmin nu tapa iti | bala iti | kiṃ tad balam iti | prāṇa iti | mā sma prāṇam atipṛccha iti mā-ācāryo 'bravīd iti ha-uvāca brahmacārī | sa ha-uvāca plakṣo daiyāmpātiḥ | yad vai brahmacārin prāṇam atyaprakṣyaḥ {,ānaṃdā. atyaprakṣyaḥ} | mūrdhā te vyapatiṣyat {,ānaṃdā. vyapatiṣyat} | aham u ta ācāryāt-śreyān bhaviṣyāmi | yo mā sāvitre samavādiṣṭa-iti |

{ 3.10.9.6} tasmāt sāvitre na saṃvadeta | sa yo ha vai sāvitraṃ viduṣā sāvitre saṃvadate | sahāsmin-śriyaṃ dadhāti | anu ha vā asmā asau tapan-śriyaṃ manyate | anv asmai śrīs tapo manyate | anv asmai tapo balaṃ manyate | anv asmai balaṃ prāṇaṃ manyate | sa yad āha | saṃjñānaṃ vijñānaṃ darśā dṛṣṭā-iti | eṣa eva tat |

{ 3.10.9.7} atha yad āha | prastutaṃ viṣṭutaṃ sutā sunvatīti | eṣa eva tat | eṣa hy eva tāny ahāni | eṣa rātrayaḥ | atha yad āha | citraḥ ketur dātā pradātā savitā prasavitā-abhiśāstā-anumantā-iti | eṣa eva tat | eṣa hy eva te 'hno muhūrtāḥ | eṣa rātreḥ |

{ 3.10.9.8} atha yad āha | pavitraṃ pavayiṣyant sahasvānt sahīyān aruṇo 'ruṇarajā iti | eṣa eva tat | eṣa hy eva te 'rdhamāsāḥ | eṣa māsāḥ | atha yad āha | agniṣṭoma ukthyo 'gnir ṛtuḥ prajāpatiḥ saṃvatsara iti | eṣa eva tat | eṣa hy eva te yajñakratavaḥ | eṣa ṛtavaḥ |

{ 3.10.9.9} eṣa saṃvatsaraḥ | atha yad āha | idānīṃ tadānīm iti | eṣa eva tat | eṣa hy eva te muhūrtānāṃ { mudvūrtānāṃ} muhūrtāḥ { muddūrtāḥ} | {e} janako ha vaidehaḥ | ahorātraiḥ samājagāma | taṃ ha-ūcuḥ | yo vā asmān veda | vijahat pāpmānam eti |

{ 3.10.9.10} sarvam āyur eti | abhi svargaṃ lokaṃ jayati | nāsyāmuṣmin-loke 'nnaṃ kṣīyata iti | vijahad dha vai pāpmānam eti | sarvam āyur eti | abhi svargaṃ lokaṃ jayati | nāsyāmuṣmin-loke 'nnaṃ kṣīyate | ya evaṃ veda | ahīnā ha-āśvatthyaḥ | sāvitraṃ vidāṃcakāra |

{ 3.10.9.11} sa ha haṃso hiraṇmayo bhūtvā | svargaṃ lokam iyāya | ādityasya sāyujyam | haṃso ha vai hiraṇmayo bhūtvā | svargaṃ lokam eti | ādityasya sāyujyam | ya evaṃ veda | devabhāgo ha śrautarṣaḥ | sāvitraṃ vidāṃcakāra | taṃ ha vāg adṛśyamānā'bhyuvāca |

{ 3.10.9.12} sarvaṃ bata gautamo veda | yaḥ sāvitraṃ veda-iti | sa ha-uvāca | kā-eṣā vāg asīti | ayam ahaṃ sāvitraḥ | devānām uttamo lokaḥ | guhyaṃ maho bibhrad iti | etāvati ha gautamaḥ | yajñopavītaṃ kṛtvā-adho nipapāta | namo nama iti |

{ 3.10.9.13} sa ha-uvāca | mā bhaiṣīr gautama | jito vai te loka iti | tasmād ye ke ca sāvitraṃ viduḥ | sarve te jitalokāḥ | sa yo ha vai sāvitrasyāṣṭākṣaraṃ padaṃ śriyā-abhiṣiktaṃ veda | śriyā haivābhiṣicyate | ghṛṇir iti dve akṣare | sūrya iti trīṇi | āditya iti trīṇi |

{ 3.10.9.14} etad vai sāvitrasyāṣṭākṣaraṃ padaṃ śriyā-abhiṣiktam | ya evaṃ veda | śriyā haivā-abhiṣicyate | tad etad ṛcā-abhyuktam | ṛco akṣare parame vyoman | yasmin devā adhi viśve niṣeduḥ | yas tan na veda kim ṛcā kariṣyati | ya it tad vidus ta ime samāsata iti | na ha vā etasya-ṛcā na yajuṣā na sāmnā-artho 'sti | yaḥ sāvitraṃ veda |

{ 3.10.9.15} tad etat pari yad devacakram | ārdraṃ pinvamānaṃ svarge loka eti | vijahad viśvā bhūtāni saṃpaśyat | ārdro ha vai pinvamānaḥ svarge loka eti | vijahan viśvā bhūtāni saṃpaśyan | ya evaṃ veda | śūṣo ha vai vārṣṇeyaḥ | ādityena samājagāma | taṃ ha-uvāca | ehi sāvitraṃ viddhi | ayaṃ vai svargyo 'gniḥ pārayiṣṇur amṛtāt saṃbhūta iti | eṣa vāva sa sāvitraḥ | ya eṣa tapati | ehi māṃ viddhi | iti haivainaṃ tad uvāca |

anuvāka 10 sāvitracayanam { 3.10.10.1} iyaṃ vāva saraghā | tasyā agnir eva sāraghaṃ madhu | yā etāḥ pūrvapakṣāparapakṣayo rātrayaḥ | tā madhukṛtaḥ | yāny ahāni | te madhuvṛṣāḥ | sa yo ha vā etā madhukṛtaś ca madhuvṛṣāṃś ca veda | kruvanti hāsyaitā agnau madhu | nāsya-iṣṭāpūrtaṃ dhayanti | atha yo na veda |

{ 3.10.10.2} na hāsyaitā agnau madhu kurvanti | dhayanty asya-iṣṭāpūrtam | yo ha vā ahorātrāṇāṃ nāmadheyāni veda | nāhorātreṣv ārtim ārcchati | saṃjñānaṃ vijñānaṃ darśā dṛṣṭā-iti | etāv anuvākau pūrvapakṣasyāhorātrāṇāṃ nāmadheyāni | prastutaṃ viṣṭutaṃ sutā sunvatī-iti | etāv anuvākāv aparapakṣasyāhorātrāṇāṃ nāmadheyāni | nāhorātreṣv ārtim ārcchati | ya evaṃ veda |

{ 3.10.10.3} yo ha vai muhūrtānāṃ nāmadheyāni veda | na muhūrteṣv ārtim ārcchati | citraḥ ketur dātā pradātā savitā prasavitā-abhiśāstā-anumantā-iti | ete 'nuvākā muhūrtānāṃ nāmadheyāni | na muhūrteṣv ārtim ārcchati | ya evaṃ veda | yo ha vā ardhamāsānāṃ ca māsānāṃ ca nāmadheyāni veda | nārdhamāseṣu na māseṣv ārtim ārcchati | pavitraṃ paviyiṣyant sahasvānt sahīyān aruṇo 'ruṇarajā iti | ete 'nuvākā ardhamāsānāṃ ca māsānāṃ ca nāmadheyāni |

{ 3.10.10.4} nārdhamāseṣu na māseṣv ārtim ārcchati | ya evaṃ veda | yo ha vai yajñakratūnāṃ ca-ṛtūnāṃ ca saṃvatsarasya ca nāmadheyāni veda | na yajñakratuṣu na-ṛtuṣu na saṃvatsara ārtim ārcchati | agniṣṭoma ukthyo 'gnir ṛtuḥ prajāpatiḥ saṃvatsara iti | ete 'nuvākā yajñakratūnāṃ ca-ṛtūnāṃ ca saṃvatsarasya ca nāmadheyāni | na yajñakratuṣu na-ṛtuṣu na saṃvatsara ārtim ārcchati | ya evaṃ veda | yo ha vai muhūrtānāṃ muhūrtān veda | na muhūrtānāṃ muhūrteṣv ārtim ārcchati |

{ 3.10.10.5} idānīṃ tadānīm iti | ete vai muhūrtānāṃ muhūrtāḥ | na muhūrtānāṃ muhūrteṣv ārtim ārcchati | ya evaṃ veda | atho yathā kṣetrajño bhūtvā-anupraviśyānnam atti | evam eva-etān kṣetrajño bhūtvā-anupraviśyānnam atti | sa eteṣām eva salokatāṃ sāyujyam aśnute | apa punarmṛtyuṃ jayati | ya evaṃ veda |

anuvāka 11 sāvitracayanam { 3.10.11.1} kaś cid dha vā asmāl lokāt pretya | ātmānaṃ veda | āyam aham asmīti | kaś cit svaṃ lokaṃ na pratiprajānāti | agnimugdho haiva dhumatāntaḥ | svaṃ lokaṃ na pratiprajānāti | atha yo haivaitam agniṃ sāvitraṃ veda | sa evāsmāl lokāt pretya | ātmānaṃ veda | ayam aham asmīti |

{ 3.10.11.2} sa svaṃ lokaṃ pratiprajānāti | eṣa u caivainaṃ { uvevainaṃ} tat sāvitraḥ | svargaṃ lokam abhivahati | ahorātrair vā idaṃ sayugbhiḥ kriyate | itirātrāyā-adīkṣiṣata | itirātrāya vratam upāgur iti | tāni hānevaṃ viduṣaḥ | amuṣmin- loke śevadhiṃ dhayanti | dhītaṃ haiva sa śevadhim anuparaiti | atha yo haivaitam agniṃ sāvitraṃ veda |

{ 3.10.11.3} tasya haivāhorātrāṇi | amuṣmin-loke śevadhiṃ na dhayanti | adhītaṃ haiva sa śevadhim anu paraiti | bharadvājo ha tribhir āyurbhir brahmacaryam uvāsa | taṃ ha jīrṇiṃ sthaviraṃ śayānam | indra upavrajya-uvāca | bharadvāja | yat te caturtham āyur dadyām | kim enena kuryā iti | brahmacaryam eva-enena careyam iti ha-uvāca |

{ 3.10.11.4} taṃ ha trīn girirūpān avijñātān iva darśayāṃ cakāra | teṣāṃ ha-ekaikasmān muṣṭin ādade | sa ha-uvāca | bharadvāja-ity āmantrya | vedā vā ete | anantā vai vedāḥ | etad vā etais tribhir āyurbhir anvavocathāḥ | atha ta itarad ananūktam eva | ehīmaṃ viddhi | ayaṃ vai sarvavidyā-iti |

{ 3.10.11.5} tasmai haitam agniṃ sāvitram uvāca | taṃ sa viditvā | amṛto bhūtvā | svargaṃ lokam iyāya | ādityasya sāyujyam | amṛto haiva bhūtvā | svargaṃ lokam eti | ādityasya sāyujyam | ya evaṃ veda | eṣo eva trayī vidyā |

{ 3.10.11.6} yāvantaṃ ha vai trayyā vidyayā lokaṃ jayati | tāvantaṃ lokaṃ jayati | ya evaṃ veda | agner vā etāni nāmadheyāni | agner eva sāyujyaṃ salokatām āpnoti | ya evaṃ veda | vāyor vā etāni nāmadheyāni | vāyor eva sāyujyaṃ salokatām āpnoti | ya evaṃ veda | indrasya vā etāni nāmadheyāni |

{ 3.10.11.7} indrasyaiva sāyujyaṃ salokatām āpnoti | ya evaṃ veda | bṛhaspater vā etāni nāmadheyāni | bṛhaspater eva sāyujyaṃ salokatām āpnoti | ya evaṃ veda | prajāpater vā etāni nāmadheyāni | prajāpater eva sāyujyaṃ salokatām āpnoti | ya evaṃ veda | brahmaṇo vā etāni nāmadheyāni | brahmaṇa eva sāyujyaṃ salokatām āpnoti | 1 ya evaṃ veda | 2 sa vā eṣo 'gnir apakṣapuccho vāyur eva | 3 tasyāgnir mukham | 4 asāv ādityaḥ siraḥ | 5 sa yad ete devate antareṇa | 6 tat sarvaṃ sīvyati | 7 tasmāt sāvitraḥ |







prapāṭhaka: 11 anuvāka 1 nāciketāgnicayanam { 3.11.1.1} loko 'si svargo 'si | ananto 'sy apāro 'si | akṣito 'sy akṣayyo 'si | tapasaḥ pratiṣṭhā | tvayīdam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartā viśvasya janayitā | taṃ tvā-upadadhe kāmadugham akṣitam | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.2} tapo 'si loke śritam | tejasaḥ pratiṣṭhā | tvayīdam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartṛ viśvasya janayitṛ | tat tvā-upadadhe kāmadugham akṣitam | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.3} tejo 'si tapasi śritam | samudrasya pratiṣṭhā | tvayīdam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartṛ viśvasya janayitṛ | tat tvā-upadadhe kāmadugham akṣitam | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.4} samudro 'si tejasi śritaḥ | apāṃ pratiṣṭhā | tvayīdam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartā viśvasya janayitā | taṃ tvā-upadadhe kāmadugham akṣitam | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.5} āpaḥ stha samudre śritāḥ | pṛthivyāḥ pratiṣṭhā yuṣmāsu | idam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartryo viśvasya janayitryaḥ | tā va upadadhe kāmadughā akṣitāḥ | prajāpatistvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.6} pṛthivyasy apsu śritā | agneḥ pratiṣṭhā | tvayīdam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartrī viśvasya janayitrī | tāṃ tvā-upadadhe kāmadughām akṣitām | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.7} agnir asi pṛthivyāṃ śritaḥ | antarikṣasya pratiṣṭhā | tvayīdam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartā viśvasya janayitā | taṃ tvā-upadadhe kāmadugham akṣitam | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.8} antarikṣam asy agnau śritam | vāyoḥ pratiṣṭhā | tvayīdam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartṛ viśvasya janayitṛ | tat tvā-upadadhe kāmadugham akṣitam | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.9} vāyur asy antarikṣe śritaḥ | divaḥ pratiṣṭhā | tvayīdam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartā viśvasya janayitā | taṃ tvā-upadadhe kāmadugham akṣitam | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.10} dyaur asi vāyau śritā | ādityasya pratiṣṭhā | tvayīdam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartrī viśvasya janayitrī | tāṃ tvā-upadadhe kāmadughām akṣitām | prajāpatistvā sādayatu tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.11} ādityo 'si divi śritaḥ | candramasaḥ pratiṣṭhā | tvayīdam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartā viśvasya janayitā | taṃ tvā-upadadhe kāmadugham akṣitam | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.12} candramā asy āditye śritaḥ | nakṣatrāṇāṃ pratiṣṭhā | tvayīdam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartā viśvasya janayitā | taṃ tvā-upadadhe kāmadugham akṣitam | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.13} nakṣatrāṇi stha candramasi śritāni | saṃvatsarasya pratiṣṭhā yuṣmāsu | idam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartṝṇi viśvasya janayitṝṇi | tāni va upadadhe kāmadughāny akṣitāni | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.14} saṃvatsaro 'si nakṣatreṣu śritaḥ | ṛtūnāṃ pratiṣṭhā | tvayīdam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartā viśvasya janayitā | taṃ tvā-upadadhe kāmadugham akṣitam | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.15} ṛtavaḥ stha saṃvatsare śritāḥ | māsānāṃ pratiṣṭhā yuṣmāsu | idam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartāro viśvasya janayitāraḥ | tān va upadadhe kāmadughān akṣitān | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.16} māsāḥ stha-ṛtuṣu śritāḥ | ardhamāsānāṃ pratiṣṭhā yuṣmāsu | idam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartāro viśvasya janayitāraḥ | tān va upadadhe kāmadughān akṣitān | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.17} ardhamāsāḥ stha māsu śritāḥ | ahorātrayoḥ pratiṣṭhā yuṣmāsu | idam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartāro viśvasya janayitāraḥ | tān va upadadhe kāmadughān akṣitān | prajāpatis tvā sadayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.18} ahorātre stho 'rdhamāseṣu śrite | bhūtasya pratiṣṭhe bhavyasya pratiṣṭhe | yuvayor idamantaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartryau viśvasya janayitryau | te vām upadadhe kāmadughe akṣite | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.19} paurṇamāsy aṣṭakā-amāvāsyā | annādāḥ sthānnadugho yuṣmāsu | idam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartryo viśvasya janayitryaḥ | tā va upadadhe kāmadughā akṣitāḥ | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.20} rāḍ asi bṛhatī śrīr asīndrapatnī dharmapatnī | viśvaṃ bhūtam anu prabhūtā | tvayīdam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartrī viśvasya janayitrī | tāṃ tvā-upadadhe kāmadughām akṣitām | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

{ 3.11.1.21} ojo 'si saho 'si | balamasi bhrājo 'si | devānāṃ dhāmāmṛtam | amartyas tapojāḥ | tvayīdam antaḥ | viśvaṃ yakṣaṃ viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ viśvaṃ subhūtam | viśvasya bhartā viśvasya janayitā | taṃ tvā-upadadhe kāmadugham akṣitam | prajāpatis tvā sādayatu | tayā devatayā-aṅgirasvad dhruvā sīda |

anuvāka 2 nāciketāgnicayanam { 3.11.2.1} tvam agne rudro asuro maho divaḥ | tvaṃ śardho mārutaṃ pṛkṣa īśiṣe | tvaṃ vātair aruṇair yāsi śaṅgayaḥ | tvaṃ pūṣā vidhataḥ pāsi nu tmanā | devā deveṣu śrayadhvam | prathamā dvitīyeṣu śrayadhvam | dvitīyās tṛtīyeṣu śrayadhvam | tṛtīyāś caturtheṣu śrayadhvam | caturthāḥ pañcameṣu śrayadhvam | pañcamāḥ ṣaṣṭheṣu śrayadhvam |

{ 3.11.2.2} ṣaṣṭhāḥ saptameṣu śrayadhvam | saptamā aṣṭameṣu śrayadhvam | aṣṭamā navameṣu śrayadhvam | navamā daśameṣu śrayadhvam | daśamā ekādaśeṣu śrayadhvam | ekādaśā dvādaśeṣu śrayadhvam | dvādaśās trayodaśeṣu śrayadhvam | trayodaśāś caturdaśeṣu śrayadhvam | caturdaśāḥ pañcadaśeṣu śrayadhvam | pañcadaśāḥ ṣoḍaśeṣu śrayadhvam |

{ 3.11.2.3} ṣoḍaśāḥ saptadaśeṣu śrayadhvam | saptadaśā aṣṭādaśeṣu śrayadhvam | aṣṭādaśā ekānnaviṃśeṣu śrayadhvam | ekānnaviṃśā viṃśeṣu śrayadhvam | viṃśā ekaviṃśeṣu śrayadhvam | ekaviṃśā dvāviṃśeṣu śrayadhvam | dvāviṃśās trayoviṃśeṣu śrayadhvam | trayoviṃśāś caturviṃśeṣu śrayadhvam | caturviṃśāḥ pañcaviṃśeṣu śrayadhvam | pañcaviṃśāḥ ṣaḍviṃśeṣu śrayadhvam |
